Do not try any of the scripts in a production cluster: they may cause some disruption or, even worse, a complete cluster outage. Cilium works with any distribution conformant with “vanilla” Kubernetes and is often even packaged and included by default with many Kubernetes distributions and services. Not everyone has the financial or computing resources to run a Kubernetes cluster.
    在本书中，你会看到用于安装、测试和配置 Cilium 的代码片段和脚本。这意味着你需要有可用的 Kubernetes 集群。**不要在生产环境的集群中尝试任何这些脚本**：它们可能造成中断，甚至更糟——导致整个集群宕机。Cilium 兼容任何符合"原生" Kubernetes 规范的发行版，并且经常被许多 Kubernetes 发行版和服务默认打包包含。并非所有人都有足够的财务或计算资源来运行 Kubernetes 集群。
    Therefore, the environment we will recommend for the vast majority of the exercises in the book is based on Kubernetes in Docker (kind), a popular tool used to run Kubernetes clusters locally in Docker containers. We recommend kind over alternatives because it is a lightweight, CNCF-certified conformant Kubernetes installer that works across multiple platforms (Linux, macOS, and Windows). If you prefer another lightweight Kubernetes solution, check the documentation to make sure it’s compatible with Cilium.
    因此，对于本书中绝大多数练习，我们推荐的环境基于 Kubernetes in Docker（kind）——一个流行的本地运行 Kubernetes 集群的工具。我们推荐 kind 而非其他替代方案，因为它轻量、通过 CNCF 认证兼容 Kubernetes，并且跨多平台（Linux、macOS 和 Windows）工作。如果你偏好其他轻量级 Kubernetes 方案，请查阅文档以确保它与 Cilium 兼容。

    Kubernetes in Docker (kind)
    Kubernetes in Docker（kind）

    This chapter assumes you have Docker or another container runtime installed, as kind requires it to launch Kubernetes clusters. If you need help installing Docker, refer to the official documentation for your operating system. If you don’t have kind installed yet, let’s do that now. For those of you on macOS, you can use Homebrew to install it:
    本章假设你已经安装了 Docker 或其他容器运行时，因为 kind 需要它来启动 Kubernetes 集群。如果你需要帮助安装 Docker，请参考对应操作系统的官方文档。如果你还没有安装 kind，现在来安装它。macOS 用户可以使用 Homebrew 安装：
    代码（原文）
    $ brew install kind
    The instructions for other platforms can be found on the kind website.
    其他平台的安装说明可以在 kind 官网上找到。

    Installing the Cilium CLI
    安装 Cilium CLI

    To manage and inspect Cilium deployments, we will use the Cilium CLI. It should be available in your operating system’s package manager. For example, you can run the following Homebrew command to install it on macOS:
    为了管理和检查 Cilium 部署，我们将使用 Cilium CLI。它应该可以在你操作系统的包管理器中获取。例如，你可以在 macOS 上运行以下 Homebrew 命令来安装它：
    代码（原文）
    $ brew install cilium-cli
    You can also download and install Cilium CLI from its GitHub repository. You’ll find detailed instructions in the Cilium docs. Once that’s done, verify that it has been installed correctly:
    你也可以从 Cilium CLI 的 GitHub 仓库下载并安装。在 Cilium 文档中可以找到详细说明。安装完成后，验证它是否正确安装：
    代码（原文）
    $ cilium version --client
cilium-cli: v0.18.7 compiled with go1.25.0 on darwin/arm64
cilium image (default): v1.18.1
cilium image (stable): v1.18.4

    Installing the Hubble CLI
    安装 Hubble CLI

    To observe network traffic in the cluster, we will use Hubble and its binary client, the Hubble CLI. You can also install Hubble with a package manager like Homebrew:
    为了观察集群中的网络流量，我们将使用 Hubble 及其二进制客户端——Hubble CLI。你也可以像 Homebrew 这样的包管理器来安装 Hubble：
    代码（原文）
    $ brew install hubble
    Alternatively, download and install the tarball from the Hubble GitHub repository. Again, detailed installation instructions are available in the Cilium docs. You can verify that the Hubble CLI has been installed correctly by running the following command:
    或者，从 Hubble GitHub 仓库下载并安装 tarball。同样，Cilium 文档中有详细的安装说明。你可以通过运行以下命令来验证 Hubble CLI 是否已正确安装：
    代码（原文）
    $ hubble version
hubble v1.16.4@HEAD-4b765dc compiled with go1.23.3 on darwin/arm64

    Installing Helm
    安装 Helm

    We will use the Kubernetes package manager Helm throughout the book. If you don’t have it installed on your machine yet, install it via your package manager (e.g., with brew install helm) or refer to the official Helm installation docs. We’ve used Helm v3 in this book.
    我们在整本书中都会使用 Kubernetes 包管理器 Helm。如果你还没有在机器上安装它，通过你的包管理器（例如 brew install helm）安装，或参考 Helm 官方安装文档。本书中我们使用的是 Helm v3。

    Deploying a kind Cluster
    部署 kind 集群

    The following YAML manifest, available in the book’s GitHub repository, includes the specification of the cluster:
    下面这个 YAML 清单可以在本书的 GitHub 仓库中找到，它包含了集群的规格说明：
    代码（原文）
    kind: Cluster                       # Declares a kind cluster
apiVersion: kind.x-k8s.io/v1alpha4  # Kind API version
nodes:
- role: control-plane               # One control-plane node
- role: worker                      # Two worker nodes
- role: worker                      # Two worker nodes
networking:
disableDefaultCNI: true           # Disables kindnetd prior to Cilium install
    The most notable aspect of the configuration is the networking.disableDefaultCNI: true setting. It specifies that the cluster must be deployed without kind’s built-in CNI (kindnetd). As you’ll recall from Chapter 1, a CNI plugin is required in order for the cluster to host and connect applications. We intentionally disable kind’s default built-in CNI so that we can install Cilium instead in the next section.
    这个配置最值得注意的方面是 networking.disableDefaultCNI: true 这个设置。它指定集群部署时不使用 kind 内置的 CNI（kindnetd）。回忆一下第 1 章，集群要能够承载和连接应用，就必须有 CNI 插件。我们刻意禁用了 kind 的默认内置 CNI，以便在下一节改为安装 Cilium。
    You can find all the YAML manifests you will use in this chapter to deploy and test Cilium in the chapter03 directory of the book’s GitHub repository. Navigate to this folder before proceeding.
    你在本章中用来部署和测试 Cilium 的所有 YAML 清单，都可以在本书 GitHub 仓库的 chapter03 目录下找到。继续之前，请先进入该文件夹。
    Let’s now deploy the kind cluster. To do that, you’ll use the following command:
    现在让我们部署 kind 集群。为此，你需要使用以下命令：
    代码（原文）
    $ kind create cluster --config kind.yaml
Creating cluster "kind" ...
✓ Ensuring node image (kindest/node:v1.33.4)
✓ Preparing nodes
✓ Writing configuration
✓ Starting control-plane
✓ Installing StorageClass
✓ Joining worker nodes
Set kubectl context to "kind-kind"
You can now use your cluster with:
kubectl cluster-info --context kind-kind
Thanks for using kind!
    With the cluster deployed, let’s use kubectl to verify its status:
    集群部署完成后，让我们用 kubectl 来验证它的状态：
    代码（原文）
    $ kubectl get nodes
NAME                 STATUS     ROLES           AGE   VERSION
kind-control-plane   NotReady   control-plane   6m    v1.33.4
kind-worker          NotReady   <none>          6m    v1.33.4
kind-worker2         NotReady   <none>          6m    v1.33.4
    The preceding terminal output shows that three nodes (one control plane and two workers) have been deployed, but they are in the NotReady state. This indicates the nodes are unhealthy and will not accept workloads. You may have already guessed the reason, but let’s confirm it by inspecting the status of one of our worker nodes:
    上面的终端输出显示已经部署了三个节点（一个控制平面节点和两个工作节点），但它们都处于 NotReady 状态。这表示节点不健康，不会接受工作负载。你可能已经猜到原因了，但让我们通过检查其中一个工作节点的状态来确认：
    代码（原文）
    $ kubectl get nodes kind-worker -o yaml
[...]
message: >
container runtime network not ready: NetworkReady=false
reason: NetworkPluginNotReady
message: Network plugin returns error: cni plugin not initialized
    As you can see, since no network plugin has been deployed, the kubelet reports it is not ready to run pods. We’ll resolve that by installing the Cilium CNI plugin in the next section.
    如你所见，由于尚未部署任何网络插件，kubelet 报告它还没准备好运行 Pod。我们将在下一节通过安装 Cilium CNI 插件来解决这个问题。

    Installing Cilium
    安装 Cilium

    Cilium supports multiple installation methods, but the two most common are using the Cilium CLI (cilium install) for quick setup and environment autodetection or Helm, which provides explicit control and aligns better with GitOps and long-term operations.
    Cilium 支持多种安装方式，但最常用的两种是：使用 Cilium CLI（cilium install）进行快速设置和环境自动检测，以及使用 Helm——它提供显式控制，更适合 GitOps 和长期运维。

    Installing Cilium Using the Cilium CLI
    使用 Cilium CLI 安装 Cilium

    The Cilium CLI simplifies the initial experience by inspecting your current Kubernetes context and generating a set of recommended Helm values based on your environment. To preview the configuration without performing the installation, run:
    Cilium CLI 通过检查你当前的 Kubernetes context，并根据你的环境生成一组推荐的 Helm values，来简化首次使用体验。要在不执行实际安装的情况下预览配置，运行：
    代码（原文）
    $ cilium install --dry-run-helm-values
    For your kind cluster, this should return:
    对于你的 kind 集群，这应该会返回：
    代码（原文）
    cluster:
name: kind-kind
ipam:
mode: kubernetes
operator:
replicas: 1
routingMode: tunnel
tunnelProtocol: vxlan
    In this configuration:
    在这个配置中：
    • The Cilium operator will be deployed with a single replica.
      Cilium Operator 将以单副本部署。
    • The IPAM mode is set to Kubernetes Host Scope mode.
      IPAM 模式设置为 Kubernetes Host Scope 模式。
    • The routing mode is set to encapsulation, with VXLAN tunnels used to encapsu‐
      路由模式设置为封装模式，使用 VXLAN 隧道在节点之间封装...
    late traffic between nodes. We’ll explore IPAM and routing modes in more detail in Chapters 4 and 5. For now, note that Cilium will form VXLAN tunnels between all nodes to enable cross-node pod communication, and it will allocate pod IPs from the subnets assigned to each node by Kubernetes. To verify the PodCIDRs Kubernetes has assigned, run:
    ...节点之间的流量。我们将在第 4 章和第 5 章更详细地探讨 IPAM 和路由模式。目前需要注意的是，Cilium 会在所有节点之间创建 VXLAN 隧道来实现跨节点的 Pod 通信，并且它会从 Kubernetes 分配给每个节点的子网中分配 Pod IP。要验证 Kubernetes 分配的 PodCIDR，运行：
    代码（原文）
    $ kubectl get nodes kind-worker -o yaml | yq .spec.podCIDR
10.244.1.0/24
$ kubectl get nodes kind-worker2 -o yaml | yq .spec.podCIDR
10.244.2.0/24
    When you deploy the sample app shortly, you will observe that Cilium assigns IPs from these ranges. Running cilium install now will install Cilium using the autogenerated settings:
    当你稍后部署示例应用时，你会看到 Cilium 从这些地址范围中分配 IP。现在运行 cilium install 会使用自动生成的设置来安装 Cilium：
    代码（原文）
    $ cilium install
Auto-detected Kubernetes kind: kind
Using Cilium version 1.18.4
Autodetected cluster name: kind-kind
Autodetected kube-proxy has been installed

    Installing Cilium Using Helm (Recommended)
    使用 Helm 安装 Cilium（推荐）

    Helm is already widely used to manage other Kubernetes applications, such as Prometheus, Grafana, cert-manager, and so on. Using it for Cilium as well ensures a consistent deployment workflow and makes it easier to integrate with continuous integration/continuous deployment (CI/CD) and GitOps practices. First, set up the Helm repository:
    Helm 已被广泛用于管理其他 Kubernetes 应用，如 Prometheus、Grafana、cert-manager 等。用它来管理 Cilium 也能确保一致的部署工作流，并更容易与持续集成/持续部署（CI/CD）和 GitOps 实践集成。首先，设置 Helm 仓库：
    代码（原文）
    $ helm repo add cilium https://helm.cilium.io/
    Then install Cilium with Helm:
    然后用 Helm 安装 Cilium：
    代码（原文）
    $ helm install cilium cilium/cilium -n kube-system \
--version 1.18.4 \
--values helm-values.yaml
    You should see something like this:
    你应该会看到类似这样的输出：
    代码（原文）
    NAME: cilium
LAST DEPLOYED: Fri Dec 19 09:03:46 2025
NAMESPACE: kube-system
STATUS: deployed
REVISION: 1
TEST SUITE: None
NOTES:
You have successfully installed Cilium with Hubble.
    You’ll find Helm values files to use throughout the rest of the book in the folders corresponding to each chapter in the book’s GitHub repository. Use them with the Helm install command shown earlier to deploy Cilium with the appropriate settings. Instead of using a values file, you can pass values inline:
    在本书 GitHub 仓库的各章节对应文件夹中，你会找到在本书剩余部分使用的 Helm values 文件。用它们配合前面展示的 Helm install 命令，以合适的设置部署 Cilium。你也可以不使用 values 文件，而是以内联方式传递值：
    代码（原文）
    $ helm upgrade --install cilium cilium/cilium -n kube-system \
--version 1.18.4 \
--set cluster.name=kind-kind \
--set ipam.mode=kubernetes \
--set operator.replicas=1 \
--set routingMode=tunnel \
--set tunnelProtocol=vxlan
    The cilium install command also accepts these Helm-style --set flags for customization. By default, both cilium install and Helm will install the latest stable version of Cilium.1 However, you can explicitly specify a version to ensure consistency across environments or to avoid unexpected changes. This is especially useful in production, where you may want to pin versions and manage upgrades intentionally.
    cilium install 命令也接受这些 Helm 风格的 --set 标志来自定义。默认情况下，cilium install 和 Helm 都会安装 Cilium 的最新稳定版本。1 不过，你可以显式指定一个版本，以确保跨环境的一致性或避免意外变更。这在生产环境中尤其有用，因为你可能希望锁定版本并有意管理升级。
    1To be precise, the Cilium CLI will install the version of Cilium that was most recent when the CLI was built.
    1 准确地说，Cilium CLI 会安装在构建该 CLI 时最新的 Cilium 版本。
    To install a specific version, use the --version flag:
    要安装特定版本，使用 --version 标志：
    代码（原文）
    $ helm upgrade --install cilium cilium/cilium \
-n kube-system --version 1.18.4 --values helm-values.yaml
    You can watch the progress of the installation by typing cilium status --wait. After a couple of minutes, Cilium should be successfully installed. Verify the installation with the cilium status command:
    你可以通过输入 cilium status --wait 来观察安装进度。过几分钟后，Cilium 应该就能成功安装了。用 cilium status 命令验证安装结果：
    代码（原文）
    $ cilium status
/¯¯\
/¯¯\__/¯¯\    Cilium:             OK
\__/¯¯\__/    Operator:           OK
/¯¯\__/¯¯\    Envoy DaemonSet:    OK
\__/¯¯\__/    Hubble Relay:       disabled
\__/       ClusterMesh:        disabled
DaemonSet              cilium                   Desired: 3, Ready: 3/3
DaemonSet              cilium-envoy             Desired: 3, Ready: 3/3
Deployment             cilium-operator          Desired: 1, Ready: 1/1
Containers:            cilium                   Running: 3
cilium-envoy             Running: 3
cilium-operator          Running: 1
clustermesh-apiserver
hubble-relay
Cluster Pods:          3/3 managed by Cilium
[...]
    In the output, you can see how the components we described in Chapter 2 are deployed. Since the Cilium agent and Envoy components are both deployed through a DaemonSet, there is an instance of each deployed on every cluster node. As you might have seen in the earlier cilium install --dry-run-helm-values output, a single Cilium operator was deployed as part of the deployment. You can verify this with the following kubectl commands:
    从输出中，你可以看到我们在第 2 章介绍的组件是如何部署的。由于 Cilium Agent 和 Envoy 组件都通过 DaemonSet 部署，集群中的每个节点上都会各有一个实例。正如你在前面 cilium install --dry-run-helm-values 输出中看到的，Cilium Operator 是以单副本作为部署的一部分来部署的。你可以用下面的 kubectl 命令来验证：
    代码（原文）
    $ kubectl get -n kube-system daemonset cilium cilium-envoy
NAME           DESIRED   READY   NODE SELECTOR
cilium         3         3       kubernetes.io/os=linux
cilium-envoy   3         3       kubernetes.io/os=linux
$ kubectl get -n kube-system deployment cilium-operator
NAME              READY   AVAILABLE
cilium-operator   1/1     1

    Deploying a Sample Application
    部署示例应用

    Let’s now deploy a sample application. The following example is a common first application deployed in Kubernetes: the lightweight NGINX web server app. We’ll use the nginx-deployment.yaml manifest file from the book’s GitHub repository:
    现在让我们部署一个示例应用。下面这个例子是在 Kubernetes 中常见的第一个应用：轻量级的 NGINX Web 服务器应用。我们将使用本书 GitHub 仓库中的 nginx-deployment.yaml 清单文件：
    代码（原文）
    apiVersion: apps/v1
kind: Deployment
metadata:
name: nginx-deployment
spec:
replicas: 1
selector:
matchLabels:
app.kubernetes.io/name: nginx
template:
metadata:
labels:
app.kubernetes.io/name: nginx
spec:
nodeSelector:
kubernetes.io/hostname: kind-worker2
containers:
- name: nginx
image: nginx
    “Pinning” the nginx-server pod to a specific worker node (kind-worker2) isn’t a best practice in production, but it’s useful here to illustrate internode traffic, which Cilium handles through VXLAN tunneling in the selected configuration mode. Let’s deploy the manifest:
    将 nginx-server Pod"固定"到特定工作节点（kind-worker2）在生产环境中并不是最佳实践，但在这里它能很好地说明跨节点流量——在我们选择的配置模式下，Cilium 通过 VXLAN 隧道处理跨节点流量。让我们部署这个清单：
    代码（原文）
    $ kubectl apply -f nginx-deployment.yaml
deployment.apps/nginx-deployment created
    We can verify that the pod was scheduled on the expected node and that it was assigned an IP address by running the following command:
    我们可以运行以下命令来验证 Pod 是否被调度到了预期的节点，以及它是否被分配了 IP 地址：
    代码（原文）
    $ kubectl get pods -o wide
NAME                               READY   IP             NODE
nginx-deployment-979f5455f-tjd2w   1/1     10.244.2.127   kind-worker2
    Next, we’ll deploy a pod on a different worker node (kind-worker), using the following manifest (netshoot-client-pod.yaml). We’re using a networking utility called netshoot, which we’ll use throughout the book because it includes many helpful networking tools. Given that it’s a debugging utility, it typically runs once and exits.
    接下来，我们将使用另一个清单（netshoot-client-pod.yaml）在不同的工作节点（kind-worker）上部署一个 Pod。我们使用一个名为 netshoot 的网络工具集，因为它包含了许多有用的网络工具，所以我们会在全书中使用它。由于它是一个调试工具，通常运行一次后就会退出。
    To keep it running, we instruct it to run sleep infinity:
    为了让它保持运行，我们让它执行 sleep infinity：
    代码（原文）
    apiVersion: v1
kind: Pod
metadata:
name: netshoot-client
labels:
app.kubernetes.io/name: netshoot-client
spec:
nodeSelector:
kubernetes.io/hostname: kind-worker
containers:
- name: netshoot
image: nicolaka/netshoot
command: ["sleep", "infinity"]
    Let’s deploy the pod and verify that it was scheduled on the kind-worker node:
    让我们部署这个 Pod 并验证它是否被调度到了 kind-worker 节点：
    代码（原文）
    $ kubectl apply -f netshoot-client-pod.yaml
pod/netshoot-client created
$ kubectl get pods -o wide
NAME                               READY   IP            NODE
netshoot-client                    1/1     10.244.1.67   kind-worker
    We should also check that we can connect from netshoot-client (located on kindworker) to the nginx-server (located on kind-worker2):2
    我们还应该检查从 netshoot-client（位于 kind-worker）到 nginx-server（位于 kind-worker2）是否可以连通：2
    代码（原文）
    $ kubectl exec pod/netshoot-client -- curl -s http://10.244.2.127
<!DOCTYPE html>
<html>
<head>
<title>Welcome to nginx!</title>
[OUTPUT TRUNCATED]
    To verify HTTP connectivity, we really only need the HTTP status code. In our case, we expect a 200 to show a successful access. The following curl command will output just the status code:
    要验证 HTTP 连通性，我们实际上只需要 HTTP 状态码。在我们的例子中，期望返回 200 表示访问成功。下面这条 curl 命令只输出状态码：
    代码（原文）
    $ kubectl exec pod/netshoot-client -- \
curl -s -o /dev/null \
-w "%{http_code}\n" \
http://10.244.2.127
    We’ve successfully deployed two pods across different nodes in the cluster, confirmed IP address assignments, and verified successful internode connectivity using Cilium’s VXLAN-based tunneling. We’ll dive deeper into VXLAN and the datapath in Chapter 5.
    我们已经成功在集群的不同节点上部署了两个 Pod，确认了 IP 地址分配，并验证了通过 Cilium 基于 VXLAN 的隧道实现了跨节点的成功连通性。我们将在第 5 章更深入地探讨 VXLAN 和数据路径。
    2Use the pod IP you got from the previous step, which probably won’t match ours.
    2 使用你在上一步得到的 Pod IP，它很可能和我们的不一样。
    While a successful connection from a client to the IP of a destination pod is a good first step, you might already know it’s not the preferred approach in Kubernetes. Kubernetes provides the service abstraction as a deterministic method for accessing applications. Upon creation, Kubernetes will assign a service a virtual IP address. When a client connects to the service’s virtual IP, traffic will be forwarded to one of the service’s endpoints.
    虽然从客户端直接连接到目标 Pod 的 IP 是成功连接的好的第一步，但你可能已经知道这不是在 Kubernetes 中推荐的做法。Kubernetes 提供了 service 抽象作为访问应用的确定性方法。创建 Service 时，Kubernetes 会为其分配一个虚拟 IP 地址。当客户端连接到 Service 的虚拟 IP 时，流量会被转发到该 Service 的某一个端点上。
    Kubernetes uses Endpoints and EndpointSlices to track the backends of a service. These objects are used for load-balancing decisions for each service. EndpointSlices, in particular, offer a more scalable way to track backends across large clusters. By contrast, Cilium uses CiliumEndpoints (CEPs) and CiliumEndpointSlices (CESs) to support network routing and policy enforcement (you will see how CEPs are used later in this book).
    Kubernetes 使用 Endpoint 和 EndpointSlice 来跟踪 Service 的后端。这些对象用于每个 Service 的负载均衡决策。EndpointSlice 尤其提供了一种在大型集群中跟踪后端的更具扩展性的方式。相比之下，Cilium 使用 CiliumEndpoint（CEP）和 CiliumEndpointSlice（CES）来支持网络路由和策略执行（你会在本书后面看到 CEP 是如何使用的）。
    To better understand how Kubernetes services work, let’s create one using the following manifest (nginx-service.yaml). This service listens on TCP port 80 and loadbalances traffic randomly toward pods labeled app.kubernetes.io/name: nginx:
    为了更好地理解 Kubernetes Service 的工作方式，让我们使用下面的清单（nginx-service.yaml）来创建一个。这个 Service 在 TCP 端口 80 上监听，并将流量随机负载均衡到带有标签 app.kubernetes.io/name: nginx 的 Pod：
    代码（原文）
    apiVersion: v1
kind: Service
metadata:
name: nginx-service
spec:
selector:
app.kubernetes.io/name: nginx
ports:
- protocol: TCP
port: 80
targetPort: 80
type: ClusterIP
    Once you’ve deployed the service, inspect it:
    部署好 Service 后，检查一下：
    代码（原文）
    $ kubectl get svc nginx-service -o yaml
apiVersion: v1
kind: Service
metadata:
[...]
name: nginx-service
namespace: default
spec:
clusterIP: 10.96.242.74
clusterIPs:
- 10.96.242.74
internalTrafficPolicy: Cluster
ipFamilies:
- IPv4
ipFamilyPolicy: SingleStack
ports:
- port: 80
protocol: TCP
targetPort: 80
selector:
app.kubernetes.io/name: nginx
sessionAffinity: None
type: ClusterIP
    The service type is ClusterIP, which means it’s only accessible from within the cluster. It was assigned a cluster-internal IP (10.96.242.74) in the 10.96.0.0/16 CIDR range (the default IPv4 service subnet in kind). You can confirm that the service is working by sending an HTTP request to its ClusterIP:
    Service 类型是 ClusterIP，这意味着它只能从集群内部访问。它被分配了一个集群内部 IP（10.96.242.74），位于 10.96.0.0/16 CIDR 范围内（kind 中默认的 IPv4 Service 子网）。你可以通过向其 ClusterIP 发送 HTTP 请求来确认 Service 正常工作：
    代码（原文）
    $ kubectl exec netshoot-client -- curl http://10.96.242.74
<h1>Welcome to nginx!</h1>
    ClusterIP is one of several Kubernetes service types, alongside NodePort and LoadBalancer. Chapter 6 will provide a refresher on service types. While ClusterIP is used for internal communication, most of your applications will need to be accessible from outside the cluster. Chapter 7 will introduce Ingress and Gateway API for handling external traffic, and Chapter 10 will explain the networking options available to expose your applications.
    ClusterIP 是 Kubernetes 中几种 Service 类型之一，其他还包括 NodePort 和 LoadBalancer。第 6 章会回顾各种 Service 类型。虽然 ClusterIP 用于内部通信，但你的大多数应用都需要能够从集群外部访问。第 7 章将介绍用于处理外部流量的 Ingress 和 Gateway API，第 10 章将说明暴露应用可用的网络选项。
    Although the client connects to the service IP, the request is forwarded to one of the backend pods selected by the service. The backend pod generates the response, but Cilium’s eBPF-based service handling rewrites it so that the client still sees the service IP as the source. Kubernetes tracks which pods belong to the service through EndpointSlices, which list the IPs of all pods matching the service’s label selector:
    虽然客户端连接的是 Service IP，但请求会被转发到 Service 选中的某个后端 Pod。后端 Pod 生成响应，但 Cilium 基于 eBPF 的 Service 处理会对响应进行重写，使客户端仍然看到 Service IP 作为源地址。Kubernetes 通过 EndpointSlice 跟踪哪些 Pod 属于该 Service——EndpointSlice 中列出了所有匹配 Service 标签选择器的 Pod 的 IP：
    代码（原文）
    $ kubectl get endpointslices.discovery.k8s.io nginx-service-66q4l
NAME                  ADDRESSTYPE   PORTS   ENDPOINTS      AGE
nginx-service-66q4l   IPv4          80      10.244.2.127   2m13s
    If you scale the deployment, Kubernetes automatically updates the endpoints:
    如果你对 Deployment 进行扩缩容，Kubernetes 会自动更新 endpoints：
    代码（原文）
    $ kubectl scale deployment nginx-deployment --replicas=2
deployment.apps/nginx-deployment scaled
    After scaling, both pods will be running and the EndpointSlices object will reflect both IPs:
    扩容后，两个 Pod 都会运行起来，EndpointSlice 对象会反映两个 IP：
    代码（原文）
    $ kubectl get pods -l app.kubernetes.io/name=nginx
NAME                               READY   STATUS    RESTARTS   AGE
nginx-deployment-979f5455f-9pm7d   1/1     Running   0          4m29s
nginx-deployment-979f5455f-xw8nz   1/1     Running   0          13s
$ kubectl get endpointslices.discovery.k8s.io nginx-service-66q4l
NAME                  ADDRESSTYPE   PORTS   ENDPOINTS                   AGE
nginx-service-66q4l   IPv4          80      10.244.2.127,10.244.2.189   14m
    Behind the scenes, these endpoint IPs are what Kubernetes uses to route traffic to pods. In many clusters, kube-proxy runs on every node and programs iptables, IPVS, or nftables rules to implement service forwarding. In our setup, kube-proxy is installed, but Cilium still intercepts ClusterIP traffic and resolves services through its eBPF datapath. kube-proxy remains present on the node, although it is not used for service lookup in this case. You can confirm that kube-proxy is running with:
    在幕后，这些 endpoint IP 就是 Kubernetes 用来将流量路由到 Pod 的。在许多集群中，kube-proxy 在每个节点上运行，通过编程 iptables、IPVS 或 nftables 规则来实现 Service 转发。在我们的设置中，kube-proxy 已被安装，但 Cilium 仍然会拦截 ClusterIP 流量，并通过其 eBPF 数据路径来解析 Service。kube-proxy 仍然存在于节点上，尽管此时它不用于 Service 查找。你可以用以下命令确认 kube-proxy 正在运行：
    代码（原文）
    $ kubectl get -n kube-system daemonset kube-proxy
NAME         DESIRED   READY   NODE SELECTOR
kube-proxy   3         3       kubernetes.io/os=linux
    In Chapter 6, you’ll learn how Cilium replaces kube-proxy entirely using eBPF, eliminating the need for iptables, IPVS, or nftables. Another benefit of leveraging a service is the automatic service DNS name generation. You can verify successful DNS resolution by accessing the nginx-service name from the client:
    在第 6 章中，你将学习 Cilium 如何完全用 eBPF 替代 kube-proxy，从而不再需要 iptables、IPVS 或 nftables。使用 Service 的另一个好处是自动生成 Service DNS 名称。你可以从客户端访问 nginx-service 名称来验证 DNS 解析是否成功：
    代码（原文）
    $ kubectl exec netshoot-client -- curl nginx-service.default.svc.cluster.local
<h1>Welcome to nginx!</h1>
    Given that the client and the NGINX servers reside in the same namespace, you only need the service name, and curl http://nginx-service would also be successful.
    由于客户端和 NGINX 服务器位于同一个命名空间，你只需要 Service 名称即可，curl http://nginx-service 也会成功。
    Cilium is not responsible for DNS activities; however, as we saw briefly in Chapter 2, it sometimes leverages a component called the DNS Proxy when users define domain-based network policies. You will learn more about that in Chapter 13.
    Cilium 不负责 DNS 活动；不过，正如我们在第 2 章简要介绍的，当用户定义基于域名的网络策略时，Cilium 有时会利用一个叫做 DNS Proxy 的组件。你将在第 13 章了解更多相关内容。

    Securing the Application with Cilium Network Policies
    使用 Cilium 网络策略保护应用

    With the application deployed and reachable, let’s apply a network policy to restrict access, allowing only trusted systems to access it. We will create a network policy that allows access only from the netshoot-client to the nginx-server pod, blocking the traffic from unauthorized systems. First, to help us demonstrate network policies, we’ll create another client pod with the following manifest (unauthorized-client.yaml):
    应用已部署并可达，接下来让我们应用一条网络策略来限制访问，只允许可信系统访问它。我们将创建一条网络策略，只允许 netshoot-client 访问 nginx-server Pod，阻止来自未授权系统的流量。首先，为了帮助演示网络策略，我们先创建另一个客户端 Pod，使用以下清单（unauthorized-client.yaml）：
    代码（原文）
    apiVersion: v1
kind: Pod
metadata:
name: unauthorized-client
spec:
containers:
- name: netshoot
image: nicolaka/netshoot
command: ["sleep", "infinity"]
    Deploy it and verify it has access to the nginx-server prior to deploying network policies. You might already be familiar with Kubernetes network policies. Cilium network policies (CNPs) build on them and provide more granular controls. Chapter 12 will cover the concepts of identity, endpoints, labels, and how to construct CNPs. For now, just know that they’re defined through the use of Kubernetes labels. Next, take a look at the following network policy (policy.yaml):
    部署它，并在部署网络策略之前验证它能否访问 nginx-server。你可能已经熟悉 Kubernetes 网络策略了。Cilium 网络策略（CNP）在此基础上构建，提供更细粒度的控制。第 12 章将介绍身份、端点、标签等概念，以及如何构建 CNP。目前，你只需要知道它们是通过 Kubernetes 标签来定义的。接下来，看看下面这条网络策略（policy.yaml）：
    代码（原文）
    apiVersion: cilium.io/v2
kind: CiliumNetworkPolicy
metadata:
name: ch03-policy
namespace: default
spec:
endpointSelector:
matchLabels:
app.kubernetes.io/name: nginx
ingress:
- fromEndpoints:
- matchLabels:
app.kubernetes.io/name: netshoot-client
toPorts:
- ports:
- port: "80"
protocol: TCP
    endpointSelector selects the pods the policy applies to: here we’re selecting pods with the label app.kubernetes.io/name: nginx. ingress defines the traffic direction the policy applies to. In this instance we are defining a rule that will authorize traffic to the pods with the app.kubernetes.io/name: nginx label (egress would instead apply to traffic from the pods). fromEndpoints determines which sources pods are allowed to connect to (here, those with the app.kubernetes.io/name: netshoot-client label). toPorts specifies which TCP/UDP ports are permitted. By default, all egress and ingress traffic is allowed for all endpoints. When an endpoint is selected by a network policy, it transitions to a default deny state, where only explicitly allowed traffic is permitted. Given that our rule has an ingress section, the endpoint goes into default deny mode for ingress. In other words, when traffic enters endpoints with the app.kubernetes.io/name: nginx label, only traffic from clients with the app.kubernetes.io/name: netshoot-client label going to port 80 will be allowed. Any other traffic to these endpoints will be denied.
    endpointSelector 选择策略应用到哪些 Pod：这里我们选择带有标签 app.kubernetes.io/name: nginx 的 Pod。ingress 定义策略作用的流量方向。在这个例子中，我们定义的规则会授权发往带有 app.kubernetes.io/name: nginx 标签的 Pod 的流量（egress 则作用于来自这些 Pod 的流量）。fromEndpoints 决定允许哪些源 Pod 发起连接（这里是带有 app.kubernetes.io/name: netshoot-client 标签的 Pod）。toPorts 指定允许哪些 TCP/UDP 端口。默认情况下，所有端点的所有出站和入站流量都是被允许的。当一个端点被网络策略选中时，它会转入默认拒绝状态，只有显式允许的流量才会放行。由于我们的规则包含 ingress 部分，该端点会对入站流量进入默认拒绝模式。换句话说，当流量进入带有 app.kubernetes.io/name: nginx 标签的端点时，只允许来自带有 app.kubernetes.io/name: netshoot-client 标签的客户端访问 80 端口的流量。发往这些端点的任何其他流量都会被拒绝。
    Isovalent offers a free online Network Policy Editor tool to help create and manage network policies.
    Isovalent 提供了免费的在线 Network Policy Editor 工具，帮助创建和管理网络策略。
    Let’s check that our netshoot-client has the right label and that unauthorizedclient does not:
    让我们检查我们的 netshoot-client 是否有正确的标签，以及 unauthorized-client 是否没有：
    代码（原文）
    $ kubectl get pods --show-labels
NAME                     READY   LABELS
netshoot-client          1/1     app.kubernetes.io/name=netshoot-client
nginx-server             1/1     app.kubernetes.io/name=nginx
unauthorized-client      1/1     <none>
    Once you’ve deployed the policy (policy.yaml) with kubectl, verify that the unauthorized-client pod can no longer access the NGINX server, while the netshoot-client still can:
    用 kubectl 部署策略（policy.yaml）后，验证 unauthorized-client Pod 是否不再能够访问 NGINX 服务器，而 netshoot-client 仍然可以：
    代码（原文）
    $ kubectl exec unauthorized-client -- curl --max-time 3 -s \
http://nginx-service
command terminated with exit code 28
$ kubectl exec netshoot-client -- curl --max-time 3 -s http://nginx-service
<h1>Welcome to nginx!</h1>
    Unlike standard Kubernetes network policies, Cilium network policies can apply at up to layer 7. This is particularly useful for securing API calls, such as those made over REST or gRPC. With layer 7 network policies, you can restrict access based on HTTP methods, headers, and paths. Traffic matching layer 7 rules is directed to Envoy, which performs HTTP inspection before forwarding or denying the request. We introduced Envoy in Chapter 2, and you will see more use cases for it in Chapter 7. By default, the NGINX server we deployed includes an error page named 50x.html. The current CiliumNetworkPolicy lets you access it with no restrictions:
    与标准 Kubernetes 网络策略不同，Cilium 网络策略可以应用到第 7 层。这对于保护 REST 或 gRPC 等 API 调用特别有用。借助第 7 层网络策略，你可以根据 HTTP 方法、请求头和路径来限制访问。匹配第 7 层规则的流量会被导向 Envoy，由 Envoy 在转发或拒绝请求之前执行 HTTP 检查。我们在第 2 章介绍过 Envoy，你会在第 7 章看到更多它的使用场景。默认情况下，我们部署的 NGINX 服务器包含一个名为 50x.html 的错误页面。当前的 CiliumNetworkPolicy 允许你无限制地访问它：
    代码（原文）
    $ kubectl exec -t netshoot-client -- curl http://nginx-service/50x.html
<html><title>Error</title><body>
<h1>An error occurred.</h1>
<p>Sorry, the page you are looking for is currently unavailable.</p>
<em>Faithfully yours, nginx.</em>
</body>
    You can refine the previous policy to allow access only to the index.html page via the HTTP GET method by adding a layer 7 rule such as the following:
    你可以通过添加如下第 7 层规则来细化之前的策略，只允许通过 HTTP GET 方法访问 index.html 页面：
    代码（原文）
    toPorts:
- ports:
- port: "80"
protocol: TCP
rules:
http:
- method: "GET"
path: "/index.html"
    Deploy the updated network policy (policy-with-l7.yaml), overwriting the previously configured policy, and then verify access to /50x.html and /index.html. You should see that the former is now blocked, while the latter is still allowed:
    部署更新后的网络策略（policy-with-l7.yaml），覆盖之前配置的策略，然后验证对 /50x.html 和 /index.html 的访问。你应该会看到前者现在被阻止了，而后者仍然被允许：
    代码（原文）
    $ kubectl exec netshoot-client -- curl -s http://nginx-service/50x.html
Access denied
$ kubectl exec netshoot-client -- curl http://nginx-service/index.html
<h1>Welcome to nginx!</h1>
    Notice that this time you receive an “access denied” error, rather than a timeout as you saw earlier when you used a layer 3 policy. While enforced layer 3 rules lead to dropped packets, enforced layer 7 rules applied by Envoy return deny codes for the application protocol. In this case, we’re parsing HTTP traffic, so it returns 403 for a denied request. Now that we have set up a security policy to control who can access our application, let’s observe its effect on actual flows.
    注意这次你收到了"access denied"错误，而不是之前使用第 3 层策略时看到的超时。第 3 层规则被执行时会导致数据包被丢弃，而由 Envoy 执行的第 7 层规则则会针对应用协议返回拒绝代码。在这个例子中，我们解析的是 HTTP 流量，所以它对被拒绝的请求返回 403。现在我们已经设置了安全策略来控制谁可以访问我们的应用，接下来让我们观察它对流的实际影响。

    Monitoring the Application with Hubble
    使用 Hubble 监控应用

    Hubble is the observability subsystem of Cilium. You cannot use Hubble without Cilium because it leverages the data collected through eBPF to provide deep visibility into network traffic and policy enforcement. Chapter 15 will cover Hubble in depth. By default, Hubble runs on the individual node on which the Cilium agent runs. This confines the network insights to the traffic observed by the local Cilium agent. As mentioned in Chapter 2, the Hubble Relay component provides a cluster-wide view of network flows. To enable Hubble Relay, use the following command:
    Hubble 是 Cilium 的可观测性子系统。没有 Cilium 你无法使用 Hubble，因为它利用通过 eBPF 收集的数据来提供对网络流量和策略执行的深度可见性。第 15 章将深入介绍 Hubble。默认情况下，Hubble 在运行 Cilium Agent 的每个节点上单独运行。这将网络洞察限定在本地 Cilium Agent 观察到的流量范围内。正如第 2 章提到的，Hubble Relay 组件提供了集群范围内的网络流视图。要启用 Hubble Relay，使用以下命令：
    代码（原文）
    $ helm upgrade cilium cilium/cilium --version 1.18.4 \
--namespace kube-system \
--reuse-values \
--set hubble.relay.enabled=true
    Alternatively, you can use the Cilium CLI command cilium hubble enable to enable Hubble. Once it is enabled, verify that the status of Hubble Relay is OK using
    或者，你可以使用 Cilium CLI 命令 cilium hubble enable 来启用 Hubble。启用后，通过运行下面的命令确认 Hubble Relay 的状态为 OK：
    代码（原文）
    cilium status.
    To access the observability data collected by Hubble, you can use the port-forward feature of Kubernetes. The following command connects the Hubble client to the local port 4245, enabling you to access the Hubble Relay service in your Kubernetes cluster. The & lets you run Hubble in the background rather than having to keep the terminal open:
    要访问 Hubble 收集的可观测性数据，你可以使用 Kubernetes 的 port-forward 功能。以下命令将 Hubble 客户端连接到本地端口 4245，使你能够访问 Kubernetes 集群中的 Hubble Relay 服务。& 让 Hubble 在后台运行，这样你就不必一直开着终端：
    代码（原文）
    $ cilium hubble port-forward &
[1] 71325
Hubble Relay is available at 127.0.0.1:4245
$
    You can now connect to the Hubble server using the Hubble UI or CLI. Let’s start by using the CLI. First, verify the status of Hubble using hubble status. This command provides a real-time snapshot of Hubble’s health, including metrics such as the rate of flows observed per second and the volume of flows currently stored locally:
    你现在可以使用 Hubble UI 或 CLI 连接到 Hubble 服务器。我们先从 CLI 开始。首先，用 hubble status 验证 Hubble 的状态。这个命令提供了 Hubble 健康状况的实时快照，包括每秒观察到的流速率和当前本地存储的流数量等指标：
    代码（原文）
    $ hubble status
Healthcheck (via localhost:4245): Ok
Current/Max Flows: 16,380/16,380 (100.00%)
Flows/s: 9.43
Connected Nodes: 3/3
    You can list all the nodes that Hubble Relay is connected to with the following command:
    你可以用以下命令列出 Hubble Relay 已连接的所有节点：
    代码（原文）
    $ hubble list nodes
NAME                           STATUS      FLOWS/S   CURRENT/MAX-FLOWS
kind-kind/kind-control-plane   Connected   1.05      4095/4095 (100.00%)
kind-kind/kind-worker          Connected   1.06      4095/4095 (100.00%)
kind-kind/kind-worker2         Connected   4.32      4095/4095 (100.00%)
    Next, use hubble observe to list all the flows collected by Hubble:
    接下来，用 hubble observe 列出 Hubble 收集的所有流：
    代码（原文）
    $ hubble observe
Mar 14 11:22:41.654: default/netshoot-client:50976 (ID:18901) ->
kube-system/coredns-668d6bf9bc-qdkn6:53 (ID:23236)
to-overlay FORWARDED (UDP)
Mar 14 11:22:41.655: default/netshoot-client:50976 (ID:18901) <-
kube-system/coredns-668d6bf9bc-qdkn6:53 (ID:23236)
to-endpoint FORWARDED (UDP)
Mar 14 11:22:41.656: default/netshoot-client:36998 (ID:18901) ->
default/nginx-deployment-979f5455f-xw8nz:80 (ID:4437)
policy-verdict:L3-L4 INGRESS ALLOWED (TCP Flags: SYN)
Mar 14 11:22:41.657: default/netshoot-client:36998 (ID:18901) ->
default/nginx-deployment-979f5455f-xw8nz:80 (ID:4437)
http-request FORWARDED
(HTTP/1.1 GET http://nginx-service/index.html)
Mar 14 11:22:41.658: default/netshoot-client:36998 (ID:18901) <-
default/nginx-deployment-979f5455f-xw8nz:80 (ID:4437)
http-response FORWARDED
(HTTP/1.1 200 1ms (GET http://nginx-service/index.html))
    The (verbose) output shows traffic from the netshoot-client pod, including a DNS query to CoreDNS (UDP/53) in a different namespace, followed by a successful HTTP request to the NGINX service. The arrow direction (<- or ->) indicates the flow of traffic, and you can see which namespace (default or kube-system) it originated from and where it was sent. The logs also show the identity of each pod and whether traffic was forwarded, dropped, or denied by a network policy. Notice the to-overlay flag in the first flow log, indicating the path the packet has followed: since the DNS server was located on a different node from the client, the traffic was sent via the VXLAN tunnel.
    （详细的）输出显示了来自 netshoot-client Pod 的流量，包括到另一个命名空间中 CoreDNS（UDP/53）的 DNS 查询，随后是到 NGINX 服务的成功 HTTP 请求。箭头方向（<- 或 ->）表示流量的流向，你可以看到流量起源于哪个命名空间（default 或 kube-system），以及被送往哪里。日志还显示了每个 Pod 的身份，以及流量是被转发、丢弃还是被网络策略拒绝。注意第一条流日志中的 to-overlay 标志，它表示数据包经过的路径：由于 DNS 服务器和客户端位于不同节点，流量是通过 VXLAN 隧道发送的。
    Sending DNS traffic outside of the node is inefficient, as it adds latency and creates unnecessary internode traffic. In Chapter 8 you will learn how Cilium can optimize DNS by intercepting these requests locally, using a feature called Local Redirect Policy.
    将 DNS 流量发送到节点外部是低效的，因为它会增加延迟并产生不必要的跨节点流量。在第 8 章中，你将学习 Cilium 如何通过一个叫做 Local Redirect Policy 的功能，在本地拦截这些 DNS 请求来进行优化。
    You can use filters to narrow down the Hubble output. For example, to list only the flows originating from netshoot-client, use:
    你可以使用过滤器来缩小 Hubble 输出范围。例如，要只列出源自 netshoot-client 的流，使用：
    代码（原文）
    $ hubble observe --from-pod netshoot-client
Jul  2 10:39:11.325: default/netshoot-client:42214 (ID:7613) ->
default/nginx-deployment-979f5455f-qcbjh:80 (ID:42054)
to-overlay FORWARDED (TCP Flags: SYN)
Jul  2 10:39:11.325: default/netshoot-client:42214 (ID:7613) ->
default/nginx-deployment-979f5455f-qcbjh:80 (ID:42054)
policy-verdict:L3-L4 INGRESS ALLOWED (TCP Flags: SYN)
Jul  2 10:39:11.325: default/netshoot-client:42214 (ID:7613) ->
default/nginx-deployment-979f5455f-qcbjh:80 (ID:42054)
to-proxy FORWARDED (TCP Flags: SYN)
    The output was recorded shortly after executing the kubectl exec netshoot-client-- curl nginx-service/index.html command. The to-proxy confirms that traffic was redirected to the Envoy proxy because of the layer 7 policy we put in place. You can combine multiple filters. For example, here we show only traffic from netshoot-client to the HTTP path "/index.html":
    这个输出是在执行 kubectl exec netshoot-client -- curl nginx-service/index.html 命令后不久录制的。to-proxy 确认流量因为我们之前配置的第 7 层策略而被重定向到了 Envoy 代理。你可以组合多个过滤器。例如，下面我们只显示从 netshoot-client 到 HTTP 路径 "/index.html" 的流量：
    代码（原文）
    $ hubble observe --from-pod netshoot-client --http-path "/index.html"
Jul  2 10:41:55.108: default/netshoot-client:59110 (ID:7613) ->
default/nginx-deployment-979f5455f-qcbjh:80 (ID:42054)
http-request FORWARDED
(HTTP/1.1 GET http://nginx-service/index.html)
    Another useful Hubble CLI filter, --label, lets you select flows based on labels:
    另一个有用的 Hubble CLI 过滤器 --label 让你可以根据标签选择流：
    代码（原文）
    $ hubble observe --label app.kubernetes.io/name=nginx
Mar 14 11:28:32.002: 10.244.1.218:51932 (ID:18901) ->
default/nginx-deployment-979f5455f-9pm7d:80 (ID:4437)
to-endpoint FORWARDED (TCP Flags: ACK, FIN)
Mar 14 11:28:32.002: 10.244.1.218:51932 (host) <-
default/nginx-deployment-979f5455f-9pm7d:80 (ID:4437)
to-stack FORWARDED (TCP Flags: ACK, FIN)
    You can also filter based on the network policy verdict and check only the flows where a network policy decision was made. You can find the full list of available filters
    你还可以根据网络策略裁决进行过滤，只查看做出了网络策略决策的流。你可以在... 中找到所有可用过滤器的完整列表
    代码（原文）
    with hubble observe --help:
$ hubble observe -t policy-verdict
Mar 21 11:18:51.917: default/netshoot-client:34898 (ID:11661) ->
default/nginx-deployment-979f5455f-bnxh7:80 (ID:5834)
policy-verdict:L3-L4 INGRESS ALLOWED (TCP Flags: SYN)
Mar 21 12:12:27.525: default/netshoot-client-worker2:48768 (ID:11661) ->
default/nginx-deployment-979f5455f-bnxh7:80 (ID:5834)
policy-verdict:L3-L4 INGRESS ALLOWED (TCP Flags: SYN)
Mar 21 12:15:40.016: default/unauthorized-client:41212 (ID:8087) <>
default/nginx-deployment-979f5455f-bnxh7:80 (ID:5834)
policy-verdict:none INGRESS DENIED (TCP Flags: SYN)
    The Hubble CLI provides useful insights into what’s happening in your cluster, but as we mentioned in Chapter 2, the Hubble UI can also consume the flows aggregated from Hubble Relay. This is a good solution for those of you who prefer a more visual representation of the network traffic. You can use the following Helm command to enable the Hubble UI:
    Hubble CLI 为你的集群中正在发生的事情提供了有用的洞察，但正如我们在第 2 章提到的，Hubble UI 也可以消费从 Hubble Relay 聚合而来的流。这是那些偏好更直观的网络流量可视化展示的用户的不错选择。你可以使用以下 Helm 命令来启用 Hubble UI：
    代码（原文）
    $ helm upgrade cilium cilium/cilium --version 1.18.4 \
--namespace kube-system \
--reuse-values \
--set hubble.relay.enabled=true \
--set hubble.ui.enabled=true
    You can also enable the UI via the Cilium CLI (with cilium hubble enable --ui), but you’ll first need to temporarily disable Hubble using cilium hubble disable. This is necessary because the Hubble UI cannot be enabled when Hubble is already running. Once you enable the Hubble UI, you will see a Hubble UI deployment being created and a Hubble UI pod consisting of a backend container and a frontend UI:
    你也可以通过 Cilium CLI 启用 UI（使用 cilium hubble enable --ui），但首先需要用 cilium hubble disable 临时禁用 Hubble。这是因为当 Hubble 已经在运行时无法启用 Hubble UI。一旦启用了 Hubble UI，你会看到一个 Hubble UI Deployment 被创建，以及一个由后端容器和前端 UI 组成的 Hubble UI Pod：
    代码（原文）
    $ kubectl -n kube-system get deployment/hubble-ui
NAME        READY   UP-TO-DATE   AVAILABLE   AGE
hubble-ui   1/1     1            1           2m22s
$ kubectl get pod hubble-ui-76d4965bb6-m8s78 -n kube-system -o yaml
containerStatuses:
- containerID:
[...]
name: backend
- containerID:
[...]
name: frontend
    To access the Hubble UI, we will once again use the Kubernetes port-forward functionality (note that the Hubble UI can also be accessed over Ingress):
    要访问 Hubble UI，我们再次使用 Kubernetes 的 port-forward 功能（注意 Hubble UI 也可以通过 Ingress 访问）：
    代码（原文）
    $ cilium hubble ui &
Opening "http://localhost:12000" in your browser...
    A browser should automatically open. If it doesn’t, navigate to http://localhost:12000 in your preferred browser. You will see the Hubble UI landing page. Just like the Hubble CLI, the Hubble UI can display flows filtered by namespace. Select the default namespace, and you’ll see the same flows you observed earlier, now shown as a visual service map that illustrates communication between services. Click on any of the pods to see the equivalent representation to hubble observe -pod. For example, you can see the flows to and from netshoot-client in Figure 3-1. You might also notice an “L7 info” column in the user interface, populated with details such as HTTP path, method, and latency. This is a side effect—and added benefit—of the layer 7–based Cilium network policy we enforced earlier. Layer 7 rules not only provide more granular control, they also enhance observability. As Envoy intercepts traffic, it enriches the network flow information with application-layer context.
    浏览器应该会自动打开。如果没有，请在你偏好的浏览器中访问 http://localhost:12000。你会看到 Hubble UI 的登陆页面。和 Hubble CLI 一样，Hubble UI 可以显示按命名空间过滤的流。选择 default 命名空间，你会看到之前观察到的那些流，现在以可视化服务映射的形式展示，描绘了服务之间的通信。点击任意一个 Pod，你会看到与 hubble observe -pod 等效的表示。例如，你可以在图 3-1 中看到与 netshoot-client 相关的入站和出站流。你可能还会注意到用户界面中有一个"L7 info"列，其中填充了 HTTP 路径、方法和延迟等详细信息。这是我们之前强制执行的基于第 7 层的 Cilium 网络策略带来的副作用——也是附加好处。第 7 层规则不仅提供了更细粒度的控制，还增强了可观测性。当 Envoy 拦截流量时，它会用应用层上下文丰富网络流信息。

    Summary
    小结

    We hope this chapter has demonstrated that getting started with Cilium is straightforward. You’ve deployed Cilium in a Kubernetes cluster and taken your first steps toward using it to secure, observe, and manage application traffic. In the next chapter, we’ll take a closer look at how Cilium handles IP address management and establishes network connectivity for pods. You’ll learn how to choose the right IPAM mode for your environment and discover the implications of using IPv4, IPv6, or both.
    希望本章能够证明，开始使用 Cilium 是很简单的。你已经在 Kubernetes 集群中部署了 Cilium，并迈出了使用它来保护、观察和管理应用流量的第一步。在下一章中，我们将更深入地了解 Cilium 如何处理 IP 地址管理以及为 Pod 建立网络连通性。你将学习如何为你的环境选择合适的 IPAM 模式，并发现使用 IPv4、IPv6 或两者兼有的含义。

    第 4 章
    IP Address Management
    IP 地址管理

    Before deploying Cilium in a Kubernetes cluster, platform engineers need to make two fundamental networking decisions: how pod IP addresses are allocated, and whether to use IPv4, IPv6, or both. Selecting the right IP address management mode is essential, as changing it later may disrupt connectivity for running workloads or may not be supported at all. Broader organizational requirements typically drive the choice between IPv4, IPv6, or dual stack, but the options also demand careful consideration by platform teams during initial cluster design. This chapter will help you make an informed decision. We will start by examining the different IPAM modes supported by Cilium and highlighting the pros and cons of each. We will then explore the dual stack and IPv6 deployment models. Sample manifests are included for you to try in your own test environments; you can find all the YAML manifests in the chapter04 directory of the book’s GitHub repository.
    在 Kubernetes 集群中部署 Cilium 之前，平台工程师需要做出两个基础性的网络决策：Pod IP 地址如何分配，以及是使用 IPv4、IPv6 还是两者并用。选择合适的 IP 地址管理模式至关重要，因为事后修改可能会中断正在运行的工作负载的连通性，或者根本不支持。更广泛的组织需求通常决定了在 IPv4、IPv6 或双栈之间的选择，但平台团队在集群初始设计阶段也需要仔细考虑各种选项。本章将帮助你做出明智的决策。我们先考察 Cilium 支持的不同 IPAM 模式并突出各自的优缺点，然后探讨双栈和 IPv6 部署模型。示例清单供你在自己的测试环境中尝试，所有 YAML 清单都可以在本书 GitHub 仓库的 chapter04 目录中找到。

    IPv4 and IPv6 Support in Cilium
    Cilium 中的 IPv4 与 IPv6 支持

    We’ll start with a brief overview of IPv4 and IPv6 support before we explain how IP addresses are assigned and how pods communicate with each other. Kubernetes supports single-stack IPv4, single-stack IPv6, and dual-stack networking, depending on the CNI you choose to implement the required functionality. With single-stack IPv4/IPv6 networking, each pod and service can be assigned a single IPv4 or IPv6 address. With dual-stack networking, each pod and service can be assigned both an IPv4 and an IPv6 address. This enables off-cluster egress routing (e.g., to the internet) via both IPv4 and IPv6 interfaces. When Cilium was first introduced, it supported only IPv6. This was a deliberate and forward-looking decision by the development team, who saw IPv6 as a natural fit for Kubernetes. Its expansive address space aligned well with the scale and dynamic nature of container workloads, where traditional IPv4 ranges can become limiting. However, it soon became clear that most users—and the broader cloud native ecosystem—were not yet prepared for IPv6-only clusters. Many tools, services, and operational practices were still built around IPv4. In response, the Cilium team added IPv4 support early in the project’s lifecycle, making it possible to run clusters in dual stack or IPv4-only mode. IPv6 has remained a core part of Cilium’s design. In some cases, new features have even been implemented with IPv6 support before IPv4. While most of the examples in this book use IPv4 (reflecting its continued prevalence in Kubernetes environments), we include IPv6 examples where relevant and note any important caveats or considerations along the way.
    在解释 IP 地址如何分配以及 Pod 之间如何通信之前，我们先简要概述 IPv4 和 IPv6 的支持情况。Kubernetes 支持单栈 IPv4、单栈 IPv6 和双栈网络，具体取决于你选择的 CNI 来实现所需功能。在单栈 IPv4/IPv6 网络中，每个 Pod 和 Service 可以被分配一个 IPv4 或 IPv6 地址。在双栈网络中，每个 Pod 和 Service 可以同时被分配一个 IPv4 和一个 IPv6 地址。这使得集群外的出站路由（例如访问互联网）可以同时通过 IPv4 和 IPv6 接口进行。Cilium 最初推出时只支持 IPv6，这是开发团队有意为之且具有前瞻性的决策，他们认为 IPv6 天然适合 Kubernetes。其庞大的地址空间与容器工作负载的规模和动态特性十分契合，而传统 IPv4 范围可能会受到限制。然而，很快就清楚了——大多数用户以及更广泛的云原生生态系统尚未为仅 IPv6 的集群做好准备。许多工具、服务和运维实践仍然围绕 IPv4 构建。作为回应，Cilium 团队在项目早期就添加了 IPv4 支持，使得集群可以运行在双栈或仅 IPv4 模式下。IPv6 始终是 Cilium 设计的核心部分。在某些情况下，新特性甚至在实现 IPv4 支持之前就先实现了 IPv6 支持。虽然本书中的大多数示例使用 IPv4（反映了它在 Kubernetes 环境中的持续普及），但我们会在相关地方包含 IPv6 示例，并指出重要的注意事项或考量。

    IP Address Management
    IP 地址管理

    Assigning IP addresses to entities is one of Cilium’s core responsibilities. In noncontainerized environments, you would have relied on DHCP or SLAAC (Stateless Address Autoconfiguration for IPv6) to dynamically assign IP addresses to machines. In Kubernetes, assigning IP addresses to pods is called IP address management (IPAM). The CNI plugin is often responsible for assigning an IP address to your pod. Cilium offers several options for this, which we will discuss in this section.
    为实体分配 IP 地址是 Cilium 的核心职责之一。在非容器化环境中，你会依赖 DHCP 或 SLAAC（IPv6 的无状态地址自动配置）来动态为机器分配 IP 地址。在 Kubernetes 中，为 Pod 分配 IP 地址被称为 IP 地址管理（IPAM）。CNI 插件通常负责为你的 Pod 分配 IP 地址。Cilium 提供了多种选项，本节将进行讨论。

    Pod IP Address Allocation
    Pod IP 地址分配

    Figure 4-1 illustrates the process for allocating pod IP addresses. When a new pod is added in Kubernetes, the Kubernetes scheduler assigns it to a node. The kubelet running on that node is notified and begins creating the pod’s containers. First, the kubelet checks the CNI configuration on the node, located in /etc/cni/net.d/. When Cilium is installed on the cluster, it creates a configuration file in this folder (05-cilium.conf) that instructs the kubelet on how to configure pod networking.
    图 4-1 展示了 Pod IP 地址分配的流程。当 Kubernetes 中添加一个新的 Pod 时，Kubernetes 调度器会将它分配到一个节点上。该节点上运行的 kubelet 会收到通知并开始创建 Pod 的容器。首先，kubelet 检查节点上的 CNI 配置，位于 /etc/cni/net.d/。当 Cilium 安装到集群上时，它会在此文件夹中创建一个配置文件（05-cilium.conf），指示 kubelet 如何配置 Pod 网络。
    Each pod receives an IP address from a subnet referred to as a PodCIDR. CIDR (short for Classless Inter-Domain Routing) defines IP address blocks using subnet prefixes. In Kubernetes, each node is assigned one or more PodCIDRs, and pods on that node receive their IPs from those ranges via the IPAM process. Cilium supports multiple IPAM modes. Some are specific to a cloud provider (such as AWS or Google Cloud), while others can be used across any Kubernetes environment. We won’t cover all of them in this book, but we will cover the ones we see used most commonly: Kubernetes Host Scope Uses PodCIDRs assigned to each node by Kubernetes. Simple but inflexible. Cluster Scope Cilium manages PodCIDR allocation via the operator. The default mode. Multi-Pool Allows pods to be assigned IPs from multiple IP pools based on namespaces or annotations. Ideal for multitenant setups. ENI IPAM Exclusive to Amazon Elastic Kubernetes Service (EKS) environments. Allocates pod IPs from AWS elastic network interfaces. This chapter focuses on the management of pod IP addresses.
    每个 Pod 从一个称为 PodCIDR 的子网接收 IP 地址。CIDR（无类域间路由缩写）使用子网前缀定义 IP 地址块。在 Kubernetes 中，每个节点被分配一个或多个 PodCIDR，该节点上的 Pod 通过 IPAM 过程从这些范围获得 IP。Cilium 支持多种 IPAM 模式。有些特定于某个云提供商（如 AWS 或 Google Cloud），而另一些可以在任何 Kubernetes 环境中使用。本书不会涵盖所有模式，但会介绍最常用的几种：Kubernetes Host Scope 使用 Kubernetes 为每个节点分配的 PodCIDR。简单但不够灵活。Cluster Scope Cilium 通过 Operator 管理 PodCIDR 分配。默认模式。Multi-Pool 允许根据命名空间或注解从多个 IP 池为 Pod 分配 IP。适合多租户设置。ENI IPAM 仅适用于 Amazon Elastic Kubernetes Service（EKS）环境。从 AWS 弹性网络接口分配 Pod IP。本章重点介绍 Pod IP 地址的管理。
    Other components of Kubernetes also require IP addresses. Nodes typically receive theirs via DHCP or SLAAC. Kubernetes services receive theirs either from the Kubernetes API server or, in the case of LoadBalancer services, via a cloud provider or a dedicated system. We’ll explore service networking in more detail in Chapter 6.
    Kubernetes 的其他组件也需要 IP 地址。节点通常通过 DHCP 或 SLAAC 获得 IP。Kubernetes Service 的 IP 要么从 Kubernetes API 服务器获取，对于 LoadBalancer Service 则通过云提供商或专用系统获取。我们将在第 6 章更详细地探讨 Service 网络。

    Kubernetes Host Scope IPAM Mode
    Kubernetes Host Scope IPAM 模式

    We’ll begin with Kubernetes Host Scope, the simplest IPAM mode to understand and deploy. In this mode, Kubernetes allocates a cluster-wide prefix and assigns each node a subnet from it. Cilium then assigns pod IPs from these subnets, relying on the PodCIDRs set by the Kubernetes controller manager, as illustrated in Figure 4-2.
    我们从 Kubernetes Host Scope 开始，这是最容易理解和部署的 IPAM 模式。在这种模式下，Kubernetes 分配一个集群范围的前缀，并从中为每个节点分配一个子网。Cilium 然后从这些子网为 Pod 分配 IP，依赖 Kubernetes Controller Manager 设置的 PodCIDR，如图 4-2 所示。
    This is the IPAM mode we used in the Chapter 3 walk-through (ipam.mode=kubernetes was set in the Helm values file). You can follow the same steps here, running kind create cluster –config kind.yaml to create the cluster and ciliuminstall (or the equivalent Helm config) to install Cilium. After installation (whose progress you can observe with cilium status --wait), let’s inspect the IP addressing situation. By default, kind uses 10.244.0.0/16 as the cluster-wide prefix. Kubernetes assigns /24 subnets to the various nodes in the cluster. You can verify this in your own cluster—you should see something like the following, although the assigned prefixes may differ in your environment since subnet allocation is random:
    这是我们在第 3 章操作演练中使用的 IPAM 模式（在 Helm values 文件中设置了 ipam.mode=kubernetes）。你可以在这里按相同步骤操作，运行 kind create cluster –config kind.yaml 创建集群，然后运行 cilium install（或等效的 Helm 配置）安装 Cilium。安装完成后（进度可以用 cilium status --wait 观察），我们来检查一下 IP 地址情况。默认情况下，kind 使用 10.244.0.0/16 作为集群范围的前缀。Kubernetes 为集群中的各个节点分配 /24 子网。你可以在自己的集群中验证——应该会看到类似以下的结果，不过由于子网分配是随机的，你环境中分配的前缀可能不同：
    代码（原文）
    $ kubectl get node kind-worker -o jsonpath='{.spec.podCIDRs}'
["10.244.1.0/24"]
    You can now deploy a pod and verify that it receives an IP address from the node’s subnet:
    现在你可以部署一个 Pod 并验证它从节点的子网接收了 IP 地址：
    代码（原文）
    $ kubectl apply -f netshoot-client-pod.yaml
pod/netshoot-client created
$ kubectl get pods -o wide
NAME              READY   STATUS    IP            NODE
netshoot-client   1/1     Running   10.244.1.20   kind-worker
    While Host Scope mode is straightforward, it comes with a few limitations:
    Host Scope 模式虽然简单直接，但有一些限制：
    • You cannot configure the size of the PodCIDRs allocated to each node, only
      你无法配置分配给每个节点的 PodCIDR 大小，只能
    globally for the cluster.
    在集群层面全局配置。
    • You cannot add CIDRs to or remove them from the cluster or individual nodes.
      你无法向集群或各个节点添加或移除 CIDR。
    This makes it difficult to grow the pool of IP addresses available to your cluster as it grows. Next, we’ll take a look at a more flexible IPAM mode.
    这使得随着集群的增长来扩展可用 IP 地址池变得困难。接下来，我们看看更灵活的 IPAM 模式。

    Cluster Scope IPAM Mode
    Cluster Scope IPAM 模式

    In Cluster Scope mode, Cilium (not Kubernetes) allocates PodCIDRs to nodes. As this mode doesn’t require a specific configuration of the Kubernetes cluster, it is the default IPAM option in Cilium. In Chapter 2 we explained that one of the Cilium operator’s responsibilities is IPAM. In Cluster Scope mode, the operator assigns subnets to each node by updating the CiliumNode custom resources (as illustrated in Figure 4-3).
    在 Cluster Scope 模式下，Cilium（而非 Kubernetes）为节点分配 PodCIDR。由于此模式不需要对 Kubernetes 集群进行特定配置，因此它是 Cilium 的默认 IPAM 选项。在第 2 章中我们解释过，Cilium Operator 的职责之一就是 IPAM。在 Cluster Scope 模式下，Operator 通过更新 CiliumNode 自定义资源来为每个节点分配子网（如图 4-3 所示）。
    To deploy Cilium in this mode, we’ll create a new cluster. If you previously deployed a cluster, delete it first:
    要以这种模式部署 Cilium，我们需要创建一个新集群。如果你之前已经部署了集群，先删除它：
    代码（原文）
    $ kind delete cluster --name kind
$ kind create cluster --config kind.yaml
    We recommend deleting the cluster because changing the IPAM mode in a live environment can cause persistent connectivity issues for existing workloads.
    我们建议删除集群，因为在生产环境中更改 IPAM 模式可能会导致现有工作负载出现持续性的连通性问题。
    Next, install Cilium (cilium-ipam-cluster-scope.yaml). This mode is sometimes also referred to as “Cluster Pool mode,” as you can see from the Helm value:
    接下来，安装 Cilium（cilium-ipam-cluster-scope.yaml）。这种模式有时也被称为"集群池模式"，从 Helm value 中可以看到：
    代码（原文）
    ipam:
mode: cluster-pool
    By default, Cilium uses the cluster-wide 10.0.0.0/8 prefix and assigns /24 subnets to CiliumNode resources. You can verify this in your cluster by checking the Cilium configuration with the Cilium CLI command cilium config view:
    默认情况下，Cilium 使用集群范围的 10.0.0.0/8 前缀，并为 CiliumNode 资源分配 /24 子网。你可以使用 Cilium CLI 命令 cilium config view 检查 Cilium 配置来验证这一点：
    代码（原文）
    $ cilium config view | grep cluster-pool
cluster-pool-ipv4-cidr                     10.0.0.0/8
cluster-pool-ipv4-mask-size                24
ipam                                       cluster-pool
    You can also list the CIDRs associated with each node:
    你还可以列出每个节点关联的 CIDR：
    代码（原文）
    $ kubectl get ciliumnode -o json | jq -r '.items[] |
"NODE: \(.metadata.name) CIDR: \(.spec.ipam.podCIDRs[0])"'
NODE: kind-control-plane CIDR: 10.0.0.0/24
NODE: kind-worker CIDR: 10.0.1.0/24
NODE: kind-worker2 CIDR: 10.0.2.0/24
    Deploy a sample pod (netshoot-client-pod.yaml) and check the verbose output of cilium-dbg status on the Cilium agent on the node hosting the pod:
    部署一个示例 Pod（netshoot-client-pod.yaml），然后在托管该 Pod 的节点上运行的 Cilium Agent 上检查 cilium-dbg status 的详细输出：
    代码（原文）
    $ kubectl -n kube-system exec -ti cilium-jhhpp -- cilium-dbg status --verbose
[...]
Allocated addresses:
10.0.1.235 (default/netshoot-client)
10.0.1.133 (router)
10.0.1.238 (health)
    One advantage Cluster Scope has over Kubernetes Host Scope is that it can allocate multiple CIDRs to the cluster instead of just one. This mode can thus provide more flexibility, although it doesn’t necessarily overcome the problem of IP address exhaustion if the overall address pool was too small to begin with. Let’s simulate such a scenario. Once again, starting with a new cluster is preferable, so rerun the commands you used earlier to delete the previously created one and create a new one. Use the following values for Cilium’s starting configuration (cilium-ipam-cluster-scope-small.yaml). In this example, Cilium will assign two small CIDRs (/28) to the cluster, and each node will receive a /29 subnet from one of the CIDRs. As two IPs are reserved for the cilium-host and cilium-health interfaces, each node will get six usable addresses:
    Cluster Scope 相对于 Kubernetes Host Scope 的一个优势是，它可以为集群分配多个 CIDR 而不仅仅是一个。因此这种模式可以提供更大的灵活性，尽管如果一开始整个地址池太小，它并不能必然解决 IP 地址耗尽的问题。我们来模拟一下这种场景。同样，最好从新集群开始，所以重新运行之前用过的命令来删除之前创建的集群并创建新集群。使用以下值作为 Cilium 的初始配置（cilium-ipam-cluster-scope-small.yaml）。在这个例子中，Cilium 会为集群分配两个小的 CIDR（/28），每个节点从其中一个 CIDR 接收一个 /29 子网。由于两个 IP 被预留给 cilium-host 和 cilium-health 接口，每个节点实际可用地址为六个：
    代码（原文）
    ipam:
mode: cluster-pool
operator:
clusterPoolIPv4MaskSize: 29
clusterPoolIPv4PodCIDRList:
- 10.0.42.0/28
- 10.0.84.0/28
    Let’s install Cilium in this mode and check the subnets assigned to the nodes:
    我们以这种模式安装 Cilium，然后检查分配给节点的子网：
    代码（原文）
    $ kubectl get ciliumnode -o json | jq -r '.items[] |
"NODE: \(.metadata.name) CIDR: \(.spec.ipam.podCIDRs[0])"'
NODE: kind-control-plane CIDR: 10.0.42.8/29
NODE: kind-worker         10.0.42.0/29
NODE: kind-worker2        10.0.84.8/29
    As a /28 prefix only allows two /29 allocations, the third node receives its subnet from the second block. Next, create a deployment with 10 pods (nginx-deployment-10-replicas.yaml) and pin them to kind-worker2. When you run kubectl get pods -o wide, you’ll notice that several pods remain stuck in the ContainerCreating stage—meaning they are not running properly. If you run cilium-dbg status on the Cilium agent running on kind-worker2, you’ll see that all available IP addresses have been allocated:
    由于一个 /28 前缀只能提供两个 /29 分配，第三个节点从第二个地址块获取子网。接下来，创建一个拥有 10 个 Pod 的 Deployment（nginx-deployment-10-replicas.yaml）并将它们固定到 kind-worker2。当你运行 kubectl get pods -o wide 时，会注意到有几个 Pod 卡在 ContainerCreating 阶段——意味着它们没有正常运行。如果你在 kind-worker2 上运行的 Cilium Agent 上执行 cilium-dbg status，会看到所有可用 IP 地址都已分配完毕：
    代码（原文）
    $ kubectl exec -it cilium-qrw2f -n kube-system -- cilium-dbg status
IPAM:                    IPv4: 6/6 allocated from 10.0.84.8/29,
    While this mode provides greater flexibility than the Kubernetes Host Scope mode, it still has important limitations. The address space assigned to the cluster is static: you cannot add or expand PodCIDRs once the cluster is running, which means IP exhaustion cannot be remedied without redeploying. In addition, Cluster Scope mode offers limited control over how pods receive IP addresses, as all pods draw from the same shared pools regardless of workload or namespace. To see how to get around these restrictions, let’s take a look at Multi-Pool IPAM mode.
    虽然此模式比 Kubernetes Host Scope 模式提供更大的灵活性，但它仍有重要局限。分配给集群的地址空间是静态的：集群运行后你无法添加或扩展 PodCIDR，这意味着不重新部署就无法补救 IP 耗尽问题。此外，Cluster Scope 模式对 Pod 获取 IP 的方式控制有限，因为所有 Pod 不管工作负载或命名空间如何，都从相同的共享池中获取地址。要了解如何绕过这些限制，我们来看看 Multi-Pool IPAM 模式。

    Multi-Pool IPAM Mode
    Multi-Pool IPAM 模式

    Multi-Pool is the most flexible IPAM mode in Cilium. It enables you to allocate PodCIDRs from multiple distinct pools, depending on user-defined workload properties such as annotations or namespaces. Pods on the same node can receive IP addresses from different ranges, and new CIDRs can be dynamically added to a node as needed. This flexibility makes Multi-Pool the recommended mode, especially for multitenant clusters or when granular IP addressing control is preferred. The IP allocation process used in Multi-Pool IPAM mode is described in Figure 4-4.
    Multi-Pool 是 Cilium 中最灵活的 IPAM 模式。它允许你根据用户定义的工作负载属性（如注解或命名空间）从多个独立的池中分配 PodCIDR。同一个节点上的 Pod 可以从不同范围获得 IP 地址，而且可以根据需要动态地向节点添加新的 CIDR。这种灵活性使 Multi-Pool 成为推荐的模式，特别是对于多租户集群或需要细粒度 IP 寻址控制时。图 4-4 描述了 Multi-Pool IPAM 模式使用的 IP 分配流程。
    Let’s deploy Cilium in Multi-Pool mode. First, delete the existing cluster and create a new one. Use the following values for Cilium’s starting configuration (cilium-ipammulti-pool.yaml):
    我们以 Multi-Pool 模式部署 Cilium。首先删除现有集群并创建新集群。使用以下值作为 Cilium 的初始配置（cilium-ipam-multi-pool.yaml）：
    代码（原文）
    ipam:
mode: multi-pool
operator:
autoCreateCiliumPodIPPools:
default:
ipv4:
cidrs: ["10.10.0.0/16"]
maskSize: 27
routingMode: native
endpointRoutes:
enabled: true
autoDirectNodeRoutes: true
ipv4NativeRoutingCIDR: 10.0.0.0/8
    In Chapter 3 we introduced the encapsulation routing mode (using VXLAN). This time we will use native routing, which sends packets directly between nodes without encapsulating them in tunnels. We will explain this in more detail in Chapter 5. The autoCreateCiliumPodIPPools option configures Cilium to create a default pool of IP addresses at start-up and to assign prefixes to nodes based on the ipam.operator.autoCreateCiliumPodIPPools.ipv4.maskSize value (in our case, 27). Once Cilium is installed, verify that a default pool has been created:
    在第 3 章我们介绍了封装路由模式（使用 VXLAN）。这次我们将使用原生路由，它在节点之间直接发送数据包，不通过隧道封装。我们将在第 5 章对此进行详细解释。autoCreateCiliumPodIPPools 选项配置 Cilium 在启动时创建一个默认 IP 地址池，并根据 ipam.operator.autoCreateCiliumPodIPPools.ipv4.maskSize 值（我们的例子中是 27）将前缀分配给节点。安装 Cilium 后，验证默认池已创建：
    代码（原文）
    $ kubectl get ciliumpodippools.cilium.io default -o yaml
apiVersion: cilium.io/v2alpha1
kind: CiliumPodIPPool
spec:
ipv4:
cidrs:
- 10.10.0.0/16
maskSize: 27
    Let’s also verify that the kind-worker node received a /27 subnet from this pool:
    我们也验证一下 kind-worker 节点是否从这个池中获得了 /27 子网：
    代码（原文）
    $ kubectl get ciliumnode kind-worker -o yaml | yq '.spec.ipam'
pools:
allocated:
- cidrs:
- 10.10.0.64/27
pool: default
requested:
- needed:
ipv4-addrs: 16
pool: default
    Deploy a pod on this specific node using the netshoot-client-pod.yaml manifest we used previously. When you verify its IP address, you should again see that the pod received an IP address from the allocated subnet (e.g., 10.10.0.93):
    使用之前用过的 netshoot-client-pod.yaml 清单在这个特定节点上部署一个 Pod。验证其 IP 地址时，应该再次看到该 Pod 从分配的子网获得了 IP 地址（例如 10.10.0.93）：
    代码（原文）
    $ kubectl get pods -o wide
NAME              READY   STATUS    RESTARTS   AGE   IP           NODE
netshoot-client   1/1     Running   0          24s   10.10.0.93   kind-worker
    So far, Multi-Pool mode functions similarly to Cluster Scope IPAM mode. One advantage it has over other IPAM modes, however, is that it gives you more control over how IP addresses are assigned to pods. You can force a pod—or all pods in a particular namespace—to receive IPs from a particular range. This is handy for multitenant environments. For example, suppose you have two tenants, ACME Corp. and Foobar Inc. Namespaces are often used in Kubernetes to differentiate tenants. While the IPAM modes we looked at previously are not namespace-aware, with Multi-Pool IPAM each namespace can draw pod IPs from its own defined address pool. This capability is particularly useful when traffic exits the cluster, as it helps engineers understand which tenant the traffic originated from.1
    到目前为止，Multi-Pool 模式的功能与 Cluster Scope IPAM 模式类似。但相对于其他 IPAM 模式，它的一个优势是能让你更多地控制 IP 地址如何分配给 Pod。你可以强制一个 Pod（或特定命名空间中的所有 Pod）从某个特定范围获取 IP。这对于多租户环境非常方便。例如，假设你有两个租户：ACME Corp. 和 Foobar Inc。在 Kubernetes 中，命名空间常用于区分租户。我们之前看的 IPAM 模式都不具备命名空间感知能力，而 Multi-Pool IPAM 中每个命名空间可以从自己定义的地址池获取 Pod IP。当流量离开集群时，这个能力尤其有用，因为它帮助工程师了解流量来自哪个租户。1
    In Chapter 11, you will learn more about what to consider when traffic leaves the cluster, including another method to map outbound traffic to a particular IP or interface: Egress Gateway.
    在第 11 章中，你将了解更多关于流量离开集群时需要考虑的因素，包括另一种将出站流量映射到特定 IP 或接口的方法：出口网关（Egress Gateway）。
    Let’s create namespaces for our two tenants:
    我们为两个租户创建命名空间：
    代码（原文）
    $ kubectl apply -f namespaces.yaml
namespace/acme-corp created
namespace/foobar-inc created
    We’ll then deploy two separate pools (acme-pool.yaml and foobar-pool.yaml):
    然后我们部署两个独立的池（acme-pool.yaml 和 foobar-pool.yaml）：
    代码（原文）
    apiVersion: cilium.io/v2alpha1
kind: CiliumPodIPPool
metadata:
name: foobar-pool
spec:
ipv4:
cidrs:
- 10.30.0.0/16
maskSize: 27
---
apiVersion: cilium.io/v2alpha1
kind: CiliumPodIPPool
metadata:
name: acme-pool
spec:
ipv4:
cidrs:
- 10.20.0.0/16
maskSize: 27
    To ensure that all workloads in a particular namespace receive an IP address from a particular IP pool, you can annotate the namespace with ipam.cilium.io/ip-
    要确保特定命名空间中的所有工作负载从特定 IP 池获取 IP 地址，你可以使用 ipam.cilium.io/ip-
    代码（原文）
    pool=pool_name:
$ kubectl annotate ns acme-corp ipam.cilium.io/ip-pool=acme-pool
namespace/acme-corp annotated
$ kubectl annotate ns foobar-inc ipam.cilium.io/ip-pool=foobar-pool
namespace/foobar-inc annotated
    1Although Multi-Pool IPAM can assign ranges at the namespace level, pods may also request a pool directly through an annotation. This means pool selection should be treated as an operational aid rather than an isolation mechanism.
    1虽然 Multi-Pool IPAM 可以在命名空间级别分配范围，但 Pod 也可以通过注解直接请求某个池。这意味着池选择应被视为运维辅助手段，而不是隔离机制。
    When deploying workloads, they will be allocated an IP address from the appropriate pool:
    部署工作负载时，它们会从相应的池获得 IP 地址：
    代码（原文）
    $ kubectl apply -f nginx-deployment-10-replicas.yaml -n acme-corp
deployment.apps/nginx-deployment created
$ kubectl apply -f nginx-deployment-10-replicas.yaml -n foobar-inc
deployment.apps/nginx-deployment created
    Here is a trimmed version of the output:
    以下是输出的精简版本：
    代码（原文）
    $ kubectl get pods -o wide -n acme-corp
NAME                               READY   STATUS    IP           NODE
nginx-deployment-979f5455f-5tkws   1/1     Running   10.20.0.25   kind-worker2
nginx-deployment-979f5455f-6dw7n   1/1     Running   10.20.0.6    kind-worker2
[...]
$ kubectl get pods -o wide -n foobar-inc
NAME                               READY   STATUS    IP           NODE
nginx-deployment-979f5455f-2p7mc   1/1     Running   10.30.0.28   kind-worker2
nginx-deployment-979f5455f-82g98   1/1     Running   10.30.0.13   kind-worker2
[...]
    Check the CiliumNode to see which PodCIDRs are assigned to each node:
    检查 CiliumNode，查看每个节点分配了哪些 PodCIDR：
    代码（原文）
    $ kubectl get ciliumnode kind-worker2 -o yaml | yq .spec.ipam
pools:

    If you need more IPs, Cilium dynamically assigns additional subnets to the node. For example, let’s scale up the ACME deployment from 10 replicas to 40:
    如果需要更多 IP，Cilium 会动态为节点分配额外子网。例如，我们将 ACME 部署从 10 个副本扩展到 40 个：
    代码（原文）
    $ kubectl scale -n acme-corp deployment nginx-deployment --replicas=40
deployment.apps/nginx-deployment scaled
    Note the needed field in the following output. Since the number of required IPs significantly increased, another /27 subnet from the cluster-wide 10.20.0.0/16 was allocated to the node:
    注意下面输出中的 needed 字段。由于所需 IP 数量大幅增加，节点又从集群范围的 10.20.0.0/16 中分配了另一个 /27 子网：
    代码（原文）
    $ kubectl get ciliumnode kind-worker2 -o yaml | yq '.spec.ipam'
pools:
allocated:
- cidrs:
- 10.20.0.0/27
- 10.20.0.32/27
