    代码（原文）
    networking:
kubeProxyMode: "none"
    Then, install Cilium in KPR mode. When using Helm, you’ll need to specify the Kubernetes API server IP and port (cilium-kpr-values.yaml):
    然后，以 KPR 模式安装 Cilium。使用 Helm 时，需要指定 Kubernetes API 服务器的 IP 和端口（cilium-kpr-values.yaml）：
    代码（原文）
    kubeProxyReplacement: true
k8sServiceHost: kind-control-plane
k8sServicePort: 6443
    Why? When kube-proxy is in place, it handles service traffic toward Kubernetes services, including the Kubernetes API server itself. Pods can connect to kubernetes.default.svc, and kube-proxy will DNAT the service IP (usually 10.96.0.1 in kind) to the actual API server endpoint. But given that we deployed our cluster without kube-proxy, there’s no component to program the rules that let the Cilium agent and operator talk to the API server through the service IP. To avoid a chicken-and-egg problem, Cilium needs to know how to contact the API server directly, bypassing the service abstractions. That’s why you need to set k8sServiceHost and k8sServicePort in the Helm values when enabling KPR. When using kind, we can just use kind-control-plane, as that hostname is resolvable from the worker containers. One advantage of using the Cilium CLI in this instance is that it can detect the Kubernetes API server’s IP address and port automatically from the kubeconfig:
    为什么？当 kube-proxy 存在时，它会处理发往 Kubernetes Service 的流量，包括 Kubernetes API 服务器本身。Pod 可以连接到 kubernetes.default.svc，kube-proxy 会将该 Service IP（在 kind 中通常是 10.96.0.1）DNAT 到实际的 API 服务器端点。但由于我们在集群部署时没有使用 kube-proxy，就没有组件来编程让 Cilium Agent 和 Operator 通过 Service IP 与 API 服务器通信的规则。为了避免鸡生蛋、蛋生鸡的问题，Cilium 需要知道如何绕过 Service 抽象层直接与 API 服务器通信。这就是在启用 KPR 时需要在 Helm 值中设置 k8sServiceHost 和 k8sServicePort 的原因。使用 kind 时，我们可以直接使用 kind-control-plane，因为该主机名可在 worker 容器中解析。在这种情况下使用 Cilium CLI 的一个优势是，它可以自动从 kubeconfig 检测 Kubernetes API 服务器的 IP 地址和端口：
    代码（原文）
    $ cilium install --set kubeProxyReplacement=true
Auto-detected Kubernetes kind: kind
Using Cilium version 1.18.2
Auto-detected cluster name: kind-kind
Detecting real Kubernetes API server addr and port on Kind
Auto-detected kube-proxy has not been installed
Cilium will fully replace all functionalities of kube-proxy
    Let’s first verify that KPR was enabled by checking the detailed configuration status with the Cilium agent’s cilium-dbg binary (introduced in Chapter 2):
    让我们首先通过 Cilium Agent 的 cilium-dbg 二进制文件（第 2 章介绍过）检查详细的配置状态，以验证 KPR 是否已启用：
    代码（原文）
    $ kubectl -n kube-system exec ds/cilium -- cilium-dbg status --verbose
[...]
KubeProxyReplacement Details:
Status:                 True
Socket LB:              Enabled
Socket LB Tracing:      Enabled
Socket LB Coverage:     Full
Devices:                eth0    172.18.0.3 [...] (Direct Routing)
Mode:                   SNAT
Backend Selection:      Random
Session Affinity:       Enabled
Graceful Termination:   Enabled
NAT46/64 Support:       Disabled
XDP Acceleration:       Disabled
Services:
- ClusterIP:      Enabled
- NodePort:       Enabled (Range: 30000-32767)
- LoadBalancer:   Enabled
- externalIPs:    Enabled
- HostPort:       Enabled
Annotations:
- service.cilium.io/node
- service.cilium.io/src-ranges-policy
- service.cilium.io/type
[...]
    This confirms that KPR is active. This shows the network devices where Cilium has attached its eBPF programs, with (direct) native routing enabled on eth0. Note that KPR works for both native and encapsulation routing modes. The translation mode is SNAT, which means incoming packets are source-NATed with the IPs of the nodes. We will see an alternative translation mode (DSR) in Chapter 8. Backend selection uses random choice by default. For more consistent load balancing across nodes, Cilium also supports Maglev hashing, which we cover in Chapter 8. Session affinity is enabled. This ensures that packets from the same client IP can be steered to the same backend pod. We discuss session affinity later in this chapter. Here we can see the service types that are implemented by KPR: ClusterIP, NodePort, LoadBalancer, externalIPs, and HostPort.
    这证实了 KPR 处于激活状态。输出显示了 Cilium 附加 eBPF 程序的网络设备，其中 eth0 上启用了（直接）原生路由。注意，KPR 同时适用于原生和封装路由模式。转换模式为 SNAT，这意味着传入数据包会使用节点的 IP 进行源地址转换。我们将在第 8 章看到另一种转换模式（DSR）。后端选择默认使用随机选择。为了在节点间实现更一致的负载均衡，Cilium 还支持 Maglev 哈希，我们将在第 8 章介绍。会话亲和性已启用，这确保来自同一客户端 IP 的数据包可以被导向同一个后端 Pod。本章稍后将讨论会话亲和性。这里我们可以看到 KPR 实现的 Service 类型：ClusterIP、NodePort、LoadBalancer、externalIPs 和 HostPort。
    Throughout this book, you’ll notice that some cilium-dbg commands are run against the entire Cilium DaemonSet (e.g., ds/cilium), while others target a specific Cilium agent pod. This is intentional. We use the DaemonSet form when querying clusterwide or uniform information (configuration, enabled features, or service maps), because it doesn’t matter which instance we land on. In contrast, when inspecting node-local state (like tunnel maps or health checks), we explicitly target a specific Cilium pod associated with the node of interest. Running a command against the DaemonSet causes Kubernetes to pick one of the available Cilium pods at random.
    在整本书中，你会注意到有些 cilium-dbg 命令是针对整个 Cilium DaemonSet（例如 ds/cilium）运行的，而另一些则针对特定的 Cilium Agent Pod。这是有意为之。当查询集群范围的或统一的信息（配置、已启用的特性或 Service 映射）时，我们使用 DaemonSet 形式，因为落在哪个实例上并不重要。相反，当检查节点本地状态（如隧道映射或健康检查）时，我们显式地针对与感兴趣的节点关联的特定 Cilium Pod 运行。针对 DaemonSet 运行命令会导致 Kubernetes 从可用的 Cilium Pod 中随机选择一个。
    Next, we’ll create the same httpd deployment (httpd-deployment.yaml) and service (httpd-service.yaml) we used in the previous section. Take note of the pod IPs, as well as the service IP:
    接下来，我们创建与上一节相同的 httpd Deployment（httpd-deployment.yaml）和 Service（httpd-service.yaml）。注意记下 Pod 的 IP 地址以及 Service IP：
    代码（原文）
    $ kubectl get pods -o wide
NAME                     READY   STATUS    IP           NODE
httpd-77b5fcff59-k6svw   1/1     Running   10.0.1.199   kind-worker2
httpd-77b5fcff59-vfnxt   1/1     Running   10.0.2.208   kind-worker
$ kubectl get service
NAME    TYPE        CLUSTER-IP     EXTERNAL-IP   PORT(S)   AGE
httpd   ClusterIP   10.96.45.144   <none>        80/TCP    17s
    We can check that there are no iptables rules created to handle traffic for this service by running the same command we used earlier:
    我们可以通过运行之前使用过的相同命令来检查是否没有创建任何 iptables 规则来处理该 Service 的流量：
    代码（原文）
    $ docker exec kind-worker iptables-save | grep KUBE-SEP | wc -l
    Instead, eBPF entries are populated with the healthy (active) backends. We can use cilium-dbg to inspect the current services and their backends:
    取而代之的是，eBPF 条目会填充健康（活跃）的后端。我们可以使用 cilium-dbg 来检查当前的 Service 及其后端：
    代码（原文）
    $ kubectl exec -n kube-system -it ds/cilium -- cilium-dbg service list
ID   Frontend               Service Type   Backend
6    10.96.45.144:80/TCP    ClusterIP      1 => 10.0.1.199:80/TCP (active)
2 => 10.0.2.208:80/TCP (active
    Now, let’s launch a client pod (netshoot-client.yaml) and, from its shell, send traffic to the service IP (10.96.45.144) and immediately inspect /proc/<pid>/net/tcp:
    现在，让我们启动一个客户端 Pod（netshoot-client.yaml），从它的 shell 向 Service IP（10.96.45.144）发送流量，并立即检查 /proc/<pid>/net/tcp：
    代码（原文）
    $ kubectl exec -it netshoot-client -- bash
netshoot-client:~# curl 10.96.45.144 && cat "/proc/$$/net/tcp"
<html><body><h1>It works!</h1></body></html>
sl  local_address    rem_address    st
0: DB01000A:D316    D002000A:0050  06
    When we run curl 10.96.45.144, the request goes to the service we just created. The HTML response confirms that the request successfully reached one of the httpd pods. Traffic was forwarded appropriately to the right backend, even though there are no matching iptables rules. On Linux, every process has a directory under /proc/<pid>/ (where <pid> is the process ID) that contains details about that process. The command cat "/proc/$$/net/tcp" lists the TCP connections for the current process ($$ expands to the current process ID). In that file, the rem_address field shows the remote IP address. Instead of being displayed in a human-readable format, the address appears in hexadecimal. For example, the value 0xD002000A corresponds to the IPv4 address D0.02.00.0A. Splitting it into byte pairs gives us the four octets of the address, and converting them to decimal produces 208.2.0.10. Because the kernel stores addresses in little-endian order (least significant byte first), we reverse the sequence to obtain the real address: 10.0.2.208. This IP belongs to one of the httpd pods. This confirms what is happening under the hood. Although we connected to the service IP 10.96.45.144, the eBPF program in the kernel rewrote the destination early in the networking stack, allowing us to observe the pod’s actual IP as the backend.
    当我们运行 curl 10.96.45.144 时，请求会发往我们刚创建的 Service。HTML 响应确认请求成功到达了其中一个 httpd Pod。即使没有匹配的 iptables 规则，流量也正确地转发到了对应的后端。在 Linux 中，每个进程在 /proc/<pid>/（其中 <pid> 是进程 ID）下都有一个目录，包含该进程的详细信息。命令 cat \"/proc/$$/net/tcp\" 列出当前进程的 TCP 连接（$$ 会扩展为当前进程 ID）。在该文件中，rem_address 字段显示的是远程 IP 地址。该地址不是以人类可读的格式显示，而是以十六进制形式出现。例如，值 0xD002000A 对应 IPv4 地址 D0.02.00.0A。按字节对拆分可以得到地址的四个八位组，转换为十进制后得到 208.2.0.10。由于内核以小端序存储地址（最低有效字节在前），我们需要反转序列才能得到真实的地址：10.0.2.208。该 IP 属于其中一个 httpd Pod。这证实了底层正在发生的事情。虽然我们连接的是 Service IP 10.96.45.144，但内核中的 eBPF 程序在网络栈的早期就重写了目的地址，使我们能够观察到作为后端的 Pod 的实际 IP。
    If we repeat the request, we may see another value, such as 0xC701000A, which translates to 10.0.1.199—the address of the second httpd pod:
    如果我们重复该请求，可能会看到另一个值，比如 0xC701000A，它转换后是 10.0.1.199——第二个 httpd Pod 的地址：
    代码（原文）
    netshoot-client:~# curl 10.96.45.144 && cat "/proc/$$/net/tcp"
<html><body><h1>It works!</h1></body></html>
sl  local_address    rem_address    st
0: DB01000A:ED76    D002000A:0050  06
1: DB01000A:85B0    C701000A:0050  06
    The value 0x0050 is the port number in hexadecimal. Converting 0x0050 to decimal gives us 80, which is the default port for HTTP.
    值 0x0050 是端口号的十六进制表示。将 0x0050 转换为十进制得到 80，这是 HTTP 的默认端口。
    Finally, let’s repeat the service scaling test and verify there are still no iptables rules being created:
    最后，让我们重复 Service 扩展测试，并验证仍然没有创建任何 iptables 规则：
    代码（原文）
    $ for i in {1..100}; do
kubectl expose deployment httpd --port=80 --name="httpd-$i"
done
service/httpd-1 exposed
service/httpd-2 exposed
[...]
service/httpd-99 exposed
service/httpd-100 exposed
$ docker exec kind-worker iptables-save | grep KUBE-SEP | wc -l

    Agent Availability and Datapath Resiliency
    Agent 可用性与数据路径弹性

    Cilium separates control-plane work from datapath forwarding. As you saw in Chapter 2, the Cilium agent watches the Kubernetes API server and pushes updates about changes to pods and their readiness. It computes the state and writes service and backend information into eBPF maps. eBPF programs in the Linux kernel read those maps to steer packets to pod backends. The programs themselves are stateless; they do not have any built-in knowledge of pods but rely entirely on the data maintained by the agent. This architecture provides a significant advantage: the datapath itself continues to operate as long as the kernel state is intact. This design means that service connectivity does not immediately break if the agent is restarted or temporarily unavailable. This resiliency is important because rolling upgrades, node restarts, and other operational events should not disrupt applications. Since the eBPF programs and maps live in the kernel, packets continue to be forwarded while the agent restarts. In fact, service connectivity is preserved even if the Cilium agent crashes or is temporarily removed. To demonstrate this, we will simulate the removal of the agent by modifying its DaemonSet:
    Cilium 将控制平面工作与数据路径转发分离。如第 2 章所述，Cilium Agent 监听 Kubernetes API 服务器，并推送关于 Pod 变化及其就绪状态的更新。它计算状态，并将 Service 和后端信息写入 eBPF 映射。Linux 内核中的 eBPF 程序读取这些映射，将数据包导向 Pod 后端。这些程序本身是无状态的；它们不具备关于 Pod 的内置知识，而是完全依赖 Agent 维护的数据。这一架构带来了显著优势：只要内核状态完好，数据路径本身就能持续运行。这种设计意味着，即使 Agent 被重启或暂时不可用，Service 连接也不会立即中断。这种弹性很重要，因为滚动升级、节点重启和其他运维事件不应影响应用。由于 eBPF 程序和映射存在于内核中，在 Agent 重启期间数据包仍会持续转发。事实上，即使 Cilium Agent 崩溃或被临时移除，Service 连接仍然可以保留。为了演示这一点，我们将通过修改其 DaemonSet 来模拟 Agent 的移除：
    代码（原文）
    $ kubectl -n kube-system patch ds cilium --type=merge \
-p '{"spec":{"template":{"spec":{"nodeSelector":{"foo":"bar"}}}}}'
daemonset.apps/cilium patched
    Setting the DaemonSet’s nodeSelector to an unused label removes all Cilium agent pods from the cluster:
    将 DaemonSet 的 nodeSelector 设置为一个未使用的标签，会从集群中移除所有 Cilium Agent Pod：
    代码（原文）
    $ kubectl get daemonset -n kube-system cilium
NAME     DESIRED   READY   NODE SELECTOR
cilium   0         0       foo=bar,kubernetes.io/os=linux
    Despite the Cilium agent being absent, however, traffic is still able to reach the service:
    然而，尽管 Cilium Agent 已经消失，流量仍然能够到达 Service：
    代码（原文）
    $ kubectl exec -it netshoot-client -- curl httpd
<html><body><h1>It works!</h1></body></html>
    As you can see, the datapath continues forwarding packets even though the controlplane component has disappeared. However, the design does have a limitation—while the agent is unavailable, eBPF maps are not updated to reflect changes in the cluster. If a pod on a remote node restarts during this time, the map still contains its IP address, and the datapath continues to forward traffic to it:
    如你所见，即使控制平面组件已经消失，数据路径仍在继续转发数据包。不过，这种设计确实存在一个局限——在 Agent 不可用期间，eBPF 映射不会更新以反映集群中的变化。如果远程节点上的某个 Pod 在此期间重启，映射中仍然包含它的 IP 地址，数据路径会继续向它转发流量：
    代码（原文）
    $ kubectl delete pod httpd-77b5fcff59-vfnxt
pod "httpd-77b5fcff59-vfnxt" deleted
$ curl -m 2 10.96.45.144
curl: (28) Connection timed out after 2004 milliseconds
    The timeout occurs because the datapath is trying to send traffic to the failed backend. Only when the agent is brought back online (by applying the correct label to the DaemonSet’s node selector) are the maps reconciled and stale entries removed:
    超时的发生是因为数据路径试图向已经失败的后端发送流量。只有当 Agent 恢复上线（通过向 DaemonSet 的节点选择器应用正确的标签）后，映射才会重新同步并清除过期条目：
    代码（原文）
    $ kubectl -n kube-system patch ds cilium --type=json \
-p='[{"op":"remove","path":"/spec/template/spec/nodeSelector/foo"}]'
daemonset.apps/cilium patched
$ kubectl get ds -n kube-system cilium
NAME     DESIRED   READY   NODE SELECTOR
cilium   3         3       kubernetes.io/os=linux
    Now everything is working as expected again, and all the healthy backends are listed for our service:
    现在一切又恢复正常，我们的 Service 中列出了所有健康的后端：
    代码（原文）
    $ kubectl get pods -o wide
NAME                     READY   IP
httpd-7ffb6c7dd7-8lxjg   1/1     10.244.1.233
httpd-7ffb6c7dd7-m65rg   1/1     10.244.1.183
httpd-7ffb6c7dd7-vj6r8   1/1     10.244.2.112
$ kubectl exec -n kube-system ds/cilium -- cilium-dbg service list
ID   Frontend               Service Type   Backend
6    10.96.130.157:80/TCP   ClusterIP      1 => 10.244.1.233:80/TCP
2 => 10.244.1.183:80/TCP
3 => 10.244.2.112:80/TCP
    This behavior highlights one of Cilium KPR’s design principles: the data plane is independent of the control plane. In summary, the datapath continues to forward traffic without the Cilium agent, giving operators resiliency during upgrades or restarts. But until the agent resumes, eBPF maps remain stale, which can lead to timeouts if backends fail. Minimizing agent downtime is therefore essential to maintain both connectivity and accuracy.
    这个行为凸显了 Cilium KPR 的一条设计原则：数据平面独立于控制平面。总结一下，数据路径在没有 Cilium Agent 的情况下继续转发流量，为运维人员在升级或重启期间提供了弹性。但在 Agent 恢复之前，eBPF 映射会保持过期状态，如果后端失败可能会导致超时。因此，最小化 Agent 停机时间对于同时保持连接性和准确性至关重要。

    KPR Service Behavior Support
    KPR 的 Service 行为支持

    So far we have seen how Cilium replaces kube-proxy, how it handles service load balancing at scale without the iptables burden, and how it continues to operate even if the agent is temporarily unavailable. The next question is what this means for the behaviors that users expect from services. Kubernetes supports a variety of options that influence how traffic is routed, including session affinity, external and internal traffic policies, and NodePort and LoadBalancer services. As you’ll see here, Cilium’s kube-proxy replacement implements all of these features, so users can rely on the same semantics that kube-proxy has while also gaining the efficiency of eBPF in the datapath.
    到目前为止，我们已经看到 Cilium 如何替代 kube-proxy、如何在没有 iptables 负担的情况下处理大规模 Service 负载均衡，以及即使 Agent 暂时不可用时如何继续运行。接下来的问题是，这对用户期望从 Service 获得的行为意味着什么。Kubernetes 支持多种影响流量路由的选项，包括会话亲和性、外部和内部流量策略，以及 NodePort 和 LoadBalancer Service。正如你将在这里看到的，Cilium 的 kube-proxy 替代实现了所有这些特性，因此用户可以依赖 kube-proxy 所具备的相同语义，同时还能享受到 eBPF 在数据路径中的效率。

    Session Affinity
    会话亲和性

    Services managed by Cilium’s KPR support session affinity, or sticky sessions. By default, when a client sends multiple requests to a service, the requests may be distributed across different pods. With session affinity enabled, the same client consistently reaches the same pod for the duration of the session. This behavior can be useful for applications that require consistent routing to a single pod, such as when storing in-memory state or maintaining a shopping cart. However, sticky sessions should be used sparingly. In most cases it is better to design applications so that they do not depend on affinity, since it can skew load distribution and reduce resiliency.
    Cilium 的 KPR 管理的 Service 支持 会话亲和性，或称 粘性会话。默认情况下，当客户端向 Service 发送多个请求时，这些请求可能会被分发到不同的 Pod。启用会话亲和性后，同一客户端在整个会话期间会稳定地访问同一个 Pod。这种行为对于需要稳定路由到单个 Pod 的应用（例如存储内存状态或维护购物车）很有用。不过，粘性会话应当谨慎使用。在大多数情况下，更好的做法是设计不依赖亲和性的应用，因为它可能导致负载分布不均衡并降低弹性。
    Sticky sessions can provide short-term relief for applications that expect stateful behavior, but they are best considered a workaround for workloads that are not cloud native. Wherever possible, refactor applications to avoid this dependency and allow requests to be load-balanced freely. To demonstrate how sticky sessions work, let’s create a simple deployment and service (affinity-deployment.yaml) in our KPR-enabled cluster. The backend pods will reply to the client with its own hostname, so we’ll know which pod serves each request and if the same one replies as we make multiple connections. We’ll use the client pod we deployed in the previous section and, from its shell, make a couple of requests to the podinfo service:
    粘性会话可以为期望有状态行为的应用提供短期缓解，但它们最好被视为非云原生工作负载的变通方案。尽可能重构应用以避免这种依赖，让请求可以自由负载均衡。为了演示粘性会话的工作方式，让我们在启用 KPR 的集群中创建一个简单的 Deployment 和 Service（affinity-deployment.yaml）。后端 Pod 会用自己的主机名回复客户端，这样我们就能知道每个请求由哪个 Pod 服务，以及当我们多次连接时是否由同一个 Pod 回复。我们将使用上一节中部署的客户端 Pod，从它的 shell 向 podinfo Service 发起几次请求：
    代码（原文）
    $ kubectl exec -it netshoot-client -- bash
netshoot-client:~# curl podinfo:9898
{ "hostname": "podinfo-849bfb5c8d-mp8l7" }
netshoot-client:~# curl podinfo:9898
{ "hostname": "podinfo-849bfb5c8d-bk452" }
    As shown here, the hostname in the response changes depending on which podinfo instance serves the request. By enabling sticky sessions, we can ensure the client reaches the same pod for subsequent requests. To do this, we’ll use the sessionAffinity field in the service specification. There are only two possible values: None (the default) Requests from the same client can be distributed across different pods.
    如这里所示，响应中的主机名会根据是哪个 podinfo 实例服务请求而变化。通过启用粘性会话，我们可以确保客户端的后续请求始终到达同一个 Pod。为此，我们将在 Service 规格中使用 sessionAffinity 字段。它只有两个可能的值：None（默认值） 来自同一客户端的请求可以分发到不同的 Pod。
    代码（原文）
    ClientIP
    Requests from the same client IP are consistently directed to the same pod. Let’s update the service specification so it looks like this:
    来自同一客户端 IP 的请求会稳定地导向同一个 Pod。让我们更新 Service 规格，使其如下所示：
    代码（原文）
    apiVersion: v1
kind: Service
metadata:
name: podinfo
spec:
sessionAffinity: ClientIP
selector:
app.kubernetes.io/name: podinfo
ports:
- port: 9898
    Now, let’s check again what happens when we make multiple requests from our client pod:
    现在，让我们再次检查从客户端 Pod 发起多次请求时会发生什么：
    代码（原文）
    netshoot-client:~# curl podinfo:9898
{ "hostname": "podinfo-849bfb5c8d-4v8sc", ... }
netshoot-client:~# curl podinfo:9898
{ "hostname": "podinfo-849bfb5c8d-4v8sc", ... }
    This time the hostname remains consistent across requests, indicating that we are always reaching the same pod. As this simple example shows, sticky sessions provide a way to keep traffic from a client directed to the same pod, which can help applications that rely on in-memory state. Be sure to use them only when necessary, though, since they reduce loadbalancing flexibility and resiliency.
    这次，主机名在所有请求中保持一致，表明我们始终访问同一个 Pod。正如这个简单示例所示，粘性会话提供了一种保持客户端流量指向同一个 Pod 的方式，这可以帮助依赖内存状态的应用。不过务必只在必要时使用，因为它降低了负载均衡的灵活性和弹性。

    Traffic Policies
    流量策略

    As mentioned previously, KPR supports both of the standard Kubernetes traffic management policies: externalTrafficPolicy and internalTrafficPolicy. Let’s briefly explore each of them.
    如前所述，KPR 支持 Kubernetes 的两种标准流量管理策略：externalTrafficPolicy 和 internalTrafficPolicy。让我们简要探讨一下它们各自的作用。

    internalTrafficPolicy internalTrafficPolicy

    By default, services use a cluster-wide internal traffic policy that distributes traffic randomly to all ready endpoints across the cluster (Figure 6-3).
    默认情况下，Service 使用集群范围的内部流量策略，将流量随机分发到整个集群中的所有就绪端点（图 6-3）。
    When you set internalTrafficPolicy to Local, however, Cilium only forwards internal traffic to endpoints that are on the same node (Figure 6-4).
    然而，当你将 internalTrafficPolicy 设置为 Local 时，Cilium 只会将内部流量转发到同一节点上的端点（图 6-4）。
    This option is useful for cases where locality matters, such as when you want to direct traffic to a node-local logging daemon or metrics agent, and for avoiding the latency or cross-node transfer costs of sending traffic elsewhere. Cilium itself leverages this feature for Hubble’s hubble-peer service, which uses node-local routing for observability traffic.
    这个选项对于位置性很重要的场景很有用，例如你希望将流量导向节点本地的日志守护进程或指标代理，以及避免将流量发送到其他地方带来的延迟或跨节点传输成本。Cilium 本身就在 Hubble 的 hubble-peer Service 中利用了这一特性，该 Service 使用节点本地路由来处理可观测性流量。
    If a node has no local endpoints for a service configured with internalTrafficPolicy: Local, pods on that node will see the service as having no backends, even if endpoints exist on other nodes.
    如果一个节点上没有为配置了 internalTrafficPolicy: Local 的 Service 提供本地端点，那么该节点上的 Pod 会认为该 Service 没有后端，即使其他节点上存在端点。
    We’ll cover more traffic engineering features in Chapter 8.
    我们将在第 8 章介绍更多流量工程特性。

    externalTrafficPolicy externalTrafficPolicy

    The external traffic policy controls how traffic that originates from outside the cluster is forwarded. It’s used for external-facing services like NodePort and LoadBalancer services (discussed in the following section). By default, externalTrafficPolicy (like internalTrafficPolicy) is set to Cluster. In this mode, any node that receives a connection can forward it to any ready backend in the cluster. This maximizes availability but comes with two caveats: it replaces the client source IP with the inbound node’s Cilium internal IP (in other words, the client IP is not preserved), and it might add an additional hop as traffic won’t necessarily enter the node closest to the backend. Figure 6-5 illustrates this: even if service-A only has one backend (on Node-1), both nodes will forward any traffic they receive for this service to that backend. For traffic entering via Node-2, the source IP will be changed to Node-2’s.
    外部流量策略控制来自集群外部的流量如何转发。它用于面向外部的 Service，如 NodePort 和 LoadBalancer Service（在下一节讨论）。默认情况下，externalTrafficPolicy（像 internalTrafficPolicy 一样）被设置为 Cluster。在这种模式下，任何接收到连接的节点都可以将其转发到集群中的任何就绪后端。这最大化了可用性，但有两个问题：它会用入站节点的 Cilium 内部 IP 替换客户端源 IP（换句话说，客户端 IP 不会被保留），而且可能会增加额外的一跳，因为流量不一定会从离后端最近的节点进入。图 6-5 说明了这一点：即使 service-A 只有一个后端（位于 Node-1 上），两个节点也都会将它们接收到的针对该 Service 的流量转发到那个后端。对于通过 Node-2 进入的流量，源 IP 会被改为 Node-2 的 IP。
    When externalTrafficPolicy is set to Local, on the other hand, nodes will only forward the incoming traffic to local backends (Figure 6-6).
    另一方面，当 externalTrafficPolicy 被设置为 Local 时，节点只会将传入流量转发到本地后端（图 6-6）。
    This preserves the original client IP. Just be aware that, if the node has no local backends, it will drop external traffic for that service, even if other nodes have healthy backends.
    这样可以保留原始客户端 IP。但要注意，如果节点没有本地后端，它会丢弃该 Service 的外部流量，即使其他节点有健康的后端也是如此。
    The external traffic policy on LoadBalancer services also impacts how BGP announcement functions, if used with Cilium’s LoadBalancer IP address management (LB IPAM) and BGP features. IP addresses for such services will be announced from all nodes if the policy is set to Cluster, but only from nodes with backends if it’s set to Local. See Chapter 10 for more details on BGP announcement of services in Cilium.
    LoadBalancer Service 上的外部流量策略也会影响 BGP 通告的行为（如果与 Cilium 的 LoadBalancer IP 地址管理（LB IPAM）和 BGP 特性配合使用）。如果策略设置为 Cluster，此类 Service 的 IP 地址会从所有节点通告；但如果设置为 Local，则只会从有后端的节点通告。关于 Cilium 中 Service 的 BGP 通告的更多细节，请参见第 10 章。
    KPR fully supports both internalTrafficPolicy and externalTrafficPolicy, so users can rely on the same semantics as with kube-proxy. In Chapter 8, we’ll look at alternatives such as Direct Server Return (DSR) that are useful for cases where client IP preservation matters but you also want to avoid source translation side effects. This brings us to another common requirement: exposing services outside the cluster.
    KPR 完全支持 internalTrafficPolicy 和 externalTrafficPolicy，因此用户可以依赖与 kube-proxy 相同的语义。在第 8 章中，我们将介绍直接服务器返回（DSR）等替代方案，它们适用于需要保留客户端 IP 但又希望避免源地址转换副作用的场景。这引出了另一个常见需求：将 Service 暴露到集群外部。

    External-Facing Services
    面向外部的服务

    Services have different types for different purposes. By default, a service is of type ClusterIP. This means it provides an in-cluster virtual IP and acts as an internal load balancer. However, a ClusterIP service is not accessible from outside the cluster. When workloads outside the cluster must access an application running inside Kubernetes, two other types of services can be used. The first is a service of type NodePort. A NodePort service exposes a port on every node in the cluster. Traffic sent to that port is forwarded to the application, but while the port number is the same across nodes, each node has a different IP address. Clients must therefore be aware of all node IPs and handle load balancing themselves, or an external load balancer application must be in front of the nodes. A service of type LoadBalancer addresses this limitation by providing a single external IP. In cloud environments such as GKE, EKS, or AKS, this process is handled automatically by the cloud controller manager. When a LoadBalancer service is created, the controller provisions a cloud-specific load balancer instance, points it at the cluster nodes, and assigns the external IP to the Kubernetes service object. The situation is different in environments without a cloud controller manager. A LoadBalancer service can still be created, but no external IP will be assigned to it. This is where Cilium’s LoadBalancer IP address management comes in. Enabling LB IPAM allows Cilium to assign IP addresses directly to LoadBalancer services, making them externally reachable even in on-premises or bare-metal environments. These IPs can then be announced using Cilium’s BGP or layer 2 announcement features, which we will cover in Chapter 10.
    服务根据用途分为不同类型。默认情况下，服务类型为 ClusterIP，这意味着它会提供一个集群内虚拟 IP，并作为内部负载均衡器使用。不过，ClusterIP 服务无法从集群外部访问。当集群外的工作负载需要访问运行在 Kubernetes 内部的应用时，可以使用另外两种类型的服务。第一种是 NodePort 类型服务。NodePort 服务会在集群中的每个节点上暴露一个端口。发送到该端口的流量会被转发到应用上；虽然所有节点上的端口号相同，但每个节点拥有不同的 IP 地址。因此客户端必须知悉所有节点 IP 并自行处理负载均衡，或者在节点前置外部负载均衡器应用。LoadBalancer 类型服务通过提供单一外部 IP 解决了这一限制。在 GKE、EKS 或 AKS 等云环境中，这一过程由云控制器管理器自动处理。创建 LoadBalancer 服务时，控制器会置备云厂商特定的负载均衡器实例，将其指向集群节点，并把外部 IP 分配给 Kubernetes Service 对象。在没有云控制器管理器的环境中情况则不同：LoadBalancer 服务仍可创建，但不会为其分配任何外部 IP。这正是 Cilium 的 LoadBalancer IP 地址管理（LB IPAM）发挥作用的地方。启用 LB IPAM 后，Cilium 可以直接为 LoadBalancer 服务分配 IP 地址，即便在本地或裸金属环境下也能让它们从外部可达。这些 IP 随后可以通过 Cilium 的 BGP 或二层通告功能进行通告，我们将在第 10 章介绍这部分内容。
    This section focuses on assigning external IP addresses to LoadBalancer services. IP address management for pods was covered in Chapter 4. ClusterIP services are given IP addresses by Kubernetes and are not affected by Cilium’s LB IPAM.
    本节重点介绍如何为 LoadBalancer 服务分配外部 IP 地址。Pod 的 IP 地址管理已在第 4 章介绍过。ClusterIP 服务的 IP 由 Kubernetes 分配，不受 Cilium LB IPAM 的影响。
    This feature is always enabled in Cilium but is dormant until the first IP pool is configured. Let’s test it (you can find the files used here in the /chapter06/lb-ipam directory of the book’s GitHub repository). If you created a cluster with Cilium earlier, you can reuse it for this example. Otherwise, create a kind cluster using the generic configuration (kind.yaml) and install Cilium as covered previously. Kind does not come with a cloud controller manager; instead, Cilium will be the system assigning the IP to our external LoadBalancer service. Create a simple service of type LoadBalancer (httpd-lb-service.yaml). Once the service is created, it will show a pending external IP:
    该特性在 Cilium 中始终启用，但在配置第一个 IP 池之前处于休眠状态。我们来测试一下（这里用到的文件可以在本书 GitHub 仓库的 /chapter06/lb-ipam 目录下找到）。如果你之前已经用 Cilium 创建过集群，可以复用它来做这个示例；否则，使用通用配置（kind.yaml）创建一个 kind 集群，并按照之前的方法安装 Cilium。Kind 自带的云控制器管理器不可用；相反，Cilium 将负责为我们的外部 LoadBalancer 服务分配 IP。创建一个简单的 LoadBalancer 类型服务（httpd-lb-service.yaml）。服务创建完成后，它会显示一个待分配的外部 IP：
    代码（原文）
    $ kubectl get svc httpd
NAME    TYPE           CLUSTER-IP     EXTERNAL-IP   PORT(S)        AGE
httpd   LoadBalancer   10.96.72.137   <pending>     80:31478/TCP   20s
    No matter how long we wait, no external IP will be assigned. To get an IP assigned, we must create a CiliumLoadBalancerIPPool object (lb-pool.yaml):
    无论我们等待多久，都不会分配到外部 IP。要获取 IP，我们必须创建一个 CiliumLoadBalancerIPPool 对象（lb-pool.yaml）：
    代码（原文）
    apiVersion: "cilium.io/v2"
kind: CiliumLoadBalancerIPPool
metadata:
name: "lb-pool"
spec:
blocks:
- cidr: "172.16.0.0/24"
    Once this object is created, the Cilium operator takes responsibility for assigning IPs from the defined pool to LoadBalancer services. If we check the service again, we will see that an external IP from the pool has been assigned:
    该对象创建后，Cilium Operator 会负责从所定义的地址池中为 LoadBalancer 服务分配 IP。再次检查服务时，我们会看到已从地址池中分配到一个外部 IP：
    代码（原文）
    $ kubectl get svc
NAME    TYPE           CLUSTER-IP     EXTERNAL-IP   PORT(S)        AGE
httpd   LoadBalancer   10.96.72.137   172.16.0.0    80:31478/TCP   48s
    By default, the Cilium operator may assign any IP within that block. In conventional networking, however, the first and last IP addresses are usually reserved. The first is the network address and the last is the broadcast address; in many cases, we want to avoid assigning these addresses to services. There are two ways to prevent the use of the first and last addresses: 11..Define a custom start and stop range that explicitly excludes these addresses. 22..Set allowFirstLastIPs: "No". This is the most scalable option and requires the least manual configuration. We’ll start with the first option. Update the pool object as follows (lb-pool-startstop.yaml):
    默认情况下，Cilium Operator 可以在该地址块内分配任意 IP。但在传统网络中，第一个和最后一个 IP 地址通常是保留的——第一个是网络地址，最后一个是广播地址；在很多情况下，我们希望避免把这些地址分配给服务。有两种方式可以阻止首尾地址的使用：1. 定义自定义的起始与结束范围，显式排除这些地址；2. 设置 allowFirstLastIPs: "No"。这是最具扩展性的方案，所需手动配置也最少。我们先从第一种方式开始。按如下内容更新地址池对象（lb-pool-startstop.yaml）：
    代码（原文）
    apiVersion: "cilium.io/v2"
kind: CiliumLoadBalancerIPPool
metadata:
name: "lb-pool"
spec:
blocks:
- start: 172.16.0.1
stop: 172.16.0.254
    Now the Cilium operator will only assign IPs from 172.16.0.1 to 172.16.0.254. Since we updated the existing pool, it will automatically reassign the external IP of the service:
    现在 Cilium Operator 只会从 172.16.0.1 到 172.16.0.254 之间分配 IP。由于我们更新了现有地址池，系统会自动重新分配该服务的外部 IP：
    代码（原文）
    $ kubectl get svc
NAME    TYPE           CLUSTER-IP     EXTERNAL-IP   PORT(S)        AGE
httpd   LoadBalancer   10.96.72.137   172.16.0.1    80:31478/TCP   1m14s
    When managing multiple blocks, it can be tedious to define start and stop values for each one manually. Instead, we can use allowFirstLastIPs: "No" to automatically exclude those IPs. Let’s update the pool object again (lb-pool-no-allow-first-last.yaml):
    在管理多个地址块时，逐个手动定义起始与结束值会比较繁琐。我们可以改用 allowFirstLastIPs: "No" 来自动排除那些 IP。再次更新地址池对象（lb-pool-no-allow-first-last.yaml）：
    代码（原文）
    apiVersion: "cilium.io/v2"
kind: CiliumLoadBalancerIPPool
metadata:
name: "lb-pool"
spec:
allowFirstLastIPs: "No"
blocks:
- cidr: "192.168.9.0/24"
- cidr: "10.234.9.0/24"
    By specifying allowFirstLastIPs: "No", we ensure that the Cilium operator does not assign 192.168.9.0, 192.168.9.255, 10.234.9.0, and 10.234.9.255.3 We can verify the IP assignment by checking the service again:
    通过指定 allowFirstLastIPs: "No"，我们可以确保 Cilium Operator 不会分配 192.168.9.0、192.168.9.255、10.234.9.0 和 10.234.9.255。我们可以再次检查服务来验证 IP 分配：
    代码（原文）
    $ kubectl get svc
NAME    TYPE           CLUSTER-IP     EXTERNAL-IP   PORT(S)        AGE
httpd   LoadBalancer   10.96.72.137   192.168.9.1   80:31478/TCP   2m11s
    You might want to create multiple CiliumLoadBalancerIPPools for different use cases. For example, you might want to reserve a specific block of addresses for services that are part of a production workload. This example restricts the use of the 100.16.38.0/24 block to services that carry the label environment: production:
    你可能希望为不同用例创建多个 CiliumLoadBalancerIPPool。例如，你可能想为属于生产工作负载的服务预留一个特定的地址块。下面这个示例将 100.16.38.0/24 地址块的使用限制在带有 environment: production 标签的服务上：
    代码（原文）
    apiVersion: "cilium.io/v2"
kind: CiliumLoadBalancerIPPool
metadata:
name: "production-pool"
spec:
allowFirstLastIPs: "No"
blocks:
- cidr: "100.16.38.0/24"
serviceSelector:
matchLabels:
environment: production
    Note that CiliumLoadBalancerIPPool is a cluster-wide resource, so any service in any namespace with the matching label can receive an IP from this pool. If you want to further restrict the pool to a specific namespace, you can use the special selector io.kubernetes.service.namespace:
    注意 CiliumLoadBalancerIPPool 是集群级资源，因此任何命名空间中带有匹配标签的服务都可以从该地址池中获得 IP。如果想进一步将地址池限制在某个特定命名空间内，可以使用特殊选择器 io.kubernetes.service.namespace：
    代码（原文）
    serviceSelector:
matchLabels:
io.kubernetes.service.namespace: payments
environment: production
    3You must enclose the "No" in quotes in your YAML, as Cilium expects this to be a string. Writing it without the quotes will cause it to be interpreted in YAML as a Boolean, and the change will fail to apply with a type error.
    3 你必须在 YAML 中将 "No" 用引号括起来，因为 Cilium 期望它是一个字符串。如果不加引号，YAML 会将其解释为布尔值，配置将因类型错误而无法应用。
    In this case, only services in the payments namespace with the label environment:production will be allocated IPs from the pool.
    在这种情况下，只有位于 payments 命名空间、且带有 environment: production 标签的服务才会从该地址池中分配到 IP。
    When using serviceSelector, avoid overlapping selectors. If multiple CiliumLoadBalancerIPPools match the same service, the outcome is undefined. This can lead to services receiving an IP from the wrong pool, which may cause incorrect or even unsafe configurations. As a best practice, either do not use selectors at all and let pools serve all services, or ensure that each selector is unique and unambiguous.
    使用 serviceSelector 时，应避免选择器重叠。如果多个 CiliumLoadBalancerIPPool 匹配到同一个服务，其行为是未定义的。这可能导致服务从错误的地址池接收 IP，进而引发错误甚至不安全的配置。最佳实践是：要么完全不使用选择器，让地址池服务于所有服务；要么确保每个选择器都是唯一且无歧义的。
    Cilium LB IPAM only assigns external IPs to services. It does not advertise those IPs to external clients. For the addresses to be routable, LB IPAM is typically combined with Cilium’s BGP or layer 2 announcement features, which we will cover in Chapter 10.
    Cilium LB IPAM 只负责为服务分配外部 IP，它不会把这些 IP 通告给外部客户端。要让这些地址具有可路由性，LB IPAM 通常需要与 Cilium 的 BGP 或二层通告功能配合使用，我们将在第 10 章介绍这些功能。

    Summary
    本章小结

    In this chapter we reviewed the fundamentals of service networking in Kubernetes and examined how Cilium enhances this area by leveraging eBPF. We looked at how services abstract and load-balance traffic to pod backends, and how kube-proxy traditionally implements this behavior using iptables. We then introduced Cilium’s kube-proxy replacement, showing how eBPF maps in the kernel enable efficient load balancing, resiliency during agent downtime, and independence between the control plane and datapath. We also explored service-specific behaviors supported by KPR, including session affinity and external and internal traffic policies, and considered their trade-offs. Finally, we discussed how external access can be provided through LoadBalancer services in environments without a cloud controller manager, using Cilium’s LoadBalancer IPAM feature with BGP and layer 2 announcements (to be covered in Chapter 10) to make these addresses routable. These features demonstrate that Cilium’s KPR not only matches the functionality of kube-proxy but provides enhanced performance, flexibility, and operational resilience. In the next chapter, we will build on this foundation by exploring Cilium’s Ingress and Gateway API. These mechanisms handle external traffic entering the cluster while also providing fine-grained control over layer 7 traffic for advanced routing and security.
    本章中，我们回顾了 Kubernetes 中服务网络的基础知识，并探讨了 Cilium 如何利用 eBPF 增强这一领域。我们了解了服务如何抽象并把流量负载均衡到 Pod 后端，以及 kube-proxy 如何传统上通过 iptables 实现这一行为。随后我们介绍了 Cilium 的 kube-proxy 替代（KPR），展示了内核中的 eBPF map 如何实现高效的负载均衡、Agent 停机时的弹性，以及控制平面与数据路径之间的解耦。我们还探讨了 KPR 所支持的服务特定行为，包括会话亲和性、外部与内部流量策略，并分析了它们各自的权衡。最后，我们讨论了如何在没有云控制器管理器的环境中，通过 LoadBalancer 服务提供外部访问，即使用 Cilium 的 LoadBalancer IPAM 功能配合 BGP 和二层通告（第 10 章介绍），让这些地址具有可路由性。这些特性表明，Cilium 的 KPR 不仅能覆盖 kube-proxy 的全部功能，还提供了更强的性能、灵活性和运维弹性。下一章我们将在此基础上继续介绍 Cilium 的 Ingress 和 Gateway API——这些机制用于处理进入集群的外部流量，同时也为高级路由与安全提供细粒度的七层流量控制。

    第 7 章
    Ingress and Gateway API
    入站与 Gateway API

    In Chapter 6, we saw how Kubernetes services enable applications to be accessible from both within and outside the cluster. While services provide a way to balance incoming traffic at layer 4, however, they lack the native ability to apply granular controls at upper layers. This means that Kubernetes services cannot perform common functions like TLS termination, application (layer 7)-level parsing, and filtering. For that, we need ingress mechanisms. In this chapter, we’ll explore how Cilium supports both Ingress and its successor, Gateway API. You will learn:
    在第 6 章中，我们看到 Kubernetes 服务如何让应用能够被集群内外访问。然而，服务提供了一种在 L4 层平衡入站流量的方式，但缺乏在上层应用细粒度控制流量的原生能力。这意味着 Kubernetes 服务无法执行 TLS 终止、应用（L7 层）解析和流量过滤等常见功能。为此，我们需要入站机制。本章将带你了解 Cilium 如何同时支持 Ingress 及其后继者 Gateway API。你将学到：
    • What they are, how they differ, and when to use each
      它们是什么、有何差异，以及何时使用
    • How to publish HTTP services with Ingress, including host and path routing and
      如何通过 Ingress 发布 HTTP 服务，包括主机路由和路径路由，以及
    TLS termination
    TLS 终止
    • How Gateway API fits together with GatewayClass, Gateway, and HTTPRoute
      Gateway API 如何与 GatewayClass、Gateway 和 HTTPRoute
    resources, and how routes attach to listeners
    资源协同工作，以及路由如何挂载到监听器
    • How to match and route traffic at layer 7 by path, headers, method, and query
      如何在 L7 通过路径、请求头、HTTP 方法和查询
    parameters
    参数匹配并路由流量
    • How to run progressive rollouts with traffic splitting (50/50 to 99/1) and validate
      如何通过流量分割（50/50 到 99/1）进行渐进式灰度发布并验证
    the results
    结果
    • How to shape traffic with header edits and HTTP redirects
      如何通过修改请求头和 HTTP 重定向来调整流量
    • How to handle gRPC with GRPCRoute, using service and method matching
      如何通过 GRPCRoute 处理 gRPC，使用服务匹配和方法匹配
    • How to route internal traffic with GAMMA (Gateway API for service mesh)
      如何通过 GAMMA（用于服务网格的 Gateway API）路由内部流量

    Handling External Traffic in Kubernetes
    在 Kubernetes 中处理外部流量

    We covered how IP addresses are assigned to pods in Chapter 4, and in Chapter 5 you learned how pods communicate with one another within the cluster. But most microservices architectures will, at some point, require exposing some services outside the cluster and securely routing traffic into it. In traditional networks, you would use tools such as reverse proxies and load balancers. In Kubernetes, Ingress has traditionally been the most commonly deployed method for routing traffic into the cluster. Ingress exposes HTTP and HTTPS routes from outside the cluster to services within the cluster. Traffic routing is controlled by rules defined on the Ingress resource. Kubernetes doesn’t provide a standard implementation for it: instead, platform engineers need to install and configure an Ingress controller, a program that will connect to the Kubernetes API server, check for Ingress resources, and implement them. There are many Ingress controllers listed in the Kubernetes documentation, from general-purpose reverse proxies to full API gateways. For Cilium, it’s just another feature that’s built in, meaning you avoid the resource and operational burdens that come with managing a separate purpose-built tool. Let’s try it.
    第 4 章介绍了 Pod IP 地址的分配方式，第 5 章讲解了 Pod 之间如何在集群内部通信。但在大多数微服务架构中，总有需要将部分服务暴露到集群之外、并将流量安全地路由进集群的需求。在传统网络中，你会使用反向代理和负载均衡器等工具。在 Kubernetes 中，Ingress 一直是最常用的将流量路由进集群的方式。Ingress 将集群外部的 HTTP/HTTPS 路由暴露到集群内部的 Service。流量路由由 Ingress 资源上定义的规则控制。Kubernetes 本身不提供 Ingress 的标准实现；平台工程师需要安装并配置一个 Ingress 控制器——它会连接到 Kubernetes API 服务器，监控 Ingress 资源，并付诸实现。Kubernetes 文档中列出了许多 Ingress 控制器，从通用的反向代理到完整的 API 网关应有尽有。对于 Cilium 来说，这只是另一个内置特性，意味着你无需承担管理独立专用工具带来的资源开销和运维负担。我们来试一试。

    Getting Started with Cilium Ingress
    Cilium Ingress 入门

    Cilium uses the standard Kubernetes Ingress resource definition, with an ingressClassName of cilium. This can be used for path-based routing and for TLS termination, as you will see shortly. You can find all the YAML manifests you will use in this chapter in the chapter07/ingress directory of the book’s GitHub repository. Deploy a kind cluster using the kind.yaml config, then install Cilium with the following Helm values (cilium-ingress-values.yaml):
    Cilium 使用标准的 Kubernetes Ingress 资源定义，其 ingressClassName 为 cilium。正如你将看到的，它可用于基于路径的路由和 TLS 终止。本章用到的所有 YAML 清单都可以在本书 GitHub 仓库的 chapter07/ingress 目录下找到。使用 kind.yaml 配置部署一个 kind 集群，然后用以下 Helm values（cilium-ingress-values.yaml）安装 Cilium：
    代码（原文）
    kubeProxyReplacement: true
l2announcements:
enabled: true
ipam:
mode: kubernetes
ingressController:
enabled: true
    Cilium Ingress requires KPR (discussed in Chapter 6). The Ingress controller creates a service of type LoadBalancer. As that service needs an IP address, we use Cilium’s LB IPAM feature (also discussed in Chapter 6). We enable the layer 2 announcement feature to make the service IP reachable from outside the cluster (we’ll cover this feature in depth in Chapter 10). We enable Ingress with ingressController.enabled=true. Ingress is disabled by default. The application we will access from outside the cluster is called Bookinfo. This demo set of microservices provided by the Istio project consists of several Kubernetes deployments and services. Deploy the manifest (bookinfo.yaml):
    Cilium Ingress 依赖 KPR（第 6 章已讨论）。Ingress 控制器会创建一个 LoadBalancer 类型的 Service。由于该 Service 需要一个 IP 地址，我们使用 Cilium 的 LB IPAM 特性（同样在第 6 章讨论过）。我们启用二层通告功能，让 Service IP 可以从集群外部访问（第 10 章将深入介绍这一特性）。我们通过 ingressController.enabled=true 启用 Ingress。Ingress 默认是关闭的。我们将从集群外部访问的应用名为 Bookinfo，这是 Istio 项目提供的一套演示性微服务，包含若干 Kubernetes Deployment 和 Service。部署清单（bookinfo.yaml）：
    代码（原文）
    $ kubectl apply -f bookinfo.yaml
    Then deploy the Ingress resource (basic-ingress.yaml):
    然后部署 Ingress 资源（basic-ingress.yaml）：
    代码（原文）
    apiVersion: networking.k8s.io/v1
kind: Ingress
metadata:
name: basic-ingress
namespace: default
spec:
ingressClassName: cilium
rules:
- http:
paths:
- backend:
service:
name: details
port:
number: 9080
path: /details
pathType: Prefix
- backend:
service:
name: productpage
port:
number: 9080
path: /
pathType: Prefix
    Note that the ingressClassName field uses the value cilium. This instructs Kubernetes to use Cilium as the Ingress controller for this resource. The Ingress resource contains one or more HTTP rules. Each HTTP rule contains the following information:
    注意 ingressClassName 字段值为 cilium。这会指示 Kubernetes 将 Cilium 用作该资源的 Ingress 控制器。Ingress 资源包含一个或多个 HTTP 规则。每条 HTTP 规则包含以下信息：
    • An optional host. In this example, since no host is specified, the rule applies to all
      可选的 host。在本示例中，由于未指定 host，该规则适用于所有
    inbound HTTP traffic.
    入站 HTTP 流量。
    • A list of paths, each of which has an associated backend defined with a service
      路径列表，每条路径都关联一个后端，后端通过 Service
    name and port name (from the service) or number. A backend is a combination of service and port names. HTTP and HTTPS requests to the Ingress resource that match the host and path of the rule are sent to the listed backend. In our instance, we are creating an Ingress resource with two backends, each pointing to a service, based on the requested path:
    名称和端口名（来自 Service）或端口号来定义。后端是 Service 与端口名的组合。匹配规则的 host 和 path 的 HTTP/HTTPS 请求会被发送到对应的后端。在我们这个例子中，我们创建了一个带有两个后端的 Ingress 资源，每个后端根据请求的路径指向一个 Service：
    • /details routes to the details service, on port 9080.
      /details 路由到端口 9080 上的 details Service。
    • / routes to the productpage service, on port 9080.
      / 路由到端口 9080 上的 productpage Service。
    Access to the Bookinfo app with Cilium ingress is illustrated in Figure 7-1.
    通过 Cilium Ingress 访问 Bookinfo 应用的过程如图 7-1 所示。
    As discussed in Chapter 2, Envoy runs as a per-node DaemonSet. When you create Ingress or Gateway API resources, Cilium’s controllers translate them into CiliumEnvoyConfig (CEC) objects and program the Envoy DaemonSet accordingly. You can verify what was rendered with kubectl get cec -A (and kubectl get ccec-A for cluster-wide configs). Any change to the Ingress or Gateway API objects updates the corresponding CECs and is reconciled to Envoy. Cilium can also program Envoy directly via CEC/CCEC without using Ingress or Gateway API. This enables custom listeners, routes, clusters, and filters for layer 7 routing and manipulation. It is a power user path with caveats (version compatibility, portability, and ownership of the config) and is outside the scope of this book.
    正如第 2 章所讨论的，Envoy 以每节点 DaemonSet 的形式运行。当你创建 Ingress 或 Gateway API 资源时，Cilium 的控制器会把它们翻译成 CiliumEnvoyConfig（CEC）对象，并相应地对 Envoy DaemonSet 进行编程。你可以用 kubectl get cec -A 查看生成的配置（用 kubectl get ccec -A 查看集群范围的配置）。Ingress 或 Gateway API 对象的任何变更都会更新对应的 CEC，并同步到 Envoy。Cilium 还可以直接通过 CEC/CCEC 编程 Envoy，而无需使用 Ingress 或 Gateway API。这为 L7 路由和流量操作提供了自定义监听器、路由、集群和过滤器。这是高阶用户路径，但有一些注意事项（版本兼容性、可移植性以及配置的所有权），超出了本书的范围。
    Next, we’ll deploy the Ingress manifest:
    接下来，我们来部署 Ingress 清单：
    代码（原文）
    $ kubectl apply -f basic-ingress.yaml
ingress.networking.k8s.io/basic-ingress created
    This creates a LoadBalancer service for the Ingress, but it won’t have an external IP yet:
    这会为 Ingress 创建一个 LoadBalancer Service，但它此时还没有外部 IP：
    代码（原文）
    $ kubectl get svc
NAME                           TYPE           CLUSTER-IP     EXTERNAL-IP
cilium-ingress-basic-ingress   LoadBalancer   10.96.162.88   <pending>
    As you’ll recall from Chapter 6, for an IP to be assigned we need to create a CiliumLoadBalancerIPPool object (pool.yaml):
    正如你在第 6 章回忆到的，要分配 IP 地址，我们需要创建一个 CiliumLoadBalancerIPPool 对象（pool.yaml）：
    代码（原文）
    apiVersion: "cilium.io/v2"
kind: CiliumLoadBalancerIPPool
metadata:
name: "pool"
spec:
blocks:
- cidr: "172.18.255.200/29"
    Deploy the pool with kubectl and verify that an IP address has been assigned to the service:
    用 kubectl 部署这个地址池，并验证是否已为该 Service 分配了 IP 地址：
    代码（原文）
    $ kubectl get svc
NAME                           TYPE           CLUSTER-IP      EXTERNAL-IP
cilium-ingress-basic-ingress   LoadBalancer   10.96.68.40     172.18.255.200
    You can also verify that the same external IP address is associated with the Ingress resource:
    你还可以验证同一个外部 IP 地址是否也关联到了 Ingress 资源：
    代码（原文）
    $ kubectl get ingress
NAME            CLASS    HOSTS   ADDRESS          PORTS   AGE
basic-ingress   cilium   *       172.18.255.200   80      13m
    Export it as a variable (INGRESS_IP=172.18.255.200, for example) so that you can reuse it. Let’s now make this IP address accessible locally by deploying the following Cilium layer 2 announcement policy (l2policy.yaml):
    将其导出为变量（例如 INGRESS_IP=172.18.255.200），以便后续复用。现在让该 IP 地址可以在本地访问，为此部署以下 Cilium 二层通告策略（l2policy.yaml）：
    代码（原文）
    apiVersion: "cilium.io/v2alpha1"
kind: CiliumL2AnnouncementPolicy
metadata:
name: policy1
spec:
loadBalancerIPs: true
interfaces:
- eth0
nodeSelector:
matchExpressions:
- key: node-role.kubernetes.io/control-plane
operator: DoesNotExist
    Layer 2 announcement lets nodes advertise service IPs on the local network using the Address Resolution Protocol (ARP) for IPv4 or Neighbor Discovery (ND) for IPv6,1 so external hosts can reach those IPs without a separate load balancer if they share a layer 2 domain. In Cilium, you enable this with a CiliumL2AnnouncementPolicy that selects which nodes and interfaces announce their IPs. You will learn how it functions in Chapter 10, but essentially the policy shown here will ensure Cilium answers ARP requests received on the worker node’s eth0 interfaces for IPs assigned to LoadBalancer services (in this case, 172.18.255.200). Next, we’ll verify that Cilium Ingress is working as we expect by accessing the Ingress IP from our terminal.
    二层通告让节点通过 ARP（IPv4）或邻居发现（IPv6）在本地网络上通告 Service IP，1 这样外部主机无需单独的负载均衡器就能访问这些 IP（只要它们处于同一个二层域内）。在 Cilium 中，你通过 CiliumL2AnnouncementPolicy 启用该功能，它会选择哪些节点和接口来通告自己的 IP。你将在第 10 章了解它的工作原理，但这里展示的策略本质上会确保 Cilium 在工作节点的 eth0 接口上响应针对 LoadBalancer Service 分配 IP（本例中即 172.18.255.200）的 ARP 请求。接下来，我们从终端访问 Ingress IP，验证 Cilium Ingress 是否如预期工作。
    On macOS, you will likely need to install Docker Mac Net Connect to access the application running in your kind cluster over Ingress/ Gateway API. This tool enables macOS users to access services running on the host network from Docker containers, working around the networking limitations of Docker Desktop on Mac. If you’d rather not install this utility, you can simply deploy another container on the same Docker network as the nodes with:
    在 macOS 上，你可能需要安装 Docker Mac Net Connect 才能通过 Ingress/Gateway API 访问 kind 集群中运行的应用。这个工具让 macOS 用户可以从 Docker 容器访问宿主机网络上运行的 Service，绕过 Docker Desktop 在 Mac 上的网络限制。如果你不想安装这个工具，可以用以下命令在与节点相同的 Docker 网络中部署另一个容器：
    代码（原文）
    $ docker run -d --net kind --rm --name client \
nicolaka/netshoot:v0.8 sleep inf
    You can then run the following commands from the client’s shell (docker exec -it client bash) to observe the same behavior.
    然后你可以在客户端 Shell（docker exec -it client bash）中运行以下命令来观察同样的行为。
    From outside the cluster, let’s send an HTTP request to the / path with curl. It takes us to the productpage service and returns HTTP code 200:
    从集群外部，用 curl 向 / 路径发送一个 HTTP 请求。它会把我们带到 productpage Service，并返回 HTTP 状态码 200：
    代码（原文）
    $ curl --fail -s -o /dev/null -w "%{http_code}\n" "http://$INGRESS_IP/"
    A cURL request to the /details/1 path takes us to the book’s details:
    向 /details/1 路径发送的 cURL 请求会把我们带到图书详情页：
    代码（原文）
    $ curl --fail -s "http://$INGRESS_IP/details/1"
{
"id": 1,
"author": "William Shakespeare",
"year": 1595,
"type": "paperback",
"pages": 200,
"publisher": "PublisherA",
"language": "English",
"ISBN-10": "1234567890",
"ISBN-13": "123-1234567890"
}
    1Introduced in Cilium v1.19.
    1Cilium v1.19 引入。
    However, you can’t directly access other URL paths that weren’t defined in basicingress.yaml. For example, you will get a 404 error if you make a request to /ratings, as it is not explicitly mapped in the Ingress resource:
    但你无法直接访问 basicingress.yaml 中未定义的其他 URL 路径。例如，向 /ratings 发起请求会得到 404 错误，因为它没有在 Ingress 资源中显式映射：
    代码（原文）
    $ curl --fail -so /dev/null -w "%{http_code}\n" "http://$INGRESS_IP/ratings"
    In the next section we’ll explore another use case for Cilium Ingress: TLS termination.
    下一节我们将介绍 Cilium Ingress 的另一个用例：TLS 终止。

    TLS Termination with Cilium Ingress
    通过 Cilium Ingress 进行 TLS 终止

    Ingress resources are commonly used as TLS termination points in Kubernetes. Because they act as the cluster’s entry proxy, terminating TLS at the Ingress simplifies certificate management and provides a secure frontend for HTTP services. In this section, we will test TLS termination using self-signed certificates generated with mkcert. While this approach is suitable for testing, it’s strongly recommended to use a certificate management system such as cert-manager in production. We’ll use the Bookinfo app again for this example, except that instead of accessing the service over HTTP (80), we will be accessing it over HTTPS (443), as illustrated in Figure 7-2.
    Ingress 资源在 Kubernetes 中常用作 TLS 终止点。由于它充当集群的入口代理，在 Ingress 处终止 TLS 可以简化证书管理，并为 HTTP 服务提供一个安全的前端。本节我们将使用 mkcert 生成的自签名证书来测试 TLS 终止。虽然这种方法适合测试，但强烈建议在生产环境中使用 cert-manager 等证书管理系统。本示例我们仍然使用 Bookinfo 应用，只不过这次通过 HTTPS（443）访问而非 HTTP（80），如图 7-2 所示。
    First, let’s generate a wildcard certificate. Install mkcert with your package manager if you don’t have it installed already, then run:
    首先，我们来生成一个通配符证书。如果你尚未安装 mkcert，请通过你的包管理器安装，然后运行：
    代码（原文）
    $ mkcert '*.cilium.rocks'
Created a new local CA
Run "mkcert -install" for certificates to be trusted automatically
Created a new certificate valid for the following names
- "*.cilium.rocks"
Reminder: X.509 wildcards only go one level deep, so this won't match
a.b.cilium.rocks
The certificate is at "./_wildcard.cilium.rocks.pem" and the key at
"./_wildcard.cilium.rocks-key.pem"
It will expire on 10 December 2027
    mkcert suggests running mkcert -install so the certificate becomes trusted systemwide, but we will not do that here. Installing a local certificate authority (CA) into the system trust store is unnecessary for this example and is discouraged on shared or production machines. The previous command created a certificate and a key. We can now create a Kubernetes TLS secret using these files:
    mkcert 建议运行 mkcert -install 让证书在系统范围内受信任，但这里我们不这样做。将本地证书颁发机构（CA）安装到系统信任存储对于本示例来说并不必要，而且不建议在共享或生产机器上执行。上一条命令创建了一个证书和一个密钥。现在我们可以用这些文件创建一个 Kubernetes TLS Secret：
    代码（原文）
    $ kubectl create secret tls demo-cert \
--key=_wildcard.cilium.rocks-key.pem \
--cert=_wildcard.cilium.rocks.pem
secret/demo-cert created
    Once the secret is in place, define an Ingress resource that references it for TLS termination. For bookinfo.cilium.rocks, the following Ingress routes /details and / to two HTTP services (details and productpage):
    Secret 创建好之后，定义一个引用它来进行 TLS 终止的 Ingress 资源。对于 bookinfo.cilium.rocks，以下 Ingress 将 /details 和 / 路由到两个 HTTP Service（details 和 productpage）：
    代码（原文）
    apiVersion: networking.k8s.io/v1
kind: Ingress
metadata:
name: tls-ingress
namespace: default
spec:
ingressClassName: cilium
rules:
- host: bookinfo.cilium.rocks
http:
paths:
- path: /details
pathType: Prefix
backend:
service:
name: details
port:
number: 9080
- path: /
pathType: Prefix
backend:
service:
name: productpage
port:
number: 9080
tls:
- hosts:
- bookinfo.cilium.rocks
secretName: demo-cert
    Once applied with kubectl apply -f tls-ingress.yaml, you should see the Ingress provisioned with a LoadBalancer address:
    用 kubectl apply -f tls-ingress.yaml 应用之后，你应该能看到 Ingress 被分配了一个 LoadBalancer 地址：
    代码（原文）
    $ kubectl get ingress tls-ingress
NAME          CLASS    HOSTS                          ADDRESS           PORTS
tls-ingress   cilium   hipstershop.cilium.rocks,...   172.18.255.201    80, 443
    This address can then be exported for use in cURL commands:
    这个地址可以导出，以便在 cURL 命令中使用：
    代码（原文）
    $ export TLS_INGRESS="$(kubectl get ingress tls-ingress \
-o jsonpath='{.status.loadBalancer.ingress[0].ip}')"
$ echo "$TLS_INGRESS"
172.18.255.201
    To avoid modifying the system trust store, we will explicitly tell cURL which CA to trust for this request. You can locate the mkcert CA directory with:
    为避免修改系统信任存储，我们会显式告诉 cURL 为该请求信任哪个 CA。你可以用以下命令找到 mkcert CA 目录：
    代码（原文）
    $ mkcert -CAROOT
~/Library/Application Support/mkcert
    Use the CA file directly when sending the HTTPS request. The --resolve flag maps the hostname to the Ingress IP:
    发送 HTTPS 请求时直接使用这个 CA 文件。--resolve 标志将主机名映射到 Ingress IP：
    代码（原文）
    $ curl -s \
--resolve "bookinfo.cilium.rocks:443:$TLS_INGRESS" \
--cacert "$(mkcert -CAROOT)/rootCA.pem" \
https://bookinfo.cilium.rocks/details/1 | jq
{ "id": 1, "author": "William Shakespeare", "year": 1595, ... }

    Ingress Limitations
    Ingress 的局限性

    Ingress provides a familiar abstraction for routing external traffic into a cluster and functions well for HTTP and HTTPS routing. However, it has several limitations that become apparent in more complex environments. These are not tied to Cilium’s implementation of Ingress, but apply to most Ingress implementations: Single shared configuration role Ingress uses a cluster-wide controller (like NGINX or Cilium) that watches all Ingress resources and applies the same global configuration to all of them. There’s no native way to delegate responsibility or apply different policies for different teams or namespaces. This means platform teams often become a bottleneck, forced to manage or approve all routing changes across the organization. Poor multitenancy support
    Ingress 为将外部流量路由进集群提供了一个熟悉的抽象，并且在 HTTP/HTTPS 路由场景中表现良好。但它在更复杂的环境中暴露出若干局限性。这些局限并非 Cilium 的 Ingress 实现独有，而是适用于大多数 Ingress 实现：单一共享的配置角色 Ingress 使用一个集群级控制器（如 NGINX 或 Cilium），它会监控所有 Ingress 资源并对其应用相同的全局配置。没有原生方式来为不同团队或命名空间委派责任或应用不同的策略。这意味着平台团队常常成为瓶颈，被迫管理或审批组织内所有的路由变更。多租户支持薄弱
    Because Ingress lacks scoping and delegation, there’s no built-in mechanism to enforce tenant boundaries or isolate configuration. Annotations or CRDs are often required to prevent one team’s configuration from affecting another’s. This limits self-service and introduces risk in shared clusters. Nonportable annotations Much of Ingress’s extended functionality relies on controller-specific annotations. These annotations are not standardized and are often tied to one specific Ingress implementation. As a result, workloads configured for one controller (e.g., NGINX) may not behave the same as or work well with another (e.g., HAProxy or Cilium), hurting portability. Hard to extend Ingress is rigid. It covers only basic HTTP routing and offers no first-class support for TCP, gRPC, advanced traffic shaping, or custom policy enforcement. Extending functionality typically requires controller-specific annotations, which are brittle and hard to manage. Platform teams have little control over layering features or composing policies in a reusable way. To address them, the broader SIG Network team2 created Gateway API.
    由于 Ingress 缺乏作用域划分和委派机制，没有内置手段来强制租户边界或隔离配置。通常需要通过注解或 CRD 来防止一个团队的配置影响另一个团队。这限制了自助服务能力，并在共享集群中引入了风险。不可移植的注解 Ingress 的许多扩展功能依赖于控制器特定的注解。这些注解没有标准化，并且通常绑定于某一种特定的 Ingress 实现。因此，为某个控制器（如 NGINX）配置的工作负载在另一个控制器（如 HAProxy 或 Cilium）上可能行为不一致或无法正常工作，损害了可移植性。难以扩展 Ingress 过于僵化。它只覆盖了基本的 HTTP 路由，对 TCP、gRPC、高级流量整形或自定义策略执行等场景没有一流的支持。扩展功能通常需要依赖控制器特定的注解，而这些注解脆弱且难以管理。平台团队几乎无法以可复用的方式进行特性分层或策略组合。为解决这些问题，更广泛的 SIG Network 团队 2 创建了 Gateway API。

    Introducing Gateway API
    Gateway API 简介

    Gateway API was created to overcome the limitations of Ingress. It provides a more expressive, extensible model for managing ingress traffic. Gateway API offers a structured and centralized way to handle external traffic. Like Ingress, it supports HTTP(S) routing, TLS termination, traffic splitting,3 header manipulation, and more. But whereas Ingress relies heavily on annotations for advanced features, Gateway API includes these capabilities natively. As a result, configurations are more portable across implementations and less prone to fragmentation. Where customization is needed, Gateway API includes clear extension points. The team behind the project continues to identify patterns shared by different implementations, promoting them into the standard API to avoid the inconsistent and vendor-specific extensions that plagued the Ingress model.
    Gateway API 旨在克服 Ingress 的局限性，为管理入站流量提供一个更具表达力、可扩展的模型。Gateway API 提供了一种结构化、集中化的方式来处理外部流量。与 Ingress 类似，它支持 HTTP(S) 路由、TLS 终止、流量分割、3 请求头操作等功能。但 Ingress 在很大程度上依赖注解来实现高级特性，而 Gateway API 原生就包含了这些能力。因此，配置在不同实现之间更具可移植性，也更不容易碎片化。在需要自定义的地方，Gateway API 提供了清晰的扩展点。项目团队持续识别不同实现之间共享的模式，并将其提升为标准 API，以避免曾困扰 Ingress 模型的那些不一致且厂商特定的扩展。
    2A Special Interest Group (SIG) in Kubernetes is a group of contributors who collaborate around a particular functional area of the project, such as networking, storage, or node management. SIG Network is responsible for the components, interfaces, and APIs that expose networking capabilities to Kubernetes users and workloads. 3Possible in some Ingress controllers with implementation-specific annotations.
    2Kubernetes 的特别兴趣小组（SIG）是一群围绕项目某一特定功能领域（如网络、存储或节点管理）协作的贡献者。SIG Network 负责向 Kubernetes 用户和工作负载暴露网络能力的组件、接口和 API。 3 某些 Ingress 控制器在实现特定注解的条件下可以实现。
    Another key design principle is role-based separation of concerns. Ingress assumed that developers managed both application and infrastructure-level networking resources. In real-world environments, however, different personas are typically involved:
    另一个关键设计原则是基于角色的关注点分离。Ingress 假设开发者同时管理应用级和基础设施级的网络资源。但在真实环境中，通常涉及不同的角色：
    • Infrastructure providers (e.g., AWS, Azure, GCP)
      基础设施提供商（如 AWS、Azure、GCP）
    • Cluster operators who manage the Kubernetes platform
      管理 Kubernetes 平台的集群运维者
    • Application developers focused on service configuration and delivery
      专注于服务配置与交付的应用开发者
    By decomposing the monolithic Ingress resource into multiple Gateway API resources (the GatewayClass, Gateway, and HTTPRoute resources we will describe next), the API enables fine-grained access control. For example, developers can be granted permission to define routes in their own namespace, while operators retain control over shared infrastructure such as Gateways, as illustrated in Figure 7-3.
    通过将庞大的 Ingress 资源拆分为多种 Gateway API 资源（接下来将介绍的 GatewayClass、Gateway 和 HTTPRoute），该 API 实现了细粒度的访问控制。例如，可以授予开发者在自己的命名空间中定义路由的权限，而运维者则保留对 Gateway 等共享基础设施的控制权，如图 7-3 所示。

    Gateway API Objects
    Gateway API 对象

    Gateway API introduces a set of Kubernetes resources that separate infrastructure concerns from application-level routing. This modular design helps teams manage ingress traffic more effectively across different roles.
    Gateway API 引入了一组 Kubernetes 资源，将基础设施关注点与应用级路由分离开来。这种模块化设计帮助不同角色的团队更高效地管理入站流量。

    GatewayClass
    GatewayClass

    GatewayClass in Gateway API plays a role similar to IngressClass for Ingress: it declares which controller manages a class of Gateways and can carry controller defaults. When Cilium’s Gateway API support is enabled, a default class named cilium is installed with the controller io.cilium/gateway-controller. Gateways that set gatewayClassName: cilium are reconciled by the Cilium Gateway API controller. It programs the Envoy DaemonSet, the same layer 7 proxy data plane used by Cilium Ingress.
    Gateway API 中的 GatewayClass 与 Ingress 中的 IngressClass 扮演类似的角色：它声明由哪个控制器来管理一类 Gateway，并可携带控制器默认值。当 Cilium 的 Gateway API 支持被启用时，会安装一个名为 cilium 的默认类，其控制器为 io.cilium/gateway-controller。设置了 gatewayClassName: cilium 的 Gateway 会由 Cilium Gateway API 控制器进行调和。它会对 Envoy DaemonSet 进行编程，而这正是 Cilium Ingress 使用的同一个 L7 代理数据平面。

    Gateway
    Gateway

    This resource represents an instance of a GatewayClass and acts as the entry point for external traffic. It defines one or more listeners, each specifying protocol (e.g., HTTP or HTTPS), port, hostname, and TLS settings if applicable.
    该资源代表 GatewayClass 的一个实例，充当外部流量的入口点。它定义一个或多个监听器，每个监听器指定协议（如 HTTP 或 HTTPS）、端口、主机名，以及 TLS 设置（如适用）。
    All files discussed in this section are in the chapter07/gateway directory of the book’s GitHub repository.
    本节讨论的所有文件都位于本书 GitHub 仓库的 chapter07/gateway 目录下。
    Here is an example configuration (basic-gateway.yaml) for a Gateway named mygateway:
    以下是名为 mygateway 的 Gateway 的示例配置（basic-gateway.yaml）：
    代码（原文）
    apiVersion: gateway.networking.k8s.io/v1
kind: Gateway
metadata:
name: my-gateway
spec:
gatewayClassName: cilium
listeners:
- protocol: HTTP
port: 80
name: web-gw
allowedRoutes:
namespaces:
from: Same
    First, note that the spec.gatewayClassName value is cilium. This refers to the previously configured cilium GatewayClass. The my-gateway Gateway will listen on port 80 for HTTP traffic coming into the cluster. The allowedRoutes field specifies the namespaces from which routes may be attached to this Gateway. Same means that only routes in the same namespace may be used. Using All instead of Same would enable this Gateway to be associated with routes in any namespace. It would also enable us to use a single Gateway across multiple namespaces that may be managed by different teams. We could specify different namespaces in the route configuration—for example, we could route traffic to https://acme.example/payments to a namespace where a payment app is deployed and traffic to https://acme.example/ads to the namespace the ads team uses for its application.
    首先，注意 spec.gatewayClassName 的值为 cilium，它引用了之前配置的 cilium GatewayClass。my-gateway 这个 Gateway 将在端口 80 上监听进入集群的 HTTP 流量。allowedRoutes 字段指定了允许将路由挂载到该 Gateway 的命名空间范围。Same 表示只有同一命名空间内的路由可以使用。如果使用 All 替代 Same，则该 Gateway 可以关联任意命名空间内的路由。这也使得我们可以在多个可能由不同团队管理的命名空间之间复用同一个 Gateway。我们可以在路由配置中指定不同的命名空间——例如，将发往 https://acme.example/payments 的流量路由到部署了支付应用的命名空间，将发往 https://acme.example/ads 的流量路由到广告团队用于其应用的命名空间。

    HTTPRoute
    HTTPRoute

    HTTPRoute is a Gateway API type for specifying routing behavior of HTTP requests from a Gateway listener to a Kubernetes service. The specification includes a set of rules that enable you to direct the traffic based on your requirements. Let’s go through an example (basic-http-route.yaml):
    HTTPRoute 是一种 Gateway API 类型，用于指定 HTTP 请求如何从 Gateway 监听器路由到 Kubernetes Service。其规范包含一组规则，让你能够根据需求指导流量方向。我们来看一个示例（basic-http-route.yaml）：
    代码（原文）
    apiVersion: gateway.networking.k8s.io/v1
kind: HTTPRoute
metadata:
name: http-app-1
spec:
parentRefs:
- name: my-gateway
namespace: default
rules:
- matches:
- path:
type: PathPrefix
value: /details
backendRefs:
- name: details
port: 9080
- matches:
- headers:
- type: Exact
name: magic
value: foo
queryParams:
- type: Exact
name: great
value: example
path:
type: PathPrefix
value: /
method: GET
backendRefs:
- name: productpage
port: 9080
    This first rule is routing HTTP traffic with a path starting with /details to the details service over port 9080. The second rule uses different matching criteria: if the HTTP request has an HTTP header named magic with a value of foo and a query parameter named great with a value of example, and the HTTP method is GET, then the traffic will be sent to the productpage service over port 9080. There are other routes available with Gateway API, such as GRPCRoute (for gRPC traffic), TCPRoute, and UDPRoute. We’ll see an example with GRPCRoute later in this chapter. At the time of writing, the latter two are not supported by Cilium.
    第一个规则将路径以 /details 开头的 HTTP 流量路由到端口 9080 上的 details Service。第二个规则使用了不同的匹配条件：如果 HTTP 请求具有值为 foo 的 HTTP 头 magic，值为 example 的查询参数 great，且 HTTP 方法为 GET，那么流量会被发送到端口 9080 上的 productpage Service。Gateway API 还有其他类型的路由，例如 GRPCRoute（用于 gRPC 流量）、TCPRoute 和 UDPRoute。本章后面会看到 GRPCRoute 的示例。撰写本书时，后两者尚未被 Cilium 支持。

    Routing HTTP Traffic with Gateway API
    通过 Gateway API 路由 HTTP 流量

    This section will get you started with Cilium Gateway API. First, delete the existing kind cluster if it still exists and deploy a new one (kind.yaml).
    本节将带你上手 Cilium Gateway API。首先，如果已存在 kind 集群请删除它，然后部署一个新的（kind.yaml）。
    We’re deploying a new cluster for simplicity, but migrating existing clusters from Cilium Ingress to Cilium Gateway API is possible. It requires activating the Gateway API feature and migrating the configuration, either by manually creating Gateway API resources based on existing Ingress resources or by leveraging the Ingress2gateway tool, which reads Ingress resources from a Kubernetes cluster and outputs YAML for equivalent Gateway API objects.
    我们部署新集群是为了简化演示，但将现有集群从 Cilium Ingress 迁移到 Cilium Gateway API 也是可行的。这需要激活 Gateway API 特性并迁移配置——要么根据现有 Ingress 资源手动创建 Gateway API 资源，要么使用 Ingress2gateway 工具，它可以从 Kubernetes 集群读取 Ingress 资源并输出等价的 Gateway API 对象的 YAML。
    Before installing Cilium with Gateway API enabled (or enabling Gateway API on an existing Cilium install), you must install the Gateway API CRDs. You’ll find these in the Gateway API GitHub repo. You might wonder why this is required, since the Cilium operator typically manages CRDs. Cilium supports both Standard (stable, backward-compatible) and Experimental (feature-rich but unstable) Gateway API channels. To avoid conflicts or broken functionality, you must install the Gateway API CRDs yourself before deploying Cilium to ensure Cilium won’t override a newer or Experimental version during the install or upgrade. If the Gateway API CRDs are not present at the time Helm is executed to install or upgrade Cilium, the cilium GatewayClass will not be created. We’ll use the Standard CRDs in our example. Cilium 1.18 conforms with Gateway API 1.3, so we will be using the 1.3 CRDs in this instance:
    在安装启用了 Gateway API 的 Cilium（或在已有的 Cilium 安装上启用 Gateway API）之前，你 必须 先安装 Gateway API CRD。你可以在 Gateway API 的 GitHub 仓库找到它们。你可能会好奇为什么这是必需的——毕竟 Cilium Operator 通常会管理 CRD。Cilium 同时支持 Standard（稳定、向后兼容）和 Experimental（功能丰富但不稳定）两种 Gateway API 通道。为避免冲突或功能异常，你必须在部署 Cilium 之前自行安装 Gateway API CRD，以确保 Cilium 不会在安装或升级过程中覆盖更新的或 Experimental 版本。如果执行 Helm 安装或升级 Cilium 时 Gateway API CRD 不存在，cilium GatewayClass 就不会被创建。我们的示例中使用 Standard CRD。Cilium 1.18 符合 Gateway API 1.3，因此本例我们使用 1.3 版本的 CRD：
    代码（原文）
    $ CRD_PATH="https://raw.githubusercontent.com/"\
"kubernetes-sigs/gateway-api/v1.3.0/config/crd/standard/"
$ kubectl apply -f "${CRD_PATH}/gateway.networking.k8s.io_gatewayclasses.yaml"
$ kubectl apply -f "${CRD_PATH}/gateway.networking.k8s.io_gateways.yaml"
$ kubectl apply -f "${CRD_PATH}/gateway.networking.k8s.io_httproutes.yaml"
$ kubectl apply -f "${CRD_PATH}/gateway.networking.k8s.io_referencegrants.yaml"
$ kubectl apply -f "${CRD_PATH}/gateway.networking.k8s.io_grpcroutes.yaml"
    Let’s now deploy Cilium using the Helm configuration cilium-gatewayapi-values.yaml. Like Cilium Ingress, Cilium Gateway API requires KPR. This feature can be enabled
    现在，使用 Helm 配置文件 cilium-gatewayapi-values.yaml 来部署 Cilium。与 Cilium Ingress 类似，Cilium Gateway API 也需要 KPR。此特性可以通过
    代码（原文）
    with gatewayAPI.enabled=true.4
    4To activate the Gateway API feature on an existing Cilium deployment, enable it with Helm/Cilium CLI and restart the operator Deployment and agent DaemonSet.
    4要在现有的 Cilium 部署上激活 Gateway API 特性，请通过 Helm/Cilium CLI 启用它，然后重启 Operator Deployment 和 Agent DaemonSet。
    We’ll also add a CiliumLoadBalancerIPPool object (pool.yaml) and a layer 2 announcement policy (l2policy.yaml) and deploy the sample Bookinfo application (bookinfo.yaml) we used in the Ingress section. Let’s now deploy the Gateway and HTTPRoute configuration we walked through earlier:
    我们还将添加 CiliumLoadBalancerIPPool 对象（pool.yaml）、二层通告策略（l2policy.yaml），并部署前面 Ingress 一节使用过的 Bookinfo 示例应用（bookinfo.yaml）。现在来部署之前讨论过的 Gateway 和 HTTPRoute 配置：
    代码（原文）
    $ kubectl apply -f basic-gateway.yaml
gateway.gateway.networking.k8s.io/my-gateway created
$ kubectl apply -f basic-http-route.yaml
httproute.gateway.networking.k8s.io/http-app-1 created
    First, we’ll verify that a cilium GatewayClass has been created and that the Gateway has received an IP address and has been “programmed”—meaning that Cilium programmed the Gateway configuration into its Envoy component:
    首先，我们来验证 cilium GatewayClass 是否已创建，Gateway 是否已获得 IP 地址并且完成了"编程"——即 Cilium 已将 Gateway 配置编程进它的 Envoy 组件：
    代码（原文）
    $ kubectl get GatewayClass
NAME     CONTROLLER                     ACCEPTED   AGE
cilium   io.cilium/gateway-controller   True       43s
$ kubectl get gateway
NAME         CLASS    ADDRESS          PROGRAMMED   AGE
my-gateway   cilium   172.18.255.200   True         27m
    Next, we’ll verify that the route was successfully attached to the Gateway. The Accepted HTTPRoute condition type indicates the configuration was correct and accepted:
    接下来，我们验证路由是否已成功挂载到 Gateway。Accepted HTTPRoute 条件类型表示配置正确且已被接受：
    代码（原文）
    $ kubectl get httproutes http-app-1 -o yaml | yq .status.parents
- conditions:
message: Accepted HTTPRoute
observedGeneration: 1
reason: Accepted
controllerName: io.cilium/gateway-controller
parentRef:
group: gateway.networking.k8s.io
kind: Gateway
name: my-gateway
namespace: default
    You should now be able to access your internal Bookinfo application from outside the cluster, as illustrated in Figure 7-4. It’s almost identical to Figure 7-1, the only difference being the use of the Gateway API objects instead of Ingress.
    你现在应该已经可以从集群外部访问内部的 Bookinfo 应用，如图 7-4 所示。它与图 7-1 几乎一模一样，唯一的区别是使用了 Gateway API 对象而非 Ingress。
    Save the Gateway IP as an environment variable, and then verify with cURL that HTTP path matching works as expected. Because the path starts with /details, this traffic matches the first rule and is proxied to the details service over port 9080. The cURL request should be successful:
    将 Gateway IP 保存为环境变量，然后用 cURL 验证 HTTP 路径匹配是否如预期工作。由于路径以 /details 开头，这条流量匹配第一条规则，被代理到端口 9080 上的 details Service。cURL 请求应该能成功：
    代码（原文）
    $ GATEWAY="$(kubectl get gateway my-gateway -o \
jsonpath='{.status.addresses[0].value}')"
$ echo "$GATEWAY"
172.18.255.200
$ curl --fail -s "http://$GATEWAY/details/1"
{ "id": 1, "author": "William Shakespeare", "year": 1595, ... }
    Next, you can verify HTTP header matching. With the following curl command, we specify a header (magic:foo) and a query parameter key/value pair (great=example). Since cURL implicitly uses the GET HTTP method, traffic will be matched by the HTTPRoute rule and directed to the productpage backend:
    接下来，你可以验证 HTTP 头匹配。在以下 curl 命令中，我们指定了一个头（magic:foo）和一个查询参数键值对（great=example）。由于 cURL 默认使用 GET HTTP 方法，流量将被 HTTPRoute 规则匹配到并转发到 productpage 后端：
    代码（原文）
    $ curl -v -H 'magic: foo' "http://$GATEWAY?great=example"
*   Trying 172.18.255.200:80...
* Connected to 172.18.255.200 (172.18.255.200) port 80
> GET /?great=example HTTP/1.1
> User-Agent: curl/8.5.0
> Accept: */*
> magic: foo
>
< HTTP/1.1 200 OK
< server: envoy
< x-envoy-upstream-service-time: 9
<
<p>
<h3>Hello! This is a simple bookstore application consisting of three
services as shown below</h3>
</p>
    We set the preceding curl command to verbose with the -v flag. It prints the HTTP data (confirming the cURL request, including the query parameters, headers, and method) for both outbound (preceded by >) and inbound (preceded by <) traffic. Notice the xenvoy-upstream-service-time line—it confirms that Envoy proxied the transaction and indicates that the upstream service took 9 ms to respond.
    我们通过 -v 标志将前面的 curl 命令设为详细模式，它会打印 HTTP 数据（确认包含查询参数、头和 HTTP 方法的 cURL 请求），同时显示出站（以 > 开头）和入站（以 < 开头）流量。注意看 x-envoy-upstream-service-time 这一行——它确认了 Envoy 代理了此次事务，并表明上游 Service 用了 9 ms 响应。

    TLS Termination with Gateway API
    通过 Gateway API 进行 TLS 终止

    Cilium Gateway API also supports TLS termination. The equivalent configuration to the one we saw earlier with Ingress looks like this (tls-gateway.yaml and httpsroute.yaml):
    Cilium Gateway API 也支持 TLS 终止。它与之前 Ingress 示例中看到的等价配置如下（tls-gateway.yaml 和 httpsroute.yaml）：
    代码（原文）
    apiVersion: gateway.networking.k8s.io/v1
kind: Gateway
metadata:
name: tls-gateway
spec:
gatewayClassName: cilium
listeners:
- name: https-1
protocol: HTTPS
port: 443
hostname: "bookinfo.cilium.rocks"
tls:
certificateRefs:
- kind: Secret
name: demo-cert
---
apiVersion: gateway.networking.k8s.io/v1
kind: HTTPRoute
metadata:
name: https-app-route-1
spec:
parentRefs:
- name: tls-gateway
hostnames:
- "bookinfo.cilium.rocks"
rules:
- matches:
- path:
type: PathPrefix
value: /details
backendRefs:
- name: details
port: 9080

    Layer 7 Load Balancing Across Backends
    第 7 层后端间负载均衡

    Cilium’s Gateway API implementation includes native support for HTTP traffic splitting. This feature allows you to send a percentage of traffic to different backend services without needing a separate service mesh or third-party tool. Common use cases include A/B testing, canary deployments, or blue/green rollouts where a new version of an application is gradually introduced. In this section, we’ll deploy two versions of a sample application and configure an HTTPRoute to split traffic evenly between them (50/50 weight). Later, we’ll adjust the weights to simulate a more pragmatic and realistic approach to production rollouts of an application.
    Cilium 的 Gateway API 实现原生支持 HTTP 流量切分。此特性允许你将一定比例的流量发送到不同的后端服务，而无需额外的服务网格或第三方工具。常见用例包括 A/B 测试、金丝雀发布，或灰度/蓝绿发布——即在这些场景中，新版本应用会被逐步引入。本节，我们将部署同一个示例应用的两个版本，并配置一个 HTTPRoute 在它们之间均匀切分流量（50/50 权重）。随后，我们会调整权重，模拟一种更加务实、贴近生产环境发布的做法。

    50/50 Load Balancing
    50/50 负载均衡

    Start by deploying a simple echo application (echo-servers.yaml). This YAML file deploys two internal (ClusterIP) services, echo-1 and echo-2, each backed by its own set of pods and listening on different ports. This app returns the name of the pod and node handling each request, allowing us to clearly observe where the traffic is routed:
    首先部署一个简单的 echo 应用（echo-servers.yaml）。这个 YAML 文件会部署两个内部（ClusterIP）服务 echo-1 和 echo-2，各自由一组独立的 Pod 支撑，且监听不同端口。该应用会返回处理每个请求的 Pod 名称与节点名称，便于我们清晰地观察流量被路由到了哪里：
    代码（原文）
    $ kubectl get pods,svc
NAME                                         READY   STATUS
pod/echo-1-65bf7cf9b5-6zsf6                  1/1     Running
pod/echo-2-569cb769b7-zh878                  1/1     Running
NAME                                 CLUSTER-IP      EXTERNAL-IP      PORT(S)
service/echo-1                       10.96.125.138   <none>           8080/TCP
service/echo-2                       10.96.74.120    <none>           8090/TCP
    To split incoming traffic between echo-1 and echo-2, apply the HTTPRoute resource (load-balancing-http-route.yaml). Note the equal 50% weight under each service:
    若要在 echo-1 和 echo-2 之间切分入站流量，请应用 HTTPRoute 资源（load-balancing-http-route.yaml）。注意每个服务下都有相等的 50% 权重：
    代码（原文）
    apiVersion: gateway.networking.k8s.io/v1
kind: HTTPRoute
metadata:
name: load-balancing-route
spec:
parentRefs:
- name: my-gateway
rules:
- matches:
- path:
type: PathPrefix
value: /echo
backendRefs:
- kind: Service
name: echo-1
port: 8080
weight: 50
- kind: Service
name: echo-2
port: 8090
weight: 50
    This route distributes HTTP traffic to the /echo path evenly between the two backend services, as illustrated in Figure 7-5.
    此路由会将发往 /echo 路径的 HTTP 流量均匀分配到两个后端服务之间，如图 7-5 所示。
    We can verify this by sending some requests to the /echo endpoint and inspecting the responses. The response body includes the pod hostname, allowing you to identify which backend received the request:
    我们可以通过向 /echo 端点发送一些请求并检查响应来进行验证。响应体中包含了 Pod 主机名，让你能够识别请求最终到达了哪个后端：
    代码（原文）
    $ curl "http://$GATEWAY/echo"
Request served by echo-2-569cb769b7-zh878
    Repeat the request several times. You should see responses alternating between echo-1 and echo-2. To verify the distribution more precisely, run the command 1,000 times and store the output in a text file:
    重复请求若干次。你应该能看到响应在 echo-1 和 echo-2 之间交替出现。若要更精确地验证分布情况，可运行该命令 1000 次，并将输出保存到一个文本文件中：
    代码（原文）
    for _ in {1..1000}; do
curl -s "http://$GATEWAY/echo" >> curlresponses.txt
done
    With grep, you should see roughly equal counts for the two services:
    使用 grep，你应该能看到两个服务的请求数量大致相等：
    代码（原文）
    $ grep -o "Request served by echo-." curlresponses.txt | sort | uniq -c
517 Request served by echo-1
483 Request served by echo-2

    Adjusting Weights: 99/1 Split
    调整权重：99/1 切分

    Let’s now simulate a canary rollout where nearly all traffic goes to one service, as shown in Figure 7-6.
    现在我们来模拟一次金丝雀发布，让几乎全部流量都指向一个服务，如图 7-6 所示。
    Edit the existing HTTPRoute resource:
    编辑现有的 HTTPRoute 资源：
    代码（原文）
    $ kubectl edit httproute load-balancing-route
