    In the backendRefs section, update the weights to 99 for echo-1 and 1 for echo-2. Repeat the traffic test and save the output to another text file (curlresponses991.txt, for example). You should now see the vast majority of requests going to echo-1, with only a small fraction reaching echo-2:
    在 backendRefs 字段中，将权重更新为：echo-1 设为 99，echo-2 设为 1。重新执行流量测试，并将输出保存到另一个文本文件（例如 curlresponses991.txt）。此时你应该会看到绝大多数请求都发往 echo-1，只有很小一部分会到达 echo-2：
    代码（原文）
    $ grep -o "Request served by echo-." curlresponses991.txt | sort | uniq -c
990 Request served by echo-1
10 Request served by echo-2

    Traffic Management
    流量管理

    In addition to path-based routing and traffic splitting, Cilium Gateway API enables powerful traffic customization at layer 7 using filters in HTTPRoute resources. These filters allow you to:
    除了基于路径的路由和流量切分之外，Cilium Gateway API 还允许通过 HTTPRoute 资源中的过滤器，在第 7 层进行强大的流量定制。这些过滤器让你可以：
    • Modify both incoming request headers and outgoing response headers.
      同时修改入站请求头与出站响应头。
    • Redirect requests by rewriting paths, hostnames, URL schemes, or status codes.
      通过重写路径、主机名、URL 方案或状态码来重定向请求。
    • Rewrite URL paths before forwarding requests to a backend.
      在将请求转发给后端之前重写 URL 路径。
    • Mirror traffic to an additional backend for testing or debugging purposes.
      将流量镜像到额外的后端，用于测试或排障。
    We’ll focus on the first two use cases here. See the Cilium documentation for more information on the many other use cases supported by Gateway API.
    我们这里重点介绍前两个用例。关于 Gateway API 支持的其他许多用例，请参阅 Cilium 文档。

    Modifying HTTP Headers with Gateway API
    使用 Gateway API 修改 HTTP 头

    In this section, we’ll explore how to add headers to HTTP requests using RequestHeaderModifier and how to add headers to HTTP responses using ResponseHeaderModifier. These techniques are useful for tasks like adding custom metadata and routing logic, A/B testing, or helping frontends adapt to different backend versions.
    本节，我们将探讨如何使用 RequestHeaderModifier 为 HTTP 请求添加头，以及如何使用 ResponseHeaderModifier 为 HTTP 响应添加头。这些技术适用于添加自定义元数据与路由逻辑、A/B 测试，或帮助前端适配不同后端版本等任务。

    Modifying HTTP request headers 修改 HTTP 请求头

    Start by deploying a route that matches HTTP requests to a specific path (echoheader-http-route.yaml):
    首先部署一条将 HTTP 请求匹配到特定路径的路由（echoheader-http-route.yaml）：
    代码（原文）
    apiVersion: gateway.networking.k8s.io/v1
kind: HTTPRoute
metadata:
name: header-http-echo
spec:
parentRefs:
- name: my-gateway
rules:
- matches:
- path:
type: PathPrefix
value: /cilium-add-a-request-header
backendRefs:
- name: echo-1
    Send a test request and inspect the echoed request headers. You will see only the default headers (host, user-agent, etc.):
    发送一个测试请求，并检查回显的请求头。你会看到只有默认的头（host、user-agent 等）：
    代码（原文）
    $ curl --fail -s "http://$GATEWAY/cilium-add-a-request-header"
Request served by echo-1-65bf7cf9b5-6zsf6
HTTP/1.1 GET /cilium-add-a-request-header
Host: 172.18.255.200
Accept: */*
User-Agent: curl/8.7.1
    Now, update the HTTPRoute by using the RequestHeaderModifier filter to add a header. Deploy the echo-header-http-route-header-modifier.yaml file from the repo:
    现在，使用 RequestHeaderModifier 过滤器更新 HTTPRoute 以添加一个头。从仓库中部署 echo-header-http-route-header-modifier.yaml 文件：
    代码（原文）
    rules:
- matches:
- path:
type: PathPrefix
value: /cilium-add-a-request-header
filters:
- type: RequestHeaderModifier
requestHeaderModifier:
add:
- name: my-cilium-header-name
value: my-cilium-header-value
    The header my-cilium-header-name with the value my-cilium-header-value will be added to HTTP requests matching the path prefix. You can repeat the cURL request to verify this. The echoed headers should now include your custom header:
    对于匹配该路径前缀的 HTTP 请求，将添加值为 my-cilium-header-value 的头 my-cilium-header-name。你可以重复 cURL 请求以验证。回显的头中现在应该包含你的自定义头：
    代码（原文）
    $ curl --fail -s "http://$GATEWAY/cilium-add-a-request-header"
Host: 172.18.255.200
Accept: */*
My-Cilium-Header-Name: my-cilium-header-value
User-Agent: curl/8.7.1
    Note that you can also remove or amend an existing header by specifying the remove or amend action instead of add.
    注意，你也可以通过指定 remove 或 amend 而非 add 来删除或修改已有的头。

    Modifying HTTP response headers 修改 HTTP 响应头

    Now let’s modify the headers in the response body. This can be useful for adding versioning information, setting custom cookies, or differentiating between beta and stable backends. Apply the following manifest (response-header-modifier-http-route.yaml). Note that only the relevant portion of the manifest is being reproduced here in the book; the full code listing is available in the book’s repo. The syntax is very similar to the previous example, but this time we’ll use the ResponseHeaderModifier filter to add multiple headers to the HTTP response coming back from the server to the client. The route modifies responses to the /multiple path by adding three custom headers:
    现在我们来修改响应体中的头。这对于添加版本信息、设置自定义 Cookie，或区分 beta 与稳定版后端都很有用。应用以下清单（response-header-modifier-http-route.yaml）。注意，本书只给出了清单的相关片段，完整代码清单可在本书仓库中找到。语法与上一个示例非常类似，但这次我们使用 ResponseHeaderModifier 过滤器，为服务器返回客户端的 HTTP 响应添加多个头。该路由通过添加三个自定义头来修改发往 /multiple 路径的响应：
    代码（原文）
    rules:
- matches:
- path:
type: PathPrefix
value: /multiple
filters:
- type: ResponseHeaderModifier
responseHeaderModifier:
add:
- name: X-Header-Add-1
value: header-add-1
- name: X-Header-Add-2
value: header-add-2
backendRefs:
- name: echo-1
port: 8080
    Send a verbose cURL request to test it:
    发送一个 verbose cURL 请求进行测试：
    代码（原文）
    $ curl -v --fail -s "http://$GATEWAY/multiple"
< HTTP/1.1 200 OK
< x-header-add-1: header-add-1
< x-header-add-2: header-add-2
    You should see that the response includes the three custom headers, confirming the my-gateway Gateway has added headers to the response successfully. Alternatively, you can observe HTTP traffic behavior using Hubble (which you can enable by following the instructions in Chapter 3), for example by matching on HTTP header values:
    你应该能看到响应中包含了这三个自定义头，说明 my-gateway Gateway 已成功向响应添加了头。或者，你也可以使用 Hubble（按照第 3 章的说明启用）观察 HTTP 流量行为，例如匹配 HTTP 头值：
    代码（原文）
    $ hubble observe --http-header "X-Header-Add-1:header-add-1"
    See Chapter 15 for more details on HTTP observability in Hubble, including how to redact sensitive headers (such as the Authorization header) from flow logs.
    关于 Hubble 中的 HTTP 可观测性（包括如何从流日志中对敏感头（如 Authorization 头）进行脱敏），详见第 15 章。

    HTTP Redirects with Gateway API
    使用 Gateway API 进行 HTTP 重定向

    Cilium Gateway API supports native HTTP redirection using filters within HTTPRoute resources. This feature allows you to modify the destination of incoming requests by changing the path, hostname, scheme, or status code. You can use redirection for a range of use cases, including application migrations, URL reorganization, and redirecting HTTP clients to HTTPS. In this section, we’ll explore four redirect scenarios, each handled by a separate rule in the same HTTPRoute manifest (redirect-route.yaml):
    Cilium Gateway API 通过 HTTPRoute 资源内的过滤器原生支持 HTTP 重定向。此特性让你能够通过修改路径、主机名、方案或状态码来更改入站请求的目的地。你可以将重定向用于多种场景，包括应用迁移、URL 重组，以及将 HTTP 客户端重定向到 HTTPS。本节，我们将探讨四种重定向场景，每种场景由同一个 HTTPRoute 清单中的独立规则处理（redirect-route.yaml）：
    代码（原文）
    apiVersion: gateway.networking.k8s.io/v1
kind: HTTPRoute
metadata:
name: redirect-path
spec:
parentRefs:
- name: my-gateway
rules:
- matches:
- path:
type: PathPrefix
value: /original-prefix
filters:
- type: RequestRedirect
requestRedirect:
path:
type: ReplacePrefixMatch
replacePrefixMatch: /replacement-prefix
- matches:
- path:
type: PathPrefix
value: /path-and-host
filters:
- type: RequestRedirect
requestRedirect:
hostname: example.org
path:
type: ReplacePrefixMatch
replacePrefixMatch: /replacement-prefix
- matches:
- path:
type: PathPrefix
value: /path-and-status
filters:
- type: RequestRedirect
requestRedirect:
path:
type: ReplacePrefixMatch
replacePrefixMatch: /replacement-prefix
statusCode: 301
- matches:
- path:
type: PathPrefix
value: /scheme-and-host
filters:
- type: RequestRedirect
requestRedirect:
hostname: example.org
scheme: "https"
    The first rule in the manifest rewrites the path prefix while preserving the hostname and protocol. The second rule changes both the hostname and the path. The third rule demonstrates how to change the HTTP status code returned by the redirect. The final rule demonstrates how to change the scheme (protocol) to redirect from HTTP to HTTPS. The requests are depicted in Figure 7-7.
    清单中第一条规则重写路径前缀，同时保留主机名和协议。第二条规则同时修改主机名和路径。第三条规则演示了如何更改重定向返回的 HTTP 状态码。最后一条规则演示了如何修改方案（协议），将 HTTP 重定向到 HTTPS。这些请求如图 7-7 所示。
    Let’s deploy the route and verify that each rule works as expected. First, verify the path prefix redirection (Figure 7-7A). Make a request to trigger the redirect and note the 302 redirect code alongside the recommended URL:
    我们来部署该路由并验证每条规则是否按预期工作。首先，验证路径前缀重定向（图 7-7A）。发起一个请求以触发重定向，记录下 302 重定向码以及推荐的 URL：
    代码（原文）
    $ curl -l -v "http://$GATEWAY/original-prefix"
< HTTP/1.1 302 Found
< location: http://172.18.255.200:80/replacement-prefix
    Next, verify the redirect to a new hostname and path (Figure 7-7B). Make a request to the /path-and-host path:
    接下来，验证重定向到新的主机名和路径（图 7-7B）。向 /path-and-host 路径发起请求：
    代码（原文）
    $ curl -l -v "http://$GATEWAY/path-and-host"
< HTTP/1.1 302 Found
< location: http://example.org:80/replacement-prefix
    As you can see, both the host and the prefix have been replaced. Now verify that Cilium Gateway API can redirect you to a permanently moved URL (Figure 7-7C). Make the request and note the permanent redirection code (301) in the reply:
    如你所见，主机名和前缀都已被替换。现在验证 Cilium Gateway API 能够将你重定向到一个永久迁移的 URL（图 7-7C）。发起请求，并注意响应中的永久重定向码（301）：
    代码（原文）
    $ curl -l -v "http://$GATEWAY/path-and-status"
< HTTP/1.1 301 Moved Permanently
< location: http://172.18.255.200:80/replacement-prefix
    Finally, verify the HTTP host and scheme redirect capability (Figure 7-7D). This feature can redirect users to use HTTPS instead of HTTP for a specific request.
    最后，验证 HTTP 主机和方案重定向能力（图 7-7D）。此特性可以将用户重定向为对特定请求使用 HTTPS 而非 HTTP。
    HTTP to HTTPS redirection is best suited to URLs that are expected to be accessed by users via web browsers and should be avoided for “API” URLs that are expected to be accessed by machines. This type of redirection can result in accidentally exposing authentication tokens. For example, machines accessing an API often attach a bearer token in the Authorization header of every request. If such a client is misconfigured to use an HTTP endpoint, it will send the entire request (including the token) in plain text before receiving the redirect to the HTTPS endpoint. The extra friction of not supporting HTTP at all is usually worth the security benefits of not risking credential exposure.
    HTTP 到 HTTPS 的重定向最适合预期由用户通过 Web 浏览器访问的 URL，而对于预期由机器访问的“API”类 URL 应避免使用。这种重定向可能会意外暴露认证令牌。例如，访问 API 的机器通常会在每个请求的 Authorization 头中附加一个 Bearer 令牌。如果这样的客户端被错误地配置为使用 HTTP 端点，它会在收到重定向到 HTTPS 端点之前，先将整个请求（包括令牌）以明文形式发送出去。不支持 HTTP 带来的一点点额外麻烦，通常远小于凭证暴露风险所带来的安全收益。
    While you can use curl for this, you can also check in your browser. Assuming the IP address assigned to your Gateway is 172.18.255.200, directly accessing http://172.18.255.200/scheme-and-host from your browser will redirect you to https://example.org/scheme-and-host.
    虽然你可以使用 curl 来测试，但也可以在浏览器中验证。假设 Gateway 分配到的 IP 地址为 172.18.255.200，那么从浏览器直接访问 http://172.18.255.200/scheme-and-host 会被重定向到 https://example.org/scheme-and-host。

    gRPC Routing
    gRPC 路由

    gRPC is a high-performance remote procedure call (RPC) framework built on HTTP/2; it provides bidirectional streaming between microservices. Gateway API provides first-class support for gRPC via GRPCRoute, which can match on host, headers, service, and method and then forward requests to the appropriate Kubernetes services.5 Ingress has no common standard for gRPC routing, so support is implementationspecific and often relies on controller-specific annotations. With GRPCRoute standardized in Gateway API since version 1.1.0, you get portable, annotation-free manifests that work across compliant implementations. Let’s try this out with Google Cloud’s Online Boutique demo application, a web-based ecommerce app allowing users to browse items, add them to a cart, and purchase them. Online Boutique is composed of 11 microservices written in different languages that communicate using gRPC. Deploy the app by applying the gcp-microservices-demo.yaml manifest, and then review the GRPCRoute manifest (grpc-route.yaml). It defines routes for gRPC requests targeting the productcatalogservice and currencyservice microservices on ports 3550 and 7000, respectively. The structure of GRPCRoute mirrors that of HTTPRoute:
    gRPC 是一种构建在 HTTP/2 之上的高性能远程过程调用（RPC）框架；它在微服务之间提供双向流传输。Gateway API 通过 GRPCRoute 为 gRPC 提供一流支持，可以根据主机、头、服务和方法进行匹配，然后将请求转发到相应的 Kubernetes 服务。5 Ingress 对 gRPC 路由没有统一标准，因此其支持因实现而异，通常依赖于控制器特定的注解。随着 Gateway API 自 1.1.0 版本起将 GRPCRoute 标准化，你就得到了可移植、无需注解的清单，能够在所有符合规范的实现上运行。下面我们用 Google Cloud 的 Online Boutique 演示应用来试一下——这是一个基于 Web 的电商应用，允许用户浏览商品、加入购物车并完成购买。Online Boutique 由 11 个用不同语言编写、通过 gRPC 通信的微服务组成。应用 gcp-microservices-demo.yaml 清单部署该应用，然后查看 GRPCRoute 清单（grpc-route.yaml）。它为分别运行在端口 3550 和 7000 上的 productcatalogservice 和 currencyservice 微服务定义了 gRPC 路由。GRPCRoute 的结构与 HTTPRoute 类似：
    5A gRPC service defines a set of methods that a client can call remotely, each representing an API endpoint with a specific request and response type.
    5 一个 gRPC 服务定义了一组客户端可远程调用的方法，每个方法代表一个带有特定请求与响应类型的 API 端点。
    代码（原文）
    apiVersion: gateway.networking.k8s.io/v1
kind: GRPCRoute
metadata:
name: productcatalogservice-rule
spec:
parentRefs:
- namespace: default
name: my-gateway
rules:
- matches:
- method:
service: hipstershop.ProductCatalogService
method:  ListProducts
backendRefs:
- name: productcatalogservice
port: 3550
---
apiVersion: gateway.networking.k8s.io/v1
kind: GRPCRoute
metadata:
name: currencyservice-rule
spec:
parentRefs:
- namespace: default
name: my-gateway
rules:
- matches:
- method:
service: hipstershop.CurrencyService
method:  GetSupportedCurrencies
backendRefs:
- name: currencyservice
port: 7000
    Attach this route to the previously deployed my-gateway entry point. Match on the fully qualified gRPC service. Match on the gRPC method. Forward to the target Kubernetes service and port. Once you’ve deployed grpc-route.yaml, you can make gRPC requests to backend services using grpcurl.6
    将该路由附加到之前部署的 my-gateway 入口点。在完全限定的 gRPC 服务上进行匹配。在 gRPC 方法上进行匹配。转发到目标 Kubernetes 服务和端口。部署完 grpc-route.yaml 后，你可以使用 grpcurl 向后端服务发起 gRPC 请求。6
    6grpcurl is a command-line tool for interacting with gRPC services. It can be installed through your package manager (e.g., with brew install grpcurl on macOS).
    6 grpcurl 是一个用于与 gRPC 服务交互的命令行工具。可通过你的包管理器安装（例如在 macOS 上执行 brew install grpcurl）。
    First, access the currency service of the application, which lists the supported currencies. Note that since gRPC is binary-encoded, you also need the proto definitions7 for the gRPC services to make gRPC requests:
    首先，访问应用的货币服务，它会列出支持的货币。注意，由于 gRPC 是二进制编码的，因此你还需要 gRPC 服务的 proto 定义 7 来发起 gRPC 请求：
    代码（原文）
    $ grpcurl -plaintext -proto ./demo.proto "$GATEWAY:80" \
hipstershop.CurrencyService/GetSupportedCurrencies
{ "currencyCodes": [ "EUR", "USD", "GBP", [...] ] }
    Next, try accessing the product catalog service. You should see a collection of JSON objects describing items such as sunglasses, a tank top, and a watch in the output:
    接下来，尝试访问产品目录服务。在输出中你应该能看到一组描述各种商品（如太阳镜、背心、手表）的 JSON 对象：
    代码（原文）
    $ grpcurl -plaintext -proto ./demo.proto "$GATEWAY:80" \
hipstershop.ProductCatalogService/ListProducts | jq
{"id":"OLJCESPC7Z","name":"Sunglasses","price":19.99}
{"id":"66VCHSJNUP","name":"Tank Top","price":18.99}

    East–West Layer 7 Routing with GAMMA and Gateway API
    使用 GAMMA 和 Gateway API 进行东西向第 7 层路由

    While this chapter has so far focused on traffic entering the cluster (north–south), Gateway API can also be used to manage traffic within the cluster (east–west). Traditionally, Kubernetes services handle east–west traffic using layer 3/4 load balancing. For more advanced layer 7 routing (such as path-based routing, traffic splitting, or request timeouts), platform teams have relied on service meshes that introduce their own APIs and components. The Gateway API for Mesh Management and Administration (GAMMA) initiative extends Gateway API to support these advanced routing use cases for internal traffic. GAMMA introduces no new resources, but instead allows HTTPRoute objects to bind directly to Kubernetes services instead of Gateways. In this section, you’ll use Cilium’s GAMMA implementation to apply Gateway API features to in-cluster traffic. First, deploy gamma-manifest.yaml, which installs two echo services, echo-v1 and echo-v2, and a client pod for internal testing. It also creates an echo service that acts as the frontend for routing (GAMMA refers to the combination of the cluster IP and its DNS name as the “frontend” of the service and to the collection of endpoint IPs as the “backend”). Let’s look at a GAMMA route used for east–west service management (gammaroute.yaml). Unlike north–south Gateway API usage, the route’s parentRefs target an internal service called echo:
    到目前为止，本章聚焦的是进入集群的流量（南北向），但 Gateway API 也可用于管理集群内部的流量（东西向）。传统上，Kubernetes 服务通过第 3/4 层负载均衡处理东西向流量。对于更高级的第 7 层路由（如基于路径的路由、流量切分或请求超时），平台团队一直依赖服务网格，而这会引入自身的 API 与组件。Gateway API 管理与管理（GAMMA）计划扩展了 Gateway API，以支持针对内部流量的这些高级路由用例。GAMMA 没有引入新资源，而是允许 HTTPRoute 对象直接绑定到 Kubernetes 服务（而非 Gateway）。本节，你将使用 Cilium 的 GAMMA 实现为集群内流量应用 Gateway API 的各种特性。首先，部署 gamma-manifest.yaml，它会安装两个 echo 服务（echo-v1 和 echo-v2），以及一个用于内部测试的客户端 Pod。它还会创建一个 echo 服务，作为路由的前端（GAMMA 将集群 IP 及其 DNS 名称的组合称为服务的“前端”，将端点 IP 的集合称为“后端”）。我们来看一个用于东西向服务管理的 GAMMA 路由（gammaroute.yaml）。不同于南北向 Gateway API 的用法，该路由的 parentRefs 指向一个名为 echo 的内部服务：
    7Proto definitions describe the structure of gRPC services, including their methods and request and response types, using the Protocol Buffers language.
    7 Proto 定义使用 Protocol Buffers 语言描述 gRPC 服务的结构，包括它们的方法、请求类型和响应类型。
    代码（原文）
    apiVersion: gateway.networking.k8s.io/v1
kind: HTTPRoute
metadata:
name: gamma-route
namespace: gamma-ns
spec:
parentRefs:
- kind: Service
name: echo
rules:
- matches:
- path:
type: PathPrefix
value: /v1
backendRefs:
- name: echo-v1
port: 80
- matches:
- path:
type: PathPrefix
value: /v2
backendRefs:
- name: echo-v2
port: 80
    Deploy the route, and then test path-based routing from the internal client:
    部署该路由，然后从内部客户端测试基于路径的路由：
    代码（原文）
    $ kubectl -n gamma-ns exec -it client -- curl http://echo/v1
$ kubectl -n gamma-ns exec -it client -- curl http://echo/v2
    You should see that requests to /v1 are routed to pods behind echo-v1 and requests to /v2 are routed to echo-v2, as illustrated in Figure 7-8.
    你应该能看到，发往 /v1 的请求被路由到 echo-v1 背后的 Pod，而发往 /v2 的请求被路由到 echo-v2，如图 7-8 所示。
    This demonstrates that layer 7 routing logic using HTTPRoute objects can apply to internal traffic just as it does to external traffic. Once a route is attached to a Kubernetes service, all the other Gateway API features you’ve seen in this chapter (traffic splitting, request and response header modifications, path rewriting and redirects, etc.) can be applied in the same way.8
    这说明，使用 HTTPRoute 对象的第 7 层路由逻辑可以像应用于外部流量一样应用于内部流量。一旦路由附加到 Kubernetes 服务，本章中你见过的所有其他 Gateway API 特性（流量切分、请求与响应头修改、路径重写和重定向等）都可以以相同的方式应用。8

    Summary
    小结

    This chapter examined how Cilium supports layer 7 traffic management through both Ingress and Gateway API. We began with Cilium’s Ingress support, showing how Cilium can route external HTTP traffic using standard Kubernetes resources and how it integrates with LoadBalancer IP address management and layer 2 announcements. Then we shifted our focus to Gateway API, which introduces more structured and extensible routing capabilities. We walked through examples demonstrating how to route traffic based on HTTP path, headers, methods, and query parameters, and we explored how to implement TLS termination, gRPC routing, request and response header manipulation, and HTTP redirects. Finally, we saw how Gateway API can be used to manage east–west traffic through GAMMA. The examples in this chapter showed how the same routing model can be used for both external and internal traffic, minimizing resource utilization by removing the need for a separate ingress or service mesh system. In the next chapter, you will learn about other Cilium features designed to optimize network performance and reduce latency.
    本章研究了 Cilium 如何通过 Ingress 和 Gateway API 两种方式支持第 7 层流量管理。我们从 Cilium 的 Ingress 支持入手，展示了 Cilium 如何使用标准 Kubernetes 资源路由外部 HTTP 流量，以及它如何与 LoadBalancer IP 地址管理和二层通告集成。随后我们将重点转向 Gateway API，它带来了更结构化、可扩展的路由能力。我们逐步演示了如何基于 HTTP 路径、头、方法和查询参数路由流量，并探讨了如何实现 TLS 终止、gRPC 路由、请求与响应头操作以及 HTTP 重定向。最后，我们看到了如何通过 GAMMA 将 Gateway API 用于管理东西向流量。本章中的示例展示了同一个路由模型可以同时用于外部和内部流量，通过消除对独立 Ingress 或服务网格系统的需求来最大限度地减少资源消耗。下一章，你将学习 Cilium 中其他旨在优化网络性能、降低延迟的特性。
    8You should also know that using GAMMA means injecting Envoy into the datapath for this flow, which comes with performance implications. You can read more about these implications in Chapter 13.
    8 你还应当知道，使用 GAMMA 意味着该流量路径会将 Envoy 注入数据路径，这会带来性能影响。有关这些影响的更多内容，将在第 13 章中介绍。

    第 8 章
    Performance Networking and Traffic Optimization
    性能网络与流量优化

    So far we have focused on getting access and connectivity working with Cilium. Outside of the eBPF-based kube-proxy replacement we covered in Chapter 6, we have yet to discuss network performance. Our goal is not just to help you run Cilium; it is to help you get as much value and performance out of it as possible. A network tool is often judged on throughput, latency, and jitter, and the same goes for Cilium. In this chapter, we will review some of the Cilium features that enable you to optimize network performance. We will not cover them all; to avoid overwhelming the reader with too much information, we decided to leave out topics like Bottleneck Bandwidth and Round-trip propagation time (BBR), BIG TCP, and eBPF host routing.
    到目前为止，我们一直专注于使用 Cilium 实现访问与连接。除了第 6 章讨论的基于 eBPF 的 kube-proxy 替代方案外，我们尚未讨论网络性能。我们的目标不仅仅是帮助你运行 Cilium，更是帮助你从中获取最大的价值和性能。网络工具通常以吞吐量、延迟和抖动来评判，Cilium 亦是如此。在本章中，我们将回顾 Cilium 的一些特性，帮助你优化网络性能。我们不会涵盖所有特性；为了不让读者因过多信息而感到负担，我们决定跳过一些主题，如瓶颈带宽与往返传播时间（BBR）、BIG TCP，以及 eBPF 主机路由。
    Excluding topics is a difficult choice. To briefly summarize their value:
    不讨论这些主题是一个艰难的选择。简要概括它们的价值：
    • BBR is a modern congestion control algorithm that improves
      BBR 是一种现代拥塞控制算法，可提升
    throughput and reduces latency on long or high-bandwidth connections.
    长连接或高带宽连接的吞吐量并降低延迟。
    • BIG TCP increases efficiency for large-scale data transfers by
      BIG TCP 通过允许更大的数据包尺寸来提升大规模数据传输的效率，
    allowing larger packet sizes, reducing per-packet overhead.
    从而减少每个数据包的开销。
    • eBPF host routing bypasses parts of the traditional networking
      eBPF 主机路由绕过传统网络协议栈的部分组件，
    stack to deliver faster packet forwarding directly within the kernel. The Isovalent blog covers BBR and BIG TCP in numerous posts. eBPF host-routing is enabled by default if your kernel supports it, so it does not require further explanation here. We will focus on a set of features that boost performance at the area, node, and edge levels by reducing routing hops (in the network and within the Linux stack) and by preferring nearby backends when possible: Service Traffic Distribution [area/topology] Prioritizes endpoints in the same area when available, helping reduce latency and inter-area costs Local Redirect Policy (LRP) [node] Prioritizes node-local backends so traffic stays on the same node, reducing crossnode hops and latency Direct Server Return (DSR) [edge] Preserves the client source IP and sends responses directly from backend pods to clients, improving return path performance eXpress Data Path (XDP) [node] Processes packets at the earliest possible point in the kernel to reduce per-packet overhead and lower latency Maglev [edge] Provides consistent hashing so the same flow lands on the same backend, improving connection stability during node changes Netkit [node] Replaces the traditional veth path with a kernel-native device model to cut overhead and bring container networking performance closer to host levels We’ll start with area-aware performance, then move to node- and edge-level techniques that reduce latency and routing hops inside and around the cluster. You can find all the YAML manifests you will use in this chapter in the chapter08 directory of the book’s GitHub repository.
    直接在内核中实现更快的数据包转发。Isovalent 博客在多篇文章中介绍了 BBR 和 BIG TCP。如果你的内核支持，eBPF 主机路由默认是启用的，因此此处无需进一步解释。我们将重点关注一组通过减少路由跳数（网络内和 Linux 协议栈内）、尽可能优先选择附近后端来提升区域级、节点级和边缘级性能的特性：服务流量分布[区域/拓扑]在可用时优先选择同一区域内的端点，有助于降低延迟和跨区域成本 本地重定向策略（LRP）[节点]优先选择节点本地的后端，使流量保持在同一节点上，减少跨节点跳数和延迟 直接服务器返回（DSR）[边缘]保留客户端源 IP，让响应直接从后端 Pod 发送给客户端，提升返回路径性能 eXpress Data Path（XDP）[节点]在内核中尽早处理数据包，以减少每个数据包的开销并降低延迟 Maglev [边缘]提供一致性哈希，让相同流落在相同后端上，提升节点变更期间的连接稳定性 Netkit [节点]以内核原生设备模型替代传统 veth 路径，减少开销，使容器网络性能更接近主机水平 我们将从区域感知性能开始，然后介绍节点级和边缘级的技术，用于降低集群内部和周边的延迟与路由跳数。你可以在本书 GitHub 仓库的 chapter08 目录下找到本章使用的所有 YAML 清单文件。

    Service Traffic Distribution
    服务流量分布

    Many Kubernetes users choose to deploy clusters across multiple zones (such as multiple availability zones in AWS, or separate physical locations for on-premises hardware), often to improve availability and resilience. However, this design can introduce trade-offs, particularly in the form of increased latency if client traffic crosses zones to reach a backend. It can also lead to higher infrastructure costs, since many cloud providers charge for interzone egress. Ideally, traffic should remain within the same zone whenever possible. This approach is known as topology-aware traffic engineering: a routing strategy that takes into account the physical or logical location of both the client and the destination endpoints during forwarding decisions. In Kubernetes, this is enabled through the Service Traffic Distribution feature.1 By setting the trafficDistribution field in a service specification, you can express a preference for how traffic should be routed. At the time of writing, the only supported value is PreferClose, which prioritizes endpoints in the same area as the client when available.
    许多 Kubernetes 用户选择将集群部署在多个可用区（如 AWS 中的多个可用区，或本地硬件的独立物理位置），通常是为了提升可用性和弹性。然而，这种设计可能会带来一些权衡，特别是当客户端流量跨可用区访问后端时会增加延迟。它还可能导致更高的基础设施成本，因为许多云服务提供商对跨区出站流量收费。理想情况下，流量应尽可能保持在同一可用区内。这种方法被称为 拓扑感知流量工程：一种在转发决策时兼顾客户端和目的端点物理或逻辑位置的路由策略。在 Kubernetes 中，这通过服务流量分布功能实现。1 通过在服务规范中设置 trafficDistribution 字段，你可以表达流量路由方式的偏好。截至撰写本书时，唯一受支持的值是 PreferClose，当可用时，它优先选择与客户端在同一区域内的端点。
    We focus on Kubernetes v1.33 (“Octarine”) in this book, but a big change was made to this feature in Kubernetes v1.34 (“Of Wind & Will”): the PreferClose setting was deprecated in favor of two new settings, PreferSameZone and PreferSameNode, with PreferClose acting as an alias of the former. If you’re following along using a newer version of Kubernetes, try using PreferSameZone in the following examples.
    本书主要关注 Kubernetes v1.33（"Octarine"），但在 Kubernetes v1.34（"Of Wind & Will"）中对此功能做了重大变更：PreferClose 设置被弃用，取而代之的是两个新设置 PreferSameZone 和 PreferSameNode，而 PreferClose 成为前者的别名。如果你使用较新版本的 Kubernetes 进行操作，请在以下示例中尝试使用 PreferSameZone。
    Let’s try it out. While this capability is especially useful for clusters that span multiple cloud zones or datacenters, we will use a kind cluster for simplicity and cost-effectiveness. We’ll deploy a four-node kind cluster and assign two nodes to zone-a and two nodes to zone-b using Kubernetes node labels, with zone-a and zone-b representing two geographically separated areas. You will observe that, without traffic distribution enabled, the traffic to a Kubernetes service is randomly distributed across all backend pods, regardless of their location (Figure 8-1).
    让我们实际尝试一下。虽然此功能对于跨越多个云可用区或数据中心的集群尤其有用，但为简便和节省成本，我们将使用一个 kind 集群。我们将部署一个四节点 kind 集群，并使用 Kubernetes 节点标签将两个节点分配到 zone-a，另外两个节点分配到 zone-b，其中 zone-a 和 zone-b 代表两个地理上分离的区域。你将观察到，在未启用流量分布的情况下，发送到 Kubernetes Service 的流量会随机分布到所有后端 Pod 上，无论它们的位置如何（见图 8-1）。
    1Service Traffic Distribution is considered the successor to another traffic engineering feature, TopologyAware Routing (which was itself previously known as Topology-Aware Hints). While Topology-Aware Routing is also supported by Cilium, we’ll only cover Service Traffic Distribution in this book.
    1 服务流量分布被视为另一项流量工程特性——拓扑感知路由（它之前称为拓扑感知提示）的继任者。虽然 Cilium 也支持拓扑感知路由，但本书只介绍服务流量分布。
    Once we set trafficDistribution: PreferClose on a service, Kubernetes prefers sending traffic to endpoints that are in the same zone as the source, helping reduce latency and interzone data transfer costs (as illustrated in Figure 8-2).
    一旦我们在服务上设置 trafficDistribution: PreferClose，Kubernetes 会优先将流量发送到与源在同一可用区内的端点，帮助降低延迟和跨区域数据传输成本（如图 8-2 所示）。
    All files discussed in this section are in the chapter08/std directory of the book’s GitHub repository.
    本节讨论的所有文件都位于本书 GitHub 仓库的 chapter08/std 目录下。
    To see this in action, deploy the cluster (kind-cluster-config-std.yaml) and verify where each worker is located. You should see that kind-worker and kind-worker2 are both located in zone-a, while kind-worker3 and kind-worker4 are located in zone-b:
    要实际查看效果，请部署集群（kind-cluster-config-std.yaml）并确认每个工作节点的位置。你应该能看到 kind-worker 和 kind-worker2 都位于 zone-a，而 kind-worker3 和 kind-worker4 位于 zone-b：
    代码（原文）
    $ kubectl get nodes --show-labels
NAME             LABELS
kind-worker      topology.kubernetes.io/zone=zone-a
kind-worker2     topology.kubernetes.io/zone=zone-a
kind-worker3     topology.kubernetes.io/zone=zone-b
kind-worker4     topology.kubernetes.io/zone=zone-b
    To enable the Service Traffic Distribution option, set the Helm value loadBalancer.serviceTopology=true. Note that KPR is required for Service Traffic Distribution. Now, install Cilium (cilium-std-values.yaml) and create a deployment (stddeployment.yaml) of eight echo pods that will respond to client requests with their own name, alongside an echo service (std-service.yaml) to front the deployment. We will start with trafficDistribution disabled. Let’s now deploy three curl pods (std-poller-distribution.yaml), each continuously sending requests to the echo service and logging which backend pod responded. The list of running pods should look like this:
    要启用服务流量分布选项，请设置 Helm 值 loadBalancer.serviceTopology=true。注意，服务流量分布需要 KPR。现在安装 Cilium（cilium-std-values.yaml），并创建一个包含八个 echo Pod 的 Deployment（stddeployment.yaml），这些 Pod 会用自己的名称响应客户端请求，同时创建一个 echo Service（std-service.yaml）作为 Deployment 的前端。我们先从禁用 trafficDistribution 开始。现在部署三个 curl Pod（std-poller-distribution.yaml），每个都持续向 echo Service 发送请求并记录哪个后端 Pod 做出了响应。运行中的 Pod 列表应该如下所示：
    代码（原文）
    $ kubectl get pods -o wide

    让我们看一下其中一个新 curl Pod 的日志（curl-664778b5b6-zr6kt，位于 kind-worker2 上，在 zone-a）。以下命令按后端 Pod 聚合响应。你可以看到流量随机分布到所有八个 echo Pod 上，无论它们的位置如何：

This time, as you can see in the following output, only the stats for the NGINX pods located in the same zone increase:
    现在，启用服务流量分布（在 std-service.yaml 清单中取消注释 trafficDistribution: PreferClose 行并重新应用它），然后再次检查 curl-664778b5b6-zr6kt Pod 上的计数器。这一次，如以下输出所示，只有位于同一可用区内的 NGINX Pod 的统计数据会增加：

    Only the counters on the pods that are in the same zone as the curl pod increase, indicating that traffic remains in the same zone. Run the same aggregation from a curl pod scheduled in the other area; you should see the counts flip, with local echo pods dominating:
    只有与 curl Pod 在同一可用区内的 Pod 的计数器会增加，这表明流量保持在同一可用区内。在另一个区域的 curl Pod 上运行相同的聚合；你应该会看到计数反转，本地 echo Pod 占据主导：
    代码（原文）

    Note that when no local backend is available, traffic is forwarded to backends outside the local zone. As a bonus exercise, you can test this yourself by destroying the echo servers and verifying that the client accesses backends in the other zone. Keep in mind these constraints around topology-aware features:
    注意，当没有可用的本地后端时，流量会转发到本地可用区之外的后端。作为额外练习，你可以通过销毁 echo 服务器并验证客户端是否访问另一个可用区的后端来自行测试。请记住拓扑感知特性的这些约束条件：
    • For a given service, if externalTrafficPolicy or internalTrafficPolicy is
      对于给定的服务，如果 externalTrafficPolicy 或 internalTrafficPolicy 被
    set to Local, that policy takes precedence over trafficDistribution for the corresponding traffic type. Refer back to Chapter 6 for an explanation of traffic policies.
    设置为 Local，则该策略优先于对应流量类型的 trafficDistribution。请回顾第 6 章了解流量策略的说明。
    • If those policy fields are set to Cluster (the default) or are not set, traffic
      如果这些策略字段设置为 Cluster（默认值）或未设置，traffic
    Distribution guides routing for that traffic type and will attempt to send traffic to an endpoint in the same area as the client.
    Distribution 会指导该流量类型的路由，并尝试将流量发送到与客户端在同一区域内的端点。
    • These mechanisms work best when incoming traffic is roughly evenly distributed
      这些机制在入站流量大致均匀分布
    across areas. If a large proportion of traffic originates from a single zone, the endpoints allocated to that zone can be overloaded; this approach is not recommended when most traffic is expected to come from one zone.
    在各个区域时效果最佳。如果很大比例的流量来自单个可用区，分配给该可用区的端点可能会过载；当预期大部分流量来自一个可用区时，不推荐使用此方案。

    Local Redirect Policy
    本地重定向策略

    While Service Traffic Distribution helps direct traffic within the same zone, reducing cross-zone charges and latency, you may want to avoid even internode traffic by directing service requests to a local backend. To achieve this, Cilium offers an eBPFbased feature called Local Redirect Policy (LRP). LRP enables traffic destined for a particular IP address and port/protocol tuple or Kubernetes service to be redirected locally to backend pods within a node, using eBPF. There are many scenarios where an application runs on every node, typically using a DaemonSet. Examples include logging agents, monitoring sidecars, or DNS forwarders. While this ensures high availability, pods do not always connect to the local instance. Without LRP, traffic may be routed to an instance on another node, leading to unnecessary cross-node traffic and added latency (Figure 8-3).
    虽然服务流量分布有助于将流量引导到同一可用区内，降低跨区费用和延迟，但你可能希望通过将服务请求引导到本地后端，甚至避免跨节点流量。为了实现这一点，Cilium 提供了一项基于 eBPF 的特性，称为本地重定向策略（LRP）。LRP 可使用 eBPF 将发往特定 IP 地址与端口/协议元组或 Kubernetes Service 的流量，在节点本地重定向到后端 Pod。有许多场景下应用会运行在每个节点上，通常使用 DaemonSet。示例包括日志代理、监控 Sidecar 或 DNS 转发器。虽然这确保了高可用性，但 Pod 并不总是连接到本地实例。没有 LRP 时，流量可能被路由到另一个节点上的实例，导致不必要的跨节点流量和额外延迟（见图 8-3）。
    LRP ensures service-bound traffic is redirected locally (Figure 8-4). The most evident use case is with NodeLocal DNSCache, an architecture that can reduce DNS lookup latency in your clusters.
    LRP 确保发往服务的流量在本地重定向（见图 8-4）。最明显的用例是 NodeLocal DNSCache——一种可以降低集群中 DNS 查询延迟的架构。
    There are two policy types:
    有两种策略类型：
    代码（原文）
    AddressMatcher
    Matches an IP:port destination and redirects locally. AddressMatcher is handy for node-local metadata caches on managed Kubernetes platforms such as EKS and GKE. For example, you can match 169.254.169.254:80 (instance metadata) and redirect those requests to a node-local cache pod. Cache misses will then go to the external metadata service.
    匹配一个 IP:port 目的地址并在本地重定向。AddressMatcher 对于 EKS、GKE 等托管 Kubernetes 平台上的节点本地元数据缓存非常实用。例如，你可以匹配 169.254.169.254:80（实例元数据）并将这些请求重定向到节点本地的缓存 Pod。缓存未命中时会转到外部元数据服务。
    代码（原文）
    ServiceMatcher
    Matches a specific service and redirects to node-local backends for that service. This is the most common policy type and the one used in the two examples covered in this chapter. Let’s take a look at how LRP works on a fundamental level before applying it to more specific use cases, such as NodeLocal DNS.
    匹配特定服务并重定向到该服务的节点本地后端。这是最常见的策略类型，也是本章两个示例中使用的类型。在将 LRP 应用到更具体的用例（如 NodeLocal DNS）之前，让我们先了解一下 LRP 的基本工作原理。

    Using LRP to Keep Traffic on the Same Node
    使用 LRP 将流量保持在同一节点

    To start, we will consider a simple example where Local Redirect Policy will redirect traffic bound for a Kubernetes service and force traffic to be forwarded only to the local backends (i.e., on the same Kubernetes node as the source client).
    首先，我们来看一个简单示例，其中本地重定向策略将重定向发往 Kubernetes Service 的流量，并强制流量仅转发到本地后端（即与源客户端在同一个 Kubernetes 节点上的后端）。
    All files discussed in this section are in the chapter08/lrp directory of the book’s GitHub repository. Deploy a kind cluster (kind-cluster-config-lrp.yaml) and install Cilium (cilium-
    本节讨论的所有文件都位于本书 GitHub 仓库的 chapter08/lrp 目录下。部署 kind 集群（kind-cluster-config-lrp.yaml）并安装 Cilium（cilium-
    lrp-values.yaml). Note the flag required to activate this feature (localRedirect
    lrp-values.yaml）。注意激活此特性所需的标志（localRedirect
    代码（原文）
    Policies.enabled=true).2
    Next, deploy the Kubernetes pod and service for which traffic needs to be redirected (lrp-deployment.yaml). Once again, we’re using a deployment of two echo servers to show which pod receives the client request, with a service named echo-server fronting the backend pods. The two replicas are scheduled on two different nodes. Let’s first see how, prior to using LRP, requests to the service are load-balanced across the two backends. Deploy the client (netshoot-client.yaml), then check with kubectl where each pod is scheduled:
    接下来，部署需要重定向流量的 Kubernetes Pod 和 Service（lrp-deployment.yaml）。同样，我们使用一个包含两个 echo 服务器的 Deployment 来展示哪个 Pod 接收到客户端请求，前端是一个名为 echo-server 的 Service。两个副本调度在两个不同的节点上。让我们先看看在使用 LRP 之前，对该 Service 的请求是如何在两个后端之间进行负载均衡的。部署客户端（netshoot-client.yaml），然后用 kubectl 检查每个 Pod 调度在哪里：
    代码（原文）
    $ kubectl get pods -o wide
NAME                           READY   STATUS    IP           NODE
echo-server-78fbb56cc7-phmpq   1/1     Running   10.0.0.209   kind-worker
echo-server-78fbb56cc7-pvkgf   1/1     Running   10.0.2.27    kind-worker2
netshoot-client                1/1     Running   10.0.2.226   kind-worker2
    Now, test access from the client to the service:
    现在测试从客户端到服务的访问：
    代码（原文）
    $ kubectl exec -it netshoot-client -- curl echo-server:8080

$ kubectl exec -it netshoot-client -- curl echo-server:8080

    The response includes the pod name, and we can see that requests are being distributed to both pods—the one on the same node as the client and the one on a different node. Verify that Cilium created a ClusterIP service entry pointing to backends located on both nodes:
    响应中包含 Pod 名称，我们可以看到请求分布到了两个 Pod 上——与客户端在同一节点的那个和在不同节点的那个。确认 Cilium 创建了指向两个节点上后端的 ClusterIP Service 条目：
    代码（原文）
    $ kubectl get svc echo-server

6   10.96.219.113:8080/TCP  ClusterIP     1 => 10.0.0.209:8080/TCP (active)
2 => 10.0.2.27:8080/TCP (active)
    Then deploy the Local Redirect Policy (svc-lrp.yaml):
    然后部署本地重定向策略（svc-lrp.yaml）：
    代码（原文）
    apiVersion: "cilium.io/v2"
kind: CiliumLocalRedirectPolicy
    2While enabling KPR is not strictly required for LRP, it is recommended. See the Cilium documentation on LRP for more information on the prerequisites.
    2 虽然启用 KPR 对 LRP 不是严格必需的，但推荐这样做。更多前置条件信息请参阅 Cilium 关于 LRP 的文档。
    代码（原文）
    metadata:
name: "echo-server-lrp"
spec:
redirectFrontend:
serviceMatcher:
serviceName: echo-server
namespace: default
redirectBackend:
localEndpointSelector:
matchLabels:
app.kubernetes.io/name: echo-server
toPorts:
- port: "8080"
protocol: TCP
    Identifies the Kubernetes service for which traffic should be intercepted. Cilium matches this service by name and namespace to determine which frontend requests are eligible for local redirection. Specifies the set of local pods (based on labels) and destination port to which frontend traffic should be redirected, if a matching pod exists on the same node. When a policy of this type is applied, the existing service entry created by Cilium will be replaced with a new service entry of type LocalRedirect. This entry can only have node-local backend pods. Let’s verify the behavior by executing cilium-dbg service list on the Cilium agent running on the same node as our client pod. The following output confirms that Cilium will forward traffic to the echo-server service only to the local backend
    标识需要拦截流量的 Kubernetes Service。Cilium 按名称和命名空间匹配此 Service，以确定哪些前端请求有资格进行本地重定向。根据标签指定本地 Pod 集合以及目的端口，前端流量应重定向到此处（如果同一节点上存在匹配的 Pod）。应用此类型的策略时，Cilium 创建的现有 Service 条目将被一个新的 LocalRedirect 类型的 Service 条目替代。此条目只能有节点本地的后端 Pod。让我们通过在与客户端 Pod 在同一节点上运行的 Cilium Agent 上执行 cilium-dbg service list 来验证该行为。以下输出确认 Cilium 将把发往 echo-server 服务的流量仅转发到本地后端
    代码（原文）
    (echo-server-78fbb56cc7-pvkgf, with IP 10.0.2.27):
$ kubectl -n kube-system exec -it cilium-5d5jq -- cilium-dbg service list
7    10.96.219.113:8080/TCP   LocalRedirect   1 => 10.0.2.27:8080/TCP (active)
    By running the curl command several times in a loop, we can see that only the pod colocated on the same node as the client receives the request:
    通过循环多次运行 curl 命令，我们可以看到只有与客户端在同一节点上的 Pod 接收到请求：
    代码（原文）
    $ for i in {1..20}; do

Request 2 -> echo-server-78fbb56cc7-pvkgf
[...]
Request 19 -> echo-server-78fbb56cc7-pvkgf
Request 20 -> echo-server-78fbb56cc7-pvkgf
    The Local Redirect Policy ensured traffic stayed on the same node.
    本地重定向策略确保了流量保持在同一节点上。

    Using LRP to Redirect DNS Traffic Locally
    使用 LRP 在本地重定向 DNS 流量

    The most common use case for LRP is to optimize DNS performance, alongside the NodeLocal DNSCache Kubernetes architecture. This architecture relies on a DNS caching agent running on each cluster node as a DaemonSet. With LRP you can be sure that DNS traffic from a pod goes to the DNS cache running on the same node as the pod, reducing latency, among other benefits. Before using LRP to redirect DNS traffic and optimize latency, we first need to create a local DNS node-cache (node-local-dns.yaml). The manifest is rather long, so let’s summarize some of the core components:
    LRP 最常见的用例是优化 DNS 性能，与 NodeLocal DNSCache Kubernetes 架构配合使用。此架构依赖于一个作为 DaemonSet 在每个集群节点上运行的 DNS 缓存代理。使用 LRP，你可以确保来自 Pod 的 DNS 流量发往与该 Pod 在同一节点上运行的 DNS 缓存，从而降低延迟，并带来其他好处。在使用 LRP 重定向 DNS 流量并优化延迟之前，我们首先需要创建一个本地 DNS 节点缓存（node-local-dns.yaml）。该清单相当长，因此我们来总结其中一些核心组件：
    • The service account named node-local-dns will be used by the DaemonSet to
      名为 node-local-dns 的 ServiceAccount 将由 DaemonSet 使用，以便在
    run with specific permissions in the cluster.
    集群中以特定权限运行。
    • The service named kube-dns-upstream exposes port 53 for DNS queries over
      名为 kube-dns-upstream 的 Service 暴露端口 53，用于通过
    both UDP and TCP and forwards them to a target port on the DNS pods selected by the k8s-app: kube-dns label (the coreDNS pods). This service is used to handle upstream DNS queries when the node-local DNS cache is not able to resolve a request.
    UDP 和 TCP 进行 DNS 查询，并将它们转发到由 k8s-app: kube-dns 标签选中的 DNS Pod（即 coreDNS Pod）上的目标端口。此服务用于在节点本地 DNS 缓存无法解析请求时处理上游 DNS 查询。
    • The ConfigMap defines the CoreDNS configuration (Corefile) for DNS caching
      ConfigMap 定义了用于节点级 DNS 缓存的 CoreDNS 配置（Corefile）
    on the node level: ——It defines behavior for DNS requests in the cluster.local domain (Kubernetes service discovery) and external DNS queries (outside cluster.local). ——Caching is configured with success and denial limits for both cluster.local and reverse lookup zones (in-addr.arpa and ip6.arpa). ——Metrics are exposed on port 9253 for Prometheus and health checks are enabled.
    ：——它定义了 cluster.local 域（Kubernetes 服务发现）和外部 DNS 查询（cluster.local 之外）中 DNS 请求的行为。——为 cluster.local 和反向查找区域（in-addr.arpa 和 ip6.arpa）配置了成功和拒绝限制的缓存。——在端口 9253 为 Prometheus 暴露指标，并启用了健康检查。
    • The DaemonSet named node-local-dns runs a node-cache container on every
      名为 node-local-dns 的 DaemonSet 在集群的每个节点上运行一个 node-cache 容器
    node in the cluster: ——Each node will have a local DNS caching service, which significantly reduces DNS lookup times for workloads on the node. ——The DaemonSet doesn’t use the cluster’s default DNS settings but instead relies on its own DNS configuration. ——The args define the local IP addresses for the cache (169.254.20.10 and 10.96.0.10), which are the node-local IPs used by this service. ——It exposes ports 53 for DNS (both TCP and UDP) and port 9253 for metrics. Health checks are configured via the /health endpoint. Once this is deployed, you will see a DNS agent running on each node:
    ：——每个节点都有一个本地 DNS 缓存服务，这显著降低了节点上工作负载的 DNS 查询时间。——DaemonSet 不使用集群的默认 DNS 设置，而是依赖自己的 DNS 配置。——args 定义了缓存的本地 IP 地址（169.254.20.10 和 10.96.0.10），这是此服务使用的节点本地 IP。——它暴露端口 53 用于 DNS（TCP 和 UDP）以及端口 9253 用于指标。健康检查通过 /health 端点配置。部署完成后，你将看到每个节点上都运行着一个 DNS 代理：
    代码（原文）
    $ kubectl get pods -n kube-system -o wide
NAME                    READY   STATUS    IP           NODE
node-local-dns-h5hgx    1/1     Running   10.0.1.252   kind-control-plane
node-local-dns-kvvmk    1/1     Running   10.0.2.20    kind-worker2
node-local-dns-xrwvl    1/1     Running   10.0.0.172   kind-worker
    Next, deploy the following Local Redirect Policy (dns-lrp.yaml). The policy will redirect traffic bound to the CoreDNS servers, forwarding it to the node-local DNS cache agents:
    接下来，部署以下本地重定向策略（dns-lrp.yaml）。该策略将重定向发往 CoreDNS 服务器的流量，将其转发到节点本地的 DNS 缓存代理：
    代码（原文）
    apiVersion: "cilium.io/v2"
kind: CiliumLocalRedirectPolicy
metadata:
name: "nodelocaldns"
namespace: kube-system
spec:
redirectFrontend:
serviceMatcher:
serviceName: kube-dns
namespace: kube-system
redirectBackend:
localEndpointSelector:
matchLabels:
k8s-app: node-local-dns
toPorts:
- port: "53"
name: dns
protocol: UDP
- port: "53"
name: dns-tcp
protocol: TCP
    Look on any of the Cilium agents and you will now see LocalRedirect entries for the DNS service, over TCP and UDP ports:
    查看任意一个 Cilium Agent，你现在应该能看到 DNS 服务的 LocalRedirect 条目，覆盖 TCP 和 UDP 端口：
    代码（原文）
    $ kubectl exec -it ds/cilium -n kube-system -- cilium-dbg service list
ID   Frontend                 Service Type    Backend
3    10.96.0.10:53/UDP        LocalRedirect   1 => 10.244.2.46:53/UDP (active)
4    10.96.0.10:53/TCP        LocalRedirect   1 => 10.244.2.46:53/TCP (active)
    You can verify that the redirects are working by checking the DNS caches’ metrics. First, let’s get the IP of the DNS cache pod on the kind-worker node (where our client was deployed):
    你可以通过检查 DNS 缓存的指标来验证重定向是否正常工作。首先，让我们获取 kind-worker 节点上的 DNS 缓存 Pod 的 IP（我们的客户端部署在此处）：
    代码（原文）
    $ DNS_LOCAL="$(kubectl -n kube-system get po \

--field-selector spec.nodeName=kind-worker \

10.0.0.172
    Let’s also get the IP of the “non-local” DNS cache pod:
    我们还来获取"非本地"DNS 缓存 Pod 的 IP：
    代码（原文）
    $ DNS_NON_LOCAL="$(kubectl -n kube-system get po \
-l k8s-app=node-local-dns \

10.0.2.20
    We can now make DNS requests from the client pod and check the DNS request counts on the local DNS cache pod and the one running on the other worker node:
    现在我们可以从客户端 Pod 发起 DNS 请求，并检查本地 DNS 缓存 Pod 和运行在另一个工作节点上的 DNS 缓存 Pod 的 DNS 请求计数：
    代码（原文）
    $ kubectl exec netshoot-client -- curl "$DNS_LOCAL:9253/metrics" | \
grep 'coredns_dns_request_size_bytes_count'
coredns_dns_request_count_total{zone="cluster.local."} 1  # local
$ kubectl exec netshoot-client -- curl "$DNS_NON_LOCAL:9253/metrics" | \
grep 'coredns_dns_request_size_bytes_count'
coredns_dns_request_count_total{zone="cluster.local."} 1  # non-local
    The values here indicate that only one request has been made to each DNS cache. Let’s run a few DNS requests, and then check the counts again:
    这里的值表明每个 DNS 缓存只收到一个请求。让我们运行几个 DNS 请求，然后再次检查计数：
    代码（原文）
    $ kubectl exec netshoot-client -- nslookup oreilly.com
$ kubectl exec netshoot-client -- nslookup isovalent.com
$ kubectl exec netshoot-client -- nslookup cilium.io
$ kubectl exec netshoot-client -- curl "$DNS_LOCAL:9253/metrics" | \
grep 'coredns_dns_request_size_bytes_count'
coredns_dns_request_count_total{zone="cluster.local."} 14  # local
$ kubectl exec netshoot-client -- curl "$DNS_NON_LOCAL:9253/metrics" \
| grep 'coredns_dns_request_size_bytes_count'
coredns_dns_request_count_total{zone="cluster.local."} 1   # non-local
    As expected, it increases for the local DNS cache, while the number for the other cache stays the same.
    正如预期，本地 DNS 缓存的计数增加了，而另一个缓存的数量保持不变。

    Direct Server Return
    直接服务器返回

    We’ll discuss controlling traffic entering the cluster from external sources and leaving the cluster (ingress and egress) in Chapters 10 and 11, respectively. For traffic returning to the client, an optional feature that can help improve performance is Direct Server Return (DSR). Consider a situation where a service is exposed externally (through NodePort or LoadBalancer) and the service endpoint runs on a different node (Node-2) from the node the client connects to (Node-1), as illustrated in Figure 8-5.
    我们将在第 10 章和第 11 章分别讨论控制从外部来源进入集群和离开集群的流量（入站和出站）。对于返回给客户端的流量，一个可以帮助提升性能的可选特性是直接服务器返回（DSR）。考虑这样一种情况：一个服务对外暴露（通过 NodePort 或 LoadBalancer），而服务端点运行在与客户端连接的节点（Node-1）不同的节点（Node-2）上，如图 8-5 所示。
    When Node-1 forwards client requests to Node-2, it will source-NAT the traffic, replacing the client’s IP address with its internal IP from its own PodCIDR. This has two negative implications: Loss of observability The backend receiving the traffic sees the packet as originating from Node-1 instead of the actual client. Indirect return path The backend traffic is sent to Node-1 and then on to the client, instead of directly to the client. Cilium’s DSR feature leverages eBPF to preserve the source IP address and to ensure traffic is returned directly to the client, as illustrated in Figure 8-6.
    当 Node-1 将客户端请求转发到 Node-2 时，它会对流量进行源地址转换，将客户端的 IP 地址替换为它自己 PodCIDR 中的内部 IP。这有两个负面影响：可观测性丢失接收流量的后端看到的数据包来自 Node-1 而非实际客户端。 间接返回路径后端流量先发送到 Node-1，然后再到客户端，而不是直接发送给客户端。Cilium 的 DSR 特性利用 eBPF 保留源 IP 地址，并确保流量直接返回给客户端，如图 8-6 所示。
    All files discussed in this section are in the chapter08/dsr directory of the book’s GitHub repository.
    本节讨论的所有文件都位于本书 GitHub 仓库的 chapter08/dsr 目录下。
    Before trying out DSR, let’s see what happens without it, which is Cilium’s default behavior. In a generic kind cluster (kind create cluster --configkind.yaml) with a default Cilium installation, deploy the following manifests (dsrdeployment.yaml and dsr-service.yaml). It’s a deployment of two replicas running a simple “What’s My IP?” application and an associated NodePort service:
    在尝试 DSR 之前，让我们先看看没有 DSR 时会发生什么——这是 Cilium 的默认行为。在一个通用 kind 集群（kind create cluster --configkind.yaml）上，使用默认 Cilium 安装，部署以下清单（dsrdeployment.yaml 和 dsr-service.yaml）。这是一个运行简单"What's My IP?"应用的两副本 Deployment 以及关联的 NodePort Service：
    代码（原文）
    apiVersion: apps/v1
kind: Deployment
metadata:
name: echo
spec:
replicas: 2
selector:
matchLabels:
app.kubernetes.io/name: echo
template:
metadata:
labels:
app.kubernetes.io/name: echo
spec:
containers:
- name: echo
image: quay.io/isovalent-dev/egressgw-whatismyip:latest
ports:
- containerPort: 8000
---
apiVersion: v1
kind: Service
metadata:
name: echo-nodeport
spec:
type: NodePort
selector:
app.kubernetes.io/name: echo
ports:
- port: 80
targetPort: 8000
protocol: TCP
nodePort: 30080
    Start a client container on the same Docker network, with the following command:
    在同一个 Docker 网络上启动一个客户端容器，使用以下命令：
    代码（原文）
    $ docker run -d --network kind --rm --name client nicolaka/netshoot:v0.8 \
sleep infinity
    Its IP is 172.18.0.5—let’s check if this is the IP returned by the “What’s My IP?” service:
    它的 IP 是 172.18.0.5——我们来检查一下这是否是"What's My IP?"服务返回的 IP：
    代码（原文）
    $ docker exec -it client ip -4 -o a show eth0 | awk '{print $4}'
172.18.0.5/16
    Now, access the service over the kind-control-plane node from the client container using the allocated node port of the service (30080 in our example):
    现在，从客户端容器通过 kind-control-plane 节点访问该服务，使用为服务分配的节点端口（在我们的示例中是 30080）：
    代码（原文）
    $ kubectl get svc
NAME            TYPE        CLUSTER-IP     EXTERNAL-IP   PORT(S)        AGE
echo-nodeport   NodePort    10.96.40.253   <none>        80:30080/TCP   10m
kubernetes      ClusterIP   10.96.0.1      <none>        443/TCP        46m
$ kubectl get nodes -o wide
NAME                 STATUS   ROLES           AGE   VERSION   INTERNAL-IP
kind-control-plane   Ready    control-plane   46m   v1.33.0   172.18.0.4
kind-worker          Ready    <none>          46m   v1.33.0   172.18.0.3
kind-worker2         Ready    <none>          46m   v1.33.0   172.18.0.2
$ docker exec -it client curl 172.18.0.4:30080
10.244.0.232
    The response shows the client’s IP address—however, it is not the client’s source IP address but an internal pod IP address (10.244.0.232) assigned to the kind-controlplane node:
    响应显示了客户端的 IP 地址——然而，这不是客户端的源 IP 地址，而是分配给 kind-controlplane 节点的内部 Pod IP 地址（10.244.0.232）：
    代码（原文）
    $ docker exec kind-control-plane ip a
[...]
13: cilium_host@cilium_net: <BROADCAST,MULTICAST,NOARP,UP,LOWER_UP> mtu 65520 qdisc
noqueue state UP group default qlen 1000
link/ether 7e:48:5d:5d:f4:c4 brd ff:ff:ff:ff:ff:ff

    This confirms that, when forwarding packets to the worker nodes where the backends reside, kind-control-plane changes the source IP to its own. Let’s now try this with DSR. Use the following Helm values (cilium-dsr-values.yaml). Set the loadBalancer.mode=dsr flag to enable DSR. Note that DSR requires KPR and is only supported in native routing mode or with Geneve (it is not currently supported with VXLAN encapsulation):
    这证实了，当将数据包转发到后端所在的工作节点时，kind-control-plane 会将源 IP 改为自己的。现在让我们试试 DSR。使用以下 Helm 值（cilium-dsr-values.yaml）。设置 loadBalancer.mode=dsr 标志来启用 DSR。注意 DSR 需要 KPR，并且仅在原生路由模式或使用 Geneve 时才受支持（目前不支持 VXLAN 封装）：
    代码（原文）
    kubeProxyReplacement: true
routingMode: native
ipv4NativeRoutingCIDR: "172.18.0.0/16"
autoDirectNodeRoutes: true
k8sServiceHost: "kind-control-plane"
k8sServicePort: 6443
loadBalancer:
mode: "dsr"
    After installation, verify that DSR is enabled:
    安装完成后，验证 DSR 是否启用：
    代码（原文）

Mode:                DSR
DSR Dispatch Mode: IP Option/Extension
    Apply the deployment (dsr-deployment.yaml) and service (dsr-service.yaml) once more before accessing the “What’s My IP?” service over NodePort again:
    再次应用 Deployment（dsr-deployment.yaml）和 Service（dsr-service.yaml），然后再通过 NodePort 访问"What's My IP?"服务：
    代码（原文）
    $ docker exec -it client curl 172.18.0.4:30080
172.18.0.5
    With DSR enabled, the address the service returns is the IP of the client. This confirms the source IP was preserved and the server replied directly back to the client. You can verify that the service entry in the eBPF map is set to dsr with:
    启用 DSR 后，服务返回的地址是客户端的 IP。这证实了源 IP 被保留，服务器直接回复给了客户端。你可以用以下命令验证 eBPF 映射中的服务条目已设置为 dsr：
    代码（原文）
    $ kubectl -n kube-system exec -it ds/cilium -- cilium-dbg bpf lb list
[...]
SERVICE ADDRESS              BACKEND ADDRESS (REVNAT_ID) (SLOT)
172.18.0.4:30080/TCP (0)    0.0.0.0:0 (7) (0) [NodePort, dsr]
    As a final note on DSR, you might recall that in Chapter 6 we introduced externalTrafficPolicy: Local as a way to preserve source IPs when services are accessed from outside the cluster. DSR achieves the same goal using a different mechanism. The key differences are:
    关于 DSR 的最后一点说明，你可能还记得第 6 章中我们介绍了 externalTrafficPolicy: Local，作为在服务从集群外部访问时保留源 IP 的一种方式。DSR 使用不同的机制实现了相同的目标。主要区别在于：
    • The Local external traffic policy is configured per service. It preserves the source
      Local 外部流量策略按服务配置。它通过确保流量仅路由到
    IP by ensuring traffic is only routed to nodes that host a backend pod. Return traffic is sent via the host network stack.
    承载后端 Pod 的节点来保留源 IP。返回流量通过宿主机网络协议栈发送。
    • DSR is enabled at the cluster level in Cilium’s configuration. It allows external
      DSR 在 Cilium 配置中的集群级别启用。它允许外部
    traffic to be received by any node, even if it does not host a backend.
    流量被任意节点接收，即使该节点不承载后端。

    eXpress Data Path
    eXpress Data Path

    The eXpress Data Path (XDP) feature allows for eBPF code to be executed in hardware directly on the physical network interface, processing packets before they even transit to the CPU and the kernel. The main use cases for XDP are protection against distributed denial-of-service (DDoS) attacks and firewalling, since XDP enables packets to be dropped early with minimal overhead. XDP also supports load balancing and forwarding, including redirection to other network interfaces or CPU cores, and it can be used for pre-stack filtering, such as dropping unwanted protocols like UDP or Stream Control Transmission Protocol (SCTP) before they reach the kernel’s networking stack. Unlike user space frameworks such as Data Plane Development Kit (DPDK), XDP still works in cooperation with the kernel. This means you can offload the fast path to XDP while leaving the kernel to handle more complex processing, such as full TCP connection tracking or policy enforcement. XDP applies only to packets that arrive through a physical network interface. Traffic between pods on the same node, for example, does not pass through the network interface controller (NIC) and will not benefit. However, for ingress traffic from external clients or LoadBalancer services, XDP can reduce per-packet latency and CPU usage by processing traffic earlier in the stack. To enable XDP with Cilium, set loadBalancer.acceleration=native. This enables native XDP mode if supported by the NIC driver. Most modern drivers support XDP, but if the hardware does not, the setting may be ignored or fall back to a safer configuration. You can also use best-effort to allow automatic fallback.
    eXpress Data Path（XDP）特性允许 eBPF 代码直接在物理网络接口的硬件中执行，在数据包尚未传输到 CPU 和内核之前对其进行处理。XDP 的主要用例是防止分布式拒绝服务（DDoS）攻击和防火墙功能，因为 XDP 能够以最小开销尽早丢弃数据包。XDP 还支持负载均衡和转发，包括重定向到其他网络接口或 CPU 核心，并且可用于协议栈之前的过滤，例如在数据包到达内核网络协议栈之前丢弃不想要的协议（如 UDP 或流控制传输协议（SCTP））。与用户空间框架（如数据平面开发套件（DPDK））不同，XDP 仍然与内核配合工作。这意味着你可以将快速路径卸载到 XDP，同时让内核处理更复杂的处理，例如完整的 TCP 连接跟踪或策略执行。XDP 仅适用于通过物理网络接口到达的数据包。例如，同一节点上 Pod 之间的流量不会经过网络接口控制器（NIC），因此无法受益。然而，对于来自外部客户端或 LoadBalancer 服务的入站流量，XDP 可以通过在协议栈中更早地处理流量来降低每个数据包的延迟和 CPU 使用率。要在 Cilium 中启用 XDP，请设置 loadBalancer.acceleration=native。如果 NIC 驱动支持，这会启用原生 XDP 模式。大多数现代驱动都支持 XDP，但如果硬件不支持，该设置可能会被忽略或回退到更安全的配置。你也可以使用 best-effort 来允许自动回退。
    XDP is not enabled by default because not all NIC drivers support native XDP mode. Enabling it unconditionally could lead to errors or degraded performance. Use loadBalancer.acceleration=native to enable XDP explicitly, or best-effort to allow fallback on unsupported hardware.
    XDP 默认未启用，因为并非所有 NIC 驱动都支持原生 XDP 模式。无条件启用可能会导致错误或性能下降。使用 loadBalancer.acceleration=native 显式启用 XDP，或使用 best-effort 允许在不受支持的硬件上回退。
    If you want to understand XDP in more detail, we recommend Learning eBPF by Liz Rice (O’Reilly), which offers a clear and accessible explanation of how XDP fits into the broader eBPF ecosystem.
    如果你想更深入地了解 XDP，我们推荐 Liz Rice（O'Reilly）的 Learning eBPF，该书对 XDP 如何融入更广泛的 eBPF 生态系统提供了清晰而易懂的解释。

    Maglev Consistent Hashing
    Maglev 一致性哈希

    By default, Cilium load-balances service-bound traffic randomly across backends. This can be verified in a cluster with Cilium’s default settings:
    默认情况下，Cilium 会将发往 Service 的流量随机负载均衡到各个后端。可以在使用 Cilium 默认配置的集群中验证这一点：
    代码（原文）
    $ kubectl -n kube-system exec -it ds/cilium -- cilium-dbg status --verbose
[...]
Backend Selection:      Random
    The problem with this approach is that, if the node handling a connection fails, the next request may land on another node that has no knowledge of which backend pod was previously selected. As a result, connection-oriented protocols like TCP may experience unexpected disruptions, because the backend selected by the new external node has no knowledge of the original connection and responds by rejecting it. Figure 8-7 illustrates random load balancing: A client connects to an external-facing node (Node 1). Node 1 randomly selects a backend (for example, Node 3) and forwards the traffic. If Node 1 fails, the client’s next packet reaches a different external node (Node 2). Node 2 also performs random load balancing and might select a different node (such as Node 4). Node 4 has no state for the original connection and responds by resetting it.3
    这种方式的问题在于，如果处理某个连接的节点发生故障，下一次请求可能会落到另一个之前并不知道选择了哪个后端 Pod 的节点上。因此，像 TCP 这样的面向连接协议可能会出现意外中断，因为新的外部节点所选择的后端不了解原始连接，会以拒绝的方式进行响应。图 8-7 展示了随机负载均衡的工作原理：客户端连接到一个对外暴露的节点（节点 1），节点 1 随机选择一个后端（例如节点 3）并转发流量。如果节点 1 发生故障，客户端的下一个数据包会到达另一个外部节点（节点 2），节点 2 同样执行随机负载均衡，可能会选择另一个不同的节点（如节点 4）。节点 4 没有原始连接的状态信息，因此会通过重置连接来响应。³
    3When a TCP packet reaches a backend pod that did not see the initial handshake, it has no session context and treats the packet as invalid. It responds with a TCP RST (reset), which instructs the client to immediately terminate the connection.
    ³当 TCP 数据包到达一个没有参与过初始握手的后端 Pod 时，它没有会话上下文，会将该数据包视为无效，并以 TCP RST（重置）进行响应，指示客户端立即终止连接。
    To address this, Maglev provides consistent hashing, ensuring that client connections identified by the same 5-tuple—source IP address, source port, destination IP address, destination port, and transport protocol—are routed to the same backend, regardless of which external node handles the request. This helps avoid connection drops during node failures or load balancer changes. Operators should consider enabling it when session stickiness and stability are critical, especially for TCP-based workloads.
    为了解决这个问题，Maglev 提供了 一致性哈希，确保由相同五元组——源 IP 地址、源端口、目的 IP 地址、目的端口和传输协议——标识的客户端连接，无论哪个外部节点处理请求，都会被路由到相同的后端。这有助于在节点故障或负载均衡器变更期间避免连接中断。运维人员应在会话粘性和稳定性至关重要时考虑启用它，尤其是对于基于 TCP 的工作负载。
    Maglev,4 originally created by Google, provides consistent hashing to minimize disruption during node or backend changes. Each load-balancing node hashes the packet’s 5-tuple against a shared lookup table. As a result, all nodes independently arrive at the same backend selection without needing to share state. This approach improves resilience and ensures stable load balancing, even as nodes or backends are added or removed. However, Maglev is not enabled by default in Cilium because it introduces a fixed-size lookup table and may slightly increase memory usage. Most users will not need it unless their workloads require strong session affinity or must gracefully handle node-level disruptions. For a deeper dive, see the Maglev whitepaper.
    Maglev⁴ 最初由 Google 创建，提供一致性哈希以最小化节点或后端变更期间的扰动。每个负载均衡节点都会将数据包的五元组哈希到一个共享查找表中。因此，所有节点无需共享状态即可独立得出相同的后端选择。这种方法提高了弹性，并在节点或后端增删时仍能保持稳定的负载均衡。不过，Maglev 在 Cilium 中默认并未启用，因为它引入了固定大小的查找表，可能会略微增加内存使用。大多数用户不需要它，除非其工作负载需要强会话亲和性或必须优雅地处理节点级别的故障。如需深入了解，请参阅 Maglev 白皮书。
    Figure 8-8 illustrates Maglev load balancing with Cilium: A client connects to an external-facing node (Node 1). Node 1 uses Maglev to deterministically select backend Node 3, based on the connection’s 5-tuple. If Node 1 fails, the client retries via Node 2. Node 2 uses the same Maglev hash and also selects Node 3. Node 3 continues handling the session without interruption.
    图 8-8 展示了 Cilium 中 Maglev 负载均衡的工作原理：客户端连接到一个对外暴露的节点（节点 1），节点 1 使用 Maglev 基于连接的五元组确定性地选择后端节点 3。如果节点 1 发生故障，客户端通过节点 2 重试。节点 2 使用相同的 Maglev 哈希，同样选择节点 3，节点 3 继续无中断地处理会话。
    4The name “Maglev” refers to magnetic levitation, the technology used in high-speed trains such as the Japanese Shinkansen. The original paper described the load balancer as fast, highly available, and easily scalable, much like its namesake.
    ⁴"Maglev" 这个名称源自 磁悬浮（magnetic levitation），即日本新干线等高速列车所使用的技术。原论文将该负载均衡器描述为快速、高可用且易于扩展，与其名称相呼应。
    All files discussed in this section are in the chapter08/maglev directory of the book’s GitHub repository.
    本节讨论的所有文件都位于本书 GitHub 仓库的 chapter08/maglev 目录中。
    To illustrate how Maglev load balancing using Cilium with BGP and DSR works in practice, we’ve provided a script (setup-maglev.sh) that automates the setup of a suitable environment. It builds a kind cluster with one control plane and four worker nodes. Two of the worker nodes are configured to act as external-facing BGP speakers, while the remaining two host backend workloads. The script also deploys and configures the BGP peer. The topology is illustrated in Figure 8-9. The script installs Cilium with KPR enabled (a requirement for Maglev) and configured for native routing, DSR, and the Maglev load balancing algorithm:
    为了演示 Cilium 配合 BGP 和 DSR 的 Maglev 负载均衡在实际中如何工作，我们提供了一个脚本（setup-maglev.sh）来自动搭建合适的环境。该脚本创建一个 kind 集群，包含一个控制平面和四个工作节点。其中两个工作节点被配置为对外暴露的 BGP 发言者，其余两个则承载后端工作负载。脚本还会部署并配置 BGP 对等体。其拓扑如图 8-9 所示。脚本在安装 Cilium 时启用 KPR（Maglev 的必要条件），并配置为原生路由、DSR 以及 Maglev 负载均衡算法：
    代码（原文）
    kubeProxyReplacement: true
loadBalancer:
mode: "dsr"
algorithm: "maglev"
bgpControlPlane:
enabled: true
    To demonstrate Maglev behavior, the script deploys an echo server with two backend pods across the kind-worker and kind-worker2 nodes. It also creates a LoadBalancer service with the IP 192.168.100.0:
    为了演示 Maglev 的行为，脚本会在 kind-worker 和 kind-worker2 节点上部署一个带有两个后端 Pod 的 echo 服务器。它还会创建一个 IP 为 192.168.100.0 的 LoadBalancer Service：
    代码（原文）
    $ kubectl get deployment echo
NAME   READY   UP-TO-DATE   AVAILABLE   AGE
echo   2/2     2            2           94s
$ kubectl get svc echo-lb
NAME      TYPE           CLUSTER-IP     EXTERNAL-IP     PORT(S)          AGE
echo-lb   LoadBalancer   10.96.67.194   192.168.100.0   8080:31024/TCP   88s
    When the cluster is ready, verify that Cilium is healthy with cilium-dbg status and that Maglev is chosen as the load balancing algorithm:
    当集群就绪后，使用 cilium-dbg status 确认 Cilium 运行正常，并验证 Maglev 已被选为负载均衡算法：
    代码（原文）
    $ kubectl -n kube-system exec -it ds/cilium -- cilium-dbg status --verbose
KubeProxyReplacement Details:
[...]
Backend Selection:      Maglev (Table Size: 16381)
    BGP peering sessions should be established between the cluster and the BGP peers:
    BGP 对等会话应已在集群和 BGP 对等体之间建立：
    代码（原文）
    $ cilium bgp peers
Node           Peer       State        Uptime  Family         Recv  Adv
kind-worker3   172.18.0.8 established  28s     ipv4/unicast   0     1
kind-worker4   172.18.0.8 established  29s     ipv4/unicast   0     1
    Access the FRRouting (FFR) device with docker exec -it frr vtysh, and then check the routing table to verify that the device has received the route to the service
    使用 docker exec -it frr vtysh 访问 FRRouting (FFR) 设备，然后检查路由表以验证该设备已收到通往该 Service 的路由
    代码（原文）
    IP 192.168.100.0/32:
75ea0285c9ea# show ip route bgp
B>* 192.168.100.0/32 [20/0] via 172.18.0.2, eth0, weight 1, 00:01:34
*                         via 172.18.0.3, eth0, weight 1, 00:01:34
    Next, access the FRR shell with docker exec -it frr bash and install curl with apkadd curl to verify successful access to the service:
    接下来，使用 docker exec -it frr bash 进入 FRR shell，并用 apk add curl 安装 curl，以验证是否能成功访问该 Service：
    代码（原文）
    bash-5.1# curl http://192.168.100.0:8080
Request served by echo-f69bb6cf9-4sm72
    In the backend’s response, you can see the pod name (here, echo-f69bb6cf9-4sm72). If you run the same command multiple times, you will see responses coming from both pods, indicating inconsistent hashing. This is because the hashing is based on the 5-tuple, and by default, the source port will automatically change. You need to fix the source port, with a command like the following:
    在后端的响应中，你可以看到 Pod 的名称（此处为 echo-f69bb6cf9-4sm72）。如果你多次运行相同的命令，会看到响应来自两个 Pod，这说明哈希结果 不一致。这是因为哈希基于五元组，而默认情况下源端口会自动变化。你需要固定源端口，使用如下命令：
    代码（原文）
    $ curl --local-port 6000 http://192.168.100.0:8080
    If you repeat this command every minute or so, you will now see the same backend pod answering, confirming consistent hashing. If you run the same command too quickly, you’re likely to see an error message
    如果你每隔一分钟左右重复执行此命令，现在应该会看到由同一个后端 Pod 响应，这确认了一致性哈希的工作。如果运行太快，可能会看到错误消息
    代码（原文）
    like curl: (45) bind failed with errno 98: Address in use. This happens
    because the source port specified with --local-port is still in use. The operating system prevents rebinding to the same local port too soon, to avoid ambiguity in connection tracking. This can be particularly problematic when trying to demonstrate connection consistency (e.g., with Maglev) where fixed source ports are required for deterministic load balancing behavior. A simple workaround is to introduce a delay (e.g., sleep 5) between connection attempts or cycle through a range of source ports. The simple-maglev-test.sh script located in the book’s GitHub repository verifies that Maglev consistently maps a given 5-tuple to the same backend pod across repeated requests. It sends traffic from the FRR client across 10 consecutive ports and prints the selected backends:
    因为用 --local-port 指定的源端口仍在使用中。操作系统会阻止过快地重新绑定到相同的本地端口，以避免连接跟踪中的歧义。当需要演示连接一致性（例如使用 Maglev 时），其中固定源端口对于确定性的负载均衡行为是必需的，这可能会特别成问题。一个简单的变通方法是在连接尝试之间引入延迟（例如 sleep 5），或者在一系列源端口之间循环。本书 GitHub 仓库中的 simple-maglev-test.sh 脚本会验证 Maglev 在多次请求中是否始终将给定的五元组映射到相同的后端 Pod。它从 FRR 客户端跨越 10 个连续端口发送流量，并打印所选择的后端：
    代码（原文）
    $ ./simple-maglev-test.sh
Simple Maglev consistency test from FRR container
Target: 192.168.100.0:8080
Testing 10 requests with fixed source ports
Testing Maglev consistency:
Format: SourcePort -> Backend
Port 60001 -> echo-f69bb6cf9-42hmc
Port 60002 -> echo-f69bb6cf9-42hmc
Port 60003 -> echo-f69bb6cf9-nscx5
Port 60004 -> echo-f69bb6cf9-nscx5
Port 60005 -> echo-f69bb6cf9-nscx5
Port 60006 -> echo-f69bb6cf9-nscx5
Port 60007 -> echo-f69bb6cf9-42hmc
Port 60008 -> echo-f69bb6cf9-42hmc
Port 60009 -> echo-f69bb6cf9-42hmc
Port 60010 -> echo-f69bb6cf9-nscx5
Test complete!
    If you repeat the same command (after waiting a few minutes, to avoid the port rebinding issue), it will generate another set of results. Compare these with the previous output. The result should be the same, confirming consistent hashing:
    如果你重复执行相同的命令（等待几分钟以避免端口重新绑定问题），它会生成另一组结果。将这些与之前的输出进行比较，结果应该相同，确认了一致性哈希的工作：
    代码（原文）
    $ ./simple-maglev-test.sh
Simple Maglev consistency test from FRR container
Target: 192.168.100.0:8080
Testing 10 requests with fixed source ports
OK: 136 MiB in 60 packages
Testing Maglev consistency:
Format: SourcePort -> Backend

Port 60002 -> echo-f69bb6cf9-42hmc

Test complete!

    Netkit
    Netkit

    Most CNI plugins attach a Kubernetes pod to its node by a virtual Ethernet (veth) pair. The pod’s eth0 interface sits in the pod network namespace and is paired with a peer in the host network namespace. This arrangement allows pods to reach other pods and external workloads through the host (refer back to Chapter 5 for a refresher on Linux and container networking primitives). If you want to confirm the pairing, a single check from within a pod is enough:
    大多数 CNI 插件通过一对虚拟以太网（veth）将 Kubernetes Pod 连接到其节点。Pod 的 eth0 接口位于 Pod 网络命名空间中，与主机网络命名空间中的对端配对。这种安排让 Pod 可以通过主机到达其他 Pod 和外部工作负载（可参阅第 5 章回顾 Linux 和容器网络原语的相关内容）。如果想确认配对情况，在 Pod 内进行一次检查即可：
    代码（原文）
    netshoot-client:~# ip a show eth0
22: eth0@if23: <BROADCAST,MULTICAST,UP,LOWER_UP> mtu 65520 qdisc noqueue state UP
group default qlen 1000
    The @ifN suffix indicates a veth peer. While veth pairs are widely adopted as the standard Linux container networking mechanism, the technology dates back over 15 years to Linux kernel 2.6.24 and carries several drawbacks. First, veth relies on layer 2 communication and uses ARP for a container to reach the peer end of the veth pair. This is unnecessary overhead: pods shouldn’t need to perform ARP resolution to reach a directly connected peer. More importantly, veth introduces performance costs. When traffic exits a pod or enters it from an external node, packets must traverse multiple layers: from the container’s network stack to the host namespace, and then through the host’s networking stack to the physical interface. This path includes transitions between namespaces and use of per-CPU backlog queues, all of which add latency and consume CPU cycles, especially under load. Netkit was introduced to the Linux kernel in version 6.7 by Daniel Borkmann and Nikolay Aleksandrov. The idea is simple: move the eBPF hook points into the pod network namespace so decisions can be made earlier, then steer packets to their next hop without incurring the extra transitions typical of the veth path. For egress traffic, that can mean forwarding directly to the physical device, avoiding parts of the host stack such as the per-CPU backlog queue (Figure 8-10).
    @ifN 后缀表示 veth 对端。虽然 veth 对已被广泛采用为 Linux 容器网络的标准机制，但这项技术可以追溯到 15 年前的 Linux 内核 2.6.24，并且存在若干不足。首先，veth 依赖二层通信，容器需要使用 ARP 来到达 veth 对的另一端。这是不必要的开销：Pod 不应该需要进行 ARP 解析才能到达直接相连的对端。更重要的是，veth 带来了性能损耗。当流量离开 Pod 或从外部节点进入时，数据包必须经过多层路径：从容器的网络栈到主机命名空间，再通过主机的网络栈到达物理接口。这条路径包含命名空间之间的切换和每 CPU 后端队列的使用，所有这些都会增加延迟并消耗 CPU 周期，尤其是在负载较高时。Netkit 由 Daniel Borkmann 和 Nikolay Aleksandrov 于 Linux 内核 6.7 版本引入。其思路很简单：将 eBPF 钩子点移入 Pod 网络命名空间，以便更早地做出决策，然后在不产生 veth 路径典型额外切换的情况下将数据包导向其下一跳。对于出站流量，这可能意味着直接转发到物理设备，绕过主机栈中的部分组件，如每 CPU 后端队列（见图 8-10）。
    In its initial implementation, netkit creates network devices as a pair: a primary device in the host namespace and a peer in the pod’s namespace. BPF program management is handled solely by the primary device, preventing pods from modifying or removing attached programs. This design avoids conflicts between competing eBPF applications and ensures consistent control by Cilium. Cilium integrates with netkit and can configure netkit devices in place of traditional veth pairs. By default, Cilium netkit operates at layer 3, which avoids ARP for local next-hop resolution. Layer 2 support is also available. Early adopters have noticed significant performance improvements, with Bytedance experiencing a 10% improvement in throughput, as documented in a case study. For more information on netkit, consult Nico’s Isovalent blog post and Jonathan Corbet’s article “The BPF-Programmable Network Device” on LWN.net.
    在其初始实现中，Netkit 将网络设备创建为一对：主机命名空间中的主设备和 Pod 命名空间中的对端设备。BPF 程序的管理完全由主设备处理，防止 Pod 修改或移除附加的程序。这一设计避免了多个 eBPF 应用之间的冲突，并确保由 Cilium 进行统一控制。Cilium 与 Netkit 集成，可以使用 Netkit 设备替代传统的 veth 对进行配置。默认情况下，Cilium Netkit 工作在三层，这避免了为本地下一跳解析而进行 ARP。同时也提供了二层支持。早期采用者已观察到显著的性能提升，据一个案例研究记录，字节跳动的吞吐量提高了 10%。关于 Netkit 的更多信息，请参阅 Isovalent 博客上 Nico 的文章以及 LWN.net 上 Jonathan Corbet 撰写的《The BPF-Programmable Network Device》一文。

    Summary
    小结

    In this chapter, we explored several Cilium features designed to improve network performance across a variety of scenarios. We began with Service Traffic Distribution, which prioritizes sending traffic to local or nearby endpoints. We then looked at LRP, which keeps traffic node-local to avoid unnecessary latency and CPU overhead. We introduced DSR as a way to preserve source IPs and optimize return-path performance and explored XDP for early packet processing at the driver level. We also discussed Maglev for consistent hashing and connection stability and netkit for removing the legacy veth bottleneck by moving eBPF logic closer to the application. This chapter is not intended to be exhaustive. Cilium includes many other performance features that were not covered here, such as BIG TCP, BBR congestion control, advanced queue management, and integration with hardware acceleration. The aim was to provide a practical tour of some of the more widely used and impactful capabilities. In the next chapter, we’ll turn our attention to multicluster networking and explore how Cilium enables seamless connectivity and identity-aware policy enforcement across Kubernetes clusters.
    本章中，我们探讨了多项旨在改进各种场景下网络性能的 Cilium 特性。我们从 Service 流量分发开始，它优先将流量发送到本地或附近的端点。随后介绍了 LRP，它将流量保持在节点本地以避免不必要的延迟和 CPU 开销。我们引入了 DSR 作为保留源 IP、优化回程路径性能的方式，并探讨了 XDP 在驱动层面对数据包进行早期处理。我们还讨论了 Maglev 的一致性哈希与连接稳定性，以及 Netkit 通过将 eBPF 逻辑移近应用来消除传统 veth 瓶颈。本章并不力求面面俱到。Cilium 还包含许多其他性能特性，这里未作涵盖，如 BIG TCP、BBR 拥塞控制、高级队列管理以及与硬件加速的集成。本章的目标是对一些更广泛使用且影响显著的功能进行一次实用的介绍。在下一章中，我们将转向多集群网络，探讨 Cilium 如何在多个 Kubernetes 集群之间实现无缝连接和基于身份的策略执行。

    第 9 章
    Multicluster Networking
    多集群网络

    As organizations grow, they often move beyond a single Kubernetes cluster. This jump from one to multiple environments presents a challenge for network security and observability. Cilium’s features are normally confined to the context of a single cluster. Identity is not represented across multiple clusters, so a policy to allow a pod in another cluster by its labels will not function, nor will observability features (see Chapter 15). One solution is to treat the clusters as truly independent, exposing services using load balancer services, Ingress, or Gateway API (see Chapters 6 and 7) and treating the remote cluster as no different from any other external endpoint. If only a few well-defined services are shared between clusters this can work well, but as the environment grows more complex it becomes unsustainable, requiring encoding specific CIDR ranges for services in the remote cluster across policies using toCIDRSet (see Chapter 12), using Egress Gateway (see Chapter 11), or incorporating additional tools for observability. Cilium Cluster Mesh exists to solve these problems. Meshed clusters share networking and identity information and avoid the need to expose services beyond the cluster altogether. In this chapter we will explore the use cases for Cluster Mesh and walk through how to configure and administrate meshed clusters. You can find all the YAML manifests you will need in the chapter09 directory of the book’s GitHub repository.
    随着组织规模扩大，它们往往会从单一 Kubernetes 集群转向多集群环境。从一个环境跨越到多个环境为网络安全与可观测性带来了挑战。Cilium 的功能通常仅限于单个集群的上下文。身份不会跨多个集群表示，因此通过标签允许另一个集群中的 Pod 的策略无法生效，可观测性功能同样如此（见第 15 章）。一种解决思路是将集群视为真正独立的实体，通过 LoadBalancer 服务、Ingress 或 Gateway API 暴露服务（见第 6、7 章），并将远端集群视为与其他外部端点无异。如果集群之间只共享少量定义明确的服务，这种方式尚能有效工作，但随着环境日趋复杂，它会变得不可持续——需要在使用 toCIDRSet（见第 12 章）的策略中为远端集群的服务硬编码特定 CIDR 范围，或者使用出口网关（见第 11 章），或引入额外的可观测性工具。Cilium Cluster Mesh 正是为解决这些问题而生的。互联集群会共享网络与身份信息，完全避免了将服务暴露在集群之外的需求。在本章中，我们将探讨 Cluster Mesh 的适用场景，并逐步演示如何配置与管理互联集群。你可以在本书 GitHub 仓库的 chapter09 目录下找到所需的全部 YAML 清单。

    Multicluster Kubernetes
    多集群 Kubernetes

    Organizations deploy multiple Kubernetes clusters for many reasons. A few common ones include: Organizational structure Organizations may find it easier to manage clusters inside the organizational units where they will be used (for example, a business unit or accounting cost center). Resilience With on-premise deployments, deploying clusters at multiple sites enables business continuity in the case of a datacenter failure at one site and “stretching” clusters across multiple regions is complex and discouraged,1 leading to multiple clusters instead. Scale limitations Beyond a certain point (usually around the 10,000-node mark), Kubernetes itself hits limitations. Multicloud setups This is often a regulatory mandate in industries such as finance or healthcare, to avoid becoming dependent on a single vendor. Regardless of the rationale, workloads will still need to communicate between clusters. If the goal is redundancy, then services will need to be deployed across multiple clusters, and client workloads will need a way of communicating across cluster boundaries. As mentioned previously, the service discovery, security, and observability features we’ve discussed so far depend on context from the cluster and don’t work outside of it. Users can treat these services as external, but this introduces significant operational complexity. Cilium Cluster Mesh offers a handy solution, extending those features across multiple clusters.
    组织出于诸多原因部署多个 Kubernetes 集群，常见的包括：组织结构 组织可能会发现，在其使用的组织单元（例如业务部门或会计成本中心）内部管理集群更为便捷。弹性 在本地部署场景下，在多个站点部署集群能够在某站点的数据中心发生故障时保障业务连续性，而“跨多个区域拉伸”集群既复杂又不被鼓励1，因此转而采用多集群。规模限制 超出一定规模后（通常在 10,000 个节点左右），Kubernetes 自身会触及限制。多云环境 这在金融或医疗等行业往往是监管要求，以避免对单一供应商形成依赖。无论出于何种理由，工作负载仍需跨集群通信。如果目标是冗余，那么服务将需要跨多个集群部署，客户端工作负载需要一种跨越集群边界通信的机制。如前所述，我们迄今讨论的服务发现、安全与可观测性功能均依赖于集群上下文，无法在集群外生效。用户可以将这些服务视为外部实体，但这会带来显著的运维复杂度。Cilium Cluster Mesh 提供了一种便捷方案，将这些功能扩展到多个集群。
    Note that when we talk about operating multiple clusters, we don’t usually mean different environments where separate clusters are used to represent different stages of a deployment cycle (e.g., production, preproduction, staging, testing, and development). These environments are generally not interconnected.
    请注意，当我们提及运维多个集群时，通常并不是指用于表示部署周期不同阶段（例如生产、预生产、灰度、测试、开发）的独立环境——这些环境通常不会互联。
    1As Josh Rosso et al. note in Production Kubernetes (O’Reilly), “The default settings in etcd are designed for the latency in a single datacenter...We strongly discourage the use of etcd clusters distributed across different regions.”
    1正如 Josh Rosso 等人在 Production Kubernetes（O’Reilly）一书中指出：“etcd 的默认设置针对单数据中心的延迟设计……我们强烈反对将 etcd 集群分布在不同区域。”

    What Cluster Mesh Solves
    Cluster Mesh 解决的问题

    We first introduced Cluster Mesh back in Chapter 1.
