This feature extends Cilium’s functionality in two specific ways: Multicluster service discovery This change is transparent to applications, which can still use local cluster IP services as normal, with no change to how they interact with them. Cilium can perform advanced routing in the eBPF datapath, including preferring samecluster endpoints if available but transparently failing over to endpoints in meshed clusters in the event of a local service failure. Multicluster network policy Network policy can be applied across clusters, and a policy can be written to target the cluster’s name as a security label. See Chapter 12 for an in-depth discussion of Cilium’s network policies. Cluster Mesh doesn’t provide connectivity between clusters (you need to implement that yourself), and it doesn’t handle routing packets between pods in different clusters beyond service discovery and encapsulation if using tunnel routing mode. We’ll discuss this more when we talk about cluster requirements later in this chapter.
    我们在第 1 章首次介绍了 Cluster Mesh。该功能从两个具体方面扩展了 Cilium 的能力：多集群服务发现 这一变更对应用透明，应用仍可照常使用本地 ClusterIP 服务，无需调整交互方式。Cilium 可以在 eBPF 数据路径中执行高级路由，包括优先选择同集群的可用端点，在本地服务发生故障时，自动故障转移到互联集群中的端点。多集群网络策略 网络策略可跨集群应用，策略可将集群名称作为安全标签进行匹配。关于 Cilium 网络策略的深入讨论见第 12 章。Cluster Mesh 不负责提供集群间的连通性（需要你自行实现），在使用隧道路由模式时，它除了服务发现和封装之外，也不处理不同集群中 Pod 之间的数据包路由。我们将在本章后面讨论集群要求时进一步阐述这一点。

    Use Cases
    用例

    Let’s consider an application hosted in a cluster in London, which must access a backend service replicated across two clusters in two different regions: Paris and Zurich. We’ll explore a few possible solutions without using Cluster Mesh, and then compare them to a solution using Cluster Mesh. The first option, shown in Figure 9-1, is to expose a LoadBalancer service (see Chapter 6) from each cluster and announce the routes over BGP to a BGP-capable network fabric (see Chapter 10). Using ExternalDNS and an internally hosted DNS server outside the cluster, a DNS name could be chosen for these services, such as backend-service.example.com, and the DNS server will then serve multiple A records for this domain name—one per cluster. This approach is relatively simple. BGP support is ubiquitous in on-premises enterprise-grade networking hardware, and most cloud providers have similar implementations in their networks (for example, Amazon Network Load Balancers). Any of the provided IP addresses will successfully route to one of the backend services over the fabric. However, this approach has several limitations. First and foremost, it depends on the clients correctly performing their own load balancing and failover. For some architectures this may be entirely acceptable, but often applications have an implicit assumption that there is a single IP address for a given destination. Improperly configured clients may simply select the first IP in the list when given multiple addresses and not correctly “fail over” to the other instances.
    假设一个应用托管在伦敦的集群中，它必须访问复制在两个不同区域（巴黎和苏黎世）的两个集群中的后端服务。我们先探讨不使用 Cluster Mesh 的几种可能方案，再与使用 Cluster Mesh 的方案进行对比。第一种方案（如图 9-1 所示）是从每个集群暴露一个 LoadBalancer 服务（见第 6 章），并通过 BGP 将路由通告到支持 BGP 的网络结构（见第 10 章）。结合 ExternalDNS 与集群外自建的 DNS 服务器，可以为这些服务选择一个 DNS 名称，例如 backend-service.example.com，随后 DNS 服务器将为该域名返回多个 A 记录——每个集群一个。这种方式相对简单，BGP 支持在本地企业级网络硬件中无处不在，大多数云提供商也在其网络中提供类似实现（例如 Amazon Network Load Balancers）。返回的任一 IP 地址都可以通过网络结构成功路由到其中一个后端服务。然而，这种方式存在若干局限。首先也是最重要的是，它依赖客户端正确执行自身的负载均衡与故障转移。对于某些架构而言这完全可以接受，但应用通常隐含地假设某个目标只有一个 IP 地址。配置不当的客户端在收到多个地址时可能只会选择列表中的第一个，无法正确地“故障转移”到其他实例。
    side load balancing Another issue is that it’s difficult to control traffic flow. For example, if administrators wish to remove a service or a cluster from operation for maintenance, they must remove the DNS records and wait for the DNS time-to-live (TTL) to expire to ensure that no clients with stale DNS records are still connected. Failing over long-lived HTTP connections, such as WebSockets, which do not refresh DNS once a long-lived TCP session is established, is not supported. It also requires management of multiple additional components, including a DNS server visible to the London cluster and ExternalDNS in the Paris and Zurich clusters. If we wanted a single IP address for the distributed application instead, a traditional solution is to deploy an external load balancer appliance, as shown in Figure 9-2 —often a physical device or hosted cloud service—and use that to map the IP to the different possible backends. These load balancers are not usually aware of the internals of the clusters, so the backends configured in the appliance must themselves be externally advertised Kubernetes LoadBalancer services.
    侧负载均衡 另一个问题是难以控制流量走向。例如，如果管理员希望移除某个服务或集群进行维护，必须删除 DNS 记录并等待 DNS 的生存时间（TTL）过期，才能确保持有过期 DNS 记录的客户端不再连接。持久化 HTTP 连接（例如 WebSocket）的故障转移不受支持，因为一旦持久化 TCP 会话建立，这类连接不会刷新 DNS。该方案还要求管理多个额外组件，包括对伦敦集群可见的 DNS 服务器，以及巴黎和苏黎世集群中的 ExternalDNS。如果我们希望为分布式应用提供单一 IP 地址，传统方案是部署一个外部负载均衡器设备（如图 9-2 所示）——通常是物理设备或托管云服务——用它将 IP 映射到不同的可用后端。这些负载均衡器通常不了解集群的内部结构，因此设备中配置的后端本身必须是对外通告的 Kubernetes LoadBalancer 服务。
    Some load balancers are aware of Kubernetes internals and can resolve target IP addresses to pod IPs without using services, but this requires natively routed clusters. This approach is also possible using more advanced network fabrics with equal-cost multi-path (ECMP) routing.
    部分负载均衡器了解 Kubernetes 内部结构，可以在不使用 Service 的情况下将目标 IP 地址解析为 Pod IP，但这需要原生路由的集群。使用支持等价多路径（ECMP）路由的更高级网络结构也可以实现该方案。
    This removes the responsibility for load-balancing decisions from the clients, as well as the need to use ExternalDNS, but it still requires deploying and configuring an external appliance. It also still requires using a DNS server, externally managing the mapping from IP address to DNS name for all clusters, and using a BGP-capable network fabric. Now let’s see how we can approach this problem using Cilium Cluster Mesh and global services. As shown in Figure 9-3, the external coordination of the IP address and service name are gone, and all the fabric needs to do is provide reachability. The application resolves the global service the same way as a local service, using a normal <service>.<namespace>.svc DNS record served by the in-cluster DNS service. The coordination to identify the remote cluster endpoints for the service and the resolution from the cluster IP service to a remote backend pod are handled entirely by Cilium.
    这将负载均衡决策的责任从客户端移除，也消除了使用 ExternalDNS 的需求，但仍然需要部署和配置外部设备，仍然需要 DNS 服务器，需要在集群外为所有集群管理 IP 地址到 DNS 名称的映射，以及需要支持 BGP 的网络结构。现在我们来看看如何使用 Cilium Cluster Mesh 和全局服务解决这个问题。如图 9-3 所示，外部协调 IP 地址与服务名称的工作被消除，网络结构只需提供连通性。应用像访问本地服务一样解析全局服务，使用集群内 DNS 服务提供的常规 <service>.<namespace>.svc DNS 记录。用于识别服务的远端集群端点、以及从 ClusterIP 服务到远端后端 Pod 的解析，完全由 Cilium 处理。
    With this approach, we also require much less of the underlying fabric—only that all nodes (for overlay routing mode clusters) or all pods (for natively routed clusters) have layer 3 connectivity. We may still use BGP, for example to advertise pod IP ranges, and BGP LoadBalancer services may still be desired for services exposed outside of the mesh, but the hard requirements on the underlying network are significantly reduced using Cluster Mesh.
    采用这种方案时，对底层网络的要求也大幅降低——仅要求所有节点（覆盖路由模式的集群）或所有 Pod（原生路由模式的集群）具备三层连通性。我们仍然可能使用 BGP，例如通告 Pod IP 范围，面向网格外暴露的服务也仍可能需要 BGP LoadBalancer 服务，但 Cluster Mesh 显著降低了对底层网络的硬性要求。

    Cluster Mesh Architecture
    Cluster Mesh 架构

    Now that you have an idea of what Cluster Mesh offers, let’s take a look at how it works. Cilium Cluster Mesh is designed to avoid a single point of failure. There is no coordinator cluster or centralized control plane. Instead, each cluster is aware of a path to each other cluster and is able to communicate with it directly. Metadata is passed between clusters using a Cluster Mesh–specific component called the Cluster Mesh API Server. This pulls information about endpoints, identities, and other settings from the Kubernetes API of its local cluster and presents an API to remote clusters to use to retrieve that information. Cluster Mesh also includes a component called KVStoreMesh2 that serves a cache of remote cluster information to Cilium agents in the local cluster. Agents then use this information to understand remote cluster identities for policy and endpoints for routing decisions. An overview of this design is shown in Figure 9-4. This architecture results in more overall connections3 but means there are no single points of failure. Cluster Mesh can continue to operate even in complex failure modes where the mesh is disconnected into groups that still have connectivity between each other, without needing any kind of leader/follower or consensus-based coordination.
    既然你已经了解了 Cluster Mesh 的能力，我们来看看它的工作原理。Cilium Cluster Mesh 的设计目标是避免单点故障。它没有协调集群或集中式控制平面。相反，每个集群都知晓到其他集群的路径，并能与它们直接通信。集群之间的元数据通过 Cluster Mesh 专用组件 Cluster Mesh API Server 传输。该组件从本地集群的 Kubernetes API 拉取端点、身份及其他设置的信息，并向远端集群提供 API 以供其检索。Cluster Mesh 还包含一个名为 KVStoreMesh2 的组件，它为本地集群的 Cilium Agent 缓存远端集群的信息。Agent 随后利用这些信息理解远端集群的身份（用于策略）和端点（用于路由决策）。该设计概览如图 9-4 所示。这种架构会带来更多的总体连接数3，但意味着没有单点故障。即使在网格断分成多个组（每组内部仍有连通性）的复杂故障模式下，Cluster Mesh 仍可继续运行，无需任何领导者/跟随者或基于共识的协调。
    2KVStoreMesh is enabled by default in Cilium v1.16+. It’s possible to disable it, but we can’t think of a good reason to do so. 3n(n − 1) / 2 total connections across a mesh of n clusters.
    2KVStoreMesh 在 Cilium v1.16+ 中默认启用。可以禁用它，但我们想不到任何充分的理由。 3n(n − 1) / 2，即由 n 个集群组成的网格的总连接数。

    Application Datapath
    应用数据路径

    Application data does not flow through the Cluster Mesh API Server. Cilium Cluster Mesh requires that all nodes in all clusters in the mesh have IP connectivity to each other on the announced node IP addresses. Clusters do not have to share a layer 2 domain. A common way to accomplish this is by using tunnels, VPNs, or other networking setups between clusters. Since the implementation details are strongly dependent on the network environment, this chapter assumes this property of the underlying network without describing how to configure it. For example, in Figure 9-5 each cluster has a /16 CIDR block, each node is allocated a /24 PodCIDR within that block, and all clusters use 10.0.0.0/8 as their native routing subnet.4 In this example, all pods have layer 3 connectivity to all other pods,5 and cluster C’s cluster IP service range is 192.0.2.0/22.
    应用数据不会流经 Cluster Mesh API Server。Cilium Cluster Mesh 要求网格中所有集群的所有节点，在已通告的节点 IP 地址上彼此具备 IP 连通性。集群无需共享二层域。实现这一点的常见方式是使用隧道、VPN 或集群间的其他网络设置。由于实现细节强烈依赖于网络环境，本章假定底层网络具备该特性，而不描述如何配置。例如，在图 9-5 中，每个集群有一个 /16 CIDR 块，每个节点在该块内分配一个 /24 PodCIDR，所有集群使用 10.0.0.0/8 作为原生路由子网4。在这个例子中，所有 Pod 与其他 Pod 都具备三层连通性5，集群 C 的 ClusterIP 服务范围为 192.0.2.0/22。
    4See Chapter 4 for a discussion of IPAM in Cilium. 5This could be accomplished by announcing the pod IP ranges over BGP or various other mechanisms.
    4关于 Cilium 中 IPAM 的讨论见第 4 章。 5这可以通过 BGP 通告 Pod IP 范围或各种其他机制实现。
    If a workload pod on node C3 wishes to resolve a local service that is configured as a global service, backed by pods in other nodes, it will see no difference between that one and any other service. We’ll explain how to configure a global service in “Global Services” on page 187. There are three backends in two other clusters, so Cilium’s eBPF cluster IP service resolution translates the destination IP to the destination pod’s IP before sending it on. This is possible because the Cilium agent on node C3 is aware of the global nature of the service and, through the Cluster Mesh API Server, is aware of the service’s backends in remote clusters.
    如果节点 C3 上的工作负载 Pod 希望解析一个配置为全局服务、后端位于其他节点的本地服务，它不会发现该服务与其他服务有任何区别。我们将在第 187 页的“全局服务”一节讲解如何配置全局服务。该服务在另外两个集群中有三个后端，因此 Cilium 的 eBPF ClusterIP 服务解析会在发送数据包之前将目的 IP 转换为目的 Pod 的 IP。这之所以可行，是因为节点 C3 上的 Cilium Agent 知晓服务的全局性质，并且通过 Cluster Mesh API Server 知晓服务在远端集群中的后端。
    If you enable the kube-proxy replacement (discussed in Chapter 6) for a cluster, then all services, including NodePort and LoadBalancer services, will work with global services. It doesn’t have to be enabled for all clusters in the mesh; however, for clusters that don’t have KPR enabled, cross-cluster service resolution will only work with ClusterIP services (since Cilium always uses eBPF for this type of service, whether KPR is enabled or not).
    如果你为某个集群启用了 kube-proxy 替代（KPR）（见第 6 章），那么包括 NodePort 和 LoadBalancer 服务在内的所有服务都可以与全局服务配合使用。这无需在网格中所有集群都启用；不过，对于未启用 KPR 的集群，跨集群服务解析仅适用于 ClusterIP 服务（因为无论是否启用 KPR，Cilium 始终为该类服务使用 eBPF）。

    Cluster Mesh API Server
    Cluster Mesh API Server

    While often viewed as a single component, the Cluster Mesh API Server itself has several components deployed as a single pod, as shown in Figure 9-6. This diagram shows two Cluster Mesh API Server pods running in two different clusters and how metadata is propagated between them.
    虽然常被视为单个组件，但 Cluster Mesh API Server 本身包含多个组件，它们作为单个 Pod 部署，如图 9-6 所示。该图展示了在两个不同集群中运行的两个 Cluster Mesh API Server Pod，以及元数据如何在它们之间传播。
    The Cluster Mesh API Server runs as a container alongside an instance of etcd. This etcd is managed entirely by Cilium and is distinct from the etcd cluster that underpins the Kubernetes API. It runs in memory as a single instance with no replication, as it stores no canonical data; it serves only as a cache for data and an API surface. When clusters are meshed together, it’s this etcd API that is exposed to other clusters. The Cluster Mesh API Server reads local state from the local Kubernetes API and writes a copy into its local etcd. Remote Cluster Mesh API Servers can then connect to that etcd instance, read the data from there, and replicate it into their local etcd instances. Finally, local Cilium agents can read the state of remote endpoints propagated into the etcd instance and program the eBPF datapath accordingly.
    Cluster Mesh API Server 以容器形式与 etcd 实例一同运行。该 etcd 完全由 Cilium 管理，与支撑 Kubernetes API 的 etcd 集群相互独立。它作为内存中的单实例运行，不进行复制，因为它不存储权威数据；仅作为数据的缓存和 API 入口。当集群互联时，暴露给其他集群的正是这个 etcd API。Cluster Mesh API Server 从本地 Kubernetes API 读取本地状态，将副本写入其本地 etcd。远端 Cluster Mesh API Server 随后可以连接到该 etcd 实例，从中读取数据，并将其复制到自身的本地 etcd 实例中。最后，本地 Cilium Agent 可以读取传播到 etcd 实例中的远端端点状态，并据此编程 eBPF 数据路径。

    Meshing Clusters
    集群网格互联

    In this section, we’ll step through an example of meshing three Kubernetes clusters. We’ll be using kind in our example, but the general approach is the same with any cluster. Once we’ve established our mesh, we’ll use it for the rest of this chapter to demonstrate Cluster Mesh features.
    本节我们将逐步演示互联三个 Kubernetes 集群的示例。示例中使用 kind，但通用方法适用于任何集群。在建立好网格后，我们将在本章余下部分用它演示 Cluster Mesh 的功能。

    Preparing Clusters
    准备集群

    There are a number of requirements for the clusters to be meshed:
    要互联集群，需要满足若干要求：
    • All the clusters have to be running Cilium as the CNI, although this can be in a
      所有集群都必须运行 Cilium 作为 CNI，不过可以处于
    chaining mode.
    链式模式。
    • All the clusters must have the same routing mode (encapsulation or native).
      所有集群必须使用相同的路由模式（封装或原生）。
    • The PodCIDR ranges cannot overlap.
      PodCIDR 范围不得重叠。
    • All nodes in all clusters must have layer 3 connectivity to each other. In native
      所有集群的所有节点之间必须具备三层连通性。在原生
    routing mode, this must be true of pods too.
    路由模式下，Pod 之间也必须满足此条件。
    • All clusters must use the same maximum mesh size, either 255 or 511 clusters.
      所有集群必须使用相同的最大网格规模，要么是 255，要么是 511 个集群。
    The default is 255, and using 511 enforces identity limitations.6 We’ll also assume that all clusters are running the same version of Cilium. In a real environment, you should limit version skew between clusters in a mesh as much as possible. In general, as long as all clusters are within a minor version of each other it’s considered a supported configuration. For example, a mesh of v1.15.8, v1.16.4, and v1.16.1 is acceptable, but a mesh of v1.15.8, v1.16.4, and v1.17.2 is not. You’ll also need to pick a domain name that will have each cluster as a subdomain, used for the TLS certificates that will identify the clusters. The default is mesh.cilium.io, but it’s possible to use your own. You can set the domain name with the Helm value clustermesh.config.domain. It must be the same on every cluster in the mesh. Finally, each cluster in the mesh will require a unique numeric ID and humanreadable name. This is configured in Helm with cluster.id and cluster.name. For example, if the mesh domain name is example.com and the cluster’s name is test, then the cluster’s domain name for the mesh is test.example.com. This domain name doesn’t have to resolve to anything via DNS; it is only referred to internally.
    默认值为 255，使用 511 会强制实施身份限制6。我们还将假设所有集群运行相同版本的 Cilium。在真实环境中，应尽可能限制网格内集群之间的版本差异。一般来说，只要所有集群处于同一次版本内，就属于受支持的配置。例如，v1.15.8、v1.16.4 和 v1.16.1 组成的网格是可以接受的，但 v1.15.8、v1.16.4 和 v1.17.2 组成的网格则不行。你还需要选择一个域名，将每个集群作为子域名，用于识别集群的 TLS 证书。默认值为 mesh.cilium.io，但也可以使用自己的域名。可通过 Helm 值 clustermesh.config.domain 设置域名，该值必须在网格中的每个集群上相同。最后，网格中的每个集群需要唯一的数字 ID 和人类可读的名称。这通过 Helm 中的 cluster.id 和 cluster.name 配置。例如，如果网格域名为 example.com，集群名称为 test，那么该集群的网格域名为 test.example.com。这个域名无需通过 DNS 解析到任何实体——它仅在内部引用。
    6Allowing 511 clusters halves the number of possible identities per cluster from 65,535 to 32,767. Unless you’re managing an extremely large estate, we recommend sticking with the default of 255.
    6允许 511 个集群会将每个集群可用的身份数量从 65,535 减半至 32,767。除非你管理的是超大规模集群阵列，否则我们建议坚持默认值 255。
    In this chapter, we’re going to mesh three clusters named red, green, and blue. Details on these clusters are given in Table 9-1.7 Table 9-1. Configuration details for our three test clusters
    在本章中，我们将互联三个集群，名称分别为 red、green 和 blue。这些集群的详细配置见表 9-17。表 9-1　三个测试集群的配置详情
    代码（原文）
    cluster.namecluster.idPodCIDR
red110.1.0.0/16
green210.2.0.0/16
blue310.3.0.0/16
    Use the provided kind configuration (kind.yaml) to deploy the three clusters. Make sure that they’re correctly named, as we’ll use the names later in the kind context:
    使用提供的 kind 配置（kind.yaml）部署这三个集群。确保命名正确，因为稍后我们将在 kind context 中使用这些名称：
    代码（原文）
    for cluster in red green blue; do
kind create cluster --config kind.yaml --name "$cluster"
done
    Once the deployment is complete, you can access the clusters with kubectl using
    部署完成后，可以通过 kubectl 配合
    代码（原文）
    --context kind-name.

    Configuring Cilium for Cluster Mesh
    为 Cluster Mesh 配置 Cilium

    Before we can write our Cilium configuration, we need to know the IP addresses of the nodes. Normally you’d want to deploy a load balancer (discussed in Chapter 10) to provide a stable IP for the Cluster Mesh API Servers, but in this simple example we’re going to make use of a NodePort service and set the communication IP to be the IP of the control-plane nodes. We’ll get these IPs with a short script (02-generatecilium-config.sh):
    在编写 Cilium 配置之前，我们需要知道节点的 IP 地址。通常需要部署负载均衡器（见第 10 章）为 Cluster Mesh API Server 提供稳定 IP，但在这个简单示例中，我们将使用 NodePort 服务并将通信 IP 设置为控制平面节点的 IP。我们将通过一个简短脚本（02-generatecilium-config.sh）获取这些 IP：
    代码（原文）
    for cluster in red green blue; do
echo "$cluster: $(kubectl --context "kind-$cluster" \
get node "$cluster-control-plane" \
-ojsonpath='{.status.addresses[?(@.type=="InternalIP")].address}')"
done
    Running this will produce output like the following:
    运行后将输出类似如下内容：
    代码（原文）
    red: 10.89.0.14
green: 10.89.0.16
blue: 10.89.0.20
    To deploy Cilium, we’ll write a single Helm template values file (cilium.template.yaml) with all of the shared configuration options, then use Helm’s --set syntax to configure the per-cluster flags differently for each cluster:
    部署 Cilium 时，我们将编写一个包含所有共享配置选项的 Helm 模板值文件（cilium.template.yaml），然后使用 Helm 的 --set 语法为每个集群分别配置差异化参数：
    7We’ve assigned each cluster a pod IP range that includes its cluster ID to make it easier for you to keep track of which IP addresses belong to each cluster. As long as each cluster has a unique ID and a unique nonoverlapping pod IP range, there is no requirement that there be any relation between the two.
    7我们为每个集群分配了包含其集群 ID 的 Pod IP 范围，以便你更容易区分哪些 IP 地址属于哪个集群。只要每个集群有唯一 ID 和唯一不重叠的 Pod IP 范围，两者之间没有任何关联要求。
    代码（原文）
    clustermesh:
useAPIServer: true
config:
enabled: true
clusters:
- name: red
port: 32379
id: 1
ips:
- 10.89.0.14
- name: green
port: 32379
ips:
- 10.89.0.16
- name: blue
port: 32379
ips:
- 10.89.0.20
apiserver:
tls:
authMode: cluster
auto:
enabled: true
method: cronJob
schedule: "0 0 1 */4 *"
    We’ve enabled Cluster Mesh by setting clustermesh.config.enabled=true, enabled the Cluster Mesh API Server with clustermesh.useAPIServer=true, and configured each cluster in the mesh as an entry under clustermesh.config.clusters. We also specified the cronJob TLS generation method for TLS certificates, which we will explain in the next section.
    我们通过设置 clustermesh.config.enabled=true 启用 Cluster Mesh，通过 clustermesh.useAPIServer=true 启用 Cluster Mesh API Server，并将网格中的每个集群配置为 clustermesh.config.clusters 下的条目。我们还指定了 TLS 证书的 cronJob 生成方法，下一节将对此进行解释。
    While it’s possible to mesh clusters imperatively using the Cilium CLI, and some documentation does so for illustrative purposes, it’s almost always preferable to do so declaratively using Helm configuration.
    可以使用 Cilium CLI 以 命令式方式互联集群，部分文档为了演示目的会这样做，但几乎总是更推荐以 声明式方式通过 Helm 配置完成。
    The previous script generates a cilium.yaml file from this template automatically. Once we have that file, we can use Helm to install Cilium. For each cluster, we’ll need to set the cluster.id, cluster.name, and ipam.operator.clusterPoolIPv4CIDRList Helm values using the information in Table 9-1. We can do this using a short bash script to address each cluster in turn (03-deploy-cilium.sh):
    上一个脚本会从该模板自动生成一个 cilium.yaml 文件。有了这个文件后，我们就可以使用 Helm 安装 Cilium。对于每个集群，我们需要根据表 9-1 中的信息设置 cluster.id、cluster.name 和 ipam.operator.clusterPoolIPv4CIDRList 等 Helm 值。可以使用一个简短的 bash 脚本依次处理每个集群（03-deploy-cilium.sh）：
    代码（原文）
    clusters=(red green blue)
for i in "${!clusters[@]}"; do
name="${clusters[$i]}"
id="$((i + 1))"
helm upgrade --install \
--kube-context "kind-$name" \
cilium cilium/cilium \
--namespace kube-system \
--version "1.18.3" \
--values cilium.yaml \
--set "cluster.id=$id" \
--set "cluster.name=$name" \
--set "ipam.operator.clusterPoolIPv4PodCIDRList=10.$id.0.0/16"
done
    After running this script, all the clusters should be active with Cilium running. However, the mesh will not be in a healthy state yet.
    运行此脚本后，所有集群都将处于运行状态，且 Cilium 已运行。不过，网格此时还处于不健康的状态。

    Propagating TLS Trust
    传播 TLS 信任

    The final step is to make the clusters trust each other. For now, although Cluster Mesh is configured, each cluster has a unique root of trust, and they will reject each other’s access attempts. We can check this by looking at the Cluster Mesh status in one of the clusters (note that the Cilium CLI supports the --context flag to specify the cluster, just like kubectl does):
    最后一步是让集群之间相互信任。目前，尽管 Cluster Mesh 已配置好，但每个集群都有独立的信任根，它们会相互拒绝访问。我们可以通过在其中一个集群中查看 Cluster Mesh 状态来验证这一点（注意 Cilium CLI 支持 --context 标志来指定集群，就像 kubectl 一样）：
    代码（原文）
    $ cilium --context kind-blue clustermesh status
3/3 nodes are not connected to all clusters [min:0 / avg:0.0 / max:0]
cilium-pq95v is not connected to cluster green: remote cluster
configuration required but not found
This is likely caused by KVStoreMesh not being connected to
the given cluster
Run 'kubectl exec -it -n kube-system clustermesh-apiserver-79fbd6596f-gxv2w
-c kvstoremesh -- /usr/bin/clustermesh-apiserver kvstoremesh-dbg
troubleshoot green' to investigate the cause
    We’ve shortened the output here, but we can see that our blue cluster is unable to access either of the others. Helpfully, the output gives us an additional debugging command to try. After running it, we get this output, again shortened for clarity:
    我们这里缩短了输出内容，但可以看到我们的 blue 集群无法访问另外两个集群。有帮助的是，输出给了我们一个额外的调试命令可供尝试。运行后得到以下输出，同样为清晰起见缩短了内容：
    代码（原文）
    Cluster "green":
Endpoints:
- https://green.mesh.cilium.io:32379
Hostname resolved to: 10.89.0.16
TCP connection successfully established to 10.89.0.16:32379
Cannot establish TLS connection to green.mesh.cilium.io:32379:
tls: failed to verify certificate: x509: certificate signed by unknown
authority (possibly because of "crypto/rsa: verification error" while
trying to verify candidate authority certificate "Cilium CA")
    The TLS certificate for the remote cluster is not being accepted. We’ll discuss models of distributing trust in “TLS Certificates in Cluster Mesh” on page 196, but for now we’re going to make each cluster trust every other cluster. Recall that when we installed Cilium, we specified the cronJob method of certificate generation. This caused Cilium to automatically generate a certificate authority when it was first installed, but it doesn’t regenerate the CA if it’s reinstalled using helm
    远端集群的 TLS 证书未被接受。我们将在第 196 页的“Cluster Mesh 中的 TLS 证书”一节讨论信任分发的模型，但目前我们将让每个集群信任其他所有集群。回想一下，我们安装 Cilium 时指定了证书生成的 cronJob 方法，这会让 Cilium 在首次安装时自动生成证书颁发机构（CA），但如果使用 helm
    代码（原文）
    upgrade.
    To fix this issue, download the generated CA certificates for each cluster and combine them into a single trust bundle using the following script (04-write-ca-bundle.sh):
    为修复此问题，可使用以下脚本（04-write-ca-bundle.sh）下载每个集群生成的 CA 证书，将它们合并为单一的信任包：
    代码（原文）
    ca_bundle="ca-bundle.crt"
rm -f "$ca_bundle"
for name in red green blue; do
kubectl \
--context "kind-$name" \
--namespace kube-system \
get secret \
cilium-ca \
-o jsonpath="{.data['ca\.crt']}" | base64 --decode >> "$ca_bundle"
done
    Then append them to the Cilium configuration as a shared trust bundle:
    然后将它们作为共享信任包追加到 Cilium 配置中：
    代码（原文）
    $ yq -i \
".tls.caBundle.content=\"$(cat ca-bundle.crt)\", \
.tls.caBundle.enabled=true" \
cilium.yaml
    Versions of Cilium prior to v1.18.5 require an additional step to make this method of TLS distribution work, due to a bug. You can find the necessary script in the book’s GitHub repository (chapter09/05-patch-sever-cert.sh).
    Cilium v1.18.5 之前的版本由于 bug，需要额外一步才能让这种 TLS 分发方式生效。所需脚本可以在本书 GitHub 仓库中找到（chapter09/05-patch-sever-cert.sh）。
    Reapply the Cilium configuration using the same helm command we used in the script at the end of the previous section and restart the Cluster Mesh API Server to pick up the new settings:
    使用上一节末尾脚本中相同的 helm 命令重新应用 Cilium 配置，并重启 Cluster Mesh API Server 以加载新设置：
    代码（原文）
    for name in red green blue; do
kubectl rollout restart \
--context "kind-$name" \
--namespace kube-system \
deploy/clustermesh-apiserver
done
    We can then confirm that Cluster Mesh is healthy using the same command as before:
    随后我们可以使用之前相同的命令确认 Cluster Mesh 处于健康状态：
    代码（原文）
    $ cilium --context kind-blue clustermesh status
All 3 nodes are connected to all clusters [min:2 / avg:2.0 / max:2]
All 1 KVStoreMesh replicas are connected to all clusters [min:2 / avg:2.0 /
max:2]
Cluster Connections:
- green: 3/3 configured, 3/3 connected - KVStoreMesh: 1/1 configured, 1/1
connected
- red: 3/3 configured, 3/3 connected - KVStoreMesh: 1/1 configured, 1/1
connected
    With our clusters meshed, nothing changes at first. All clusters continue to operate as they were before—but this set of changes enables us to use Cluster Mesh’s features.
    集群互联后，起初不会有任何变化。所有集群继续像之前一样运行——但这一系列变更让我们能够使用 Cluster Mesh 的功能。

    Testing Cluster Mesh
    测试 Cluster Mesh

    We can use Cilium’s built-in connectivity test to verify Cluster Mesh behavior between any two clusters in our mesh (here, the red and green clusters):
    我们可以使用 Cilium 内置的连通性测试，验证网格中任意两个集群之间的 Cluster Mesh 行为（此处以 red 和 green 集群为例）：
    代码（原文）
    $ cilium connectivity test --context kind-red --multi-cluster kind-green
    The --context and --multi-cluster flags both take Kubernetes context names, like the --context and --kube-context flags in previous commands, not cluster names as configured in Helm as cluster.name.
    --context 和 --multi-cluster 标志都接受 Kubernetes context 名称，就像之前命令中的 --context 和 --kube-context 标志一样，而不是 Helm 中配置的 cluster.name 集群名称。
    One of the tests performed by cilium connectivity test verifies that no errors exist in the Cilium logs. If you’ve been following this chapter verbatim, there will be errors from when we first set up Cluster Mesh without the shared certificate authorities. You can run the test anyway to verify that there are no other errors, or you can skip that test with the extra flag --test '!check-log-errors/
    cilium connectivity test 执行的其中一项测试会验证 Cilium 日志中没有错误。如果你完全按照本章操作，会存在首次设置 Cluster Mesh 时（未共享 CA）产生的错误。你仍然可以运行测试来验证是否有其他错误，或者可以通过额外标志 --test '!check-log-errors/
    代码（原文）
    no-errors-in-logs'.
    These tests only test connectivity between a given pair of clusters. You can iterate through every pair using this script (06-connectivity-test.sh):
    这些测试仅测试指定集群对之间的连通性。可以使用此脚本（06-connectivity-test.sh）遍历所有集群对：
    代码（原文）
    clusters=(red green blue)
for i in "${!clusters[@]}"; do
for j in "${!clusters[@]}"; do
if [ "$j" -gt "$i" ]; then
cluster_a="${clusters[$i]}"
cluster_b="${clusters[$j]}"
echo "Testing $cluster_a and $cluster_b"
cilium connectivity test \
--context "kind-$cluster_a" \
--multi-cluster "kind-$cluster_b" \
--test '!check-log-errors/no-errors-in-logs'
fi
done
done
    The script runs for each pair of clusters in the clusters array, meaning n(n − 1) / 2 tests, where each test can take several minutes. We don’t recommend this approach for larger meshes, as it will take a long time.
    脚本会对 clusters 数组中的每对集群运行测试，共 n(n − 1) / 2 次测试，每次测试可能需要数分钟。对于较大规模的网格，我们不推荐这种方法，因为耗时会非常久。

    Global Services
    全局服务

    Now that we have our meshed clusters configured, we can begin to use Cilium’s features across the mesh. The first feature we’ll look at is cross-cluster services. In Kubernetes, a service is a networking abstraction over a group of backend pods, providing a stable network identity in an environment where pods may be created, removed, or replaced at any moment. Using a global service, we can accomplish the same service indirection for backends in other clusters in the mesh.
    现在我们已经配置好了集群网格，可以开始跨网格使用 Cilium 的各项功能。首先来看跨集群服务。在 Kubernetes 中，服务是对一组后端 Pod 的网络抽象，在 Pod 随时可能被创建、删除或替换的环境中提供稳定的网络身份。通过使用 全局服务，我们可以为网格中其他集群的后端实现相同的服务间接调用能力。

    Configuring Global Services
    配置全局服务

    Cilium implements cluster IP services using the eBPF datapath. Figure 9-7 provides an overview of this process (see Chapter 6 for details). The translation from a service IP to a backend pod IP occurs in eBPF as the packet exits the pod.
    Cilium 使用 eBPF 数据路径来实现 ClusterIP 服务。图 9-7 展示了这一过程的概览（详见第 6 章）。从服务 IP 到后端 Pod IP 的转换在数据包离开 Pod 时通过 eBPF 完成。
    By creating two services with the same name and namespace in two different clusters and annotating them with service.cilium.io/global: "true",8 we can make Cilium import information about endpoints in other clusters across the mesh. Cilium’s eBPF datapath can then select the endpoints in other clusters the same way as local ones.
    通过在两个不同集群中创建名称和命名空间相同的两个服务，并为它们添加 service.cilium.io/global: "true" 的注解，我们可以让 Cilium 跨网格导入其他集群中的端点信息。之后 Cilium 的 eBPF 数据路径就能够像选择本地端点一样选择其他集群中的端点。
    8This is an annotation, not a label.
    8这是一个注解，不是标签。
    The quotes around "true" are required. If you put service.cilium.io/global: true in your YAML without quotes, this will be interpreted as a string key to a Boolean value, and Kubernetes expects annotations to be a map of strings to strings, not strings to Booleans. If you try it, you’ll get an error like
    "true" 周围的引号是必需的。如果在 YAML 中写入没有引号的 service.cilium.io/global: true，YAML 会将其解释为一个布尔值的字符串键，而 Kubernetes 要求注解是字符串到字符串的映射，不是字符串到布尔值的映射。如果你尝试这样写，会得到一个类似下面的错误：
    代码（原文）
    json: cannot unmarshal bool into Go struct field Object
Meta.metadata.annotations of type string.
    It’s important to note that the services only need to have the same name and namespace in each cluster. It doesn’t matter if the cluster IP service range isn’t the same, and the two services will not synchronize their service IPs. In Figure 9-8 you can see the two services have different IPs in the two clusters, but both clusters can view all endpoints in either cluster. This is an important consequence of the decentralized design of Cluster Mesh: there is no single canonical view of a service across all clusters; it only ensures that global services in different clusters can use each other’s endpoints.
    需要注意的是，每个集群中的服务只需要名称和命名空间相同即可。ClusterIP 服务的 IP 范围不必一致，两个服务之间也不会同步服务 IP。在图 9-8 中，你可以看到两个服务在两个集群中拥有不同的 IP，但两个集群都能看到任一集群中的所有端点。这一重要特性源自 Cluster Mesh 的去中心化设计：不存在一个跨所有集群的服务权威视图；它只确保不同集群中的全局服务能够使用彼此的端点。
    Let’s configure a service in our example clusters to show this feature in action. We’ll deploy a service to every cluster that responds with the current node name. Because our nodes are named after the clusters, we can use this to understand which cluster the response is coming from. Here’s the service portion of the manifest (global-service.yaml):
    让我们在示例集群中配置一个服务来演示该功能。我们将在每个集群中部署一个服务，它会返回当前节点的名称。因为我们的节点以集群名称命名，所以可以据此判断响应来自哪个集群。以下是清单文件（global-service.yaml）中服务部分的内容：
    代码（原文）
    apiVersion: v1
kind: Service
metadata:
name: nginx
annotations:
service.cilium.io/global: "true"
spec:
selector:
app.kubernetes.io/name: nginx
ports:
- port: 80
targetPort: 8080
protocol: TCP
    Using a short script (07-deploy-global-service.sh), we can quickly apply it to all of our clusters:
    通过一个简短的脚本（07-deploy-global-service.sh），我们可以快速将其应用到所有集群：
    代码（原文）
    for cluster in red green blue; do
kubectl apply \
--context "kind-$cluster" \
--filename global-service.yaml
done
    Then we can test the service’s behavior with a test pod:
    然后我们可以用一个测试 Pod 来测试该服务的行为：
    代码（原文）
    $ kubectl run test \
--context kind-green -it --rm \
--image nicolaka/netshoot -- bash
test:~# curl nginx
<html><body><p>Hello from blue-worker</p></body></html>
test:~# curl nginx
<html><body><p>Hello from red-worker</p></body></html>
test:~# curl nginx
<html><body><p>Hello from blue-worker</p></body></html>
test:~# curl nginx
<html><body><p>Hello from red-worker</p></body></html>
test:~# curl nginx
<html><body><p>Hello from blue-worker</p></body></html>
test:~# curl nginx
<html><body><p>Hello from blue-worker</p></body></html>
test:~# curl nginx
<html><body><p>Hello from green-worker2</p></body></html>
test:~# curl nginx
<html><body><p>Hello from red-worker</p></body></html>
    We can see that we’re getting a random backend responding each time since Cilium balances our requests across the possible backends of the global service.
    可以看到每次都会有一个随机后端响应，因为 Cilium 会将请求均衡到全局服务的所有可能后端上。

    Endpoint Affinity
    端点亲和性

    At the moment, our global service allows routing from a source in any of the configured clusters to an endpoint in any other cluster, and the flow is chosen randomly for each connection. We can, however, use other annotations to control the preference between local and remote endpoints. This is useful when you’d prefer to use a local version of a service but want to transparently fail over to a remote copy when the local version isn’t available. This sort of affinity is shown in Figure 9-9.
    目前，我们的全局服务允许从任意已配置集群中的源路由到任意其他集群中的端点，并且每次连接都会随机选择流。不过，我们可以使用其他注解来控制本地端点与远程端点之间的偏好。当你希望优先使用服务的本地版本、但希望在本地版本不可用时透明地故障切换到远程副本时，这一功能非常有用。这种亲和性如图 9-9 所示。
    This kind of cross-region transparent failover can lead to issues in some cases. Cilium will dutifully fail over to remote endpoints, but if the remote clusters have a much higher latency, and the workloads cannot handle higher latencies from cross-cluster traffic, then such a failover can contribute to cascading failures. It’s impossible to predict this in isolation, so you should always perform regular tests of failover mechanisms—including Cluster Mesh—in non-production environments to test how your application and particular networking and cluster setup will behave during a cross-region failover.
    这种跨区域透明故障切换在某些情况下可能引发问题。Cilium 会尽职尽责地故障切换到远程端点，但如果远程集群的延迟高得多，而工作负载无法承受跨集群流量带来的更高延迟，那么这种故障切换就可能加剧连锁故障。单独来看这一问题无法完全预判，因此你应该始终在非生产环境中定期测试故障切换机制——包括 Cluster Mesh——以检验你的应用以及特定的网络和集群设置在跨区域故障切换时会表现如何。
    To show endpoint affinity in action, let’s start by annotating one of our services to use local affinity. We’ll use the one in the green cluster as an example:
    为了演示端点亲和性，我们先来为其中一个服务添加注解，启用本地亲和性。我们以 green 集群中的服务为例：
    代码（原文）
    $ kubectl \
--context kind-green \
annotate service nginx \
'service.cilium.io/affinity=local'
service/nginx annotated
    Using the same test pod as before, we can see the change in behavior:
    使用和之前相同的测试 Pod，我们可以观察到行为的变化：
    代码（原文）
    $ kubectl run test --context kind-green -it --rm \
--image nicolaka/netshoot -- bash
test:~# curl nginx
<html><body><p>Hello from green-worker</p></body></html>
test:~# curl nginx
<html><body><p>Hello from green-worker</p></body></html>
test:~# curl nginx
<html><body><p>Hello from green-worker</p></body></html>
    The endpoint in the local cluster now always responds. If we remove that backend pod, however, the service will still use remote endpoints:
    现在本地集群中的端点始终响应。不过，如果我们删除了这个后端 Pod，服务仍会使用远程端点：
    代码（原文）
    $ kubectl \
--context kind-green \
scale deployment nginx \
--replicas=0
deployment.apps/nginx scaled
    Pods don’t need to be restarted to pick up this change, as it’s performed in the eBPF datapath. We’re only showing the test pod being launched every time for clarity. You can leave a single test pod running during all of this configuration if you prefer.
    Pod 不需要重启就能感知到这一变化，因为它是在 eBPF 数据路径中处理的。我们每次都重新启动测试 Pod 只是为了演示清楚。如果你愿意，可以在整个配置过程中让一个测试 Pod 一直运行。
    Let’s see what happens when we run our test now:
    现在让我们看看测试会发生什么：
    代码（原文）
    $ kubectl run test \
--context kind-green \
-it \
--rm \
--image nicolaka/netshoot \
-- bash
test:~# curl nginx
<html><body><p>Hello from blue-worker2</p></body></html>
test:~# curl nginx
<html><body><p>Hello from red-worker2</p></body></html>
test:~# curl nginx
<html><body><p>Hello from blue-worker2</p></body></html>
    Once again, a random backend responds to each request. Note that service affinity only affects the cluster in which it’s set on the service. For example, if we repeat the same test on the blue cluster, it won’t show service affinity:
    同样，每次请求都会由一个随机后端响应。注意，服务亲和性只影响设置了该注解的那个集群。例如，如果我们在 blue 集群上重复相同的测试，它不会表现出服务亲和性：
    代码（原文）
    $ kubectl \
--context kind-green \
scale deployment nginx \
--replicas=1
deployment.apps/nginx scaled
$ kubectl run test \
--context kind-blue \
-it \
--rm \
--image nicolaka/netshoot -- bash
test:~# curl nginx
<html><body><p>Hello from green-worker2</p></body></html>
test:~# curl nginx
<html><body><p>Hello from green-worker2</p></body></html>
test:~# curl nginx
<html><body><p>Hello from red-worker2</p></body></html>
test:~# curl nginx
<html><body><p>Hello from blue-worker2</p></body></html>
    This is a consequence of Cluster Mesh’s distributed design. Because there is no global coordinated view of a service—only services that can communicate with each other across the mesh—the settings that affect routing behavior are local to the cluster using that service.
    这是 Cluster Mesh 分布式设计的必然结果。因为不存在一个全局协调的服务视图——只有能够跨网格通信的各个服务——影响路由行为的设置是本地的，只对使用该服务的那个集群生效。

    Global Policy
    全局策略

    Cilium’s network policy engine (discussed in Chapter 12) assigns every endpoint an identity. Identities for pods derive their labels from the pod’s labels, which means that you can write a network policy to target other pods by their labels—for example, allowing only pods with role: backend to access a database. These identities are also shared across the mesh, meaning that a network policy can select endpoints from other clusters using the same fromEndpoints and toEndpoints statements used for local policy. Without Cluster Mesh, when services are exposed to other clusters using load balancers of some kind (see “Use Cases” on page 173 ), egress policy is possible—if error prone—by applying policy to the CIDR ranges corresponding to services exposed in other clusters or by using FQDN policy (see Chapter 13). Ingress policy is much harder, since traffic from an unmeshed external cluster will use the same IP range for all nodes (and for pods, for a natively routed cluster), so different workloads cannot be differentiated. The only mechanism at layer 3 is to use Cilium’s Egress Gateway (discussed in Chapter 11) to assign external IP addresses for egress traffic that are then used as part of fromCIDR statements in the other cluster. All of these approaches are error prone and add complexity. Using Cluster Mesh, we can rely on Cilium to automatically share endpoint identity information between clusters and write policy in largely the same way as we do with one cluster.
    Cilium 的网络策略引擎（第 12 章讨论）为每个端点分配一个 身份。Pod 的身份从 Pod 的标签派生，这意味着你可以编写网络策略，通过标签来针对其他 Pod——例如，只允许具有 role: backend 标签的 Pod 访问数据库。这些身份也会在网格中共享，也就是说，网络策略可以使用与本地策略相同的 fromEndpoints 和 toEndpoints 语句来选择其他集群中的端点。没有 Cluster Mesh 时，当服务通过某种负载均衡器暴露给其他集群时（见第 173 页的"使用场景"），可以通过两种方式实现入站/出站策略——虽然容易出错：一是对暴露在其他集群中的服务对应的 CIDR 范围应用策略，二是使用 FQDN 策略（见第 13 章）。而入站策略则困难得多，因为来自未建立网格的外部集群的流量会使用相同的 IP 范围来标识所有节点（对于原生路由集群还包括 Pod），因此无法区分不同的工作负载。在三层可用的唯一机制是使用 Cilium 的出口网关（第 11 章讨论）为出站流量分配外部 IP 地址，然后在另一个集群的 fromCIDR 语句中使用这些 IP 地址。所有这些方法都容易出错且增加复杂度。使用 Cluster Mesh，我们可以依靠 Cilium 在集群之间自动共享端点身份信息，编写策略的方式与在单个集群中基本一致。
    This behavior changed in Cilium v1.19. In Cilium v1.18, which we’re using here, and in earlier versions, Cilium policy automatically selected endpoints from any cluster in the mesh unless the cluster name was specified. From Cilium v1.19, policy only selects endpoints from the local cluster by default.
    这一行为在 Cilium v1.19 中发生了变化。在我们当前使用的 Cilium v1.18 及更早版本中，Cilium 策略会自动选择网格中任意集群的端点，除非指定了集群名称。从 Cilium v1.19 开始，策略默认只选择 本地集群中的端点。
    Much like with services, while Cluster Mesh allows policy in meshed clusters to refer to endpoints in other clusters using security labels, it does not automatically synchronize policy. For example, a CiliumClusterwideNetworkPolicy with an endpointSelector set to select all endpoints will apply only to endpoints in the cluster in which it is installed. We can use our existing red, green, and blue clusters as an example. The red cluster will contain our client application, while the green and blue clusters will contain copies of the server. We already have a global service and backend pods deployed in each, so we’ll reuse that deployment here. It’s important to keep the global service in the red cluster, but we want to remove the pods that could be a part of it locally:
    与服务类似，虽然 Cluster Mesh 允许网格中的策略使用安全标签引用其他集群中的端点，但它不会自动同步策略。例如，设置了选择所有端点的 endpointSelector 的 CiliumClusterwideNetworkPolicy 只会应用到它所在集群中的端点。我们可以用现有的 red、green 和 blue 三个集群作为例子。red 集群中放我们的客户端应用，而 green 和 blue 集群中放服务器的副本。我们已经在每个集群中部署了全局服务和后端 Pod，所以这里复用那个部署。我们需要保留 red 集群中的全局服务，但希望删除本地可能属于该服务的 Pod：
    代码（原文）
    $ kubectl delete --context kind-red deployment nginx
deployment.apps "nginx" deleted
    We see the result of this in Figure 9-10, where the red cluster does not have any backends for the nginx service but can still refer to the remote endpoints in the green and blue clusters.
    图 9-10 展示了这一结果：red 集群中没有 nginx 服务的后端，但仍然可以引用 green 和 blue 集群中的远程端点。
    We’ll secure the NGINX pods on the blue and green clusters first (nginx-policy.yaml), using the identity label io.cilium.k8s.policy.cluster to refer to endpoints specifically in other clusters:
    我们先来保护 blue 和 green 集群上的 NGINX Pod（nginx-policy.yaml），使用身份标签 io.cilium.k8s.policy.cluster 来专门引用其他集群中的端点：
    代码（原文）
    apiVersion: "cilium.io/v2"
kind: CiliumNetworkPolicy
metadata:
name: nginx
spec:
endpointSelector:
matchLabels:
app.kubernetes.io/name: nginx
ingress:
- fromEndpoints:
- matchLabels:
app.kubernetes.io/name: frontend
io.cilium.k8s.policy.cluster: red
toPorts:
- ports:
- port: "8080"
protocol: TCP
    We need to apply this policy only to the blue and green clusters, as the red cluster has no NGINX pods anymore:
    我们只需要将此策略应用到 blue 和 green 集群，因为 red 集群中已经没有 NGINX Pod 了：
    代码（原文）
    $ kubectl apply --context kind-blue --filename nginx-policy.yaml
ciliumnetworkpolicy.cilium.io/nginx created
$ kubectl apply --context kind-green --filename nginx-policy.yaml
ciliumnetworkpolicy.cilium.io/nginx created
    We can then apply the egress policy (frontend-policy.yaml) in the red cluster and select our test pod by specifying the required label app.kubernetes.io/name:
    然后我们可以在 red 集群中应用出站策略（frontend-policy.yaml），通过指定所需的标签 app.kubernetes.io/name: 来选择测试 Pod：
    代码（原文）
    frontend:
apiVersion: "cilium.io/v2"
kind: CiliumNetworkPolicy
metadata:
name: frontend
spec:
endpointSelector:
matchLabels:
app.kubernetes.io/name: frontend
egress:
- toEndpoints:
- matchLabels:
"k8s:io.kubernetes.pod.namespace": kube-system
"k8s:k8s-app": kube-dns
toPorts:
- ports:
- port: "53"
protocol: ANY
rules:
dns:
- matchPattern: "nginx.default.svc.cluster.local."
- toEndpoints:
- matchLabels:
app.kubernetes.io/name: nginx
matchExpressions:
- key: io.cilium.k8s.policy.cluster
operator: In
values:
- green
- blue
toPorts:
- ports:
- port: "8080"
protocol: TCP
    The details of this policy are covered in Chapter 12, but it constrains the egress traffic of pods with the app.kubernetes.io/name: frontend label in the clusters where it is applied. Egress traffic has to match one of two egress rules to be permitted: either DNS requests to CoreDNS for the NGINX service, or port 8080 TCP connections to NGINX backends in the green or blue clusters. After applying this policy we can again test it with our test pod, noting that we have to add the frontend label:
    该策略的细节在第 12 章介绍，但它限制了在其被应用的集群中带有 app.kubernetes.io/name: frontend 标签的 Pod 的出站流量。出站流量必须匹配以下两条出站规则之一才能被允许：要么是向 CoreDNS 发起的 NGINX 服务 DNS 请求，要么是发往 green 或 blue 集群中 NGINX 后端的 8080 端口 TCP 连接。应用此策略后，我们可以再次用测试 Pod 进行测试，注意需要加上 frontend 标签：
    代码（原文）
    $ kubectl apply --context kind-red --filename frontend-policy.yaml
ciliumnetworkpolicy.cilium.io/frontend created
$ kubectl run test --context kind-red \
-it --rm --image nicolaka/netshoot \
--labels 'app.kubernetes.io/name=frontend' -- bash
test:~# curl nginx
<html><body><p>Hello from blue-worker2</p></body></html>
test:~# curl nginx
<html><body><p>Hello from blue-worker2</p></body></html>
test:~# curl nginx
<html><body><p>Hello from green-worker2</p></body></html>
    Using a different pod, we can test the permissions. If we relaunch our test pod without the app.kubernetes.io/name label we can verify that the ingress permissions are working:
    换一个不同的 Pod 可以测试权限。如果我们在没有 app.kubernetes.io/name 标签的情况下重新启动测试 Pod，就能验证入站权限是否正常工作：
    代码（原文）
    $ kubectl run test --context kind-red -it --rm \
--image nicolaka/netshoot -- bash
test:~# curl nginx -m2
curl: (28) Connection timed out after 2002 milliseconds
    This use of cross-cluster policy allows applications to apply the same fine-grained security controls as they can in-cluster. Finally, we’ll tear down the environment by deleting the clusters:
    这种跨集群策略的使用让应用能够应用与集群内相同粒度的安全控制。最后，我们通过删除集群来清理环境：
    代码（原文）
    for cluster in red green blue; do
kind delete cluster --name "$cluster"
done

    TLS Certificates in Cluster Mesh
    Cluster Mesh 中的 TLS 证书

    The internals of Cluster Mesh use TLS certificates to secure components as they communicate. These not only encrypt the metadata of the cluster as it is in transit, but also prevent attackers from pulling metadata from a cluster with Cluster Mesh enabled or imitating another cluster in the mesh. Figure 9-11 illustrates our previous example, in which each cluster autogenerated a self-signed certificate authority and used that to sign the various certificates for internal usage. When we needed to mesh clusters, we distributed the certificate authorities between clusters to allow the remote connections to trust each other.
    Cluster Mesh 的内部使用 TLS 证书来保护组件之间的通信。这些证书不仅对传输中的集群元数据进行加密，还能防止攻击者从启用了 Cluster Mesh 的集群中获取元数据或冒充网格中的其他集群。图 9-11 展示了我们之前的示例，其中每个集群自动生成了一个自签名证书授权（CA），并使用它为内部使用的各种证书签名。当我们需要建立集群网格时，在集群之间分发证书授权以让远程连接彼此信任。
    Each cluster has its own CA to sign certificates. With Cluster Mesh enabled, each cluster will trust certificates signed by any of the CAs in the trust bundle. While we avoided transferring private key material, this isn’t a very sustainable option. Any time we needed to add a new cluster, or change the CA on a cluster, we’d have to update the trust bundles on every other cluster in the mesh. A better approach is to use a single certificate authority. There are four certificates used in Cluster Mesh, stored as Kubernetes secrets. One of them is a server certificate, and the other three are client certificates. The client certificates have a common name derived from the cluster’s defined name (see Table 9-2). Table 9-2. Cluster Mesh client certificates
    每个集群都有自己的 CA 来签名证书。启用 Cluster Mesh 后，每个集群都会信任信任包中任意 CA 签名的证书。虽然我们避免了传输私钥材料，但这并不是一个非常可持续的方案。每当我们需要添加新集群，或修改某个集群上的 CA 时，都必须更新网格中所有其他集群的信任包。更好的做法是使用单一的证书授权。Cluster Mesh 中使用了四类证书，存储在 Kubernetes Secret 中。其中一个是服务器证书，另外三个是客户端证书。客户端证书的通用名从集群定义的名称派生（见表 9-2）。表 9-2　Cluster Mesh 客户端证书

    CertificateCommon namePurpose 证书通用名用途

    clustermesh-apiserver-admin-${cluster.name}The Cluster Mesh API Server presents this to its clustermesh-apiserver-admin-${cluster.name}The Cluster Mesh API Server 将此证书呈现给其

    admin-certsidecar etcd. admin-certsidecar etcd

    clustermesh-apiserver-remote-${cluster.name}The Cluster Mesh API Server presents this to other clustermesh-apiserver-remote-${cluster.name}The Cluster Mesh API Server 将此证书呈现给远程

    remote-certetcd instances in remote clusters. remote-cert集群中的其他 etcd 实例

    clustermesh-apiserver-local-${cluster.name}Cilium agents present this to their local Cluster clustermesh-apiserver-local-${cluster.name}Cilium Agent 将此证书呈现给其本地 Cluster

    local-certMesh API Server’s etcd. local-certMesh API Server 的 etcd

    The server certificate is stored in the secret clustermesh-apiserver-server-cert and is served by etcd from connections using all of the client certificates. This server TLS certificate needs to, between its common name and Subject Alternative Names, be signed for:
    服务器证书存储在 Secret clustermesh-apiserver-server-cert 中，由 etcd 为使用所有客户端证书的连接提供。此服务器 TLS 证书在其通用名和主题备用名称之间需要为以下内容签名：
    代码（原文）
    ••Domain name ${cluster.name}.${clustermesh.config.domain}
••Domain name clustermesh-apiserver.${cilium-namespace}.svc
    • Domain name localhost
      域名 localhost
    • IP address 127.0.0.1
      IP 地址 127.0.0.1
    • IP address ::1
      IP 地址 ::1
    where cluster.name and clustermesh.config.domain are the Helm values set for that cluster, and cilium-namespace is the Kubernetes namespace Cilium was installed in for that cluster.
    其中 cluster.name 和 clustermesh.config.domain 是为该集群设置的 Helm 值，cilium-namespace 是 Cilium 安装到该集群的 Kubernetes 命名空间。
    If not using IPv6, the certificate does not need to be signed for ::1. It’s harmless to include it if IPv6 is not enabled, but omitting it in a dual-stack cluster can lead to difficult-to-debug problems where Cluster Mesh functioning depends on whether IPv4 or IPv6 is used. Therefore, we recommend including it even in certificates intended for IPv4-only clusters, to make any future IPv6 migration easier. See Chapter 4 for more on dual-stack configuration.
    如果不使用 IPv6，证书不需要为 ::1 签名。未启用 IPv6 时包含它不会有问题，但在双栈集群中省略它可能导致难以调试的问题——Cluster Mesh 的运行取决于使用的是 IPv4 还是 IPv6。因此，我们建议即使是面向纯 IPv4 集群的证书也包含它，以便将来更容易迁移到 IPv6。关于双栈配置的更多内容见第 4 章。
    Figure 9-12 illustrates how the Cilium agent and the Cluster Mesh API server authenticate and communicate using the four certificates described in the text (one server certificate and three client certificates).
    图 9-12 展示了 Cilium Agent 和 Cluster Mesh API Server 如何使用文中介绍的四类证书（一个服务器证书和三个客户端证书）来完成身份认证和通信。
    Building a Cluster Mesh deployment with a custom certificate authority is complex and beyond the scope of this book. See the Cilium documentation for the full details.
    使用自定义证书授权来构建 Cluster Mesh 部署是一项复杂的工作，超出了本书的范围。完整细节请参阅 Cilium 文档。

    Summary
    小结

    Most users will never enable Cluster Mesh. It implies both an architecture that requires multiple intracomponent flows and a compelling use case for multicluster Kubernetes. Individual Kubernetes clusters can scale to thousands of nodes and accommodate all but the very largest applications. Multiregion or multicloud mandates are rare outside of regulated industries such as healthcare or finance, and going multicluster for extra redundancy is often overshadowed by easier ways to accomplish the same goal. For some, Cluster Mesh is a feature for contingency planning. You don’t need it now, but you don’t know if you will in the future, and if you do, you don’t want to have to rearchitect your entire application to fix it. With Cilium, you keep the option to use it later open, without needing to change anything else.
    大多数用户永远不会启用 Cluster Mesh。它既意味着一种需要多个组件间流的架构，也意味着多集群 Kubernetes 的紧迫使用场景。单个 Kubernetes 集群可以扩展到数千个节点，能够满足除了最大规模应用之外的几乎所有需求。除了医疗、金融等受监管行业外，多区域或多云要求并不常见，而为了额外冗余而使用多集群，其收益往往被更简单的替代方案所掩盖。对部分用户来说，Cluster Mesh 是一个应急方案特性。你现在不需要它，但不知道将来会不会需要；而如果将来确实需要，你也不想为此必须重新设计整个应用。使用 Cilium，你可以保留将来使用它的选择，而无需做任何其他修改。
    If cluster mesh is on your future roadmap at all, our parting advice is to always pick a unique pod IP range for each cluster, even if you think you may never mesh them. This makes it easy to do so in the future without having to perform an IPAM migration.
    如果集群网格完全在你的未来路线图中，我们的临别建议是：始终为每个集群选择唯一的 Pod IP 范围，即使你认为永远不会建立网格。这样将来需要建立网格时会容易很多，无需进行 IPAM 迁移。
    For those few who do find themselves truly needing multicluster Kubernetes, Cluster Mesh can replace an entire category of load balancers, switches, DNS servers, and complex network infrastructure with a handful of lines of YAML. In some cases, a desired architecture might not be possible any other way. For those users, Cluster Mesh might be Cilium’s greatest feature.
    对于那些确实真正需要多集群 Kubernetes 的少数用户来说，Cluster Mesh 可以用几行 YAML 替代整整一类的负载均衡器、交换机、DNS 服务器和复杂的网络基础设施。在某些情况下，某些期望的架构可能没有其他方式实现。对于这些用户，Cluster Mesh 或许是 Cilium 最强大的特性。

    第 10 章
    Cluster Access
    集群访问

    Accessing applications inside a Kubernetes cluster from the outside world is a common requirement. An external client, whether it’s a user’s laptop, an upstream router, or another application, needs a stable way to reach a service inside the cluster. In Chapter 7, we introduced Cilium’s support for Kubernetes Ingress and Gateway API as mechanisms to load-balance and manipulate traffic once it reaches the cluster. However, we have not yet explained how external clients become aware of the cluster’s external-facing IP address. That is the focus of this chapter. The most straightforward option provided by Kubernetes is a service of type NodePort. With a NodePort service, Kubernetes opens the same port number on every node in the cluster and forwards traffic from that port to the service’s backend pods. This works without additional setup and is often used in simple clusters where clients know the node IPs directly. NodePort introduces two layers of selection. At the first layer, the client chooses which node to contact. At the second layer, the node that receives the traffic forwards it to one of the healthy backend pods, as explained in Chapter 6. The second layer works consistently because Kubernetes tracks pod health and removes failing backends. The first layer is where problems appear. If the client chooses a node that later becomes unavailable, there is no mechanism in Kubernetes that automatically redirects the connection to another node. The client must detect the failure and retry a different node IP. This creates a fragile dependency on client behavior and the surrounding network environment, where firewalls or upstream systems may expect a single stable destination IP rather than a list of node addresses and port numbers. Figure 10-1 illustrates both layers of behavior. On the left, the client selects a node and the cluster forwards the request to a random healthy backend pod. On the right, the chosen node has failed. Although other nodes and pods remain available, the path is broken because the client keeps sending packets to the originally selected node. It’s only when the client realizes the node is down (via an intermediary load balancer tracking the node health, for example) that traffic can be routed to an alternate, healthy node.
    从外部访问 Kubernetes 集群内部的应用是一个常见需求。外部客户端——无论是用户的笔记本电脑、上游路由器还是其他应用——都需要一种稳定的方式来到达集群内的服务。在第 7 章，我们介绍了 Cilium 对 Kubernetes Ingress 和 Gateway API 的支持，作为在流量到达集群后对其进行负载均衡和处理的机制。然而，我们尚未解释外部客户端如何获知集群对外暴露的 IP 地址，这正是本章的重点。Kubernetes 提供的最直接选项是 NodePort 类型的 Service。借助 NodePort Service，Kubernetes 会在集群中的每个节点上打开相同的端口号，并将该端口的流量转发到 Service 的后端 Pod。这种方式无需额外设置即可工作，常用于客户端直接知晓节点 IP 的简单集群。NodePort 引入了两层选择机制。第一层，客户端选择要联系的节点。第二层，接收流量的节点将其转发到某个健康的后端 Pod，如第 6 章所述。第二层能一致工作，是因为 Kubernetes 会跟踪 Pod 的健康状况并移除故障后端。问题出在第一层。如果客户端选择的节点后来不可用了，Kubernetes 中没有机制能自动将连接重定向到另一个节点。客户端必须检测到故障并重试另一个节点 IP。这就造成了对客户端行为和周边网络环境的脆弱依赖——防火墙或上游系统可能期望一个稳定的目的 IP，而不是节点地址和端口号列表。图 10-1 展示了这两层行为。左侧，客户端选择一个节点，集群将请求转发到随机的健康后端 Pod。右侧，被选节点已故障。尽管其他节点和 Pod 仍然可用，路径已经中断，因为客户端一直在向最初选择的节点发送数据包。只有当客户端意识到节点已下线（例如通过跟踪节点健康状况的中间负载均衡器），流量才能被路由到另一个健康节点。
    failure conditions To overcome these issues, Cilium introduces two mechanisms that make external access seamless:
    故障状况 为克服这些问题，Cilium 引入了两种机制，使外部访问变得无缝：
    • L2 Announcements, which make services appear as directly connected IPs on the
      二层通告，使服务在本地网络上表现为直接连接的 IP 地址
    local network
    本地网络
    • BGP, which advertises service IPs into the routing fabric for scalable and resilient
      BGP，将服务 IP 通告到路由网络中，以实现可扩展且有弹性的
    connectivity In this chapter, we will explore both approaches, step through their configuration, and highlight their strengths and trade-offs.
    连通性。本章我们将探索这两种方法，逐步演示它们的配置，并突出其优势与权衡。

    L2 Announcements
    二层通告

    Cilium’s L2 Announcements feature, sometimes called L2 Aware LB, makes Kubernetes services visible and reachable on the local area network. This approach is especially useful in environments that lack BGP support or where running BGP is considered too complex. When this feature is enabled, Cilium responds to ARP queries for external IPs and/or LoadBalancer IPs. These IPs are virtual; they do not belong to any specific network interface. Instead, one node is elected as the announcer for each virtual IP (VIP). That node replies to ARP requests using its own MAC address.1 From the client’s perspective, the VIP looks like a directly attached IP, and traffic is forwarded through the elected node, which then load-balances it to the service’s backend pods. In contrast, NodePort places the burden on the client to select and track node IPs. If the chosen node fails, the IP and port combination becomes unusable. L2 Announcements mitigate this by allowing the VIP to move automatically to another node.
    Cilium 的 二层通告功能，有时也称为 L2 Aware LB，使 Kubernetes Service 在局域网上可见且可达。这种方式在缺少 BGP 支持或运行 BGP 过于复杂的环境中尤为有用。启用此功能后，Cilium 会响应针对外部 IP 和/或 LoadBalancer IP 的 ARP 查询。这些 IP 是虚拟的，不属于任何特定的网络接口。相反，Cilium 会为每个虚拟 IP（VIP）推选一个节点作为通告者，该节点使用自己的 MAC 地址回复 ARP 请求。从客户端的角度看，VIP 看起来像是直接附着的 IP，流量经由被推选的节点转发，该节点再将其负载均衡到服务的后端 Pod。相比之下，NodePort 将负担放在客户端身上——客户端需要自己选择和跟踪节点 IP。如果所选节点故障，IP 与端口的组合将无法使用。二层通告通过允许 VIP 自动迁移到另一节点来缓解这一问题。
    You can find all the YAML manifests used in this section in the chapter10/l2 directory of the book’s GitHub repository.
    本节使用的所有 YAML 清单都可以在本书 GitHub 仓库的 chapter10/l2 目录中找到。

    Setting Up the Environment
    搭建环境

    To test L2 Announcements, we will create a kind cluster without kube-proxy. The only requirement for this feature is KPR, which we introduced in Chapter 6. Create the cluster (kind-cluster-config.yaml) and install Cilium using Helm with values defined in cilium-l2-values.yaml:
    为了测试二层通告，我们将创建一个不包含 kube-proxy 的 kind 集群。此功能唯一的要求是 KPR，我们在第 6 章介绍过。创建集群（kind-cluster-config.yaml）并使用 Helm 安装 Cilium，使用 cilium-l2-values.yaml 中定义的值：
    代码（原文）
    kubeProxyReplacement: true
k8sServiceHost: kind-control-plane
k8sServicePort: 6443
l2announcements:
enabled: true
    Next, start a client container on the same Docker network so that it can send traffic directly to the cluster:
    接下来，在同一个 Docker 网络上启动一个客户端容器，使其能直接向集群发送流量：
    代码（原文）
    $ docker run -d --network kind --rm --name client \
nicolaka/netshoot:v0.8 sleep infinity
    1This book is based on Cilium 1.18. IPv6 support for L2 Announcements was added in Cilium 1.19. IPv6 relies on the Neighbor Discovery (ND) protocol rather than ARP.
    1本书基于 Cilium 1.18。二层通告的 IPv6 支持在 Cilium 1.19 中添加，IPv6 依赖邻居发现（ND）协议而非 ARP。

    Configuring L2 Announcements
    配置二层通告

    Before creating the service, verify that all cluster nodes and the client are on the same network. In this example, the network is 172.18.0.0/16. From within the client container, run the following command to display its IP configuration:
    创建 Service 之前，确认所有集群节点和客户端都在同一个网络上。在本例中，网络为 172.18.0.0/16。在客户端容器内运行以下命令，显示其 IP 配置：
    代码（原文）
    $ docker exec -it client ip -4 a show eth0
11: eth0@if34: <BROADCAST,MULTICAST,UP,LOWER_UP> mtu 65535 qdisc
noqueue state UP group default link-netnsid 0
inet 172.18.0.5/16 brd 172.18.255.255 scope global eth0
valid_lft forever preferred_lft forever
    It’s important that the service IP you assign comes from the same network range, so the client can reach it directly. In the next step we will create a CiliumLoadBalancerIPPool that allocates addresses from the block 172.18.255.200/29 (we covered LB IPAM pools in Chapter 6). This block lies within the client’s 172.18.0.0/16 network, ensuring that traffic is delivered without requiring any routing changes.
    重要的是，你分配的 Service IP 必须来自同一网络范围，这样客户端才能直接到达。下一步我们将创建一个 CiliumLoadBalancerIPPool，从地址块 172.18.255.200/29 分配地址（我们在第 6 章介绍过 LB IPAM 池）。该地址块位于客户端的 172.18.0.0/16 网络范围内，确保流量交付无需任何路由变更。
    You can assign a VIP from another subnet, but the client’s routing table must be configured accordingly. Otherwise, the client assumes the IP is outside the local network and sends the traffic to the default gateway instead.
    你可以从其他子网分配 VIP，但客户端的路由表必须相应配置。否则，客户端会认为该 IP 位于本地网络之外，将流量发送到默认网关。
    Deploy the IP pool (pool.yaml) and the simple httpd application with its associated LoadBalancer service (app.yaml). The service is labeled announcement: l2 so it matches the L2 Announcement policy we will deploy next:
    部署 IP 池（pool.yaml）以及简单的 httpd 应用及其关联的 LoadBalancer Service（app.yaml）。该 Service 带有 announcement: l2 标签，以便匹配我们接下来要部署的二层通告策略：
    代码（原文）
    apiVersion: apps/v1
kind: Deployment
metadata:
name: httpd
spec:
replicas: 2
selector:
matchLabels:
app.kubernetes.io/name: httpd
template:
metadata:
labels:
app.kubernetes.io/name: httpd
spec:
containers:
- name: httpd
image: httpd:2.4
ports:
- containerPort: 80
---
apiVersion: v1
kind: Service
metadata:
name: httpd
labels:
announcement: l2
spec:
type: LoadBalancer
selector:
app.kubernetes.io/name: httpd
ports:
- port: 80
    Check the service to confirm that it has been assigned an IP from the pool:
    检查 Service，确认它已从池中分配到 IP：
    代码（原文）
    $ kubectl get svc httpd
NAME    TYPE           CLUSTER-IP    EXTERNAL-IP      PORT(S)        AGE
httpd   LoadBalancer   10.96.84.34   172.18.255.200   80:32179/TCP   20m
    At this point, the service has an external IP, but clients still cannot reach it. No node is yet responding to ARP queries for that address. In the next step, we will apply the L2 Announcement policy to make the service accessible.
    此时，Service 已有外部 IP，但客户端仍无法到达它——还没有节点在响应该地址的 ARP 查询。下一步，我们将应用二层通告策略，使该 Service 可被访问。

    Applying the L2 Announcement Policy
    应用二层通告策略

    To make the service reachable, we need to tell Cilium which services to announce and on which nodes. This is done with a CiliumL2AnnouncementPolicy. Apply the manifest (l2policy.yaml):
    为使 Service 可达，我们需要告诉 Cilium 要通告哪些 Service，以及在哪些节点上通告。这通过 CiliumL2AnnouncementPolicy 完成。应用清单（l2policy.yaml）：
    代码（原文）
    apiVersion: "cilium.io/v2alpha1"
kind: CiliumL2AnnouncementPolicy
metadata:
name: l2-policy
spec:
serviceSelector:
matchLabels:
announcement: l2
nodeSelector:
matchExpressions:
- key: node-role.kubernetes.io/control-plane
operator: DoesNotExist
interfaces:
- ^eth0
loadBalancerIPs: true
    Selects services that carry the label announcement: l2 (such as the httpd service we just created)
    选择带有 announcement: l2 标签的 Service（例如我们刚创建的 httpd Service）
    Excludes nodes with the control-plane role, so only worker nodes can become announcers2 Restricts announcements to the eth0 interface (by default, Cilium announces services on all node interfaces)3 Enables announcements for LoadBalancer IPs
    排除具有控制平面角色的节点，仅允许工作节点成为通告者2；将通告限制到 eth0 接口（默认情况下 Cilium 在所有节点接口上通告 Service）3；启用对 LoadBalancer IP 的通告。
    This interface selector is not a security feature. Services may still be reachable through unadvertised interfaces, for example if ARP entries are manually configured.
    此接口选择器并非安全功能。Service 仍可能通过未通告的接口访问，例如手动配置 ARP 条目的情况。
    Once the policy is applied, Cilium will elect one of the worker nodes as the announcer for the service external IP. You can identify the current leader (in this case, kind-worker2) by inspecting the Kubernetes leases:
    策略应用后，Cilium 将从工作节点中选举一个作为 Service 外部 IP 的通告者。你可以通过检查 Kubernetes Lease 来识别当前的领导者（本例中为 kind-worker2）：
    代码（原文）
    $ kubectl -n kube-system get lease | grep l2announce
cilium-l2announce-default-httpd        kind-worker2

    Inspecting ARP Traffic
    检查 ARP 流量

    Now that the service has been assigned an external IP and an announcer node has been elected, let’s inspect what happens on the wire when the client tries to reach it. Our objective is to capture the ARP request sent by the client for the service IP (172.18.255.200 in this example), to observe how the elected node responds with its MAC address and to show the TCP handshake and the first HTTP response. First, check the MAC address on the kind-worker2 node:
    既然 Service 已分配了外部 IP，且通告者节点已选举完成，我们来观察客户端尝试访问时线上发生了什么。我们的目标是捕获客户端发送的针对 Service IP（本例为 172.18.255.200）的 ARP 请求，观察被选节点如何用其 MAC 地址响应，并展示 TCP 握手和首个 HTTP 响应。首先，检查 kind-worker2 节点上的 MAC 地址：
    代码（原文）
    $ docker exec -it kind-worker2 ip link show dev eth0
11: eth0@if32: <BROADCAST,MULTICAST,UP,LOWER_UP> mtu 65535 qdisc
noqueue state UP mode DEFAULT group default
link/ether ea:f5:fb:51:a1:17 brd ff:ff:ff:ff:ff:ff link-netnsid 0
    2By default, Cilium considers all nodes to be potential announcers, but in most clusters the control-plane nodes are reserved for system components and are not used for forwarding application traffic. Restricting announcements to workers avoids putting datapath responsibilities on the control plane. 3Selectors in Cilium CRDs support RE2-compatible regular expressions. RE2 is a regular expression engine implemented by Google that is considered a fast, safe, thread-friendly alternative to backtracking regular expression engines.
    2默认情况下，Cilium 将所有节点视为潜在的通告者，但在大多数集群中，控制平面节点被预留用于系统组件，不用于转发应用流量。将通告限制到工作节点可避免将数据路径职责放到控制平面上。3Cilium CRD 中的选择器支持 RE2 兼容的正则表达式。RE2 是 Google 实现的正则表达式引擎，被认为是传统回溯正则表达式引擎的快速、安全、线程友好的替代方案。
    Next, open two terminal windows side by side:
    接下来，并排打开两个终端窗口：
    • In the first window, run tcpdump inside the client container to capture ARP
      在第一个窗口中，在客户端容器内运行 tcpdump 以捕获 ARP
    traffic.
    流量。
    • In the second window, generate traffic from the client to the service IP.
      在第二个窗口中，从客户端向 Service IP 发送流量。
    In the first terminal, first check the ARP cache. There should be no entry for the service IP as the client has yet to try to access it and has not made an ARP request yet:
    在第一个终端中，先检查 ARP 缓存。此时应该没有 Service IP 的条目，因为客户端尚未尝试访问它，也还没有发送 ARP 请求：
    代码（原文）
    $ docker exec -it client ip neigh
    Start tcpdump to observe ARP and HTTP traffic:
    启动 tcpdump 以观察 ARP 和 HTTP 流量：
    代码（原文）
    $ docker exec -it client tcpdump -i eth0 -n
    In the second terminal, access the service IP. The request triggers ARP resolution and completes successfully:
    在第二个终端中，访问 Service IP。该请求触发 ARP 解析并成功完成：
    代码（原文）
    $ docker exec -it client curl http://172.18.255.200
<html><body><h1>It works!</h1></body></html>
    In the tcpdump output, you will see a broadcast ARP request from the client asking “who-has >172.18.255.200.” Immediately after, the elected node responds with its own MAC address:
    在 tcpdump 输出中，你将看到来自客户端的广播 ARP 请求，询问"who-has 172.18.255.200"。紧接着，被选节点用其自身的 MAC 地址响应：
    代码（原文）
    ARP, Request who-has 172.18.255.200 tell 172.18.0.5, length 28
ARP, Reply 172.18.255.200 is-at ea:f5:fb:51:a1:17, length 28
    Figure 10-2 shows both sides of the ARP process. The client sends a broadcast ARP request for the service IP, which is received by all nodes. The elected announcer node responds with an ARP reply containing its MAC address. Once the client learns this MAC address, it can send traffic directly to the node that currently holds the announcement lease.
    图 10-2 展示了 ARP 过程的两个方向。客户端发送针对 Service IP 的广播 ARP 请求，该请求被所有节点接收。被推选的通告者节点回复一条包含其 MAC 地址的 ARP 应答。客户端获知该 MAC 地址后，即可直接向当前持有通告租约的节点发送流量。
    That ARP information is now saved in the client’s ARP table:
    该 ARP 信息现在已保存在客户端的 ARP 表中：
    代码（原文）
    $ docker exec -it client ip neigh
172.18.255.200 dev eth0 lladdr ea:f5:fb:51:a1:17 REACHABLE
    Immediately after this, the client establishes a TCP connection. The complete tcpdump output will look like this:
    紧接着，客户端建立 TCP 连接。完整的 tcpdump 输出如下所示：
    代码（原文）
    ARP, Request who-has 172.18.255.200 tell 172.18.0.5, length 28
ARP, Reply 172.18.255.200 is-at ea:f5:fb:51:a1:17, length 28
IP 172.18.0.5.45300 > 172.18.255.200.80: Flags [S], ...
ARP, Request who-has 172.18.0.5 tell 172.18.0.3, length 28
ARP, Reply 172.18.0.5 is-at 76:0f:e0:c7:27:fe, length 28
IP 172.18.255.200.80 > 172.18.0.5.45300: Flags [S.], ...
IP 172.18.0.5.45300 > 172.18.255.200.80: Flags [.], ...
IP 172.18.0.5.45300 > 172.18.255.200.80: Flags [P.], ... HTTP GET
IP 172.18.255.200.80 > 172.18.0.5.45300: Flags [.], ...
IP 172.18.255.200.80 > 172.18.0.5.45300: Flags [P.], ... HTTP 200 OK
IP 172.18.0.5.45300 > 172.18.255.200.80: Flags [.], ...
IP 172.18.0.5.45300 > 172.18.255.200.80: Flags [F.], ...
IP 172.18.255.200.80 > 172.18.0.5.45300: Flags [F.], ...
IP 172.18.0.5.45300 > 172.18.255.200.80: Flags [.], ...
    Client asks “who-has 172.18.255.200” (ARP request) Elected announcer node replies with its MAC address (ARP reply) Client sends SYN to initiate connection ([S])
    客户端询问"who-has 172.18.255.200"（ARP 请求）；被选通告者节点用其 MAC 地址回复（ARP 应答）；客户端发送 SYN 发起连接（[S]）。
    Announcer node asks “who-has 172.18.0.5” (ARP request) Client replies with its MAC address (ARP reply) Announcer node responds to SYN with SYN-ACK ([S.]) Client sends ACK, completing the three-way TCP handshake ([.]) Client sends HTTP request (GET) as a PSH+ACK ([P.]) Server acknowledges the request ([.]) One of the httpd pods responds with HTTP 200 OK ([P.]) Client acknowledges the HTTP response ([.]) Client sends FIN to close the connection ([F.]) Server responds with its own FIN ([F.]) Client acknowledges, completing the TCP termination ([.]) Note that the announcer node performs its own ARP lookup for the client’s IP (172.18.0.5) and learns its MAC address. Once ARP resolution has completed, the elected node receives all traffic for the service IP, forwards each request to one of the httpd pods, and returns the pod’s response to the client.
    通告者节点询问"who-has 172.18.0.5"（ARP 请求）；客户端用其 MAC 地址回复（ARP 应答）；通告者节点用 SYN-ACK 响应 SYN（[S.]）；客户端发送 ACK，完成 TCP 三次握手（[.]）；客户端以 PSH+ACK 发送 HTTP 请求 GET（[P.]）；服务器确认收到请求（[.]）；某个 httpd Pod 用 HTTP 200 OK 响应（[P.]）；客户端确认收到 HTTP 响应（[.]）；客户端发送 FIN 关闭连接（[F.]）；服务器用自己的 FIN 响应（[F.]）；客户端确认，完成 TCP 终止（[.]）。注意，通告者节点也会自行对客户端的 IP（172.18.0.5）执行 ARP 查找，并获知其 MAC 地址。ARP 解析完成后，被选节点接收所有发往 Service IP 的流量，将每个请求转发到某个 httpd Pod，并将 Pod 的响应返回给客户端。

    Simulating a Node Failure
    模拟节点故障

    With the service now reachable through the elected announcer node, we can explore what happens when the node becomes unavailable. The objective of this exercise is to show how the lease is transferred to another node and how connectivity is restored, but it will also reveal some of the inner limitations of L2 Announcement. Let’s simulate a node failure by stopping the elected announcer node:
    既然 Service 已可通过被推选的通告者节点访问，我们可以探索该节点不可用时会发生什么。本练习的目标是展示租约如何转移到另一个节点、连通性如何恢复，同时也会揭示二层通告的一些内在局限。我们通过停止被推选的通告者节点来模拟节点故障：
    代码（原文）
    $ docker stop kind-worker2
    At this point, the client’s ARP cache still maps the service IP (172.18.255.200) to the MAC address of the stopped node, so until the cache expires, packets are sent into a black hole:
    此时，客户端的 ARP 缓存仍将 Service IP（172.18.255.200）映射到已停止节点的 MAC 地址，因此在缓存过期之前，数据包会被发送到黑洞中：
    代码（原文）
    $ docker exec -it client curl -m 5 172.18.255.200
curl: (28) Connection timed out after 5001 milliseconds
    Meanwhile, Cilium’s leader election process continues in the background. Nodes that participate in announcements monitor the lease, and if the current holder does not renew it within the configured lease duration, they request ownership from the API server. The first request that succeeds becomes the new leader and begins advertising the service IP. When a node takes over, it sends a gratuitous ARP4 so that clients can update their neighbor tables without waiting for their ARP entries to expire. If the client accepts the gratuitous ARP, failover happens almost immediately because the new leader’s announcement updates the client’s neighbor table at once. However, many clients and network devices ignore these messages because they can be used for ARP spoofing. Clients that ignore the gratuitous ARP will continue using the stale ARP entry until it expires. When that happens, the client sends a fresh ARP request, learns the MAC address of the new leader, and connectivity is restored:
    与此同时，Cilium 的领导者选举过程在后台持续运行。参与通告的节点会监控租约，如果当前持有者在配置的租约期限内未续租，它们会向 API 服务器请求所有权。第一个成功的请求者成为新的领导者并开始通告 Service IP。节点接管时，它会发送免费 ARP（gARP）4，以便客户端无需等待 ARP 条目过期就能更新其邻居表。如果客户端接受了免费 ARP，故障切换几乎立即发生，因为新领导者的通告会立刻更新客户端的邻居表。然而，许多客户端和网络设备会忽略这些消息，因为它们可能被用于 ARP 欺骗。忽略免费 ARP 的客户端将继续使用过期的 ARP 条目，直到其过期。届时，客户端发送一条新的 ARP 请求，获知新领导者的 MAC 地址，连通性恢复：
    代码（原文）
    $ docker exec -it client curl -m 5 172.18.255.200
<html><body><h1>It works!</h1></body></html>
    You can verify the lease has been transferred to kind-worker with this command:
    你可以用此命令确认租约已转移到 kind-worker：
    代码（原文）
    $ kubectl -n kube-system get lease | grep l2announce
cilium-l2announce-default-httpd        kind-worker
    It is possible to use L2 Announcements for pod IPs rather than service IPs. This can be enabled by setting the Helm value l2podAnnouncements.enabled=true. However, this is generally discouraged since pods are ephemeral and advertising pod IPs directly bypasses the service abstraction and its load-balancing behavior.
    也可以对 Pod IP 而非 Service IP 使用二层通告。将 Helm 值 l2podAnnouncements.enabled=true 设置为 true 即可启用。不过，通常不建议这么做，因为 Pod 是短命的，直接通告 Pod IP 会绕过 Service 抽象及其负载均衡行为。
    4A gratuitous ARP (gARP) is an ARP reply sent without an initial request, used to refresh ARP tables on other devices.
    4免费 ARP（gARP）是一种未经请求就发送的 ARP 应答，用于刷新其他设备上的 ARP 表。
    As this discussion has illustrated, while Cilium’s L2 Announcements feature is useful in simpler environments, it has some notable limitations:
    如讨论所示，虽然 Cilium 的二层通告功能在较简单的环境中很有用，但它存在一些显著局限：
    • Only one node handles traffic for each VIP, so there is no load balancing between
      每个 VIP 仅由一个节点处理流量，因此节点之间没有
    nodes.
    负载均衡。
    • Failover depends on ARP cache expiry, which can be slow.
      故障切换依赖 ARP 缓存过期，可能较慢。
    • Faster failover relies on gratuitous ARP, but many clients ignore these messages
      更快的故障切换依赖免费 ARP，但出于安全考虑，许多客户端会忽略这些
    for security reasons. Because of these drawbacks, this feature is better suited for development or constrained environments than for production systems where failover speed and security are critical. For those systems, it’s generally preferable to use the other mechanism Cilium provides for exposing services to external clients: BGP.
    消息。由于这些缺点，此功能更适合开发或受限环境，而非故障切换速度和安全性至关重要的生产系统。对于这类系统，通常更适合使用 Cilium 提供的另一种对外暴露服务的机制：BGP。

    BGP
    BGP

    Cilium’s BGP functionality provides a more flexible and robust alternative to L2 Announcements. Rather than depending on ARP, BGP enables routing of service IPs at layer 3, making it suitable for larger or more complex environments—especially those already running routing protocols and infrastructure. The BGP functionality we cover in this book is referred to as Cilium BGP Control Plane. BGP is particularly useful in production environments where high availability, fast failover, and load balancing across nodes are important requirements. It does introduce operational overhead because it must be configured and maintained, and it requires BGP-capable devices or software. However, BGP is widely used across datacenter networks, so many environments already provide the necessary routing infrastructure. Figure 10-3 illustrates how Cilium BGP Control Plane functions. With BGP enabled, Cilium selects which services to advertise using label selectors. It determines which nodes should act as BGP speakers, and then initiates route announcements to configured peers. The BGP peers learn the routes and may redistribute them further into the network. External clients can then reach these services using standard IP routing.
    Cilium 的 BGP 功能为二层通告提供了更灵活、更健壮的替代方案。BGP 不依赖 ARP，而是在第 3 层路由 Service IP，使其适合更大或更复杂的环境——尤其是已在运行路由协议和基础设施的环境。本书中介绍的 BGP 功能称为 Cilium BGP Control Plane。BGP 在生产环境中尤其有用，那里高可用性、快速故障切换和跨节点负载均衡是重要需求。它确实带来了运维开销，因为需要配置和维护，并且要求具备 BGP 能力的设备或软件。不过，BGP 在数据中心网络中被广泛使用，许多环境已经提供了必要的路由基础设施。图 10-3 展示了 Cilium BGP Control Plane 的工作方式。启用 BGP 后，Cilium 使用标签选择器选择要通告的 Service。它确定哪些节点应作为 BGP 发言者，然后向配置的对等体发起路由通告。BGP 对等体获知路由后，可以进一步将其重新分发到网络中。外部客户端随后即可使用标准 IP 路由到达这些 Service。
    You can find all the YAML manifests used in this section in the chapter10/l3 directory of the book’s GitHub repository.
    本节使用的所有 YAML 清单都可以在本书 GitHub 仓库的 chapter10/l3 目录中找到。

    Setting Up the Environment
    搭建环境

    Start by deploying your cluster using the same kind-cluster-config.yaml we used previously. Next, install Cilium using Helm with the values defined in cilium-bgpvalues.yaml. bgpControlPlane.enabled=true enables BGP in Cilium. BGP peerings and Autonomous System Numbers (ASN) configurations are then defined via custom resources. To see the Cilium BGP feature in action, we need a BGP-capable device to peer with. For this example, we’ll deploy a Free Range Routing (FRR) instance outside of the cluster but connected to the same Docker network. FRR is an open source routing suite supporting BGP, Open Shortest Path First (OSPF), and other protocols on Linux and other UNIX-like systems. The configuration for FRR in this section is available in the chapter10/l3/frr directory of the book’s GitHub repository. It contains three files:
    首先使用之前相同的 kind-cluster-config.yaml 部署集群。然后使用 Helm 安装 Cilium，使用 cilium-bgpvalues.yaml 中定义的值。bgpControlPlane.enabled=true 启用 Cilium 中的 BGP。BGP 对等关系和自治系统号（ASN）配置随后通过自定义资源定义。为了看到 Cilium BGP 功能的实际运行，我们需要一个具备 BGP 能力的设备来建立对等关系。在本例中，我们将在集群外部但连接到同一 Docker 网络的位置部署一个 Free Range Routing（FRR）实例。FRR 是一个开源路由套件，在 Linux 和其他类 UNIX 系统上支持 BGP、开放最短路径优先（OSPF）及其他协议。本节中 FRR 的配置文件可在本书 GitHub 仓库的 chapter10/l3/frr 目录中找到，包含三个文件：
    • frr.conf.template is the template configuration file.
      frr.conf.template 是模板配置文件。
    • daemons enables the BGP daemon (bgpd=yes).
      daemons 启用 BGP 守护进程（bgpd=yes）。
    • vtysh.conf contains minimal configuration for the vtysh CLI.
      vtysh.conf 包含 vtysh CLI 的最小配置。
    You only need to generate frr.conf from the template. First, capture the IP addresses of the worker nodes:
    你只需从模板生成 frr.conf。首先，捕获工作节点的 IP 地址：
    代码（原文）
    $ kubectl get nodes -o wide
NAME                 STATUS   ROLES           INTERNAL-IP
kind-worker          Ready    <none>          172.18.0.2
kind-worker2         Ready    <none>          172.18.0.3
$ WORKER_IP=172.18.0.2
$ WORKER2_IP=172.18.0.3
    Next, generate an FRR configuration file from the provided template and use the UNIX utility sed to modify the template values with the IP addresses of the worker nodes:
    接下来，从提供的模板生成 FRR 配置文件，并使用 UNIX 工具 sed 用工作节点的 IP 地址修改模板值：
    代码（原文）
    $ sed -e "s/NEIGHBOR1/${WORKER_IP}/" \
-e "s/NEIGHBOR2/${WORKER2_IP}/" \
frr.conf.template | tee frr.conf
frr version 10.3.1
frr defaults traditional
hostname frr
log syslog
