    代码（原文）
    spec:
egress:
- {}
    This policy has no ingress rules and a single egress rule that is blank with no statements. Because there is a rule at all, Cilium enforces the default deny behavior on egress, but this rule does not match any traffic, so all egress will be blocked. Applying webserver-no-egress.yaml and testing again from our debug ephemeral container in the NGINX pod, we can see that outbound traffic is blocked:
    这个策略没有入站规则，只有一个空白的出站规则，没有任何语句。因为存在一条规则，Cilium 对出站流量强制执行默认拒绝行为，但这条规则不匹配任何流量，所以所有出站流量都会被阻断。应用 webserver-no-egress.yaml 后，再次从 NGINX Pod 中的调试临时容器测试，我们可以看到出站流量被阻断了：
    代码（原文）
    webserver-ff7cc8f4c-4wvtw:~# curl -m1 https://cilium.io
curl: (28) Resolving timed out after 1002 milliseconds
    An important observation, however, is that this restriction does not apply to responses to incoming traffic that is permitted. If we launch a test pod like before, we can still get a response to our HTTP request even though all egress traffic is blocked:
    然而，一个重要的发现是，这个限制 不适用于 对已被允许的入站流量的响应。如果我们像之前一样启动一个测试 Pod，即使所有出站流量都被阻断，我们仍然能获得 HTTP 请求的响应：
    代码（原文）
    test:~# curl -s webserver
Hello from Cilium Up and Running!
    Note also that we could have added this rule to the existing policy instead of creating a new one—it makes no difference to the policy engine if rules are located together in a single policy or split across several different ones.2
    还要注意，我们本可以将这条规则添加到现有策略中，而不是创建一个新的——规则是集中在单个策略中还是分散在多个不同策略中，对策略引擎来说没有区别。2

    Identity
    身份

    Policy based on ports has uses for blanket restrictions, but it’s only part of the story. The core of any network security solution is identity. To apply rules that allow or block certain traffic, you must know the identities of the endpoints that are communicating.
    基于端口的策略适用于广泛的限制，但这只是故事的一部分。任何网络安全解决方案的核心都是 身份。要应用允许或阻断特定流量的规则，你必须知道正在通信的端点的身份。

    The Foundations of Security
    安全基础

    Broadly speaking, solutions determine identity either at layer 3, based on IP addresses, or at layer 7, based on authentication data contained in the traffic itself. Layer 3 security doesn’t place restrictions on the type of traffic, which could be TCP or UDP. It usually requires little to no coordination with the applications themselves and is often implemented in extremely efficient network appliances. With the exception of “stateful” layer 4 firewalls,3 layer 3 enforcement is typically entirely stateless, so load can be shared across multiple appliances without requiring state coordination. However, this approach relies on ascribing meaning to an IP address.
    广义上讲，解决方案要么在 L3 层基于 IP 地址确定身份，要么在 L7 层基于流量本身携带的认证数据确定身份。L3 安全不限制流量类型，可以是 TCP 或 UDP。它通常几乎不需要与应用本身协调，并且经常在极其高效的网络设备中实现。除了"有状态"的 L4 防火墙之外，3 L3 强制执行通常完全是无状态的，因此负载可以在多个设备之间共享，而不需要状态协调。然而，这种方法依赖于为 IP 地址赋予语义。
    2That said, as we’ll see in “Combining Statements” on page 261, if any rule in a policy is invalid, the whole policy is not applied. For this reason, it can sometimes be preferable to break them up. 3Layer 4 firewalls track expected responses to UDP traffic or allow TCP responses on the same port, but identity is still derived from IP address.
    2 话虽如此，正如我们将在第 261 页"组合语句"中看到的，如果策略中的任何规则是 无效的，整个策略就不会被应用。出于这个原因，有时将它们拆分会更好。 3 L4 防火墙会跟踪 UDP 流量的预期响应或允许同一端口上的 TCP 响应，但身份仍然是从 IP 地址推导得出的。
    Sysadmins of eld may have assigned specific IP addresses or ranges to different types of physical or virtual machines by their function. For example, assigning the subnet 198.51.100.0/24 to application servers means that a firewall can enforce the rule “only application servers may access the database servers” by allowing only TCP traffic on port 5432 from 198.51.100.0/24.4 This assignment is brittle. If done by convention, a future misconfiguration may invalidate security assumptions. For instance, if an entire /24 subnet of 255 addresses is used for five application servers, a future administrator might shrink the CIDR range for the servers to consume only 198.51.100.104/29 and reallocate the other addresses to other functions. But if the firewalls are not updated, these other servers will have unintended database access. Even if the allocations and any changes are adequately documented, this approach makes policy hard to read and requires referencing a separate IP address mapping document. It also requires that devices cannot spoof IP addresses on the network fabric, for example by issuing gratuitous ARP (IPv4) or ND (IPv6) packets to confuse the network into incorrectly assigning IP addresses to unexpected devices.5 Layer 7 security mechanisms, such as bearer tokens or mutual TLS (mTLS) in HTTP, embed identity in the application protocol. This approach can make use of advanced cryptography such as SPIFFE identity documents, but it is dependent on the protocol supporting it and must be implemented in the application itself or reverse proxies.
    早年的系统管理员可能会根据功能为不同类型的物理或虚拟机分配特定的 IP 地址或地址范围。例如，将子网 198.51.100.0/24 分配给应用服务器意味着防火墙可以通过只允许来自 198.51.100.0/24 的 TCP 流量访问端口 5432 来强制执行"只有应用服务器可以访问数据库服务器"这一规则。4 这种分配方式很脆弱。如果按惯例执行，未来的错误配置可能会使安全假设失效。例如，如果一个包含 255 个地址的整个 /24 子网仅用于五个应用服务器，未来的管理员可能会将服务器的 CIDR 范围缩减为只使用 198.51.100.104/29，并将其他地址重新分配给其他用途。但如果防火墙没有更新，这些其他服务器就会意外地获得数据库访问权限。即使分配和任何变更都有充分的文档记录，这种方法也使策略难以阅读，需要参考单独的 IP 地址映射文档。它还要求设备不能在网络结构上欺骗 IP 地址，例如通过发送免费的 ARP（IPv4）或 ND（IPv6）数据包，误导网络将 IP 地址错误分配给非预期的设备。5 L7 安全机制（如 HTTP 中的持有者令牌或双向 TLS [mTLS]）将身份嵌入应用协议中。这种方法可以利用 SPIFFE 身份文档等高级密码学，但它依赖于协议的支持，必须在应用本身或反向代理中实现。

    Identity in Cilium
    Cilium 中的身份

    Cilium’s policy engine ascribes identity at layer 3 based on the source or destination IP address of the packet, but it uses Kubernetes’s knowledge of the entire cluster to accurately and automatically map in-cluster IP addresses to concrete identities. Because Cilium is a CNI, it must be consulted before any pods can be started or stopped. This means a Cilium agent always has an up-to-date view of which IPs are used for which identities. Cilium doesn’t maintain a direct mapping of IP addresses to policy decisions. Such a mapping, even if updated automatically, would need to change every time a pod launched or was terminated. Instead, Cilium has a layer of indirection called a Ciliumidentity. Each pod has exactly one Cilium identity, and every IP address that the Cilium datapath might encounter, be it internal or external, maps to exactly one identity. This mapping is stored in a per-node eBPF map called the IP cache. An example of an IP cache map is shown in Table 12-1. In a dual-stack cluster (see Chapter 4) the same map contains both IPv4 and IPv6 entries. You can consult the IP
    Cilium 的策略引擎根据数据包的源 IP 或目的 IP 地址在三层赋予身份，但它利用 Kubernetes 对整个集群的认知，将集群内 IP 地址准确、自动地映射到具体身份。因为 Cilium 是 CNI，任何 Pod 启动或停止前都必须经过它。这意味着 Cilium Agent 始终掌握最新视图，知道哪些 IP 正用于哪些身份。Cilium 并不维护 IP 地址到策略决策的直接映射。这样的映射即使能够自动更新，也需要在每次 Pod 启动或终止时随之改变。Cilium 采用了一层间接机制，称为 Ciliumidentity。每个 Pod 恰好拥有一个 Cilium 身份，而 Cilium 数据路径可能遇到的每个 IP 地址（无论是内部还是外部）也都恰好映射到一个身份。这个映射存储在每个节点上一个名为 IP cache 的 eBPF map 中。IP cache map 的一个示例见表 12-1。在双栈集群中（见第 4 章），同一个 map 同时包含 IPv4 和 IPv6 条目。你可以在 Agent 容器内查询节点上的 IP
    4This is the default port used by the popular open source database PostgreSQL. 5Enterprise-grade switches often have protections for this.
    4 这是流行的开源数据库 PostgreSQL 使用的默认端口。 5 企业级交换机通常对此有保护措施。
    cache on your node with the command cilium-dbg bpf ipcache list from within the agent’s container. Table 12-1. An example IP cache map
    缓存，使用的命令是 cilium-dbg bpf ipcache list。Table 12-1. An example IP cache map

    IP rangeIdentityExplanation IP 范围　身份　说明

    192.0.2.33/3230525A pod’s IPv4 address 192.0.2.33/32　30525　Pod 的 IPv4 地址

    0.0.0.0/02A “catch-all” IPv4 rule for the world identity 0.0.0.0/0　2　用于 world 身份的“捕获全部”IPv4 规则

    ::/010A “catch-all” IPv6 rule for the world identity ::/0　10　用于 world 身份的“捕获全部”IPv6 规则

    233.252.0.17/321The host’s IPv4 address 233.252.0.17/32　1　主机的 IPv4 地址

    203.0.113.0/2416777218A defined IPv4 CIDR range from a policy 203.0.113.0/24　16777218　策略中定义的 IPv4 CIDR 范围

    2001:db8:aa::e8/1281The host’s IPv6 address 2001:db8:aa::e8/128　1　主机的 IPv6 地址

    2001:db8::34c9/12830525A pod’s IPv6 address
    2001:db8::34c9/128　30525　Pod 的 IPv6 地址

    2001:db8:ffff::/4816777219A defined IPv6 CIDR range from a policy 2001:db8:ffff::/48　16777219　策略中定义的 IPv6 CIDR 范围

    Each entry associates a CIDR with a single identity. The map operates with longest prefix match (LPM) semantics: if multiple entries match a single IP, the one with the longest prefix—the “most specific” one—is used. For example, 192.0.2.33 matches both 192.0.2.33/32 and 0.0.0.0/0, but the former is more specific, so it’s the one that is chosen. For individual resources such as pods, we use a /32 (IPv4) or /128 (IPv6) CIDR to select only a single IP, but wider ranges can be used when implementing CIDR network policies (see “Beyond the Cluster” on page 263). Notice also the /0 entries used for the “catch-all” world identity; we’ll talk more about that later in the chapter.
    每个条目将一个 CIDR 与一个身份关联。该映射采用最长前缀匹配（LPM）语义：如果多个条目匹配同一个 IP，会使用具有最长前缀的那个——即"最具体"的那个。例如，192.0.2.33 同时匹配 192.0.2.33/32 和 0.0.0.0/0，但前者更具体，所以会选用前者。对于 Pod 等单个资源，我们使用 /32（IPv4）或 /128（IPv6）CIDR 来只选择单个 IP，但在实施 CIDR 网络策略时可以使用更宽的范围（见第 263 页"超越集群"）。还要注意用于"捕获全部" world 身份的 /0 条目；我们将在本章稍后更多讨论它。
    Cilium doesn’t iterate over all of the IP cache entries to find the most specific one for every packet. Instead, the IP cache is implemented as an LPM trie,6 a type of data structure called a radix tree. With this implementation, the time it takes to find the match is proportional to the length of an IP address, which is fixed, and not the number of entries in the map. For more information, see Chapter 12 of Thomas Cormen et al.’s Introduction to Algorithms, 3rd edition (MIT Press).
    Cilium 不会为每个数据包遍历所有 IP 缓存条目来找到最具体的一个。相反，IP 缓存被实现为一个 LPM 前缀树（trie），6 一种称为 基数树 的数据结构。通过这种实现，查找匹配所需的时间与 IP 地址的长度成正比（长度是固定的），而不是映射中的条目数量。更多信息请参阅 Thomas Cormen 等人著、MIT Press 出版的《算法导论》第 3 版的第 12 章。
    Note also that if Cilium is already aware of a source’s identity via other means, it does not need to consult the IP cache. When applying ingress policy to traffic from a pod on the same node, for example, no lookup is necessary. And as we discussed in Chapter 5, when Cilium is running in encapsulation mode, the source identity
    还要注意，如果 Cilium 已经通过其他方式知道了源的身份，它就不需要查询 IP 缓存。例如，在对来自同一节点上 Pod 的流量应用入站策略时，就不需要查找。而且正如我们在第 5 章讨论过的，当 Cilium 运行在封装模式下时，节点间传输的流量的源身份
    6This isn’t a typo of “tree,” although a trie is a type of tree.
    6 这不是 "tree" 的错别字，尽管前缀树是树的一种。
    of traffic that passes between nodes is encoded in the VNI field in the VXLAN or Geneve header, which avoids the need for an identity lookup. Cilium stores metadata about cluster identities in the cluster itself using the CiliumIdentity custom resource. We can see the identities for the pods we’ve already created by viewing this resource:
    被编码在 VXLAN 或 Geneve 头部的 VNI 字段中，从而避免了身份查找的需要。Cilium 使用 CiliumIdentity 自定义资源在集群自身中存储关于集群身份的元数据。我们可以通过查看此资源来查看已创建 Pod 的身份：
    代码（原文）
    $ kubectl get ciliumidentity -o yaml /
-l 'io.kubernetes.pod.namespace=default'
items:
- apiVersion: cilium.io/v2
kind: CiliumIdentity
metadata:
name: "22727"
security-labels:
k8s:io.kubernetes.pod.namespace: default
k8s:run: test
- apiVersion: cilium.io/v2
kind: CiliumIdentity
metadata:
name: "24505"
security-labels:
k8s:io.kubernetes.pod.namespace: default
k8s:app.kubernetes.io/name: webserver
    The CiliumIdentity resource isn’t namespaced, even though the identities themselves usually are. This means that to limit our listing to only identities for pods in the default namespace, we can’t use --namespace in the command like we normally would. Instead, we use a label filter for io.kubernetes.pod.namespace, as shown here. Somewhat confusingly, this filter applies to the label on the CiliumIdentity object, not to the security labels associated with that identity (which are listed under the security-labels key). That brings us to the last important property of identities: we never refer to them directly, and instead select them using security labels. The primary source of these labels is the labels on the pod. For example, we can see that one of our identities has the security label k8s:app.kubernetes.io/name: webserver, from the label on our NGINX pod. If multiple pods have the same labels, Cilium will assign them the same identity. This is useful if you have multiple replicas of a single service that are, from a policy perspective, the same. Our webserver pods were a good example of this, as they shared the same identity even though there were multiple pods.
    CiliumIdentity 资源不是命名空间范围的，即使身份本身通常是。这意味着要将列表限制为仅 default 命名空间中 Pod 的身份，我们不能像通常那样在命令中使用 --namespace。相反，我们使用 io.kubernetes.pod.namespace 的标签过滤器，如此处所示。令人困惑的是，此过滤器应用于 CiliumIdentity 对象上的标签，而不是 与该身份关联的安全标签（列在 security-labels 键下）。这让我们想到身份的最后一个重要属性：我们从不直接引用它们，而是用安全标签来选择它们。这些标签的主要来源是 Pod 上的标签。例如，我们可以看到其中一个身份具有安全标签 k8s:app.kubernetes.io/name: webserver，来自我们 NGINX Pod 上的标签。如果多个 Pod 具有相同的标签，Cilium 会为它们分配相同的身份。如果你有多个从策略角度来看是相同的 Service 副本，这会很有用。我们的 webserver Pod 就是一个很好的例子，因为即使有多个 Pod，它们也共享同一个身份。

    Limiting Identity-Relevant Labels
    限制身份相关标签

    One of the most important optimizations for large clusters is to restrict the number of identity-relevant labels. By default Cilium considers almost any label on a pod to be identity relevant, and all of them will be used as security labels on the identity. However, this can have unintended consequences where labels have high cardinality that isn’t used in policy. For example, batch processing systems often apply a “task identifier” label to pods they create. This can mean that every task launched will have a unique value for this label and will therefore create a new identity. Creating new identities causes every agent in the cluster to recalculate the policy maps for every single endpoint on the local node. On high-churn clusters, or clusters with large numbers of pods, this can cause maps to become exhausted (see Chapter 16), or the amount of policy regeneration may lead to degraded performance. The solution is to limit the identity-relevant labels so that these labels are ignored for identity generation, often meaning that new pods will not require new identities. The flipside of this is that if labels are not on security identities, they cannot be used in fromEntities statements in policy and they cannot be used to select endpoints with
    大型集群最重要的优化之一是限制 身份相关 标签的数量。默认情况下，Cilium 认为 Pod 上几乎任何标签都是身份相关的，所有这些标签都会作为安全标签使用在身份上。然而，这可能会产生非预期的后果——那些不在策略中使用的、具有高基数的标签。例如，批处理系统通常会为它创建的 Pod 应用一个"任务标识符"标签。这可能意味着每个启动的任务都会为此标签获得一个唯一值，因此会创建一个新的身份。创建新身份会导致集群中的每个 Agent 重新计算本地节点上每个端点的策略映射。在高变动集群或拥有大量 Pod 的集群中，这可能导致映射耗尽（见第 16 章），或者策略重新生成的数量可能导致性能下降。解决方案是限制身份相关标签，让这些标签在身份生成时被忽略，这通常意味着新 Pod 不需要新身份。反过来，如果标签不在安全身份上，它们就不能在策略的 fromEntities 语句中使用，也不能用于选择带
    代码（原文）
    endpointSelector.
    You can configure identity-relevant labels using the labels Helm value, either selecting labels to include or excluding labels by placing a ! before the label name.
    你可以使用 labels Helm 值配置身份相关标签，要么选择要包含的标签，要么通过在标签名称前加 ! 来排除标签。

    The Policy Datapath
    策略数据路径

    After the identity is determined, Cilium has to decide what to do with the packet. Each endpoint has its own policy map, computed ahead of time by the Cilium agent. It encodes the action to take for each unique mapping of identity, direction (ingress/ egress), protocol, and port.7 Figure 12-1 shows a simplified example of the policy flow for a natively routed cluster. First, it uses the IP cache to determine the source identity, mapping 10.0.0.68/32 to resolve identity 22727. It can then use this identity along with other information about the packet, such as the protocol and port, to check the policy map and decide what to do with the packet. In this case, it will match the policy rule for (22727, Ingress, TCP, 8080) → ALLOW. The purpose of identity in Cilium is to provide indirection between an IP address and a policy decision. All policy decisions are precomputed ahead of time by the Cilium agent and encoded into the datapath as
    身份确定之后，Cilium 必须决定如何处理数据包。每个端点都有自己的策略 map，由 Cilium Agent 预先计算好。它为身份、方向（ingress/egress）、协议和端口的每个唯一组合编码了要执行的操作。7 图 12-1 展示了原生路由集群中策略流程的简化示例。首先，它用 IP cache 确定源身份，将 10.0.0.68/32 解析为身份 22727。随后，它可以把这个身份与数据包的其他信息（如协议和端口）结合起来，查询策略 map 并决定如何处理数据包。在本例中，它将匹配策略规则 (22727, Ingress, TCP, 8080) → ALLOW。身份在 Cilium 中的作用，是在 IP 地址与策略决策之间提供一层间接。所有策略决策都由 Cilium Agent 预先计算，并以这些简单的“IP → 身份”查找以及随后的“身份、方向、协议、端口 → 决策”查找的形式编码进数据路径，
    7This is why there is no fromPorts policy in Cilium: the datapath can’t encode a source port requirement.
    7 这就是为什么 Cilium 中没有 fromPorts 策略：数据路径无法编码源端口要求。
    these simple lookups of IP → identity and then identity, direction, protocol, port →decision.
    这些简单的查找：先查找 IP → 身份，再查找 身份、方向、协议、端口 → 决策。
    The possible policy outcomes are that the packet is accepted and forwarded to the container, dropped with no response sent, or sent to a user space proxy for further processing. We’ll discuss the role of proxies in layer 7 policy in Chapter 13, but even in a layer 7 case, it’s the eBPF datapath that is programmed to send traffic to a proxy.
    可能的策略结果包括：数据包被接受并转发到容器、被丢弃且不发送响应，或被发送到用户空间代理进行进一步处理。我们将在第 13 章讨论 L7 策略中代理的作用，但即使是在 L7 场景下，也是由 eBPF 数据路径被编程来将流量发送到代理。

    Selecting Identities with Policy
    用策略选择身份

    Now that we understand how identities and policy outcomes are determined, let’s turn our attention to how to define policies to control which identities may communicate.
    现在我们理解了身份和策略结果是如何确定的，让我们将注意力转向如何定义策略来控制哪些身份可以通信。

    From Endpoints
    来自端点

    Let’s rewrite our policy for the web server to use the fromEndpoints statement. This is the first of the “identity-limiting” policy statements we’ll cover, and it allows us to apply our policies to any identities that match certain security labels. The list of ingress rules in the policy will look like this (webserver-8080-from-endpoints.yaml):
    让我们重写 Web 服务器的策略，使用 fromEndpoints 语句。这是我们将介绍的第一个"限制身份"的策略语句，它允许我们将策略应用于匹配特定安全标签的任何身份。策略中的入站规则列表将如下所示（webserver-8080-from-endpoints.yaml）：
    代码（原文）
    ingress:
- fromEndpoints:
- matchLabels:
app.kubernetes.io/name: test
toPorts:
- ports:
- port: "8080"
protocol: TCP
    We now have two statements within that single ingress rule: the toPorts statement from before and a new fromEndpoints statement. When multiple statements exist in the same rule, traffic must match all of the statements to match the rule. Here, traffic must be both destined for TCP port 8080 and coming from an endpoint with the security label app.kubernetes.io/name: test. Let’s apply this policy to our cluster and verify that our existing test pod, without a label, can’t access port 8080:
    现在我们在那条单独的入站规则中有两个语句：之前的 toPorts 语句和一个新的 fromEndpoints 语句。当同一条规则中有多个语句时，流量必须匹配 所有 语句才能匹配该规则。这里，流量必须既发往 TCP 端口 8080，又 来自具有安全标签 app.kubernetes.io/name: test 的端点。让我们将此策略应用到集群，验证我们没有标签的现有测试 Pod 无法访问端口 8080：
    代码（原文）
    $ kubectl apply --filename webserver-8080-from-endpoints.yaml
ciliumnetworkpolicy.cilium.io/webserver configured
$ kubectl run test -it --rm --image nicolaka/netshoot -- bash
test:~# curl -m1 webserver
curl: (28) Connection timed out after 1004 milliseconds
    At this point, the pod cannot access the server at all. Next, we’ll re-create our test pod with the label expected by the policy, app.kubernetes.io/name: test, and try again:
    此时，该 Pod 完全无法访问服务器。接下来，我们将使用策略期望的标签 app.kubernetes.io/name: test 重新创建测试 Pod，然后再试一次：
    代码（原文）
    $ kubectl run test -it --rm --image nicolaka/netshoot \
--labels "app.kubernetes.io/name=test" -- bash
test:~# curl -sm1 webserver
Hello from Cilium Up and Running!
test:~# curl -m1 webserver-metrics:9113/metrics
curl: (28) Connection timed out after 1002 milliseconds
    Once we relaunch the test pod with the correct label, we are able to access the server. However, we’re still not able to access the metrics service.
    一旦我们使用正确的标签重新启动测试 Pod，就能访问服务器了。然而，我们仍然无法访问指标服务。

    Policy with Multiple Rules
    多规则策略

    We can take this a step further by introducing a second ingress rule authorizing a different identity to scrape the web server’s metrics. The new rule looks like this (webserver-ingress-all.yaml):
    我们可以更进一步，引入第二条入站规则，授权另一个身份来抓取 Web 服务器的指标。新规则如下（webserver-ingress-all.yaml）：
    代码（原文）
    - fromEndpoints:
- matchLabels:
io.kubernetes.pod.namespace: metrics
app.kubernetes.io/name: metrics-collector
toPorts:
- ports:
- port: "9113"
protocol: TCP
    Instead of adding an additional statement to our existing rule, we’ve added a second rule to our policy. This one permits access to TCP port 9113 from identities with the
    我们没有向现有规则添加额外的语句，而是向策略添加了第二条规则。这条规则允许具有
    代码（原文）
    security labels io.kubernetes.pod.namespace: metrics and app.kubernetes.io/
    name: metrics-collector. As with a normal label selector in Kubernetes, when we have multiple labels, an identity needs to match all of them to be selected. The io.kubernetes.pod.namespace label is a special one populated by Cilium: it contains the name of the namespace the pod associated with the identity belongs to.8 Taken together, this new ingress rule permits TCP access to port 9113 from pods that have the label app.kubernetes.io/name: metrics-collector and are in the metrics namespace. While the statements and selectors inside a rule are additive, rules themselves are not. Figure 12-2 shows a graphical representation of the updated policy.
    name: metrics-collector。与 Kubernetes 中普通的标签选择器一样，当存在多个标签时，身份必须匹配全部标签才会被选中。io.kubernetes.pod.namespace 是 Cilium 填充的一个特殊标签：它包含与该身份关联的 Pod 所属命名空间的名称。8 综合起来，这条新的入站规则允许来自同时带有 app.kubernetes.io/name: metrics-collector 标签并且位于 metrics 命名空间中的 Pod 访问 TCP 端口 9113。规则内的语句和选择器是叠加关系，但规则本身并非如此。图 12-2 以图形方式展示了更新后的策略。
    Traffic must match at least one rule, making each rule an “or,” but to match a rule traffic must match all statements, making statements within a rule “and.”
    流量必须至少匹配一条规则，因此每条规则之间是"或"的关系；但要匹配一条规则，流量必须匹配其中所有语句，因此规则内的语句之间是"与"的关系。
    8You can’t “trick” the policy engine by setting a pod label literally named io.kubernetes.pod.namespace. Cilium will ignore labels on pods that would overlap with the special labels.
    8 你不能通过将 Pod 标签字面上设为 io.kubernetes.pod.namespace 来"欺骗"策略引擎。Cilium 会忽略 Pod 上那些可能与特殊标签重叠的标签。

    Multiple Statements Versus Multiple Rules
    多个语句对比多条规则

    There’s a subtlety we glossed over when talking about rules with multiple statements and then multiple rules in a policy. Consider the two following fragments of policies, listing only the ingress rules:
    在讨论具有多个语句的规则以及策略中的多条规则时，我们略过了一个细微之处。考虑以下两个策略片段，仅列出入站规则：
    代码（原文）
    ingress:
- fromEndpoints:
- matchLabels:
app.kubernetes.io/name: test
toPorts:
- ports:
- port: "8080"
protocol: TCP
---
ingress:
- fromEndpoints:
- matchLabels:
app.kubernetes.io/name: test
- toPorts:
- ports:
- port: "8080"
protocol: TCP
    Can you see the difference? The first policy fragment has no dash before toPorts. It defines a single rule with a fromEndpoints and a toPorts statement. The second policy fragment has a dash at the start of that line, which splits the list into two ingress rules: one that contains the fromEndpoints statement and another ingress rule that contains the toPorts statement. Figure 12-3 shows these two policy fragments graphically.
    你能看出区别吗？第一个策略片段中 toPorts 前面没有短横线。它定义了一条同时包含 fromEndpoints 和 toPorts 语句的规则。第二个策略片段中该行开头有短横线，这将列表分成了两条入站规则：一条包含 fromEndpoints 语句，另一条包含 toPorts 语句。图 12-3 以图形化方式展示了这两个策略片段。
    Applying the second policy fragment would mean that endpoints with the label app.kubernetes.io/name: test could reach any port on our workload, including any sensitive ports, and any identity—including ones external to the cluster—could access TCP port 8080. Depending on the nature of the misconfiguration, this small difference can lead to a significant security risk.
    应用第二个策略片段意味着，具有标签 app.kubernetes.io/name: test 的端点可以到达工作负载上的 任何 端口（包括任何敏感端口），而 任何 身份——包括集群外部的身份——都可以访问 TCP 端口 8080。根据错误配置的性质，这个微小差异可能导致重大的安全风险。

    Cluster-wide Policy
    集群范围策略

    Normally, a CiliumNetworkPolicy is only able to select endpoints in the same namespace as the policy. The rules in that policy may refer to endpoints in other namespaces, as we’ve seen, but the policy itself can’t affect endpoints outside that namespace. This is an important property of Kubernetes namespace isolation, and it means that in a multitenant cluster where namespaces are used for tenant isolation, a user creating a network policy in one namespace can’t impact pods in other namespaces. However, sometimes we do want to apply policy to every endpoint in every namespace at the same time. In our previous example, we added an ingress rule to allow the metrics collector to reach the metrics endpoint. We’d rather not have to specify in every single endpoint’s policy that it should permit access from the collector. For that, we can use a CiliumClusterwideNetworkPolicy. The cluster-wide policy supports all the same features as a namespaced one, and the implementation is the same. The only difference is that the object isn’t namespaced, so the endpointSelector can select endpoints in any namespace in the cluster.
    通常，CiliumNetworkPolicy 只能选择与策略处于同一命名空间内的端点。正如我们所见，该策略中的规则可以引用其他命名空间中的端点，但策略本身无法影响该命名空间之外的端点。这是 Kubernetes 命名空间隔离的一个重要特性，意味着在使用命名空间进行租户隔离的多租户集群中，在一个命名空间创建网络策略的用户无法影响其他命名空间中的 Pod。然而，有时我们确实希望同时将策略应用到所有命名空间中的每个端点。在前面的示例中，我们添加了一条入站规则，允许 metrics 收集器访问 metrics 端点。我们不希望不得不在每个单独端点的策略中都指定它应该允许来自收集器的访问。为此，我们可以使用 CiliumClusterwideNetworkPolicy。集群范围策略支持与命名空间策略相同的所有功能，实现方式也一致。唯一的区别是该对象不受命名空间约束，因此 endpointSelector 可以选择集群中任意命名空间内的端点。
    Cluster-wide policy is a powerful tool, but that capability comes with risk when you can impact the entire cluster at once. If you select all endpoints (using endpointSelector: {}) with a clusterwide policy and don’t disable default deny, as we described earlier in this chapter, you can easily “lock out” access to a cluster by accident, preventing any pods from communicating.
    集群范围策略是一个强大的工具，但当你可以一次性影响整个集群时，这种能力也伴随着风险。如果你使用集群范围策略选择所有端点（使用 endpointSelector: {}）且没有像本章前面描述的那样禁用默认拒绝，很容易意外"锁定"对集群的访问，导致所有 Pod 都无法通信。
    Let’s write a new cluster-wide policy (clusterwide-metrics.yaml) to select all endpoints so that the metrics collector can access them on port 9113:
    我们来编写一个新的集群范围策略（clusterwide-metrics.yaml），选择所有端点，以便 metrics 收集器可以通过端口 9113 访问它们：
    代码（原文）
    apiVersion: cilium.io/v2
kind: CiliumClusterwideNetworkPolicy
metadata:
name: clusterwide-metrics
spec:
endpointSelector: {}
enableDefaultDeny:
ingress: false
ingress:
- fromEndpoints:
- matchLabels:
io.kubernetes.pod.namespace: metrics
app.kubernetes.io/name: metrics-collector
toPorts:
- ports:
- port: "9113"
protocol: TCP
    This way, we don’t need a policy for every endpoint to allow the metrics service to scrape them. You can repeat the test from the previous section (after adjusting the namespaced policy to not mention port 9113) to see this in action.
    这样，我们就不需要为每个端点都编写一个策略来允许 metrics 服务抓取它们。你可以重复上一节中的测试（在调整命名空间策略使其不再提及端口 9113 之后）来实际验证这一点。

    Reserved Labels, Reserved Identities, and Entities
    保留标签、保留身份与实体

    We’ve seen that Cilium dynamically creates identities for workloads and attaches security labels to those identities. This forms the foundation of Cilium’s identity and security model, letting us apply policy to endpoints by selecting their identities using a policy’s endpointSelector and refer to other identities with fromEndpoints and toEndpoints statements in our policy rules. Cilium also has a number of hardcoded labels and identities, along with a mechanism to make use of common groupings of label selectors called entities. Reserved labels, reserved identities, and entities are closely entwined in their behavior and often referred to interchangeably, but it’s important to understand the differences between them:
    我们已经看到 Cilium 会为工作负载动态创建身份并将安全标签附加到这些身份上。这构成了 Cilium 身份与安全模型的基础，让我们能够通过在策略中使用 endpointSelector 选择器来按身份对端点应用策略，并在策略规则中通过 fromEndpoints 和 toEndpoints 语句引用其他身份。Cilium 还有一些硬编码的标签和身份，以及一种机制可以利用常见的标签选择器分组，称为 实体。保留标签、保留身份和实体在行为上紧密相关且经常互换使用，但理解它们之间的差异很重要：
    • A reserved label uses the reserved: prefix. Cilium has special handling for how
      保留标签 使用 reserved: 前缀。Cilium 对每种保留标签如何
    it applies each reserved label to identities, and they can’t be sourced from pod or namespace labels.
    将每种保留标签应用到身份上，而且它们不能来源于 Pod 或命名空间的标签。
    • A reserved identity is a special hardcoded identity for a particular purpose, usu‐
      保留身份 是一种用于特定目的的特殊硬编码身份，通
    ally with at least one reserved label applied to it. Some of these are listed in Table 12-2; for the full list, see the Cilium documentation.
    常会应用至少一个保留标签。表 12-2 列出了其中一部分；完整列表请参阅 Cilium 文档。
    • An entity is a built-in shorthand for a set of label selectors. You can write
      实体 是一组标签选择器的内置简写。你可以编写
    policy using toEntities/fromEntities with these instead of using toEndpoints/ fromEndpoints. Some of these are listed in Table 12-3, and again, you can find a full list in the Cilium documentation. Sometimes there is a clear correspondence between all three—for example, the ingress entity selects the reserved:ingress label, which is only present on the ingress reserved identity. On the other hand, the world entity selects the
    使用 toEntities/fromEntities 的策略，而不必使用 toEndpoints/fromEndpoints。表 12-3 列出了其中一部分，同样你可以在 Cilium 文档中找到完整列表。有时三者之间存在明确的对应关系——例如，ingress 实体选择 reserved:ingress 标签，而该标签仅存在于 ingress 保留身份上。另一方面，world 实体选择
    代码（原文）
    reserved:world, reserved:world-ipv4, and reserved:world-ipv6 labels, each
    of which has a corresponding reserved identity, but Cilium also places the reserved:world label on other identities, such as CIDR and FQDN identities that we’ll cover later in this chapter and in Chapter 13, respectively. This can make it a little unclear what we’re talking about if we simply use a term like “world identity”— do we really mean the world reserved identity, or do we mean the reserved:world security label? Often this is obvious in context, but when debugging issues or handling corner cases, it’s useful to remember that they are not the same. Table 12-2. Some of the reserved identities in Cilium
    这些标签各自都有对应的保留身份，但 Cilium 还会将 reserved:world 标签附加到其他身份上，例如 CIDR 和 FQDN 身份，我们将分别在本章后面和第 13 章介绍。如果我们只是简单地使用诸如"world 身份"这样的术语，可能会让人不太清楚我们到底指的是什么——我们是真的指 world 保留身份，还是指 reserved:world 安全标签？通常在上下文中这很明显，但在调试问题或处理边缘情况时，记住它们并不是一回事是很有用的。表 12-2　Cilium 中的部分保留身份

    IDIdentity nameSecurity labelsDescription ID 身份名称 安全标签 描述

    1hostreserved:hostThe current host
    1hostreserved:host 当前主机

    2worldreserved:worldAny endpoint outside of the cluster 2worldreserved:world 集群外部的任意端点

    6remote-nodereserved:remote-nodeAny other node in the cluster 7kube-apiserverreserved:kube-apiserverThe Kubernetes API server
    6remote-nodereserved:remote-node 集群中的任意其他节点；7kube-apiserverreserved:kube-apiserver Kubernetes API 服务器
    代码（原文）
    reserved:remote-node

    8ingressreserved:ingressTraffic to a pod in the cluster that has 8ingressreserved:ingress 集群中经过 Cilium Ingress 或 Gateway API

    passed through Cilium’s Ingress or Gateway API 实现处理（第 7 章讨论）的 Pod

    implementation (discussed in Chapter 7) 的入站流量

    10world-ipv6reserved:world-ipv6External IPv6 traffic
    10world-ipv6reserved:world-ipv6 外部 IPv6 流量
    The entities map closely to the reserved identities, with most having a 1:1 correspondence via the reserved label. The cluster entity is an unusual one, selecting a large number of the reserved labels and also the security label for endpoints on this cluster. Table 12-3. Some of the entities in Cilium
    实体与保留身份紧密映射，其中大多数通过 reserved 标签存在一对一对应关系。cluster 实体比较特殊，它选择大量 reserved 标签，同时也选择了本集群端点的安全标签。表 12-3　Cilium 中的部分实体

    Entity nameSelector 实体名称 选择器

    allSpecial wildcard selector all 特殊通配符选择器

    代码（原文）
    worldreserved:world
reserved:world-ipv4
reserved:world-ipv6
hostreserved:host
ingressreserved:ingress
remote-nodereserved:remote-node
kube-apiserverreserved:kube-apiserver

reserved:remote-note

reserved:ingress

reserved:kube-apiserver
    k8s:io.cilium.k8s.policy.cluster matching the cluster name The cluster and world entities may seem like opposites, but that’s not always the case. For example, if the Kubernetes API server is outside the cluster, its identity can have both the reserved:kube-apiserver and reserved:world labels and be included in both of those entities. The reserved identities can be given other labels in some cases. For example, on a control-plane node—a Kubernetes node that runs control-plane components such as the kube-apiserver—the host identity will also have the reserved:kube-apiserver label. Let’s put this into practice and write a policy for CoreDNS. We want it to only be able to make and accept the connections we expect it to. We can start by writing out the flows we expect:
    k8s:io.cilium.k8s.policy.cluster（匹配集群名称）cluster 和 world 实体看似对立，但情况并非总是如此。例如，如果 Kubernetes API 服务器位于集群外部，它的身份可以同时拥有 reserved:kube-apiserver 和 reserved:world 标签，因此会被归入这两个实体。保留身份在某些情况下也可以被赋予其他标签。例如，在控制平面节点上——运行 kube-apiserver 等控制平面组件的 Kubernetes 节点——host 身份也会有 reserved:kube-apiserver 标签。让我们把这付诸实践，为 CoreDNS 编写一个策略。我们希望它只能建立和接收我们预期的连接。我们可以先写出预期的流：
    • Other pods and nodes in the cluster should be able to make DNS requests. DNS
      集群中的其他 Pod 和节点应该能够发起 DNS 请求。DNS
    always uses port 53, but it can run over UDP or TCP.
    始终使用端口 53，但可以通过 UDP 或 TCP 传输。
    • CoreDNS needs to communicate with the kube-apiserver to retrieve informa‐
      CoreDNS 需要与 kube-apiserver 通信以获取
    tion about locations inside the cluster that it should provide DNS names for.
    集群内部需要提供 DNS 名称的位置信息。
    • CoreDNS needs to be able to make DNS queries to upstream DNS servers.
      CoreDNS 需要能够向上游 DNS 服务器发起 DNS 查询。
    Each flow is represented by a rule in our policy (coredns.yaml):
    每条流都由我们策略中的一条规则表示（coredns.yaml）：
    代码（原文）
    apiVersion: cilium.io/v2
kind: CiliumNetworkPolicy
metadata:
name: coredns
namespace: kube-system
spec:
endpointSelector:
matchLabels:
k8s-app: kube-dns
ingress:
- toPorts:
- ports:
- port: "53"
protocol: ANY
fromEntities:
- cluster
egress:
- toPorts:
- ports:
- port: "6443"
protocol: TCP
toEntities:
- kube-apiserver
- toPorts:
- ports:
- port: "53"
protocol: ANY
toEntities:
- world
    This policy doesn’t select which pods are able to send traffic to CoreDNS, but it ensures that traffic from pods that are able to do so will be accepted.
    此策略并未选择哪些 Pod 能够向 CoreDNS 发送流量，但它确保了来自这些 Pod 的流量会被接受。

    Combining Statements
    语句的组合

    We’ve introduced two different ways of filtering destinations by identity, and we’ll cover a few more later in this chapter and in Chapter 13. However, these statements cannot be combined in the same rule. For example, this egress rule is invalid:
    我们已经介绍了两种按身份过滤目的的不同方式，我们将在本章后面和第 13 章介绍更多方式。但是，这些语句不能在同一条规则中组合。例如，以下出站规则是无效的：
    代码（原文）
    egress:
- toEndpoints:
- matchLabels:
externally-exposed: "true"
toEntities:
- world
toPorts:
- ports:
- port: "80"
protocol: TCP
    Cilium will not reject policy with invalid rules, but the entire policy will be marked as invalid. This means no rules in the policy will be applied, even if some would be valid independently. We can check the validity of an applied policy with kubectl. For example, for the previous policy (assuming it’s named invalid-policy), we would see:
    Cilium 不会拒绝包含无效规则的策略，但整个策略会被标记为无效。这意味着策略中的所有规则都不会被应用，即使某些规则单独来看是有效的。我们可以使用 kubectl 检查已应用策略的有效性。例如，对于上面的策略（假设名为 invalid-policy），我们会看到：
    代码（原文）
    $ kubectl get cnp invalid-policy
NAME             AGE   VALID
invalid-policy   48s   False
$ kubectl describe cnp invalid-policy
[...]
Status:
Conditions:
Message: combining ToEndpoints and ToEntities is not supported yet
    In this case, we could fix the problem by rewriting the policy to use two different rules—one for endpoints and one for entities. It’s not always obvious which statements in a rule can be combined and which cannot. For example, both toEndpoints and toEntities can be combined with toPorts, but they can’t be combined with each other. You can see a summary of the different possible statements in egress and ingress rules in Figures 12-4 and 12-5. These are nonexhaustive and only list the most commonly used policy statements.
    在这种情况下，我们可以通过重写策略，使用两条不同的规则——一条用于端点，一条用于实体——来解决问题。规则中哪些语句可以组合、哪些不能，并非总是一目了然。例如，toEndpoints 和 toEntities 都可以与 toPorts 组合，但它们彼此不能组合。你可以在图 12-4 和图 12-5 中看到入站和出站规则中各种可能语句的概要。这些并非全部，仅列出了最常用的策略语句。
    We haven’t introduced all the concepts in Figure 12-4 yet. We’ll cover fromCIDR and fromCIDRSet in the next section. We don’t cover icmps in this book as ICMP policies are only useful if all of your nodes are in the same layer 2 domain, but they can be used to restrict traffic at layer 2, such as for ICMP ping. The important point for now is that in general, you can have one statement that selects the source’s identity and another that selects something about the traffic.
    我们尚未介绍图 12-4 中的所有概念。我们将在下一节介绍 fromCIDR 和 fromCIDRSet。本书不涉及 icmps，因为 ICMP 策略仅在所有节点都处于同一二层域时才有用，但它们可以用来在二层限制流量，例如 ICMP ping。目前重要的一点是，一般来说，你可以有一条语句选择源的身份，另一条语句选择关于流量的某些特征。
    The common statements in egress rules are very similar, with most having an equivalence. toFQDNs is used for a special type of rule called an FQDN rule that we’ll cover in Chapter 13. toServices can be used to select identities by entities that are themselves selected by a particular Kubernetes service. It can’t be combined with toPorts because toServices inherits the port information from the service in question.
    出站规则中的常见语句非常相似，大多数都有对应关系。toFQDNs 用于一种特殊类型的规则，称为 FQDN 规则，我们将在第 13 章介绍。toServices 可以用于按实体选择身份，而这些实体本身由特定 Kubernetes 服务选择。它不能与 toPorts 组合，因为 toServices 会从相关服务继承端口信息。

    Beyond the Cluster
    集群之外

    So far we’ve taken advantage of Cilium’s ability as the CNI to be aware of every pod and node in the cluster, but we can extend this concept of identity beyond the cluster. We’ve already seen the world identity and entity, which can be used to describe endpoints outside of the cluster but not inside it. We can take this a step further using a CIDR range policy.
    到目前为止，我们利用了 Cilium 作为 CNI 能够感知集群中每个 Pod 和节点的能力，但我们可以将身份概念扩展到集群之外。我们已经见过 world 身份和实体，它可以用来描述集群外部但不是内部的端点。我们可以使用 CIDR 范围策略更进一步。

    CIDR Ranges
    CIDR 范围

    As indicated in Figures 12-4 and 12-5, we can use toCIDR/fromCIDR and toCIDRSet/ fromCIDRSet statements in our policies. Like the corresponding statements for endpoints and entities, these allow us to define traffic sources and destinations, and they can be used anywhere you’d expect in policy. Using toCIDR/fromCIDR is a simpler way of specifying a CIDR identity, while using toCIDRSet/fromCIDRSet allows you to “carve out” exceptions from otherwise continuous CIDR ranges. A new identity is created for each CIDR range specified, and the IP caches on each node are updated to map the given range to that identity. Note that CIDR ranges in network policies do not apply to IP addresses that already have an identity, such as those used for pods or cluster services. For example, if you have a pod with IP 233.252.0.17, adding an ingress rule on another pod to permit 233.252.0.0/24 would not permit traffic from that pod. Always use fromEndpoints or fromEntities to select traffic from locations inside the cluster.
    如图 12-4 和图 12-5 所示，我们可以在策略中使用 toCIDR/fromCIDR 和 toCIDRSet/fromCIDRSet 语句。与针对端点和实体的对应语句类似，它们允许我们定义流量的源和目的，并且可以在策略中预期的任何地方使用。使用 toCIDR/fromCIDR 是指定 CIDR 身份的更简单方式，而使用 toCIDRSet/fromCIDRSet 允许你从原本连续的 CIDR 范围中"挖除"例外。为每个指定的 CIDR 范围创建一个新的身份，每个节点上的 IP 缓存会更新以将给定范围映射到该身份。注意，网络策略中的 CIDR 范围 不适用于已有身份的 IP 地址，例如 Pod 或集群服务使用的 IP。例如，如果有一个 IP 为 233.252.0.17 的 Pod，在另一个 Pod 上添加一条允许 233.252.0.0/24 的入站规则 不会允许来自该 Pod 的流量。始终使用 fromEndpoints 或 fromEntities 来选择来自集群内部的流量。

    CIDR Policy
    CIDR 策略

    Let’s add a CIDR egress policy to our workload (tocidr.yaml). This time we’ll apply the policy to our test pod, and we’ll restrict egress traffic to only be possible to 1.1.1.1:
    我们为工作负载添加一个 CIDR 出站策略（tocidr.yaml）。这次我们将策略应用到测试 Pod 上，并将出站流量限制为只能发往 1.1.1.1：
    代码（原文）
    apiVersion: cilium.io/v2
kind: CiliumNetworkPolicy
metadata:
name: client
spec:
endpointSelector:
matchLabels:
app.kubernetes.io/name: test
egress:
- toCIDR:
- "1.1.1.1/32"
    After applying this policy, we can verify the behavior in our test pod. Remember that for it to be selected by the policy, you must launch your test pod with --labels
    应用此策略后，我们可以在测试 Pod 中验证其行为。请记住，要让它被策略选中，你必须使用 --labels
    代码（原文）
    "app.kubernetes.io/name=test":
$ kubectl apply -f tocidr.yaml
ciliumnetworkpolicy.cilium.io/client configured
$ kubectl run test -it --rm \
--image nicolaka/netshoot \
--labels "app.kubernetes.io/name=test" -- bash
test:~# curl -m1 https://1.1.1.2
curl: (28) Connection timed out after 1003 milliseconds
test:~# curl -sI https://1.1.1.1 | head -n1
HTTP/2 301
    The first request times out because traffic to 1.1.1.2 is not allowed by the policy. The second request succeeds with an HTTP 301 response, which indicates that the connection was permitted.
    第一个请求超时，因为发往 1.1.1.2 的流量未被策略允许。第二个请求成功，返回 HTTP 301 响应，这表明连接已被许可。

    Identity with Ingress and Gateway API
    Ingress 与 Gateway API 场景下的身份

    When Cilium is acting as an Ingress controller or Gateway API implementation (see Chapter 7), traffic to the Ingress or Gateway IP is passed to Cilium’s Envoy proxy. After it’s forwarded to the backend pod, it no longer has the original client IP as its origin; this is replaced by an IP that is used internally for this ingress traffic. This is shown in Figure 12-6, with 10.0.0.207 being used as the internal ingress IP. This IP is allocated by Cilium automatically from the node’s pod IP range.
    当 Cilium 作为 Ingress 控制器或 Gateway API 实现时（参见第 7 章），发往 Ingress 或 Gateway IP 的流量会被传递到 Cilium 的 Envoy 代理。在被转发到后端 Pod 后，流量不再以原始客户端 IP 为来源；而是被替换为一个内部用于此入站流量的 IP。如图 12-6 所示，10.0.0.207 用作内部入站 IP。该 IP 由 Cilium 从节点的 Pod IP 范围内自动分配。
    Where pods are expected to be backends for Ingress or Gateway API, they must permit access from the ingress identity, typically by using a fromEntities rule permitting the ingress entity.
    如果 Pod 预期作为 Ingress 或 Gateway API 的后端，它们必须允许来自 ingress 身份的访问，通常通过使用一条 fromEntities 规则允许 ingress 实体。

    Deny Rules
    拒绝规则

    Just as we can allow traffic with an ingress or egress rule and rely on default deny behavior to block unspecified traffic, we can use an ingressDeny or egressDeny rule to do the opposite and explicitly block certain traffic. Perhaps we want to prevent services from accessing the Kubernetes API at all. The API server will block access to unauthenticated clients, but you may still wish to restrict access anyway to protect against authentication bugs in the Kubernetes API server. Deny rules count as rules for deciding if default deny should apply to an endpoint, so if we only block traffic and don’t disable default deny we’ll block everything by accident. Putting this all together, we can write a cluster-wide policy (kube-api-deny.yaml) to block any pod that isn’t in the kube-system namespace from accessing the Kubernetes API. Note that other services, such as cert-manager and Argo CD, commonly deploy into their own namespaces and also require Kubernetes API access, so the namespace exclusion list may need to be expanded for your environment:
    正如我们可以用入站或出站规则允许流量并依赖默认拒绝行为来阻止未指定的流量一样，我们可以使用 ingressDeny 或 egressDeny 规则做相反的事情——显式阻止某些流量。也许我们想要完全阻止服务访问 Kubernetes API。API 服务器会阻止未认证客户端的访问，但你可能仍然希望无论如何都要限制访问，以防止 Kubernetes API 服务器中的认证 Bug。拒绝规则也计入决定默认拒绝是否应应用于端点的规则中，因此如果我们只阻止流量且不禁用默认拒绝，我们会意外地阻止所有流量。综合考虑，我们可以编写一个集群范围策略（kube-api-deny.yaml）来阻止任何不在 kube-system 命名空间中的 Pod 访问 Kubernetes API。注意，其他服务（如 cert-manager 和 Argo CD）通常部署在自己的命名空间中，也需要访问 Kubernetes API，因此你的环境可能需要扩展命名空间排除列表：
    代码（原文）
    apiVersion: cilium.io/v2
kind: CiliumClusterwideNetworkPolicy
metadata:
name: kube-api-deny
spec:
endpointSelector:
matchExpressions:
- key: io.kubernetes.pod.namespace
operator: NotIn
values:
- kube-system
enableDefaultDeny:
egress: false
egressDeny:
- toEntities:
- kube-apiserver
    A detail we’ve left implicit until now is that Cilium policy is unordered. The ordering of statements within a rule doesn’t matter, nor does the ordering of rules within a policy, or even if they’re in different policies. The datapath is fundamentally unordered too, with per-identity policy decisions being generated ahead of time. So far, this hasn’t mattered: traffic was allowed if a policy allowed it, and if an endpoint was selected by any policy that didn’t have enableDefaultDeny turned off, then all other traffic was dropped. With the addition of deny rules, however, we are left with a question: What happens if traffic is both denied and allowed by different policies? In Cilium’s case, deny always takes priority over allow, no matter what. It doesn’t matter if one is cluster-wide and the other is a namespaced policy, or if one is “broader” than another. This is very useful for setting cluster-wide baseline policy that can’t be overridden even if individual teams can write their own network policy, but it makes deny policy a bit of a blunt instrument. Once you’ve denied a particular flow, you can’t make exceptions without modifying the policy.
    一个我们目前默认的细节是，Cilium 策略是 无序的。规则内语句的顺序无关紧要，策略内规则的顺序也无关紧要，甚至它们是否在不同的策略中都不重要。数据路径从根本上也是无序的，每个身份的策略决策是预先生成的。到目前为止，这并不重要：如果某条策略允许流量，流量就被允许；如果端点被任何未关闭 enableDefaultDeny 的策略选中，那么所有其他流量都会被丢弃。但是，随着拒绝规则的加入，我们面临一个问题：如果流量同时被不同的策略拒绝和允许，会发生什么？在 Cilium 中，无论如何，拒绝 始终优先于允许。无论一个是集群范围策略、另一个是命名空间策略，或者一个比另一个"更宽泛"，都不重要。这对于设置集群范围的基线策略非常有用，即使各个团队可以编写自己的网络策略，基线策略也不能被覆盖，但这也使拒绝策略成为一种比较粗暴的手段。一旦你拒绝了某条特定的流，不修改策略就无法创建例外。

    Summary
    小结

    Policy is a powerful part of Cilium’s toolset. The mapping from IP to identity allows the datapath to be simultaneously expressive and performant. We’ve seen how policy can be used to protect workloads on the cluster, prevent lateral movement, and stop pods from egressing unexpectedly. In the next chapter, we’ll cover advanced use cases of Cilium’s policy engine that go beyond simple layer 3 filtering.
    策略是 Cilium 工具集中的强大组成部分。从 IP 到身份的映射让数据路径既能保持表达力又能保持高性能。我们已经看到策略如何被用来保护集群中的工作负载、防止横向移动、阻止 Pod 意外出站。在下一章中，我们将介绍 Cilium 策略引擎中超越简单三层过滤的高级用例。

    第 13 章
    Layer 7 and FQDN Policy
    第 7 层与 FQDN 策略

    In this chapter, we’ll cover a number of advanced use cases for network policy that build on the foundation of network policy we established in Chapter 12. If you haven’t read that chapter, you should at least be familiar with how rules are structured and how the policy datapath works. Our focus here will be on layer 7 policy for HTTP, HTTPS, DNS, and FQDN policy. All the manifests for this chapter are in the chapter13 directory of the book’s GitHub repository.
    本章将介绍若干网络策略的进阶用例，这些用例基于我们在第 12 章中建立的网络策略基础。如果你尚未阅读该章，至少应当熟悉规则的结构以及策略数据路径的工作原理。本章将聚焦 HTTP、HTTPS、DNS 的第 7 层策略以及 FQDN 策略。本章的所有清单文件都位于本书 GitHub 仓库的 chapter13 目录下。

    Layer 7 Policy
    第 7 层策略

    In the previous chapter, we applied policy purely at layer 3. We used various ways of understanding the identity of the traffic, such as toEndpoints, toEntities, and toCIDR egress rule statements and their ingress equivalents, but ultimately all of the policy we’ve applied has acted based on the IP address and port information in the packet. If we can uniquely identify all of the resources we want to secure purely based on these factors, that’s great—but often we need to go further. For example, what if we want a workload’s access to an external service to be read-only? If it’s a service over HTTP, we can limit egress to only the relevant IP address or identity and only TCP port 80, but our policy will apply equally to GET and POST requests. Similarly, such a policy will not allow us to restrict access to only some URL paths, such as /api. What if we wanted to secure an HTTP server where we expect different clients to use different paths? Or what if multiple virtual servers are hosted at the same IP address and traffic to them is filtered and routed based on the HTTP Host header or TLS Server Name Indication (SNI) header? This is especially important for external services that might be hosted on content delivery networks (CDNs) or managed services where the same IP address may be used to serve several different services. For example, if we wanted to allow our pods to access an API on api.weather.example, we might know a specific IP range is used for that site and use a toCIDR rule to permit access to that range on port 443 or port 80. But if the range in question is owned by a CDN, other sites—say, a file hosting site filehost.example—may be using it too. This would inadvertently allow the pods we permitted to access api.weather.example to also access filehost.example. In isolation, this may not be an issue, but an attacker could potentially exploit this flaw to exfiltrate sensitive data from a compromised pod or implement a “command and control” mechanism for malware. These examples may be abstract, involving lots of “ifs” and “coulds,” but they are indicative of a very real limitation in IP-based identity when used with HTTP and HTTPS services with virtual servers or CDNs.
    在上一章中，我们完全在第 3 层上应用策略。我们使用了多种方式来理解流量的身份，例如出口规则语句中的 toEndpoints、toEntities、toCIDR 及其对应的入站等价语句，但归根结底，我们所应用的所有策略都是基于数据包中的 IP 地址和端口信息来生效的。如果我们能仅凭这些因素唯一标识出所有希望保护的资源，那当然最好——但实际往往还需要更进一步。例如，假设我们希望某个工作负载对外部服务的访问为只读模式。如果该服务基于 HTTP，我们可以将出口限制为仅访问相关的 IP 地址或身份，并且只允许 TCP 80 端口，但我们的策略会对 GET 和 POST 请求同样生效。同样，这样的策略无法让我们将访问限制在某些 URL 路径上，例如 /api。如果我们要保护的 HTTP 服务器期望不同的客户端使用不同的路径该怎么办？或者如果多个虚拟服务器托管在同一个 IP 地址上，并且对它们的流量是基于 HTTP Host 头或 TLS Server Name Indication (SNI) 头进行过滤和路由的呢？这对于托管在内容分发网络（CDN）或托管服务上的外部服务尤为重要，因为相同的 IP 地址可能被用来提供多个不同的服务。例如，如果我们想允许 Pod 访问 api.weather.example 上的 API，我们可能知道该站点使用某个特定的 IP 范围，于是用 toCIDR 规则来允许在 443 或 80 端口上访问该范围。但如果该范围归某个 CDN 所有，其他站点——比如说文件托管站点 filehost.example——也可能正在使用它。这就会在无意中让被允许访问 api.weather.example 的 Pod 也能访问 filehost.example。单独来看这可能不是问题，但攻击者有可能利用这一缺陷从被攻陷的 Pod 中窃取敏感数据，或者为恶意软件建立"命令与控制"机制。这些例子看起来可能有些抽象，涉及大量的"如果"和"可能"，但它们确实反映了基于 IP 的身份在配合使用带有虚拟服务器或 CDN 的 HTTP/HTTPS 服务时所面临的真实局限。

    Securing HTTP
    保护 HTTP

    To improve upon the layer 3 policies and provide greater flexibility for securing HTTP services, Cilium network policies support layer 7 policy rules. To illustrate, we’ll deploy a test cluster, Cilium, and the same web server we used in Chapter 12 to demonstrate layer 3 network policy, but with a slightly different configuration (webserver.yaml) to provide different responses on different HTTP paths: “API” from /api and “Admin” from /admin. We’ll also apply the same ingress security policy, using fromEndpoints. This will allow only endpoints with the label app.kubernetes.io/name: test to access TCP port 8080 on the web server. After deploying a test pod, we can see that we’re able to access all paths and that we get different responses on different paths:
    为了在第 3 层策略的基础上加以改进，为保护 HTTP 服务提供更大的灵活性，Cilium 网络策略支持第 7 层策略规则。为说明这一点，我们将部署一个测试集群、Cilium，以及我们在第 12 章用于演示第 3 层网络策略的同一个 Web 服务器，但使用略有不同的配置（webserver.yaml），以便在不同的 HTTP 路径上返回不同的响应：/api 返回 "API"，/admin 返回 "Admin"。我们还将应用相同的入站安全策略，使用 fromEndpoints。这将只允许带有 app.kubernetes.io/name: test 标签的端点访问 Web 服务器上的 TCP 8080 端口。部署完测试 Pod 后，我们可以看到能够访问所有路径，并且不同路径返回不同的响应：
    代码（原文）
    $ kubectl apply --filename webserver.yaml
deployment.apps/webserver created
configmap/nginx-config created
service/webserver created
service/webserver-metrics created
$ kubectl apply -f webserver-8080-from-endpoints.yaml
ciliumnetworkpolicy.cilium.io/webserver created
$ kubectl run test -it --rm \
--labels "app.kubernetes.io/name=test" \
--image nicolaka/netshoot -- bash
test:~# curl -s webserver
Hello from Cilium Up and Running!
test:~# curl -s webserver/api
API
test:~# curl -s webserver/admin
Admin
test:~# curl -m1 webserver-metrics:9113/metrics
curl: (28) Connection timed out after 1003 milliseconds
    Layer 7 policy builds upon the port definition of a layer 3 policy by adding a rules block to a toPorts statement as part of an ingress or egress rule. Let’s update our network policy to include a layer 7 rule:
    第 7 层策略在第 3 层策略的端口定义基础上，通过在作为入站或出站规则一部分的 toPorts 语句中添加 rules 块来构建。让我们更新网络策略，加入一条第 7 层规则：
    代码（原文）
    toPorts:
- ports:
- port: "8080"
protocol: TCP
rules:
http:
- method: GET
path: "/api"
    The new rule allows HTTP GET requests on the /api path. After applying the policy (webserver-http-rule.yaml), let’s run the tests from our test pod again:
    新规则允许在 /api 路径上的 HTTP GET 请求。应用该策略（webserver-http-rule.yaml）后，让我们在测试 Pod 中再次运行测试：
    代码（原文）
    test:~# curl -s webserver
Access denied
test:~# curl -s webserver/api
API
test:~# curl -s webserver/admin
Access denied
test:~# curl -m1 webserver-metrics:9113/metrics
curl: (28) Connection timed out after 1004 milliseconds
    We can access our webserver pod on /api, and the connection to the metrics server still times out, but connection attempts to other paths result in an “Access denied” response If we check the headers on the response, we can learn a little more:
    我们可以在 /api 路径访问 webserver Pod，与 metrics 服务器的连接仍然会超时，但对其他路径的连接尝试会收到"Access denied"（访问被拒绝）响应。如果我们检查响应的头信息，可以了解更多：
    代码（原文）
    test:~# curl -D - webserver
HTTP/1.1 403 Forbidden
content-length: 15
content-type: text/plain
date: Thu, 23 Oct 2025 20:25:41 GMT
server: envoy
Access denied
    We can now see that the response code is 403 Forbidden. We can also see there is a server header that reports as Envoy, not NGINX. It’s useful to remember that the behavior of blocked traffic is different on layer 3 and layer 7. The connection to port 9113 times out, but the blocked connections to port 8080 are established and then responded to with a 403. The path in a layer 7 HTTP rule is a regular expression (regex). This means that /api matches /api exactly and will not allow access to /api/ or sub-URLs like /api/v1/posts. To allow access to an entire subtree, you would need to use a wildcard match such as path: /api/.*. (Note that path: /api.* would likely be wrong, as it would permit access to other directories in the path whose names begin with api, such
    现在我们可以看到响应码是 403 Forbidden。我们还能看到有一个 server 头，报告为 Envoy，而不是 NGINX。需要记住的一点是，被拦截的流量在第 3 层和第 7 层上的行为有所不同。与 9113 端口的连接会超时，但 8080 端口上被拦截的连接会被建立，然后收到带有 403 的响应。第 7 层 HTTP 规则中的 path 是一个正则表达式（regex）。这意味着 /api 会 精确匹配 /api，不会允许访问 /api/ 或诸如 /api/v1/posts 之类的子 URL。要允许访问整个子树，你需要使用通配符匹配，例如 path: /api/.*。（注意 path: /api.* 可能是错误的，因为它会允许访问路径中名称以 api 开头的其他目录，例
    代码（原文）
    as /api-beta/v1.)

    The Envoy Datapath
    Envoy 数据路径

    When evaluating ingress traffic, the Cilium eBPF datapath follows the same identity resolution and policy lookup flow described in “The Policy Datapath” on page 252. The datapath resolves the identity of the source based on the source IP, determines whether the packet belongs to an existing connection, and then inspects the endpoint’s policy map using the source identity, protocol, and destination port. Layer 7 policy builds on this same foundation. When traffic targets a port with layer 7 rules attached, the datapath redirects the packet to Envoy for layer 7 processing. As you may recall from Chapter 2, Envoy is not deployed as a sidecar container and injected into the webserver pod; it’s deployed as a DaemonSet, so each node has its own dedicated instance that Cilium communicates with locally. Because Cilium decides whether layer 7 enforcement is required on a per-port and per-protocol basis, traffic that does not require it is not redirected to Envoy. Figure 13-1 illustrates the layer 7 packet flow for a client accessing the web server.
    在评估入站流量时，Cilium eBPF 数据路径遵循与第 252 页"策略数据路径"一节中描述相同的身份解析和策略查询流程。数据路径根据源 IP 解析源的身份，判断数据包是否属于现有连接，然后使用源身份、协议和目的端口检查端点的策略映射。第 7 层策略建立在这同一基础之上。当流量发往一个绑定了第 7 层规则的端口时，数据路径会将数据包重定向到 Envoy 进行第 7 层处理。你可能还记得第 2 章中提到，Envoy 并不是以 Sidecar 容器的形式注入到 webserver Pod 中的；它是以 DaemonSet 的形式部署的，因此每个节点都有自己独立的实例，Cilium 与该实例在本地通信。由于 Cilium 按端口和协议决定是否需要执行第 7 层强制，不需要第 7 层处理的流量不会被重定向到 Envoy。图 13-1 展示了客户端访问 Web 服务器时的第 7 层数据包流。
    The TCP handshake between the client and the service is performed by Envoy. Only once the HTTP request has been fully sent and Envoy has evaluated it against the configured rules does it decide whether to respond with a 403 or to proxy the request to the backend. In the latter case, Envoy acts as a user space proxy, forming its own TCP connection to the backend and performing an equivalent HTTP request. We can see this by inspecting the access logs on the web server. Let’s scale our deployment down to one pod so we can know which web server served our request and view its access logs:
    客户端与服务之间的 TCP 握手由 Envoy 执行。只有当 HTTP 请求被完整发送、且 Envoy 根据配置的规则完成评估后，它才会决定是以 403 响应，还是将请求代理到后端。在后一种情况下，Envoy 作为用户空间代理，与后端建立自己的 TCP 连接，并发起一个等价的 HTTP 请求。我们可以通过检查 Web 服务器上的访问日志来验证这一点。让我们将部署的副本数缩减到一个，这样就能知道是哪个 Web 服务器响应了我们的请求，并查看它的访问日志：
    代码（原文）
    $ kubectl scale deploy/webserver --replicas=1
deployment.apps/webserver scaled
$ kubectl rollout restart deploy/webserver
deployment.apps/webserver restarted
    Then, in our test pod, we’ll perform some requests:
    然后，在我们的测试 Pod 中执行一些请求：
    代码（原文）
    test:~# curl -s webserver
Access denied
test:~# curl -s webserver/api
API
    Finally, we can check the IP of the test pod and view the logs on the webserver pod:
    最后，我们可以查看测试 Pod 的 IP，并查看 webserver Pod 上的日志：
    代码（原文）
    $ kubectl get po test -o jsonpath='{.status.podIP}'
10.0.0.212
$ kubectl logs deploy/webserver -c nginx | grep "curl"
10.0.0.196 <truncated> "GET /api HTTP/1.1" 200 4 "-" "curl/8.14.1" "-"
    There are two useful things to notice here. First, the request that was denied by policy didn’t make it to the backend pod because it was blocked by Envoy. Second, although we can see that the IP of the test pod is 10.0.0.212, NGINX is reporting the source IP of the request as 10.0.0.196. This special IP is reserved out of the node’s pod IP range for use by Envoy when communicating with the local pods. It’s referred to by Cilium as the CiliumInternalIP, and we can see it on the CiliumNode object:
    这里有两点值得注意。第一，被策略拒绝的请求没有到达后端 Pod，因为它被 Envoy 拦截了。第二，虽然我们可以看到测试 Pod 的 IP 是 10.0.0.212，但 NGINX 报告的请求源 IP 却是 10.0.0.196。这个特殊 IP 是从节点的 Pod IP 范围中预留出来的，专供 Envoy 在与本地 Pod 通信时使用。Cilium 将其称为 CiliumInternalIP，我们可以在 CiliumNode 对象上看到它：
    代码（原文）
    $ kubectl get ciliumnode -o yaml | yq '.items[].spec.addresses'
- ip: 10.89.0.3
type: InternalIP
- ip: 10.0.0.196
type: CiliumInternalIP

    Performance
    性能

    Envoy is a high-performance layer 7 proxy, and it was chosen by the Cilium project to form its layer 7 datapath in part because of this performance. However, it can never be as fast as eBPF. Although it may be minimal, there will always be some latency added to connections that use layer 7 policy. There may also be decreased throughput or increased requirements on the node’s CPU and memory resources, depending on the configuration. Most services will not be impacted, but if your application is particularly latency-sensitive or if you have a deep microservices architecture where a single user request can result in many chained HTTP requests within your cluster, the increased latency may become problematic.
    Envoy 是一个高性能的第 7 层代理，它被 Cilium 项目选为第 7 层数据路径的一部分，原因之一正是其性能。不过，它永远无法像 eBPF 那样快。虽然延迟增幅可能很小，但使用第 7 层策略的连接总会增加一些延迟。吞吐量可能会下降，或者节点对 CPU 和内存资源的需求会增加，具体取决于配置。大多数服务不会受到影响，但如果你的应用对延迟特别敏感，或者你的微服务架构很深——单个用户请求可能会在集群内引发大量链式 HTTP 请求——那么增加的延迟就可能成为问题。
    The per-node Envoy instance discussed here is the same as the one that handles connections for Gateway API and Ingress. The configurations are independent, but there are cases where traffic can flow into Envoy multiple times. For example, traffic to a pod with a layer 7 ingress rule that applies to the ingress identity that is behind a Gateway API gateway will transit Envoy twice: once on the ingress node to resolve the gateway and any HTTP rules, and then a second time on the backend pod’s node (which may be the same node) to apply layer 7 policy. In general, we don’t recommend applying layer 7 policy to the ingress identity, as most filtering you would want to do can be accomplished on the gateway level instead. If you’re applying layer 7 policy to in-cluster (or meshed cluster) endpoints and also have a gateway, you can add a rule to allow all traffic on the HTTP port from the gateway using fromEntities: [ingress] while still keeping the layer 7 rules on other in-cluster sources using
    这里讨论的每个节点上的 Envoy 实例，与处理 Gateway API 和 Ingress 连接的实例是同一个。它们的配置是独立的，但存在流量可能多次进入 Envoy 的情况。例如，发往一个 Pod 的入站流量，如果该 Pod 应用了第 7 层入站规则，且规则匹配 Gateway API 网关之后的 ingress 身份，那么该流量将两次经过 Envoy：一次在入站节点上解析网关和任何 HTTP 规则，第二次在后端 Pod 的节点上（可能是同一个节点）应用第 7 层策略。一般来说，我们不建议对 ingress 身份应用第 7 层策略，因为你想做的大多数过滤都可以在网关层面完成。如果你正在对集群内（或网格化集群内）的端点应用第 7 层策略，同时又有网关，那么可以添加一条规则，使用 fromEntities: [ingress] 允许来自网关的 HTTP 端口上的所有流量，同时通过
    代码（原文）
    fromEndpoints.
    There’s nothing wrong with introducing a proxy for layer 7 security, but it does introduce extra hops and latency. We can test this using some network performance tools. These aren’t available in the debug image we’ve been using, so we’re going to use a different container for this purpose. Because we’ll need to do some setup, we’ll leave this one running. We’ll launch a Kali Linux container, install a performance testing application called Siege, and disable verbose output by editing the config file with sed:
    为实现第 7 层安全而引入代理本身并没有 错，但它确实带来了额外的跳数和延迟。我们可以使用一些网络性能工具来验证。这些工具在我们一直在用的调试镜像中不可用，所以我们会换一个容器来做这个测试。由于需要一些准备工作，我们会让这个容器持续运行。我们将启动一个 Kali Linux 容器，安装一个名为 Siege 的性能测试应用，并使用 sed 编辑配置文件来关闭冗余输出：
    代码（原文）
    $ kubectl run perf --labels "app.kubernetes.io/name=test" \
--image kalilinux/kali-rolling -- sleep infinity
pod/perf created
$ kubectl exec -it perf -- bash
┌──(root㉿perf)-[/]
└─# apt update && apt install -y siege
[output omitted]
┌──(root㉿perf)-[/]
└─# siege --config
[output omitted]
┌──(root㉿perf)-[/]
└─# sed -i 's/verbose = true/verbose = false/g' ~/.siege/siege.conf
    Siege is a benchmarking tool capable of performing large load tests on web servers. As with all security and testing tools, we don’t advise running them on servers you don’t control. You’re not likely to be able to cause enough traffic by yourself for a large website to be adversely affected, but you might get blocked by automated protection systems. Running these tests on your company’s internal servers may also get you an annoyed email from your IT department.
    Siege 是一个能够在 Web 服务器上执行大型负载测试的基准测试工具。与所有安全和测试工具一样，我们不建议在你无法控制的服务器上运行它们。你自己产生的流量不太可能多到对大型网站造成不利影响，但你可能会被自动化保护系统拦截。在公司内部服务器上运行这些测试，也可能会让你收到 IT 部门语气不悦的邮件。
    This testing was performed on a kind cluster inside a Linux virtual machine running on a Mac. This is far from an ideal case for Cilium’s deployment and performance. The results here, including the scale of the difference, will be much worse than in real deployments. The key point is that there is a difference in performance when using layer 7 policy, not the absolute numbers.
    本次测试是在运行于 Mac 上的 Linux 虚拟机内的 kind 集群中执行的。这 远非 Cilium 部署和性能的理想场景。此处的结果，包括差异的幅度，会比真实部署中的情况糟糕得多。关键要点在于，使用第 7 层策略确实会带来性能差异，而不是具体的绝对值。
    We’re going to keep our single replica on a single node. First we’ll reset the network policy to be the pure layer 3 approach from Chapter 12 (webserver-8080from-endpoints.yaml) and perform a simple test by using kubectl exec in our performance testing pod:
    我们保持单副本、单节点。首先，我们将网络策略重置为第 12 章中纯第 3 层的方式（webserver-8080-from-endpoints.yaml），然后在我们的性能测试 Pod 中通过 kubectl exec 执行一个简单测试：
    代码（原文）
    $ kubectl apply --filename webserver-8080-from-endpoints.yaml
ciliumnetworkpolicy.cilium.io/webserver configured
$ kubectl exec perf -- siege --benchmark --concurrent 25 --reps 100 \

    We’ve gotten some pretty useful information out of this test. We told it to use 25 concurrent connections with 100 attempts each, meaning 2,500 total connections. From this we see 244.86 transactions per second with a response time of around 30 ms. Now let’s apply our layer 7 policy (webserver-http-rule.yaml) and retry:
    从这个测试中我们得到了一些非常有用的信息。我们告诉它使用 25 个并发连接，每个连接尝试 100 次，也就是总共 2500 个连接。从结果中我们可以看到每秒 244.86 个事务，响应时间约为 30 ms。现在让我们应用第 7 层策略（webserver-http-rule.yaml）并重试：
    代码（原文）
    $ kubectl apply -f webserver-http-rule.yaml
ciliumnetworkpolicy.cilium.io/webserver configured
$ kubectl exec perf -- siege --benchmark --concurrent 25 --reps 100 \

** SIEGE 4.1.6
** Preparing 25 concurrent users for battle.
The server is now under siege...
