    We can see a big difference, with our transaction rate being cut in half, although the individual response time is still around 20 ms. Again, these numbers are not representative of real workloads, and it’s not generally true that adding layer 7 policy reduces performance by 50%. You’ll need to test throughput in your particular environment, and the results are highly dependent on the hardware configuration used. However, we did want to illustrate that there is a performance cost to using layer 7 policy.
    我们可以看到明显的差异，事务率降低了一半，不过单个响应时间仍然在 20 ms 左右。再次强调，这些数字并不代表真实工作负载的情况，而且添加第 7 层策略会导致性能下降 50% 也不是普遍规律。你需要在自己的特定环境中测试吞吐量，结果高度依赖所用的硬件配置。不过，我们确实想说明使用第 7 层策略是有性能代价的。

    DNS Policy
    DNS 策略

    Cilium can also intercept and handle other types of layer 7 traffic, including DNS traffic. DNS rules allow us to restrict the kinds of DNS queries that a client can make. Let’s rewrite our policy (dns-policy.yaml) to allow egress traffic on ports 80 and 443 to any destination, but restrict DNS requests (on port 53) to only cilium.io:
    Cilium 还可以拦截和处理其他类型的第 7 层流量，包括 DNS 流量。DNS 规则允许我们限制客户端可以发起的 DNS 查询类型。让我们重写策略（dns-policy.yaml），允许向任意目的地的 80 和 443 端口发起出站流量，但将 DNS 请求（53 端口）限制为仅能访问 cilium.io：
    代码（原文）
    apiVersion: cilium.io/v2
kind: CiliumNetworkPolicy
metadata:
name: http-egress
spec:
endpointSelector:
matchLabels:
app.kubernetes.io/name: test
egress:
- toPorts:
- ports:
- port: "80"
protocol: TCP
- port: "443"
protocol: TCP
toEntities:
- world
- toPorts:
- ports:
- port: "53"
protocol: ANY
rules:
dns:
- matchPattern: "cilium.io"
toEndpoints:
- matchLabels:
k8s-app: kube-dns
io.kubernetes.pod.namespace: kube-system
    As in our CoreDNS policy in Chapter 12, we’ve set the protocol for port 53 to ANY to allow both TCP and UDP traffic. Let’s apply this policy, deploy a test pod, and run a few tests:
    与我们在第 12 章中的 CoreDNS 策略一样，我们将 53 端口的协议设置为 ANY 以允许 TCP 和 UDP 流量。让我们应用这个策略，部署一个测试 Pod，然后运行几个测试：
    代码（原文）
    $ kubectl apply --filename dns-policy.yaml
ciliumnetworkpolicy.cilium.io/http-egress created
$ kubectl run test -it --rm --labels "app.kubernetes.io/name=test" \
--image nicolaka/netshoot -- bash
test:~# curl -sI https://cilium.io. | head -n1
HTTP/2 200
test:~# curl -m1 https://example.com
curl: (6) Could not resolve host: example.com
    We’re able to connect to cilium.io, but not example.com. Note that we use cilium.io. in the curl command, with a trailing dot. Without the dot, the pod will first attempt to resolve cilium.io using its configured search domains, which will fail. Note that this blocking is only at the DNS level. Because egress is enabled via port 443, we can actually “smuggle” a DNS request out another way, bypassing this policy. For example, in our pod we can use DNS over HTTPS with a known HTTPS resolver at 1.1.1.1, and then use the IP we’re given to directly communicate with our intended host with no DNS request:
    我们能够连接到 cilium.io，但无法连接到 example.com。注意我们在 curl 命令中使用了 cilium.io.，末尾带一个点。如果没有这个点，Pod 会先尝试使用其配置的搜索域来解析 cilium.io，这会失败。还需要注意的是，这种拦截 仅发生在 DNS 层面。因为 443 端口的出站是开放的，我们实际上可以通过另一种方式"偷渡"DNS 请求，绕过这个策略。例如，在我们的 Pod 中，可以使用 DNS over HTTPS，通过已知的 HTTPS 解析器 1.1.1.1 来查询，然后用得到的 IP 直接与目标主机通信，而不需要 DNS 请求：
    代码（原文）
    test:~# curl -s -H 'accept: application/dns-json' \
'https://1.1.1.1/dns-query?name=example.com&type=A' | jq -r \
'.Answer[0].data'
23.220.75.245
test:~# curl -s --resolve example.com:443:23.220.75.245 https://example.com
[...]
<head><title>Example Domain</title>
    Coincidentally, many content filtering systems operate using only DNS blocking. Please follow the rules of common sense when it comes to bypassing security tooling, and if you get an uncomfortable visit from your corporate IT department, don’t blame us.
    巧合的是，许多内容过滤系统也仅通过 DNS 拦截来运行。在绕过安全工具方面，请遵守常识规则；如果你因此被公司 IT 部门找上，别怪我们。

    FQDN Policy
    FQDN 策略

    Cilium has an alternative to pushing all egress traffic through a layer 7 proxy to check the host field—one that works even for non-HTTP traffic. It ties back to Cilium’s understanding of identity, discussed in Chapter 12. If we want pods to be able to reach cilium.io but we don’t want to allow other egress, we can look up the IP address for that domain and use a CIDR policy to allow only access to it. If all we want is host-based security, not filtering based on other factors of the HTTP request, such as the path or method, then this approach is fine. There’s no Envoy interception, so it’s more performant and reliable, and we don’t need to intercept every port 80 or port 443 egress connection to make it work. The only problem is the static IPs in such policies. We have to know somehow that those IPs are the correct ones, and we must remember to update them if things change. If the cluster is dual stack, we need to handle IPv6 addresses too. If only we could somehow automate that synchronization, we could make this approach much more robust. Enter FQDN policy.
    Cilium 还有一种替代方案，不需要将所有出站流量都推入第 7 层代理来检查 host 字段——这种方案甚至适用于非 HTTP 流量。它可以追溯到我们在第 12 章中讨论过的 Cilium 对身份的理解。如果我们希望 Pod 能够访问 cilium.io，但又不想允许其他出站流量，我们可以查询该域名的 IP 地址，然后使用 CIDR 策略来只允许访问它。如果我们只需要基于主机的安全，而不需要根据 HTTP 请求的其他因素（如路径或方法）进行过滤，那么这种方案就足够好了。它没有 Envoy 拦截，因此性能更好、更可靠，而且我们不需要拦截 每个 80 或 443 端口的出站连接就能让它生效。唯一的问题在于这类策略中的 IP 是静态的。我们必须以某种方式确认这些 IP 是正确的，并且记得在情况变化时更新它们。如果集群是双栈的，我们还需要处理 IPv6 地址。要是能以某种方式自动完成这种同步，就能让这个方案变得更健壮。这就是 FQDN 策略。

    Writing FQDN Policy
    编写 FQDN 策略

    A fully qualified domain name (FQDN) is a complete and unambiguous domain name that uniquely identifies a host in the Domain Name System—something like cilium.io. or webserver.default.svc.cluster.local.. You’ll notice that the FQDNs used in this section all end with a dot. This is what distinguishes a “fully qualified” domain name (like cilium.io.) from a regular one (like cilium.io). The dot represents the root of the DNS hierarchy and is usually implicit, so in practice we often omit it. However, the presence or absence of the dot matters in the context of FQDN policy.
    完全限定域名（FQDN）是在域名系统中唯一标识一台主机的完整且无歧义的域名——比如 cilium.io. 或 webserver.default.svc.cluster.local.。你会注意到本节中使用的 FQDN 都以点结尾。这就是"完全限定"域名（如 cilium.io.）与普通域名（如 cilium.io）的区别所在。这个点代表 DNS 层级的根，通常是隐含的，所以在实践中我们经常省略它。不过，在 FQDN 策略的语境中，这个点的有无很重要。

    Non-FQDNs and Search Domains
    非 FQDN 与搜索域

    A non-fully qualified domain name (without a trailing dot) can be resolved using DNS search domains, where the resolver appends configured suffixes to the name until a match is found. We can see this behavior in action by using dig, a commandline tool for querying DNS servers, on a test pod. Note that the pod we’re using here doesn’t have the app.kubernetes.io/name label, so it won’t be selected by our DNS policy from the previous section (dns-policy.yaml):
    非完全限定域名（没有末尾的点）可以通过 DNS 搜索域来解析，解析器会不断为域名附加配置好的后缀，直到找到匹配为止。我们可以通过在测试 Pod 上使用 dig（一个用于查询 DNS 服务器的命令行工具）来实际观察这个行为。注意，我们这里使用的 Pod 没有 app.kubernetes.io/name 标签，因此不会被上一节的 DNS 策略选中（dns-policy.yaml）：
    代码（原文）
    $ kubectl run dnstest -it --rm --image nicolaka/netshoot -- bash
dnstest:~# cat /etc/resolv.conf
search default.svc.cluster.local svc.cluster.local cluster.local
nameserver 10.96.0.10
options ndots:5
dnstest:~# dig example +showsearch | grep "IN\tA"
;example.default.svc.cluster.local. IN  A
;example.svc.cluster.local.     IN      A
;example.cluster.local.         IN      A
;example.                       IN      A
dnstest:~# dig example. +showsearch | grep "IN\tA"
;example.                       IN      A
    By including the +showsearch query option, we instruct dig to display the sequence of domain names the resolver tries during resolution. When we request example. instead of example, the name is treated as fully qualified and no suffixes are appended to the domain name. Using FQDNs (including the trailing dot) becomes important when considering DNS caching behavior or building DNS-based tooling, as we’re about to.
    通过加入 +showsearch 查询选项，我们指示 dig 显示解析器在解析过程中尝试的域名序列。当我们请求 example. 而不是 example 时，该名称会被视为完全限定的，不会附加任何后缀。在考虑 DNS 缓存行为或构建基于 DNS 的工具时，使用 FQDN（包括末尾的点）就变得很重要，正如我们接下来要看到的。
    When a client attempts to reach a server, it will query its configured DNS server to resolve the requested domain name to an IP address and will then use that IP address to request the content. Cilium allows us to automate the connection between an FQDN lookup and a CIDR policy using a toFQDN rule. This is implemented using the built-in DNS proxy we introduced in Chapter 2. Let’s try it out (fqdn-policy.yaml):
    当客户端尝试访问服务器时，它会查询其配置的 DNS 服务器，将请求的域名解析为 IP 地址，然后使用该 IP 地址请求内容。Cilium 允许我们使用 toFQDN 规则自动建立 FQDN 查询与 CIDR 策略之间的关联。这是通过我们在第 2 章中介绍的内置 DNS 代理实现的。让我们试试看（fqdn-policy.yaml）：
    代码（原文）
    apiVersion: cilium.io/v2
kind: CiliumNetworkPolicy
metadata:
name: fqdn-egress
spec:
endpointSelector:
matchLabels:
app.kubernetes.io/name: test
egress:
- toFQDNs:
- matchName: "cilium.io"
toPorts:
- ports:
- port: "80"
protocol: TCP
- port: "443"
protocol: TCP
- toEndpoints:
- matchLabels:
k8s-app: kube-dns
io.kubernetes.pod.namespace: kube-system
toPorts:
- ports:
- port: "53"
protocol: ANY
rules:
dns:
- matchPattern: "*"
    We once again have two egress rules. The first is our FQDN rule, where we’re allowing the same two ports as before. The DNS rule is a bit different: we’re using a match pattern of * to allow all DNS requests. From the pod’s perspective this is functionally the same as not intercepting DNS requests at all, but this is a critical part of how FQDN policy functions. After applying this policy, we can test again. Remember to remove any leftover network policy from previous steps:
    我们又有两条出站规则。第一条是我们的 FQDN 规则，在这里我们允许与之前相同的两个端口。DNS 规则略有不同：我们使用匹配模式 * 来允许所有 DNS 请求。从 Pod 的角度来看，这在功能上与完全不拦截 DNS 请求是一样的，但这是 FQDN 策略运作的关键部分。应用完这个策略后，我们可以再次测试。记得清理掉之前步骤遗留的网络策略：
    代码（原文）
    $ kubectl apply --filename fqdn-policy.yaml
ciliumnetworkpolicy.cilium.io/fqdn-egress created
$ kubectl run test -it --rm --labels "app.kubernetes.io/name=test" \
--image nicolaka/netshoot -- bash
test:~# curl -sI https://cilium.io | head -n1
HTTP/2 200
test:~# curl -m1 https://example.com
curl: (28) Connection timed out after 1001 milliseconds
    Our request to the allowed domain worked, while our request to example.com was rejected. We also can test that the technique we used earlier to bypass DNS policy doesn’t work here:
    我们对允许域名的请求成功了，而对 example.com 的请求被拒绝了。我们还可以测试之前用来绕过 DNS 策略的技巧在这里是否行不通：
    代码（原文）
    test:~# curl -m1 -H 'accept: application/dns-json' \
'https://1.1.1.1/dns-query?name=example.com&type=A'
curl: (28) Connection timed out after 1000 milliseconds
test:~# curl -m1 --resolve example.com:443:23.220.75.245 https://example.com
curl: (28) Connection timed out after 1001 milliseconds
    We can’t access our DNS server, and even if we could, or if we got the IP through some other mechanism, we wouldn’t be able to access it.
    我们无法访问 DNS 服务器，而且即便我们能访问，或者通过其他方式获得了 IP，我们也无法用它来访问目标站点。

    FQDN Identity
    FQDN 身份

    Functionally, FQDN policy works much like a CIDR policy that auto-updates based on the response to the DNS request that’s being proxied by the node. In Figure 13-2, we walk through the expected flow of a request and see how Cilium updates itself to track these details.
    从功能上讲，FQDN 策略的运作方式很像一种 CIDR 策略，它会根据被节点代理的 DNS 请求响应自动更新。在图 13-2 中，我们走一遍请求的预期流程，看看 Cilium 是如何更新自身来追踪这些细节的。
    Let’s go through this step by step: The container makes a DNS request for an A record for cilium.io.. This leaves the pod via a UDP packet destined for the CoreDNS service IP. The eBPF datapath translates the CoreDNS service IP to the CoreDNS pod’s IP, then applies policy. It uses the IP cache (discussed in Chapter 12) to map that IP to the pod’s identity, and uses the policy map for the pod to determine what to do with the packet. Because of our DNS filtering rule, the eBPF datapath forwards the packet to the node’s Cilium agent. The Cilium agent checks the DNS request against its internal policy map to determine if the DNS name is permitted, and if it is, it proxies the request on to the destination DNS server. The cluster’s DNS server responds, as it’s not a local endpoint it has an entry for, and if it doesn’t have the name cached, it reaches out to its configured upstream resolver to get the public IP hosting cilium.io.. The agent reads the response from CoreDNS and updates the FQDN cache with the mapping of the returned IP to cilium.io.. If an identity already exists for the FQDN, it will update the IP cache to map this IP to that identity. Otherwise, it will create a new identity and map the IP to it before responding. This may trigger a policy regeneration on the node, for example if the new identity needs to be inserted into the pod’s policy map. The pod gets its response to the DNS request and immediately makes a TCP connection to the IP. The packet once again transits the eBPF datapath. This time, the IP resolves to the FQDN identity and the policy map allows the traffic out to the external endpoint. We can see from this how Cilium intercepts the DNS traffic and uses it to build automatically updating mappings from special FQDN identities to IP addresses. A side effect of this FQDN identity mapping is that the resulting traffic is evaluated based on layer 3 rules. Therefore, this method works even for traffic that is not HTTP, including FTP, SSH, and other protocols.
    让我们一步步过一遍：容器为 cilium.io. 的 A 记录发起 DNS 请求。这个请求以一个 UDP 数据包的形式，发往 CoreDNS 服务的 IP，离开了 Pod。eBPF 数据路径将 CoreDNS 服务 IP 转换为 CoreDNS Pod 的 IP，然后应用策略。它使用 IP 缓存（在第 12 章中讨论过）将该 IP 映射到 Pod 的身份，并使用该 Pod 的策略映射来决定如何处理这个数据包。由于我们的 DNS 过滤规则，eBPF 数据路径将数据包转发到节点上的 Cilium Agent。Cilium Agent 检查 DNS 请求是否与其内部策略映射允许的 DNS 名称匹配；如果允许，它就将请求代理到目标 DNS 服务器。集群的 DNS 服务器做出响应——由于它不是有记录的本地端点，而且没有缓存这个名称，所以它会联系其配置的上游解析器来获取托管 cilium.io. 的公网 IP。Agent 读取来自 CoreDNS 的响应，并将返回的 IP 到 cilium.io. 的映射更新到 FQDN 缓存中。如果该 FQDN 已有身份，它将更新 IP 缓存，把这个 IP 映射到那个身份。否则，它会创建一个新身份，先把 IP 映射到该身份，然后再返回响应。这可能会触发节点上的策略重新生成，例如当新身份需要插入 Pod 的策略映射时。Pod 获得 DNS 请求的响应后，立即向该 IP 发起 TCP 连接。数据包再次经过 eBPF 数据路径。这一次，该 IP 会解析到 FQDN 身份，策略映射允许该流量出站发往外部端点。从中我们可以看到 Cilium 是如何拦截 DNS 流量，并利用它自动维护从特殊 FQDN 身份到 IP 地址的映射的。这种 FQDN 身份映射的一个副作用是，由此产生的流量是根据第 3 层规则来评估的。因此，这个方法甚至适用于非 HTTP 流量，包括 FTP、SSH 以及其他协议。

    FQDN Cache Poisoning
    FQDN 缓存中毒

    When using this kind of DNS interception with FQDN policy, you should take care to restrict it only to trusted DNS servers. If you allow DNS interception to any DNS servers, an attacker could use a poisoned DNS server to inject arbitrary IP addresses into the FQDN→IP mapping, allowing for policy bypass. For example, if a compromised client is only allowed to egress to api.example but the attacker controls a DNS server, they could make a DNS request for api.example to their poisoned server and associate any IP with that address, thereby allowing the workload to make a request to any location via CIDR. This won’t allow bypassing egress policy to in-cluster endpoints because they can’t overlap with FQDN identities. This is particularly bad if you’re using toFQDN rules in an egress deny policy. If the pods backing a service are not permitted to access api.example but can access metrics.example, then a malicious pod on the same node could use this technique to make a mapping for api.example that resolves to the same IP as metrics.example, and because these mappings are node-wide, the first pod would have its access blocked at layer 3. These are edge cases, and they can be avoided by restricting DNS queries to the cluster’s standard CoreDNS proxy—a good practice anyway, as arbitrary DNS requests could be used for data exfiltration.
    在使用这种配合 FQDN 策略的 DNS 拦截时，你应当注意将其限制在可信的 DNS 服务器范围内。如果你允许对任意 DNS 服务器进行 DNS 拦截，攻击者就可能使用被污染的 DNS 服务器向 FQDN→IP 映射中注入任意 IP 地址，从而绕过策略。例如，如果一个被攻陷的客户端仅被允许向 api.example 出站，但攻击者控制了一个 DNS 服务器，他们就可以向该被污染的服务器发起对 api.example 的 DNS 请求，将任意 IP 与该地址关联，从而让该工作负载能够通过 CIDR 向任意位置发起请求。这不会允许绕过发往集群内端点的出站策略，因为它们不能与 FQDN 身份重叠。如果你在出站 拒绝策略中使用 toFQDN 规则，问题会尤其严重。如果某个服务后端的 Pod 不允许访问 api.example 但允许访问 metrics.example，那么同一节点上的一个恶意 Pod 就可以利用该技巧为 api.example 建立一个与 metrics.example 相同 IP 的映射，而由于这些映射是节点范围的，第一个 Pod 的访问将在第 3 层被拦截。这些都是边缘场景，通过将 DNS 查询限制在集群标准的 CoreDNS 代理上就可以避免——这本身就是一个好习惯，因为任意 DNS 请求可能被用来泄露数据。

    Content Delivery Networks
    内容分发网络

    In the modern era, many websites are hosted on third-party content delivery networks (CDNs) such as Cloudflare, Amazon Cloudfront, or Netlify. These products provide a range of services, including acting as distributed global caches to reduce load on backend services, caching content geographically closer to users for lower latency, implementing geo-blocks, and providing DDoS protection. All of these ultimately rely on the CDN provider terminating TCP and TLS connections on their content network and the customer updating their DNS names to resolve to the CDN’s endpoints. This can pose a problem for toFQDN policy, as the IP address for a particular FQDN may be shared by many sites.
    在当今时代，许多网站都托管在 Cloudflare、Amazon Cloudfront 或 Netlify 等第三方内容分发网络（CDN）上。这些产品提供一系列服务，包括作为分布式全球缓存以降低后端服务的负载、将内容缓存在地理上更靠近用户的位置以降低延迟、实施地理封锁，以及提供 DDoS 防护。所有这些最终都依赖于 CDN 提供商在其内容网络上终止 TCP 和 TLS 连接，以及客户更新其 DNS 名称以解析到 CDN 的端点。这会给 toFQDN 策略带来一个问题，因为某个特定 FQDN 的 IP 地址可能被多个站点共享。
    CDN collisions are difficult to demonstrate since they’re dependent on the DNS setup, but the following example works at the time of writing.
    CDN 冲突很难演示，因为它们取决于 DNS 的配置，但下面这个例子在写作之时是成立的。
    For example, it just so happens that cilium.io and isovalent.com are hosted on the same CDN. Let’s see what happens if we deploy a test pod in our cluster with the same FQDN policy from earlier (fqdn-policy.yaml) and attempt to access both of these domains:
    例如，碰巧 cilium.io 和 isovalent.com 托管在同一个 CDN 上。让我们看看如果在集群中部署一个带有前面相同 FQDN 策略的测试 Pod（fqdn-policy.yaml），并尝试访问这两个域名会发生什么：
    代码（原文）
    $ kubectl run test -it --rm --labels "app.kubernetes.io/name=test" \
--image nicolaka/netshoot -- bash
test:~# curl -sI https://cilium.io | head -n1
HTTP/2 200
test:~# curl -m1 https://isovalent.com
curl: (28) Connection timed out after 1001 milliseconds
test:~# curl -s --resolve "isovalent.com:443:$(dig +short cilium.io)" \
https://isovalent.com | \
grep -Eo 'Isovalent was founded[[:alnum:][:space:]]*' | head -n1
Isovalent was founded by the creators and maintainers of Cilium and eBPF
    The first request to cilium.io succeeds because it matches the allowed FQDN policy. The direct request to isovalent.com fails, as expected, because that domain is not permitted. However, by forcing cURL to resolve isovalent.com using the IP for cilium.io, we bypass the FQDN policy. Because both sites share a CDN, the CDN can serve traffic for isovalent.com from either IP address. In practice, this kind of bypass is only possible in cases where domains share CDN IPs and the client deliberately overrides DNS resolution. It’s also dependent on CDN behavior. If you need to be absolutely sure that clients are accessing the correct domain, you’ll have to use a layer 7 HTTP policy that filters on the host field and proxies through Envoy. This issue can also occur if you’re intercepting external traffic through a non-Cilium layer 7 egress proxy outside of the cluster, as that may cause all external addresses to resolve to the same IP address from the perspective of workloads in the cluster.
    第一个对 cilium.io 的请求成功了，因为它匹配了允许的 FQDN 策略。直接对 isovalent.com 的请求如预期失败了，因为该域名不被允许。但是，通过强制 cURL 使用 cilium.io 的 IP 来解析 isovalent.com，我们绕过了 FQDN 策略。由于两个站点共享同一个 CDN，CDN 可以从任一 IP 地址为 isovalent.com 提供流量。在实践中，这种绕过只有在域名共享 CDN IP 且客户端故意覆盖 DNS 解析的情况下才有可能发生。它还取决于 CDN 的行为。如果你需要绝对确保客户端访问的是正确的域名，你就必须使用按 host 字段过滤并通过 Envoy 代理的第 7 层 HTTP 策略。如果你通过集群外的非 Cilium 第 7 层出站代理拦截外部流量，也可能出现这个问题，因为从集群内工作负载的角度来看，所有外部地址都会解析到同一个 IP 地址。

    TLS Interception
    TLS 拦截

    TLS interception1 is when Cilium’s Envoy proxy terminates outbound TLS connections from workloads (using certificates signed by an internal CA), inspects the decrypted traffic for layer 7 policy enforcement, and then establishes a separate TLS connection to forward the request to the external destination. In this example, we’re going to restrict a pod’s egress traffic to an external HTTPS API. Figure 13-3 shows the certificates and trust bundles we’ll need to configure to:
    TLS 拦截1 是指 Cilium 的 Envoy 代理终止来自工作负载的出站 TLS 连接（使用内部 CA 签发的证书），对解密后的流量进行七层检查以执行策略，然后再建立一条新的 TLS 连接将请求转发到外部目标。在本例中，我们将把 Pod 的出站流量限制到一个外部 HTTPS API。图 13-3 展示了需要配置的证书与信任包：
    • Provide Cilium with a set of public CA certificates to trust for the upstream
      向 Cilium 提供一组用于信任上游的公共 CA 证书
    connection. Without this trust bundle, Cilium won’t be able to verify the identity of the upstream provider.
    连接。如果没有这个信任包，Cilium 将无法验证上游提供者的身份。
    • Provide Cilium with a certificate to present for the proxied connection.
      向 Cilium 提供一份证书，在代理连接时出示。
    • Override the client pod’s trust bundle with the CA for the provided certificate.
      用所提供证书对应的 CA 覆盖客户端 Pod 的信任包。
    1Sometimes anachronistically referred to as “man-in-the-middle.”
    1有时也被不合时宜地称为"中间人"。
    A trust bundle is a group of certificate authorities that a given client trusts to issue certificates for a TLS connection. Most containers are built with a trust bundle of commonly trusted public certificate authorities, provided by the underlying Linux distribution used to build them.
    信任包是一组证书颁发机构，客户端信任它们为 TLS 连接签发证书。大多数容器在构建时会内置一个包含常用公共证书颁发机构的信任包，这些 CA 由构建容器所用的底层 Linux 发行版提供。

    Secrets and Security in Cilium
    Cilium 中的密钥与安全

    As you know, Cilium deploys a single Envoy instance per node that performs all layer 7 filtering for workloads on that node, configured by the Cilium agent on the node. All Cilium agent instances use the same Kubernetes service account, so they share the same Kubernetes permissions. Because any node could, in theory, host any workload, all Envoy instances need to include any secrets used by any TLS policy. This presents a problem, as permission to read secrets in Kubernetes is a very broad permission that carries a high risk of exposing sensitive data. To mitigate this risk, Cilium does not grant the agent access to all secrets by default. Instead, it’s granted read access to all secrets in a special namespace, usually cilium-secrets. We can either write our secrets into that namespace for Cilium to use or make use of Cilium’s secret sync feature to copy relevant secrets into the namespace. (This requires that the Cilium operator has the ability to read all secrets, but since the operator isn’t directly exposed to any external connections, this presents a smaller “attack surface.”)
    如你所知，Cilium 在每个节点上部署一个 Envoy 实例，负责该节点上所有工作负载的七层过滤，由节点上的 Cilium Agent 进行配置。所有 Cilium Agent 实例共用同一个 Kubernetes Service Account，因此它们拥有相同的 Kubernetes 权限。由于理论上任何节点都可以承载任何工作负载，所有 Envoy 实例都需要加载任何 TLS 策略所使用的密钥。这就带来一个问题：在 Kubernetes 中读取 Secret 的权限范围很广，一旦被滥用，极有可能泄露敏感数据。为降低这一风险，Cilium 默认不会授予 Agent 访问所有 Secret 的权限。相反，Agent 仅被授予读取一个特殊命名空间（通常是 cilium-secrets）中所有 Secret 的权限。我们可以将密钥直接写入该命名空间供 Cilium 使用，或者利用 Cilium 的 Secret 同步功能将相关密钥复制到该命名空间。（这要求 Cilium Operator 具备读取所有 Secret 的能力，但由于 Operator 不直接暴露于任何外部连接，其"攻击面"相对较小。）
    You can still grant Envoy permissions to read from all namespaces if you want, by setting the Helm value tls.readSecretsOnlyFromSecretsNamespace=false. It’s not a recommended approach, but if it’s preferable to copying the secrets you can configure Cilium in this way.
    如果你愿意，也可以通过设置 Helm 值 tls.readSecretsOnlyFromSecretsNamespace=false 来允许 Envoy 从所有命名空间读取。这不是推荐的做法，但如果你觉得复制密钥更麻烦，可以按这种方式配置 Cilium。
    In this example we have full control of the certificates, so we’re going to disable secret sync and populate our secrets directly into the cilium-secrets namespace. The eventual approach will be as seen in Figure 13-4. Note that this diagram includes only trust bundles and certificates that will be read by Cilium, not the trust bundle for the internal issuer that will be mounted into the client pod.
    在本例中我们完全控制证书，因此会禁用 Secret 同步，将密钥直接写入 cilium-secrets 命名空间。最终的方案如图 13-4 所示。请注意，此图仅包含 Cilium 将读取的信任包和证书，不包含将挂载到客户端 Pod 中的内部签发方信任包。

    Writing HTTPS Policy
    编写 HTTPS 策略

    We can use the cert-manager and trust-manager projects to create our internal CA, sign certificates, and distribute the trust bundles. Set up a test cluster with kind and configure Cilium with tls.secretSync.enabled: false. Once Cilium is installed, install cert-manager and trust-manager. We’re installing them using the oci:// paths instead of using a Helm repo (the recommended method from the cert-manager documentation), so no helm repo add command is required:
    我们可以使用 cert-manager 和 trust-manager 项目来创建内部 CA、签发证书并分发信任包。使用 kind 搭建一个测试集群，并通过 tls.secretSync.enabled: false 配置 Cilium。Cilium 安装完成后，安装 cert-manager 和 trust-manager。这里使用 oci:// 路径进行安装（cert-manager 文档推荐的方式），而不是通过 Helm 仓库，因此不需要执行 helm repo add 命令：
    代码（原文）
    $ helm upgrade --install \
cert-manager oci://quay.io/jetstack/charts/cert-manager \
--version v1.19.0 \
--namespace cert-manager \
--create-namespace \
--set crds.enabled=true
$ helm upgrade --install \
trust-manager oci://quay.io/jetstack/charts/trust-manager \
--version v0.20.2 \
--namespace cert-manager \
--set secretTargets.enabled=true \
--set "secretTargets.authorizedSecrets[0]=public-ca-bundle"
    We’re setting options on trust-manager to allow it to write to secrets. In this case, it’s only allowed to write a secret named public-ca-bundle in all namespaces. This is because the trust bundle that Cilium loads to verify the external TLS connection must be in a secret, not in a config map.
    我们为 trust-manager 配置了一些选项，以允许它向 Secret 写入数据。在本例中，它只被允许在所有命名空间中写入名为 public-ca-bundle 的 Secret。这是因为 Cilium 加载的、用于验证外部 TLS 连接的信任包必须存在于 Secret 中，而不是 ConfigMap。

    Public Trust
    公共信任

    Getting a public trust bundle is the easiest step. We’ll use trust-manager’s default public CA bundle (tls/public-ca-bundle.yaml) and have it inject the certificates from the bundle into a secret in the cilium-secrets namespace:2
    获取公共信任包是最简单的一步。我们将使用 trust-manager 的默认公共 CA 包（tls/public-ca-bundle.yaml），让它把该包中的证书注入到 cilium-secrets 命名空间的一个 Secret 中：2
    代码（原文）
    apiVersion: trust.cert-manager.io/v1alpha1
kind: Bundle
metadata:
name: public-ca-bundle
spec:
sources:
- useDefaultCAs: true
target:
secret:
key: "trust-bundle.pem"
namespaceSelector:
    2See the trust-manager documentation for details on how the useDefaultCAs source is populated and how to keep it updated.
    2关于 useDefaultCAs 源的填充方式以及如何保持其更新，请参见 trust-manager 文档。
    代码（原文）
    matchLabels:
secrets-namespace: "true"
    We’ll apply a custom label to the namespace so that trust-manager can find it. After creating the namespace and applying this bundle, we can see that the bundle and secret have been created:
    我们会给这个命名空间添加一个自定义标签，以便 trust-manager 能够找到它。创建命名空间并应用这个 Bundle 之后，我们可以看到 Bundle 和 Secret 都已创建完成：
    代码（原文）
    $ kubectl label --context kind-tls namespace cilium-secrets \
secrets-namespace="true"
namespace/cilium-secrets labeled
$ kubectl apply --context kind-tls --filename public-ca-bundle.yaml
bundle.trust.cert-manager.io/public-ca-bundle created
$ kubectl get --context kind-tls bundle
NAME               CONFIGMAP TARGET   SECRET TARGET   SYNCED   REASON   AGE
public-ca-bundle   trust-bundle.pem                   True     Synced   8m41s
$ kubectl get secrets --context kind-tls --namespace cilium-secrets
NAME               TYPE     DATA   AGE
public-ca-bundle   Opaque   1      39s
    We’ve chosen to include a standard bundle of public CAs, instead of only the ones for our targeted site. If you’re writing TLS policy for just one site, you could elect to include only the public CA for that site. However, this can be quite brittle as sites may change their public CA at any time without warning, and they won’t expect this change to affect users. If you control the external site as well, you might consider this option, but for a third-party public site, it’s best to use a standard bundle.
    我们选择了包含一组标准公共 CA，而不是只包含目标站点对应的那些。如果你只为某个站点编写 TLS 策略，也可以选择只包含该站点的公共 CA。但这种做法相当脆弱，因为站点可能随时在没有任何预警的情况下更换公共 CA，而它们并不会预料到这一变化会影响用户。如果你同时也控制着这个外部站点，可以考虑这种方案；但对于第三方公共站点，最好还是使用标准包。

    Internal CA
    内部 CA

    Now we need to create an internal certificate authority (tls/internal-ca.yaml), which we’ll use to sign certificates for outgoing connections. We can use cert-manager to create a self-signed issuer:
    现在我们需要创建一个内部证书颁发机构（tls/internal-ca.yaml），用来为出站连接签发证书。我们可以使用 cert-manager 创建一个自签名 Issuer：
    代码（原文）
    apiVersion: cert-manager.io/v1
kind: Issuer
metadata:
name: selfsigned
namespace: cert-manager
spec:
selfSigned: {}
    Then, we can use this self-signed issuer to create a root certificate, and finally create a certificate authority from this root:
    然后，用这个自签名 Issuer 创建根证书，最后从这个根证书创建一个证书颁发机构：
    代码（原文）
    apiVersion: cert-manager.io/v1
kind: Certificate
metadata:
name: root
namespace: cert-manager
spec:
isCA: true
commonName: internal-ca
secretName: root
privateKey:
algorithm: ECDSA
size: 256
rotationPolicy: never
issuerRef:
name: selfsigned
kind: Issuer
group: cert-manager.io
---
apiVersion: cert-manager.io/v1
kind: ClusterIssuer
metadata:
name: internal-ca
spec:
ca:
secretName: root
    We can now sign certificates for our intercepted TLS connections with this issuer. As we’re connecting to cilium.io, we’ll sign a certificate for that domain using our new internal certificate authority (tls/cilium-io-cert.yaml):
    现在我们就可以用这个 Issuer 为拦截后的 TLS 连接签发证书了。由于我们要连接到 cilium.io， 会用刚创建的内部证书颁发机构为该域名签发一份证书（tls/cilium-io-cert.yaml）：
    代码（原文）
    apiVersion: cert-manager.io/v1
kind: Certificate
metadata:
name: cilium-io-cert
namespace: kube-system
spec:
commonName: cilium.io
secretName: cilium-io-cert
privateKey:
algorithm: ECDSA
size: 256
issuerRef:
name: internal-ca
kind: Issuer
group: cert-manager.io
    We now have a root certificate, a CA for that root certificate, and a certificate issued by that CA for cilium.io, just as we described in Figure 13-3.
    现在我们拥有了根证书、基于该根证书的 CA，以及由该 CA 为 cilium.io 签发的证书，正如图 13-3 中描述的那样。

    Overriding Pod CA Certificates
    覆盖 Pod 的 CA 证书

    For our test pod to trust the internal CA, we’ll need to mount the CA certificate. We’ll use trust-manager again to populate a ConfigMap with the internal CA in it (tls/internal-ca-bundle.yaml). This will create the ConfigMap in every namespace, so any pod can mount it:
    为了让测试 Pod 信任这个内部 CA，我们需要挂载 CA 证书。我们再次使用 trust-manager，把包含内部 CA 的内容填充到一个 ConfigMap 中（tls/internal-ca-bundle.yaml）。这个 ConfigMap 会在每个命名空间中都创建一份，因此任何 Pod 都可以挂载它：
    代码（原文）
    apiVersion: trust.cert-manager.io/v1alpha1
kind: Bundle
metadata:
name: internal-ca-bundle
spec:
sources:
- secret:
name: root
key: "ca.crt"
target:
configMap:
key: "ca.crt"
    We don’t have to tell trust-manager which namespace the “root” secret for the internal CA is in—trust-manager can only read secrets from the “trust namespace,” which is the cert-manager namespace by default. See the trust-manager documentation for details. Next, we’ll create a pod and mount this ConfigMap (tls/test-pod.yaml). In netshoot’s case it’s a Debian-based image, and the trust bundle is expected to be at /etc/ssl/certs/ca-certificates.crt. By using a volume mount over this path, we can overwrite whatever TLS certificates are already in the container image with our new bundle:
    我们无需告诉 trust-manager 内部 CA 的"root" Secret 位于哪个命名空间——trust-manager 只能从"信任命名空间"中读取 Secret，默认是 cert-manager 命名空间，详见 trust-manager 文档。接下来，我们要创建一个 Pod 并挂载这个 ConfigMap（tls/test-pod.yaml）。以 netshoot 为例，它是基于 Debian 的镜像，信任包预期位于 /etc/ssl/certs/ca-certificates.crt。通过对这个路径使用卷挂载，我们可以用新的信任包覆盖容器镜像中原本的 TLS 证书：
    代码（原文）
    apiVersion: v1
kind: Pod
metadata:
labels:
app.kubernetes.io/name: test
name: test
spec:
containers:
- name: netshoot
image: nicolaka/netshoot
args:
- sleep
- infinity
volumeMounts:
- name: ca-certs
mountPath: /etc/ssl/certs/
volumes:
- name: ca-certs
configMap:
name: internal-ca-bundle
items:
- key: ca.crt
path: ca-certificates.crt
    Because we need to mount a volume, we can’t shortcut this with kubectl run, so create the pod manually and use kubectl exec instead. Note that we’ve fully replaced the container’s provided certificates. Because our internal bundle didn’t also contain the public certificates, this container will be unable to establish TLS connections to any public endpoints. To trust both internal and public endpoints, include the default CAs in the internal bundle.
    由于需要挂载卷，我们不能用 kubectl run 简化这一过程，所以要手动创建 Pod，然后改用 kubectl exec。请注意，我们完全替换了容器自带的证书。因为内部信任包没有 同时包含公共证书，这个容器将无法与任何公共端点建立 TLS 连接。如果要同时信任内部和公共端点，需要在内部信任包中包含默认 CA。

    TLS Policy
    TLS 策略

    We’ve got our pod running with an internal certificate authority present. We also have a certificate for cilium.io issued by that CA. We now have everything we need to intercept TLS and enforce policy at layer 7 for this pod. Suppose we want to only allow access to the Cilium blog on the /blog/ path. We’ll write our network policy just like a normal HTTP policy, but with two new statements, originatingTLS and terminatingTLS (tls/policy.yaml):
    我们的 Pod 已经在运行，并且拥有内部证书颁发机构。我们也已经有了由该 CA 为 cilium.io 签发的证书。现在一切就绪，可以对这个 Pod 进行 TLS 拦截并在七层执行策略。假设我们希望只允许访问 Cilium 博客的 /blog/ 路径。我们会像编写普通 HTTP 策略那样编写网络策略，但会多出两个新语句：originatingTLS 和 terminatingTLS（tls/policy.yaml）：
    代码（原文）
    apiVersion: cilium.io/v2
kind: CiliumNetworkPolicy
metadata:
name: client
spec:
endpointSelector:
matchLabels:
app.kubernetes.io/name: test
egress:
- toPorts:
- ports:
- port: "443"
protocol: TCP
originatingTLS:
secret:
namespace: cilium-secrets
name: public-ca-bundle
key: trust-bundle.pem
terminatingTLS:
secret:
namespace: cilium-secrets
name: cilium-io-cert
rules:
http:
- host: cilium.io
path: /blog/.*
- toPorts:
- ports:
- port: "53"
protocol: ANY
toEndpoints:
- matchLabels:
k8s-app: kube-dns
io.kubernetes.pod.namespace: kube-system
    If we apply this policy and exec into our test pod, we can view it all working as expected:
    如果应用这条策略并 exec 进入测试 Pod，就能看到一切都按预期运行：
    代码（原文）
    $ kubectl exec test -it -- bash
test:~# curl -sI https://cilium.io/blog/ | head -n1
HTTP/1.1 200 OK
test:~# curl -sI https://cilium.io/blog/2025/05/20/cilium-l7-policies/ | \
head -n1
HTTP/1.1 200 OK
test:~# curl -sI https://cilium.io | head -n1
HTTP/1.1 403 Forbidden
    Note that the URL scheme here was https://, cURL provided no certificate errors, and we didn’t ignore certificate errors in cURL with -k. As far as the client application was concerned, this was a perfectly normal TLS connection, intercepted by Cilium. With TLS connections, this kind of path-based policy isn’t possible without interception as the path being requested is part of the encrypted communication, and without terminating and re-originating TLS using a proxy the path cannot be observed in transit.
    请注意，这里的 URL 方案是 https://，cURL 没有报告任何证书错误，我们也没有在 cURL 中用 -k 忽略证书错误。就客户端应用而言，这是一条完全正常的 TLS 连接，只是被 Cilium 拦截了。对于 TLS 连接，如果不进行拦截，这种基于路径的策略是不可能实现的——因为请求的路径属于加密通信的一部分，不经由代理终止并重新发起 TLS，路径在传输过程中是无法被观察到的。

    Limitations
    局限性

    There are a number of limitations and drawbacks to TLS interception in Cilium. The major one is the intermediate certificate itself. While wildcard certificates are possible, most TLS clients will not accept multiple levels of wildcard for all possible combinations of subdomains. This means that you can’t use a single certificate for all TLS connections and instead have to use a certificate per domain. In the setup we used here, the certificate for cilium.io is presented for any connections to port 443. Here’s what will happen if we attempt to reach example.com:
    Cilium 的 TLS 拦截存在若干限制与不足。最主要的问题就是中间证书本身。虽然可以使用通配符证书，但大多数 TLS 客户端不会接受多层通配符来覆盖所有可能的子域组合。这意味着你不能用一张证书覆盖所有 TLS 连接，而必须按域名分别使用不同的证书。在我们这里所采用的方案中，对任何连接到 443 端口的请求都会出示为 cilium.io 配置的证书。如果我们尝试访问 example.com，会发生以下情况：
    代码（原文）
    test:~# curl https://example.com
curl: (60) SSL: certificate subject name 'cilium.io' does not match target
hostname 'example.com'
More details here: https://curl.se/docs/sslcerts.html
curl failed to verify the legitimacy of the server and therefore could not
establish a secure connection to it. To learn more about this situation and
how to fix it, please visit the webpage mentioned above.
    Even though we connected to example.com, Cilium presented the configured certificate for cilium.io. If we ignore the certificate error we can get as far as Envoy denying the access:
    尽管我们连接的是 example.com，Cilium 却出示了为 cilium.io 配置的证书。如果忽略这个证书错误，我们会走到 Envoy 拒绝访问这一步：
    代码（原文）
    test:~# curl -k https://example.com
Access denied
    This means that TLS certificate management can become difficult. It’s possible to combine layer 7 filtering with toFQDN rules to serve the correct TLS certificate, but there’s no SNI header capability in layer 7 policy to handle multiple certificates for different domain names. In general, TLS interception isn’t well suited to being a blanket policy, where all TLS traffic is intercepted. Instead, it’s best used for targeted policy where layer 3 or layer 4 policy isn’t enough.
    这意味着 TLS 证书管理会变得很困难。可以将七层过滤与 toFQDN 规则结合来出示正确的 TLS 证书，但七层策略并不具备 SNI 头部能力来为不同域名处理多张证书。一般来说，TLS 拦截不太适合作为一种覆盖所有 TLS 流量的通用策略，它更适合在三层或四层策略不够用时，作为针对性的策略来使用。

    Summary
    小结

    In this chapter we’ve covered a large number of advanced use cases for Cilium’s network policy. We’ve also expanded the identity model and filtering beyond the eBPF datapath and into the realm of layer 7 proxies. Both of these represent an expansion of Cilium’s scope, from a “simple” CNI to a fully fledged network appliance and a core part of modern cloud native networking infrastructure.
    本章我们讨论了 Cilium 网络策略的大量高级用例。我们还将身份模型与过滤从 eBPF 数据路径扩展到了七层代理领域。两者都标志着 Cilium 范围的延伸——从一个"简单"的 CNI 发展为一个功能完备的网络设备，并成为现代云原生网络基础设施的核心组成部分。

    第 14 章
    Transparent Encryption
    透明加密

    Cilium’s Transparent Encryption feature is capable of encrypting traffic as it transits between Cilium managed endpoints. It’s called transparent because it is not visible to the pods on each end and requires no coordination from the workloads themselves. Cilium encrypts packets as they leave the source node and decrypts them as they enter the destination node. It handles all aspects of the process, including key distribution and management. Transparent Encryption can use either WireGuard or IPsec as the underlying encryption technology. Both of these are industry-standard encapsulation methods. We’ll use WireGuard in this chapter, but IPsec has similar concepts and the same flows are encrypted with both. IPsec mode takes more work to set up, requiring the administrator to create shared secrets and configure encryption algorithms, but if you have regulatory reasons to require specific algorithms or key lengths it may be an attractive alternative to WireGuard’s automatic configuration. You can find all the YAML manifests you will use in this chapter, along with several convenient scripts, in the chapter14 directory of the book’s GitHub repository.
    Cilium 的透明加密功能能够对 Cilium 管理的端点之间传输的流量进行加密。它之所以被称为透明，是因为对两端的 Pod 而言该过程不可见，且无需工作负载自身进行任何协调。Cilium 在数据包离开源节点时进行加密，在进入目的节点时进行解密。它处理该过程的所有环节，包括密钥分发与管理。透明加密可以使用 WireGuard 或 IPsec 作为底层加密技术，二者都是行业标准的封装方法。本章将使用 WireGuard 进行说明，但 IPsec 的概念类似，且两种方式加密的流量相同。IPsec 模式的设置工作量更大，需要管理员创建共享密钥并配置加密算法，但如果你因监管原因而被要求使用特定算法或密钥长度，它可能是比 WireGuard 自动配置更具吸引力的替代方案。你可以在本书 GitHub 仓库的 chapter14 目录中找到本章使用的所有 YAML 清单文件以及若干便捷脚本。

    Use Cases
    应用场景

    The two primary use cases for Transparent Encryption are to meet a compliance requirement for in-flight encryption or as part of an overall security posture. The two motivations often coexist. We recommend keeping focused on what you are specifically protecting against instead of enabling encryption simply because of a vague sense that it is “more secure.” In this section, we will briefly discuss compliance and threat modeling and highlight how Cilium can help. However, these are complex topics, and this book will not go into great detail on them. We recommend consulting additional resources for further information, such as Threat Modeling by Izar Tarandach and Matthew J. Coles (O’Reilly).
    透明加密的两个主要应用场景是满足传输中加密的合规要求，或作为整体安全态势的一部分。这两种动机通常同时存在。我们建议你专注于自己具体要防范什么，而不是仅仅因为模糊地觉得加密"更安全"就启用它。在本节中，我们将简要讨论合规和威胁建模，并强调 Cilium 能如何提供帮助。不过，这些都是复杂话题，本书不会深入展开。我们建议查阅额外资源以获取更多信息，例如 Izar Tarandach 和 Matthew J. Coles 所著的 Threat Modeling（O'Reilly）。

    Compliance
    合规性

    Compliance frameworks are lists of requirements and controls, usually maintained by a third-party organization and often enforced by external auditors. They are common in many countries for regulated industries such as healthcare and finance, where failure to comply can have a significant impact on an organization’s ability to do business. They can also be transitive, such that if your organization provides services to a regulated organization, you may need to follow the same rules as a condition of doing business with them. Compliance frameworks often mandate exact technical details or architectures, such as listing cryptographic ciphers or key lengths that are acceptable. When assessing Transparent Encryption against a compliance framework, it’s important to open a discussion with your auditors to understand exactly what the requirements are and how Cilium can be configured to meet them. Transparent Encryption is often useful in situations where a platform team that manages the cluster is distinct from application teams that manage the workloads. In this model, the platform team can enforce an “encryption in flight” requirement across the whole cluster without having to coordinate with each individual application team to ensure that they are correctly implementing encryption in their applications.
    合规框架是一系列需求和控制措施的列表，通常由第三方组织维护，并经常由外部审计师强制执行。在许多国家，这类框架在医疗和金融等受监管行业中十分常见，不符合合规要求可能会严重影响组织开展业务的能力。它们也可能具有传递性，即如果你的组织为一家受监管的组织提供服务，你可能需要遵循相同的规则，作为与其开展业务的条件。合规框架通常会明确规定技术细节或架构，例如列出可接受的加密密码或密钥长度。在根据合规框架评估透明加密时，与你的审计师展开讨论非常重要，以便确切了解需求是什么，以及 Cilium 可以如何配置来满足这些需求。透明加密在以下场景中特别有用：管理集群的平台团队与管理工作负载的应用团队相互独立。在这种模型中，平台团队可以在整个集群范围内强制实施"传输中加密"的要求，而无需与每个独立的应用团队协调，以确保他们在自己的应用中正确实现了加密。

    Threat Models
    威胁模型

    As part of a larger security approach, Transparent Encryption can be used to preserve the confidentiality and integrity of data as it transits the underlying network infrastructure between Cilium nodes. This is useful for protecting against an attacker who may have gained the ability to observe or modify data in transit, as shown in Figure 14-1, but who doesn’t have elevated permissions on the nodes themselves.
    作为更大安全方案的一部分，透明加密可用于在数据在 Cilium 节点之间的底层网络基础设施中传输时保护其机密性和完整性。这对于防范可能已经获得观察或修改传输中数据的能力但在节点本身没有提升权限的攻击者非常有用，如图 14-1 所示。
    From Cilium’s perspective, the nature of the underlying network fabric doesn’t matter. All that matters is that it provides layer 2 or layer 3 connectivity between nodes. It could be as simple as a single switch that connects each node together in a single layer 2 domain, or a complex leaf/spine setup with bonded connections to each node. Some features, such as BGP announcement and native routing, may require other features of the underlying fabric.
    从 Cilium 的角度来看，底层网络结构的性质并不重要。重要的只是它提供节点之间的二层或三层连接。它可以像一台将所有节点连接到同一个二层域的单一交换机那样简单，也可以像每个节点都有绑定连接的复杂叶脊（leaf/spine）架构那样复杂。某些特性（如 BGP 通告和原生路由）可能需要底层结构的其他特性支持。
    If the Cilium nodes are virtual machines, it might matter how those virtual machines are attached to the physical network. As shown in Figure 14-2, if the hypervisor provides networking and acts as a switch,1 an attacker could observe packets here instead.
    如果 Cilium 节点是虚拟机，这些虚拟机如何连接到物理网络可能会变得相关。如图 14-2 所示，如果虚拟机管理程序提供网络功能并充当交换机1，攻击者就可能在这里观察数据包。

    Traffic Flow
    流量流向

    Transparent Encryption does not use mutual TLS (mTLS) and does not require a layer 7 proxy to establish a TCP session with the workload. When a packet is sent between managed pods on two different nodes, Cilium encrypts the original packet and encapsulates it in a new WireGuard packet. This new packet is sent to the remote node, and the content is decapsulated and decrypted before being delivered to the target pod.
    透明加密不使用双向 TLS（mTLS），也不需要七层代理与工作负载建立 TCP 会话。当在两个不同节点上的受管理 Pod 之间发送数据包时，Cilium 会将原始数据包加密并封装在一个新的 WireGuard 数据包中。这个新数据包会被发送到远程节点，在被交付到目标 Pod 之前，其内容会被解封装和解密。
    1In some hypervisor setups, individual virtual machines are exposed directly to the underlying network using technologies such as SR-IOV. This is highly implementation-specific and depends on the hardware, network interfaces, hypervisor, and underlying network design.
    1在某些虚拟机管理程序的设置中，单个虚拟机会使用 SR-IOV 等技术直接暴露到底层网络。这与具体实现高度相关，取决于硬件、网络接口、虚拟机管理程序和底层网络设计。
    It’s important to note that packets are only encrypted if they are traveling between two Cilium-managed pods and those pods are on different nodes. Packets are not encrypted if they are traveling between:
    需要注意的是，只有在数据包在两个 Cilium 管理的 Pod 之间传输且这些 Pod 位于不同节点时才会进行加密。在以下情况之间传输的数据包不会被加密：
    • Pods on the same node
      同一节点上的 Pod
    • A pod and a host process or host network namespace pod
      Pod 与宿主机进程或宿主网络命名空间 Pod
    • Two host processes or host network namespace pods
      两个宿主机进程或宿主网络命名空间 Pod
    • Any process and a destination outside of the cluster
      任意进程与集群外的目标
    • Any communication that does not use the network, such as HTTP over UNIX
      不使用网络的通信，如基于 UNIX
    socket
    套接字
    • Processes within the same pod
      同一 Pod 内的进程
    Figure 14-3 shows these flows with only the cross-node communication between pods in a cluster passing through the encrypted WireGuard tunnel.
    图 14-3 展示了这些流量场景，其中只有集群中 Pod 之间的跨节点通信会经过加密的 WireGuard 隧道。
    A common question is why Cilium does not encrypt traffic between pods on the same node. It may seem like a strange omission, but the rationale is simple: when packets travel between pods on the same node, Cilium’s eBPF datapath moves them directly from one pod’s network interface to the other pod’s, in kernel. There is no Linux bridge, virtual switch, or other networking system between the pods. If Cilium did perform encryption here, it would encrypt traffic only to immediately decrypt it again, so it would provide no benefit. (See Chapter 5 for details of the intranode datapath.) Transparent Encryption is performed after any resolution of ClusterIP services, so packets bound for a service’s virtual IP will be encrypted only if the selected backend is on another node. Similarly, with layer 7 network policy, the packets are encrypted only after leaving the per-node Envoy proxy on egress and are decrypted on ingress just before entering the Envoy proxy on the remote node. The encryption and encapsulation in the datapath are performed entirely using the kernel’s implementation of WireGuard.2 Cilium is responsible for key distribution, configuring the WireGuard tunnels, and steering packets into those tunnels.
    一个常见的问题是，Cilium 为什么不对同一节点上 Pod 之间的流量进行加密。这看似是一个奇怪的遗漏，但理由很简单：当数据包在同一节点上的 Pod 之间传输时，Cilium 的 eBPF 数据路径会在内核中直接将数据包从一个 Pod 的网络接口移动到另一个 Pod 的网络接口。Pod 之间不存在 Linux 网桥、虚拟交换机或其他网络系统。如果 Cilium 在这里也执行加密，就会对流量进行加密后立即解密，所以毫无意义。（关于节点内数据路径的细节，请参阅第 5 章。）透明加密在任何 ClusterIP Service 的解析之后执行，因此发往 Service 虚拟 IP 的数据包只有在所选后端位于另一节点时才会被加密。类似地，在启用七层网络策略时，数据包在出站时会先经过每个节点的 Envoy 代理，然后才被加密；在入站时，数据包在远程节点上进入 Envoy 代理之前被解密。数据路径中的加密和封装完全通过内核的 WireGuard 实现来完成2。Cilium 负责密钥分发、配置 WireGuard 隧道以及将数据包引导至这些隧道。

    Encapsulation
    封装

    WireGuard uses a layer 3 encapsulation method—the packet up to the IP header (including TCP/UDP headers and any layer 7 headers) is encrypted unaltered and sent as a payload in a WireGuard UDP packet on port 51871, which is the default WireGuard port used in Cilium. On the remote node, the encapsulated packet is unwrapped and the original packet decrypted before being delivered to the target pod. As shown in Figure 14-4, for clusters using tunneling routing modes such as VXLAN, the encryption occurs after the tunnel encapsulation is performed. Because VXLAN encapsulates at layer 2, a packet on the wire between nodes has an encrypted payload containing the application payload,3 inner TCP header, inner IP header, inner Ethernet frame, VXLAN header, outer UDP header, and outer IP header. The encrypted payload has a WireGuard header, UDP header, IPv4 header, and Ethernet frame header, along with a WireGuard footer.
    WireGuard 使用三层封装方法——原始数据包（从二层以太网帧到传输层 TCP/UDP 报头及所有七层应用数据）保持不变地被加密，作为载荷在端口 51871（Cilium 中使用的默认 WireGuard 端口）上的 WireGuard UDP 数据包中发送。在远程节点上，封装的数据包被解包，原始数据包被解密后交付到目标 Pod。如图 14-4 所示，对于使用 VXLAN 等隧道路由模式的集群，加密发生在隧道封装之后。由于 VXLAN 在二层进行封装，节点之间的线路上的一个数据包会包含加密载荷（其中包含应用载荷3、内层 TCP 报头、内层 IP 报头、内层以太网帧）、VXLAN 报头、外层 UDP 报头和外层 IP 报头。加密载荷具有一个 WireGuard 报头、UDP 报头、IPv4 报头和以太网帧报头，以及一个 WireGuard 尾部。
    2This requires Linux kernel 5.6 or later, but nearly all modern distributions meet this requirement (with the exception of older long-term support releases such as Ubuntu 20.04 LTS). 3VXLAN encapsulation can occur after packet fragmentation, so a single VXLAN packet may not contain a complete layer 3 packet.
    2这要求 Linux 内核版本为 5.6 或更高，但几乎所有现代发行版都满足这一要求（旧的长期支持版本如 Ubuntu 20.04 LTS 除外）。3VXLAN 封装可能在数据包分片之后发生，因此单个 VXLAN 数据包可能不包含完整的三层数据包。
    For clusters in native routing mode, there is no double encapsulation from VXLAN, but pod-to-pod packets are still encapsulated in a WireGuard packet before being sent between nodes, as shown in Figure 14-5.
    对于使用原生路由模式的集群，虽然没有 VXLAN 带来的双重封装，但 Pod 间的数据包在节点之间发送之前仍会被封装到一个 WireGuard 数据包中，如图 14-5 所示。
    Even in a natively routed cluster using Transparent Encryption, it’s still recommended to make your pod CIDR ranges routable to the underlying network (see Chapters 5 and 10 for details) to handle node-to-pod, pod-to-node, and north–south traffic from pods.
    即使在使用透明加密的原生路由集群中，仍然建议将 Pod CIDR 范围配置为可路由到底层网络（详见第 5 章和第 10 章），以处理节点到 Pod、Pod 到节点以及来自 Pod 的南北向流量。

    Encryption and Authentication
    加密与认证

    WireGuard prevents observers on the network from observing the contents of the packets, or even directly observing which pods are communicating, by encrypting traffic. However, they can still see that communication is taking place. For example, an attacker could observe which nodes are communicating, how big the packets are, and how regular they are. This information could be used to infer the difference between streaming content (regular packets of similar size) and request/response interactions (a small set of packets in one direction followed by a larger burst of packets in the opposite direction), or to “map” an application topology between nodes. If this kind of implied data is sensitive in your application, then evaluating Transparent Encryption for your use case is beyond the scope of this book. WireGuard is also responsible for authentication between nodes. When the Cilium agent starts with WireGuard encryption enabled, it generates a public/private key pair and attaches the public key to its CiliumNode object as an annotation named network.cilium.io/wg-pub-key. Other nodes then use these annotations to discover the public keys of their peers in the cluster. The WireGuard tunnels are configured to only accept traffic signed with the expected key pair. This effectively protects traffic in transit from tampering or spoofing, because key distribution is done entirely via the Kubernetes API, and the security of the WireGuard encryption is predicated on the security of the Kubernetes API plane.
    WireGuard 通过加密流量来防止网络上的观察者看到数据包的内容，甚至无法直接看到哪些 Pod 正在通信。不过，他们仍然可以看到通信正在发生。例如，攻击者可以观察到哪些节点在通信、数据包有多大以及传输的规律性如何。这些信息可以用来推断流式内容（大小相近且规律的数据包）和请求/响应交互（一个方向上的少量数据包后跟相反方向上的大量数据包）之间的区别，或者用来在节点之间"绘制"应用拓扑结构。如果你的应用中这类可以被推断的数据属于敏感信息，那么是否使用透明加密需要超出本书范围的进一步评估。WireGuard 还负责节点之间的认证。当 Cilium Agent 以启用 WireGuard 加密的方式启动时，它会生成一对公钥/私钥，并将公钥作为注解 network.cilium.io/wg-pub-key 附加到其 CiliumNode 对象上。其他节点随后会使用这些注解来发现集群中对等节点的公钥。WireGuard 隧道被配置为仅接受使用预期密钥对签名的流量。这有效地保护了传输中的流量免受篡改或欺骗，因为密钥分发完全通过 Kubernetes API 完成，而 WireGuard 加密的安全性依赖于 Kubernetes API 平面的安全性。

    Configuring and Validating Transparent Encryption
    配置与验证透明加密

    Encryption is enabled in Cilium using the following Helm values:
    Cilium 通过以下 Helm 值启用加密：
    代码（原文）
    encryption:
enabled: true
type: wireguard
    If you are enabling encryption on an existing cluster, you must restart the Cilium agents and the operator to see the changes apply. You should expect to see traffic drops and disruptions during this process. In this example, we’ll be using a local cluster with kind. To be able to demonstrate routing between nodes, we’re going to create a cluster with one control-plane node and two worker nodes. We’ll use native routing and enable the kube-proxy replacement to simplify the datapath and let us concentrate on observing the encryption mode. Launch the test cluster (kind.yaml) with kind create cluster --configchapter14/kind.yaml --name ch14, taking care to name it ch14, and install Cilium with Helm using the provided values file (cilium.yaml). Once the cluster is running and Cilium is installed and ready, we can test that Transparent Encryption is enabled by checking the output of cilium status. Run the following command from the Cilium CLI. The encryption status isn’t visible in the normal output, but it is in the extended JSON output:
    如果你要在现有集群上启用加密，必须重启 Cilium Agent 和 Operator 才能使变更生效。在这个过程中，你应该预期会出现流量丢失和中断。在本示例中，我们将使用一个基于 kind 的本地集群。为了能够演示节点之间的路由，我们将创建一个包含一个控制平面节点和两个工作节点的集群。我们将使用原生路由并启用 kube-proxy 替代，以简化数据路径并让我们专注于观察加密模式。使用 kind create cluster --configchapter14/kind.yaml --name ch14 启动测试集群（kind.yaml），注意将其命名为 ch14，并使用提供的值文件（cilium.yaml）通过 Helm 安装 Cilium。集群运行且 Cilium 安装完成并就绪后，我们可以通过检查 cilium status 的输出来验证透明加密是否已启用。在 Cilium CLI 中运行以下命令。加密状态在正常输出中不可见，但在扩展的 JSON 输出中可以看到：
    代码（原文）
    $ cilium status -o json \
| jq '.cilium_status[].encryption.wireguard.interfaces'
[
{
"listen-port": 51871,
"name": "cilium_wg0",
"peer-count": 3,
"peers": null,
"public-key": "itVM9s3GXsjJa9xwNlwSxfT1VKGdyOaomN7wDE7mqwE="
}
],
[...]
    This shows us the public key for the node, and the fact that WireGuard is enabled. We can also view the public keys in the CiliumNode object in Kubernetes as an annotation:
    这向我们展示了该节点的公钥以及 WireGuard 已启用这一事实。我们还可以在 Kubernetes 的 CiliumNode 对象中将这些公钥作为注解查看：
    代码（原文）
    $ kubectl get ciliumnode -o json | jq "$(cat <<EOF
.items[] | {
"name": .metadata.name,
"wg-pub-key": .metadata.annotations["network.cilium.io/wg-pub-key"]
}
EOF
)"
{
"name": "ch14-control-plane",
"wg-pub-key": "qkCH1M3cYPFUzd/lyMRIGFMQxJ9bAPVwn6MrKakNGnw="
}
{
"name": "ch14-worker",
"wg-pub-key": "itVM9s3GXsjJa9xwNlwSxfT1VKGdyOaomN7wDE7mqwE="
}
{
"name": "ch14-worker2",
"wg-pub-key": "ZLCwOShY+Vj67R9yu4oQJwEshD9NAdeAtPT666wMb1k="
}
    These public keys are exchanged using the Kubernetes API, meaning that the authentication to the Kubernetes API itself is the root of trust for Transparent Encryption.
    这些公钥通过 Kubernetes API 进行交换，这意味着对 Kubernetes API 的认证本身就是透明加密的信任根。

    Testing Encryption
    测试加密

    We can test that Transparent Encryption is working by observing network traffic between nodes, just as our hypothetical attacker in Figure 14-1 would do. The testing we’re doing here isn’t enough to conclusively prove the strength of the encryption, only that the expected traffic is being passed through a WireGuard tunnel and is not transmitted in plain text. Comprehensive cryptanalysis is beyond the scope of this book.
    我们可以通过观察节点之间的网络流量来测试透明加密是否正常工作，就像图 14-1 中的假想攻击者所做的那样。我们这里进行的测试不足以确凿地证明加密的强度，只能证明预期的流量正在通过 WireGuard 隧道传输且没有以明文形式发送。全面的密码分析超出了本书的范围。

    Viewing Unencrypted Traffic
    查看未加密流量

    To begin with, we’ll get an idea of what unencrypted traffic looks like. Disable encryption by setting encryption.enabled=false and restarting the Cilium agent and operator:
    首先，我们来看看未加密流量的样子。通过设置 encryption.enabled=false 并重启 Cilium Agent 和 Operator 来禁用加密：
    代码（原文）
    $ helm upgrade --install \
cilium cilium/cilium \
--kube-context kind-ch14 \
--namespace kube-system \
--reuse-values \
--set "encryption.enabled=false"
$ helm rollout restart \
--context kind-ch14 \
--namespace kube-system \
deployment/cilium-operator \
daemonset/cilium
    We can view intranode traffic on our kind cluster by observing network flows on the host running our kind container images. How you access this host will depend on your personal setup, but once you have access, you can observe traffic using tcpdump. In our case, because we’re using kind containers on a virtual machine, the nodes are connected together using a network bridge in the VM. We can access it directly and observe traffic on the bridge. First, we’ll deploy our test pods—one for an NGINX web server and another for a client—along with a service for the web server (testbed.yaml). We use Kubernetes anti-affinity rules to force the two pods to be deployed on different nodes.4 If we didn’t do this, they could end up on the same node, and we wouldn’t be able to see the communication in this example. Once the pods are deployed and running, in another terminal we can begin to capture packets. By running a container directly on the Docker host with --networkhost and --privileged, we can access the network bridge used by kind. We can use
    我们可以通过在运行 kind 容器镜像的宿主机上观察网络流来查看 kind 集群上的节点内流量。你访问该宿主机的方式取决于你的个人设置，但一旦获得访问权限，就可以使用 tcpdump 观察流量。在我们的示例中，因为我们在虚拟机上使用 kind 容器，节点通过虚拟机中的网络网桥相互连接。我们可以直接访问它并在该网桥上观察流量。首先，我们将部署测试 Pod——一个 NGINX Web 服务器 Pod 和一个客户端 Pod——以及 Web 服务器的 Service（testbed.yaml）。我们使用 Kubernetes 反亲和性规则强制两个 Pod 部署在不同的节点上4。如果不这样做，它们最终可能位于同一节点，那样我们在这个示例中就无法看到通信流量。Pod 部署并运行后，我们可以在另一个终端中开始捕获数据包。通过在 Docker 宿主机上使用 --networkhost 和 --privileged 直接运行一个容器，我们可以访问 kind 使用的网络网桥。我们可以使用
    4See the Kubernetes documentation for more information on affinity and anti-affinity.
    4关于亲和性和反亲和性的更多信息，请参阅 Kubernetes 文档。
    docker network list to get the details on which bridge this is and dump packets from it in text using -A, with -i "<interface>" to select the bridge and -l to flush to stdout so we can filter in real time using grep. We’re looking for exposed bearer tokens in plain text:
    docker network list 来获取该网桥的详细信息，并使用 -A 以文本形式转储其中的数据包，用 -i "<interface>" 选择网桥，用 -l 刷新到标准输出，这样我们就可以使用 grep 实时过滤。我们要寻找的是以明文暴露的 bearer token：
    代码（原文）
    $ bridge_name="$(docker network list \
--format json \
| jq -r '.[] | select(.name == "kind") | .network_interface')"
$ docker run \
--network host \
--privileged \
-it \
nicolaka/netshoot \
tcpdump -lAi "$bridge_name" \
| grep "Bearer"
    With this running, we can issue a curl request from our test pod to our web server, which due to our anti-affinity rules we know will transit through the bridge:
    在这个运行中的同时，我们可以从测试 Pod 向 Web 服务器发起 curl 请求——由于我们设置了反亲和性规则，我们知道该请求一定会经过这个网桥：
    代码（原文）
    $ kubectl exec deploy/client \
-- curl -sIH "Authorization: Bearer MYSECRETTOKEN" \
webserver | head -n1
HTTP/1.1 200 OK
    Our NGINX server isn’t actually configured to require a bearer token, but this form of token in the header mechanism is common in HTTP services. Regardless, we can still see it in the output from our packet capture. You should see MYSECRETTOKEN output in your packet capture logs in your other terminal as you perform this test.
    我们的 NGINX 服务器实际上并没有配置为需要 bearer token，但这种形式的 Token 在 HTTP 服务的 Header 机制中很常见。无论如何，我们仍然可以在数据包捕获的输出中看到它。执行此测试时，你应该能在另一个终端中的数据包捕获日志里看到 MYSECRETTOKEN 被输出。

    Viewing Encrypted Traffic
    查看加密流量

    Let’s turn encryption back on and see what effect that has on our capture:
    让我们重新开启加密，看看对我们的捕获会有什么影响：
    代码（原文）
    $ helm upgrade --install \
cilium cilium/cilium \
--kube-context kind-ch14 \
--namespace kube-system \
--reuse-values \
--set "encryption.enabled=true"
$ helm rollout restart \
--context kind-ch14 \
--namespace kube-system \
deployment/cilium-operator \
daemonset/cilium
    Now when we run the same curl command, we won’t see the token exposed in the output. If we search for different terms, we won’t see any plain-text HTTP content at all. Instead, all traffic is encrypted and sent over a WireGuard tunnel on port 51871; the underlying network fabric only sees UDP traffic. If we capture traffic on the WireGuard tunnel port instead, we can see the encrypted data. We won’t use the plain-text output this time, but instead will get the hex output with -XX and verbose header information with -v. Instead of using grep, we’ll pass a PCAP filter directly to tcpdump for udp and port 51871:
    现在，当我们运行相同的 curl 命令时，不会再在输出中看到暴露的 Token。如果我们搜索不同的关键词，也不会看到任何明文 HTTP 内容。取而代之的是，所有流量都被加密并通过端口 51871 上的 WireGuard 隧道发送；底层网络结构只能看到 UDP 流量。如果我们改为在 WireGuard 隧道端口上捕获流量，就可以看到加密的数据。这次我们不会使用明文输出，而是使用 -XX 获取十六进制输出，用 -v 获取详细的报头信息。我们不会使用 grep，而是直接将 PCAP 过滤器传递给 tcpdump，过滤条件为 udp and port 51871：
    代码（原文）
    $ docker run \
--network host \
--privileged \
-it \
nicolaka/netshoot \
tcpdump -vlXXi "$bridge_name" udp and port 51871
    Once the capture is running, try issuing the curl command again in another terminal. You should see output like the following in the first terminal:
    捕获开始运行后，试着在另一个终端中再次发出 curl 命令。你应该会在第一个终端中看到类似如下的输出：
    代码（原文）
    tcpdump: listening on docker1, link-type EN10MB (Ethernet), snapshot length
262144 bytes

0x0050:  6165 3c1c bece 4f1d 5d08 52ad 2167 521e  ae<...O.].R.!gR.

    From this, we can see that otherwise unencrypted traffic is correctly encrypted and that this change is invisible to the underlying pods.
    从这里我们可以看到，原本未加密的流量被正确地加密了，而且这种变化对底层的 Pod 是不可见的。

    Viewing Unencrypted Flows
    查看未加密流

    Finally, as a counterexample, we can investigate what happens for traffic between a node and a pod. We’ll patch the client test deployment to use the host network namespace instead:
    最后，作为一个反例，我们来研究一下节点与 Pod 之间的流量会发生什么。我们将把客户端测试部署修改为使用宿主网络命名空间：
    代码（原文）
    $ kubectl patch \
--context kind-ch14 \
deploy client \
--type json \
--patch-file /dev/stdin <<EOF
[
{
"op": "replace",
"path": "/spec/template/spec/hostNetwork",
"value": true
},
{
"op": "replace",
"path": "/spec/template/spec/dnsPolicy",
"value": "ClusterFirstWithHostNet"
}
]
EOF
deployment.apps/client patched
    Once the deployment has completed, we can reissue our curl command. This time, we’ll see that even with Transparent Encryption enabled, traffic from a node—in this case, a pod in the host’s network namespace—to a pod on another node is not encrypted.
    部署完成后，我们可以重新发出 curl 命令。这一次，我们会看到，即使启用了透明加密，来自节点（在本例中是宿主网络命名空间中的一个 Pod）发往另一节点上 Pod 的流量也不会被加密。

    Summary
    小结

    Transparent Encryption covers a common service mesh use case by enforcing encryption of data in transit between nodes across the network. Cilium accomplishes this without using mTLS, allowing it to function on application traffic that is not HTTP, including UDP traffic. The encryption is completely transparent to the applications and requires no changes from them to be compatible.
    透明加密通过在整个网络中强制对节点之间的传输中数据进行加密，覆盖了服务网格的一个常见应用场景。Cilium 在不使用 mTLS 的情况下实现这一目标，这使其能够对非 HTTP 的应用流量（包括 UDP 流量）也正常工作。加密对应用完全透明，应用无需做出任何变更即可兼容。

    第 15 章
    Observability with Hubble
    使用 Hubble 实现可观测性

    Hubble is built on top of Cilium and eBPF, providing deep visibility into how applications communicate and behave. It focuses primarily on networking observability, helping operators understand dependencies between services and identify performance or security issues. What makes Hubble particularly valuable is that it provides visibility not only at layers 3 and 4, where most flow tools operate, but also at layer 7. This means it can reveal application-level information such as HTTP methods or DNS queries, giving you a more complete picture of traffic behavior. Hubble also surfaces security-related context, such as blocked connections and the policies that caused them, allowing you to refine network policies based on observed flows. All of this happens transparently, without requiring any modification to the applications themselves. In addition to live flow visibility, Hubble exposes Prometheus metrics, enabling integration with common observability tools such as Prometheus and Grafana for monitoring and alerting.
    Hubble 构建在 Cilium 和 eBPF 之上，能够深入洞察应用程序的通信方式和行为。它主要聚焦于网络可观测性，帮助运维人员理解服务之间的依赖关系，并识别性能或安全问题。Hubble 的特别之处在于，它不仅在大多数流工具所工作的第 3 层和第 4 层提供可见性，还在第 7 层提供可见性。这意味着它能够揭示 HTTP 方法或 DNS 查询等应用层信息，让你对流量行为有更完整的认知。Hubble 还会呈现安全相关的上下文，例如被阻断的连接以及导致阻断的策略，使你能够根据观察到的流来优化网络策略。所有这一切都是透明完成的，无需对应用程序本身做任何修改。除了实时流可见性之外，Hubble 还暴露了 Prometheus 指标，可与 Prometheus、Grafana 等常见可观测性工具集成，用于监控和告警。

    Hubble Architecture
    Hubble 架构

    Before we start using Hubble, it is worth taking a moment to understand how its main components fit together. Hubble collects information by tapping into Cilium’s eBPF datapath. A Hubble agent runs on every node as part of the Cilium DaemonSet; it is not a separate pod. Each agent reports flow data from its own node only, which means querying a single agent provides a limited view of cluster-wide activity. To gain a more holistic picture of flows across the cluster, Hubble introduces an aggregation layer called Hubble Relay. Relay runs as a deployment and aggregates data from all node-local agents, gathering their flow data and exposing it through a single API endpoint. Most users interact with Relay rather than with the agents directly, since it provides a unified cluster-wide view of network flows. Figure 15-1 illustrates that architecture.
    在开始使用 Hubble 之前，有必要花点时间了解其主要组件是如何协同工作的。Hubble 通过接入 Cilium 的 eBPF 数据路径来收集信息。Hubble Agent 作为 Cilium DaemonSet 的一部分运行在每个节点上，它不是一个独立的 Pod。每个 Agent 仅报告来自其所在节点的流数据，这意味着查询单个 Agent 只能获得集群范围活动的有限视图。为了获得跨集群流的更整体视图，Hubble 引入了一个名为 Hubble Relay 的聚合层。Relay 以 Deployment 形式运行，聚合所有节点本地 Agent 的数据，收集它们的流数据并通过统一的 API 端点对外暴露。大多数用户都是与 Relay 交互，而非直接与各个 Agent 交互，因为 Relay 提供了跨集群的统一网络流视图。图 15-1 展示了这一架构。
    You can find the YAML manifests you will use in this chapter in the chapter15 directory of the book’s GitHub repository.
    本章使用的 YAML 清单文件可在本书 GitHub 仓库的 chapter15 目录中找到。
    When Cilium is installed, the Hubble agent is automatically enabled by default. You can validate this on any Cilium-enabled cluster—for example, a generic kind cluster (kind.yaml) with a default Cilium installation (cilium install). You can then verify it from inside the Cilium agent, since the Hubble agent runs as part of that:
    安装 Cilium 后，Hubble Agent 默认会自动启用。你可以在任意启用了 Cilium 的集群上验证这一点——例如，一个通用的 kind 集群（kind.yaml）搭配默认的 Cilium 安装（cilium install）。随后你可以从 Cilium Agent 内部进行验证，因为 Hubble Agent 是作为 Cilium Agent 的一部分运行的：
    代码（原文）
    $ kubectl exec -n kube-system ds/cilium -c cilium-agent -- hubble status
Healthcheck (via unix:///var/run/cilium/hubble.sock): Ok
Current/Max Flows: 4,095/4,095 (100.00%)
Flows/s: 11.72
    The output confirms the Hubble agent is running and healthy. You can then query Hubble directly by connecting to the agent using kubectl exec. This method allows you to view current flows (from the current node only) by executing the following hubble observe command:
    输出结果确认 Hubble Agent 正在运行且健康。你可以通过使用 kubectl exec 连接到 Agent 来直接查询 Hubble。此方法允许你通过执行以下 hubble observe 命令查看当前节点的流（仅限当前节点）：
    代码（原文）
    $ kubectl exec -n kube-system ds/cilium -c cilium-agent -- hubble observe
10.244.0.10:33666 (host) ->
kube-system/coredns-674b8bbfcf-6wl44:8181 (ID:8159)
to-endpoint FORWARDED (TCP Flags: ACK, PSH)
10.244.0.10:59786 (host) ->
kube-system/coredns-674b8bbfcf-hjd28:8181 (ID:8159)
to-endpoint FORWARDED (TCP Flags: SYN)
10.244.0.10:59786 (host) <-
kube-system/coredns-674b8bbfcf-hjd28:8181 (ID:8159)
to-stack FORWARDED (TCP Flags: SYN, ACK)
    Each line represents a flow record that includes direction, source and destination information, and TCP flags. You can think of this as a context-aware version of tcpdump: it provides packet-level visibility but enriches it with Kubernetes metadata. In the example output shown here, the source is identified as the host, while the destination is one of the CoreDNS pods in the kube-system namespace.1 Each record also includes a numeric ID, which represents the endpoint’s identity within Cilium. You encountered these identities earlier in Chapter 12, where you saw how they are used to apply network policy and track flows.
    每一行代表一条流记录，包含方向、源与目的信息以及 TCP 标志。你可以将其视为具有上下文感知能力的 tcpdump：它提供数据包级别的可见性，但通过 Kubernetes 元数据进行了丰富。在上面的示例输出中，源被标识为主机，而目的是 kube-system 命名空间中的某个 CoreDNS Pod。1 每条记录还包含一个数字 ID，代表该端点在 Cilium 内部的安全身份。你在第 12 章已经接触过这些身份，了解到它们是如何被用来应用网络策略和跟踪流的。
    Be cautious about who is granted access to any of the Hubble data. Flow logs can include potentially sensitive information, such as source and destination addresses or HTTP headers. It’s best to limit access to only those who truly need it.
    需要注意授予谁访问 Hubble 数据的权限。流日志可能包含潜在的敏感信息，例如源和目的地址或 HTTP 头。最好将访问权限限制为真正需要的人员。
    By default, hubble observe only shows flows from the node where you run it, which may not be particularly helpful in a real-world scenario with numerous applications spread across multiple nodes. To aggregate flows from all nodes, you will need to use Hubble Relay.
    默认情况下，hubble observe 仅显示你运行命令所在节点的流，这在真实场景中（多个应用分布在多个节点上）可能不太实用。要聚合来自所有节点的流，你需要使用 Hubble Relay。

    Hubble Relay
    Hubble Relay

    Hubble Relay is not enabled by default. To enable it, you will need to add the following to the Cilium Helm values file (or, as shown in Chapter 3, simply use
    Hubble Relay 默认不会启用。要启用它，你需要在 Cilium Helm 值文件中添加以下配置（或者如第 3 章所示，直接使用
    代码（原文）
    cilium hubble enable):
    1In case you are wondering why CoreDNS is being accessed on ports 8181 and 8080 instead of the default port 53 for DNS, it’s because these ports are configured for readiness and liveness probes in the CoreDNS deployment.
    1 如果你好奇为什么 CoreDNS 在端口 8181 和 8080 而非默认 DNS 端口 53 上被访问，那是因为 CoreDNS Deployment 将这两个端口配置为就绪探针和存活探针。
    代码（原文）
    hubble:
relay:
enabled: true
    You should then see a separate deployment in the kube-system namespace:
    随后你应该会在 kube-system 命名空间中看到一个独立的 Deployment：
    代码（原文）
    $ kubectl get deployment -n kube-system hubble-relay
NAME           READY   UP-TO-DATE   AVAILABLE   AGE
hubble-relay   1/1     1            1           38m
    There are other ways to access Hubble besides using kubectl exec on the agent pod. This matters because kubectl exec grants broad privileges, given that the agent runs as a privileged pod. If you want to limit access to network observability only, use kubectl port-forward to connect to Hubble Relay instead:
    除了在 Agent Pod 上使用 kubectl exec 之外，还有其他方式访问 Hubble。之所以要关注这一点，是因为 Agent 以特权 Pod 运行，而 kubectl exec 会授予广泛的权限。如果你希望只限制对网络可观测性的访问，改用 kubectl port-forward 连接到 Hubble Relay 会更合适：
    代码（原文）
    $ kubectl port-forward -n kube-system deploy/hubble-relay 4245
Forwarding from 127.0.0.1:4245 -> 4245
Forwarding from [::1]:4245 -> 4245
    Using kubectl port-forward allows you to access Hubble Relay without exposing the entire node, making it a safer alternative for monitoring network traffic. You can then use the Hubble CLI2 to target Hubble Relay at 127.0.0.1:4245 without shell access to a privileged container.
    使用 kubectl port-forward 可以在不暴露整个节点的情况下访问 Hubble Relay，这是监控网络流量的更安全方式。之后你就可以使用 Hubble CLI2 连接到 127.0.0.1:4245 上的 Hubble Relay，而无需获得特权容器的 shell 访问权限。

    Hubble CLI
    Hubble CLI

    In the CLI, start by checking the status of the Hubble deployment with hubble
    在 CLI 中，首先使用 hubble
    代码（原文）
    status:

Connected Nodes: 3/3
    Through Relay, you can visualize flows across the whole cluster, rather than on a per-node basis:
    通过 Relay，你可以可视化整个集群范围内的流，而不是按节点分别查看：
    代码（原文）
    $ hubble observe

    2We covered installing the Hubble CLI in Chapter 3.
    2 我们在第 3 章介绍过安装 Hubble CLI 的方法。
    代码（原文）
    192.168.97.2:4244 (kube-apiserver)

192.168.97.2:4244 (kube-apiserver)
